Apotheosis – Ascension to Godhood
Vol.8

Chapter 701
Back then, after Ning Yudie was rescued and sent to the Cloud Sect to be nurtured, she carried the hatred of her entire family being annihilated. However, Ning Yudie had never cried.
In her little head, the only thought she had was to avenge her family.
Compared to the Blue Cloud Sect, the competition in the Cloud Sect was much more intense.
After all, the Blue Cloud Sect was located in a corner of the Cloud Sect. Those who competed with Luo Zheng were only children of landowning households. Those so-called great landowning households were not worth mentioning in the Central Domain.
The Cloud Sect, on the other hand, consisted of children from various third-rank aristocratic families. Although Ning Yudie was able to stand out in the Cloud Sect due to her outstanding talent, more importantly, Ning Yudie's perseverance.
At that time, she was just a little girl. Moreover, she was much smaller than the other disciples. Not to mention, she was also a foreigner. Naturally, she was constantly ostracized in the Cloud Sect.
However, Ning Yudie relied on her perseverance, gritted her teeth, and took one step at a time. Without anyone's support, she stood out among the millions of disciples in the Cloud Sect. At the same time, she also obtained the first place in the Road of Trials!
After entering the Cloud Hall, she became a dazzling star. She became Grandma Yu's disciple and was taught the 《Empyrean Fairy Heart Sutra》. After dispersing her cultivation eight times, Ning Yudie was able to sit on the position of Temple Master.
Ning Yudie was not a particularly strong person. In fact, she still had the mentality of a little girl. However, she firmly suppressed all her grievances, fear, and laziness, these negative emotions deep in her heart. She had never once vented them in front of others!
Even in the face of death, she was still calm and composed.
However, even though she had such self-control, after seeing Luo Zheng, she suddenly burst into tears, unable to control herself.
As early as when competing for the Rising Dragon Platform, Ning Yudie had already secretly given her heart to Luo Zheng. This young man suddenly broke into her heart. However, at that time, Ning Yudie still had many misgivings in her heart. It was because of these misgivings that she was unable to recognize her true feelings.
Later, after igniting the spirit lamp in the immortal tomb and entering the tomb, although she obtained abundant treasures, Luo Zheng disappeared.
At that time, Ning Yudie realized how important Luo Zheng was to her.
In only two years, there was no news of Luo Zheng. Ning Yudie once sent people from the Cloud Hall to search the entire Central Domain. She even issued several missions to find Luo Zheng........ the rewards of these missions were shocking..... it was said that many disciples of the Cloud Hall shared these missions to other sects.... and even disciples of the World Business Alliance and Void Spirit Sect. and even the disciples of the World Business Sect and the other sects and even the disciples of the World to the Cloud Hall.........
Because of these missions, Ning Yudie had been criticized by many Elders of the Cloud Hall.
. but Ning Yudie made them shut up with one sentence.
First of all, those rewards were paid by Ning Yudie. Moreover, Luo Zheng's importance to the Cloud Hall was self-evident. He had the strongest sect protection array in the entire Central Domain. This kind of talent would be worshipped like an ancestor in any sect. The Cloud Hall had no reason to not value him.
But even after mobilizing countless warriors to search for Luo Zheng, there was still no news. Luo Zheng seemed to have disappeared into thin air. A year later, Ning Yudie had no choice but to cancel this mission.
Even if she couldn't find Luo Zheng, Ning Yudie firmly believed that he would appear in front of her one day.
She just didn't expect that Luo Zheng would appear so suddenly and in such a timely manner …
"Are you done crying?" Luo Zheng stared at Ning Yudie's jade-like face and asked.
Ning Yudie's eyes were like pear blossoms with water. After her emotions were released like a floodgate, she gradually returned to her normal state. Thinking that she had lost her composure in front of Luo Zheng, her face suddenly became embarrassed. Her face blushed, and she turned away from Luo Zheng's embrace. She pursed her lips and asked, "Luo Zheng, where have you been these two years?"
Seeing Ning Yudie instantly return to her normal state with a serious and hurried look, Luo Zheng felt funny. However, he knew that Ning Yudie was thin-skinned. Perhaps speaking to him as the Hall Master was in line with her behavior.
Luo Zheng nodded, but then he smiled mischievously. "I went to a place you don't know."
Looking at Luo Zheng's mischievous smile, Ning Yudie's small mouth twitched. "Be serious! As a disciple of the Cloud Hall, you left the Cloud Hall without permission. According to the Cloud Hall's rules, you will be punished. "
Not far away, Xun heard Ning Yudie's words and couldn't help but roll her eyes. Compared to Ning Yudie, Xun's character was more straightforward. If she were Ning Yudie, she would definitely not talk to Luo Zheng like this. Why bother suppressing her innermost thoughts?
She should just hug Luo Zheng properly. She suppressed herself, but in the end, she was the one who suffered. She deserved it, Xun thought in her heart.
To put it bluntly, Xun thought that Ning Yudie was a little "pretentious".
However, Xun didn't understand Ning Yudie's experience growing up. Two equally outstanding women had completely different experiences growing up. Ning Yudie had almost become used to suppressing her innermost feelings. Just now, she was on the verge of collapse because she was too happy, so she broke down the fence that was suppressing herself. Now that she had calmed down, she naturally returned to her original self.
However, in Luo Zheng's eyes, Ning Yudie's "pretentious" behavior was a little cute. He still smiled and replied, "Then how will you punish me?"
Ning Yudie smiled. "I haven't thought about it yet! When I think about it, I will punish you! "
However, at this moment, Xun slowly floated over from the side and said to Ning Yudie, "How about I think about it for you?"
It wasn't that Ning Yudie didn't notice Xun's existence, but she was too far away to notice Xun's movements. She thought that Xun was just Luo Zheng's sword spirit. Now that Xun floated over and spoke, Ning Yudie was shocked.
After looking at Xun, she turned to Luo Zheng and asked, "Luo Zheng, what's the matter with your sword spirit?"
Luo Zheng shrugged. "As you can see, there is a soul in my sword spirit. You can say that she is the body of the sword spirit. Her name is Xun."
"Xun? Are you an alien? "Ning Yudie stared at Xun and asked. There was a hint of vigilance in her eyes.
Ning Yudie had been very curious for a long time. Why would Luo Zheng turn his sword spirit into such a beautiful woman? This woman, from her hair to her footsteps, from her face to her figure, was perfect except for a pair of pointed ears. Even Ning Yudie couldn't be compared with her. After all, Ning Yudie's height was a shortcoming.
Xun's height was almost the same as Luo Zheng's, so Ning Yudie was also a head shorter than Xun.
Xun glanced at Ning Yudie indifferently. How could she not feel a hint of hostility from Ning Yudie?
Ning Yudie had watched Luo Zheng enter the Cloud Hall and rise rapidly. She considered herself to be Luo Zheng's Hall Master, mentor, and guide. Moreover, she had already developed feelings of love for Luo Zheng. Therefore, when a strange woman suddenly appeared by Luo Zheng's side, even though she was only his sword spirit, she still felt some fear and vigilance.
Xun stood at a very high position, so she could see everything clearly. She had lived for a very long time, so she understood human nature better than Luo Zheng and Ning Yudie. Facing Ning Yudie's question, Xun smiled faintly. "Yes, I am from the Yao Ye Clan."
The Demon Clan and the Yao Clan had appeared in the Central Domain before, but the Yao Ye Clan had never appeared before. Ning Yudie was completely unfamiliar with this clan. Seeing Xun's smile, Ning Yudie felt a wave of displeasure. Therefore, she asked again, "Why are you staying by Luo Zheng's side?"
Xun blinked, a mischievous look in her eyes. Since you like to suppress your feelings, then I'll let you suppress it a little more. She giggled. "I am Luo Zheng's sword spirit. We were originally one."
Originally one … …
These words sounded very ambiguous, as if they were a conversation between lovers. Before Luo Zheng found a new body for Xun, Xun would always exist in the form of a sword spirit. Moreover, she would always reside in Luo Zheng's body.
Xun's words were indeed correct.
Ning Yudie frowned and stared at Luo Zheng. Clearly, she wanted Luo Zheng to explain the reason.
At this time, Luo Zheng said to Xun, "Xun, don't talk nonsense. It's a long story. I'll explain it to you later. Right now, you still have to deal with the Cloud Hall."

Chapter 702
"Something about the Cloud Hall? What's the matter? "Ning Yudie's eyes flashed.
"You'll know when we get out." Luo Zheng turned and walked towards the entrance of the ice palace.
At this moment, Ning Yudie and Xun looked at each other. Xun looked at Ning Yudie deeply. They seemed to understand something from each other's eyes.
Then, Xun turned into sword spirits and merged into Luo Zheng's body. Her consciousness was hidden in Luo Zheng's body. Her consciousness was curled up like a baby lying in Luo Zheng's body. Her face was slightly disappointed.
At this moment, Qinglong's consciousness secretly communicated with Xun. "Little girl, what are you disappointed about?"
Xun replied coldly, "Don't talk to me!"
The Azure Dragon was rebuffed and could only chuckle. On the other hand, the Red Dragon laughed and said, "This little girl also has something on her mind!"
"You keep calling me a little girl. Even if you're true dragons, you might not be older than me!" Xun replied coldly.
Xun was a half-step Sector Lord in the upper realm. Moreover, she was the ruler of the demon night clan. She was the spiritual totem of the entire demon night clan. With her experience, she really didn't care about the old dragons in Luo Zheng's mind.
Xun also knew about the true dragon world and the origin of the nine true dragons. However, they lived in Luo Zheng's mind while Xun was Luo Zheng's sword spirit. Everyone relied on Luo Zheng and didn't interfere with each other's business.
Now that Xun was in a bad mood, she wouldn't have anything good to say to them.
"You warriors don't measure your age by age. That's what mortals say. With your current attitude, if you're not a little girl, what are you? "Red Dragon didn't mind.
Xun closed her eyes and didn't say anything. She was still very depressed.
Luo Zheng naturally didn't know what had happened inside his body. Speaking of which, Luo Zheng's body was quite crowded. Not only were there nine true dragons, but there was also Smoke. As for the black fire in the mysterious furnace, it seemed to have its own consciousness, but its intelligence was relatively low …
Daoist Master Big Dream and the other two False Tribulation elders were still waiting outside the ice palace. The scene just now had truly frightened them.
However, Ning Yudie was finally alright, which was enough to make them feel gratified. As for the relationship between the Palace Master and Luo Zheng, they didn't need to guess. They could just pretend that they didn't see anything!
Fortunately, Ning Yudie didn't know that Daoist Master Big Dream and the others had already entered. Otherwise, she would have a psychological burden.
After strolling out of the Ice Palace, she faintly glanced at Daoist Dameng and immediately said, "Dameng, Luo Zheng said something happened in the Cloud Hall when I was attacking the Life and Death Realm. What happened? Was it serious? "
Daoist Master Big Dream hurriedly came forward and bowed before saying, "Replying to the Palace Master, a few days ago when you were attacking the Life and Death Realm, Mister Zhuo led the Zhuo, Fei, Shen, and other aristocratic families in a rebellion!"
"What!" Ning Yudie's expression changed. She didn't expect that a rebellion would happen when she was attacking the Life and Death Realm.
Although Ning Yudie already knew that Mister Zhuo wasn't convinced and was brooding over Ning Yudie's position, this had never happened in the Cloud Hall before.
However, such a thing had never happened in the history of Cloud Palace. One had to admit that Ning Yudie was too naive and inexperienced. If it were the previous Palace Masters of Cloud Palace, they would not have chosen to break through to Life and Death Realm at this time.
"However, the rebellion has already been quelled. Mister Zhuo was personally killed by Luo Zheng, and the other rebels have already been captured!" Daoist Master Big Dream reported.
Ning Yudie took a deep breath, and her expression gradually turned cold, "Looks like those third rank aristocratic families aren't convinced. Then let's make them understand who the Palace Master of the Cloud Hall is!"
Before long, the entire Cloud Hall slowly floated towards Cloud Sea City. The Cloud Hall floated above Cloud Sea City all year round, and it would only leave Cloud Sea City temporarily when something major happened.
Many third rank aristocratic families in Cloud Sea City were looking forward to it!
Although the rebellion was led by Mister Zhuo, it was also the result of the support of many third rank aristocratic families. All the third rank aristocratic families in Cloud Sea City were extremely disappointed that Ning Yudie had taken the position of Palace Master. This position originally belonged to the Zhuo Family, Chen Family, Fei Family, and Wu Family!
Now, it had fallen into the hands of an outsider, and it was even a little girl. How could these third rank aristocratic families swallow this insult?
So, as soon as Mister Zhuo stepped forward, he quickly obtained the support of the other third rank aristocratic families. The Patriarch of the Fei Family, Fei Longxiang, even personally stepped forward and participated in the rebellion. However, the dignified Patriarch of the Fei Family was instantly annihilated by Hua Tianming!
However, news of what happened in the Cloud Hall hadn't spread to Cloud Sea City. After the Cloud Hall left Cloud Sea City, all information about it had been completely sealed off. For the sake of avoiding any accidents, Mister Zhuo didn't allow any cultivators to enter or leave after he activated a barrier!
So, the news of Fei Longxiang's death and Mister Zhuo's death at the hands of Luo Zheng hadn't spread from the Cloud Hall, and many aristocratic families in Cloud Sea City weren't aware of it as well.
"Look! The Cloud Hall is back! "A cultivator from the Zhuo Family said.
"Looks like Mister Zhuo has probably succeeded. That little bitch Ning Yudie has probably been executed, right?" It was a cultivator from the Fei Family who spoke. He smacked his lips and said, "What a pity. Such a tasty beauty has fallen just like that. It's quite a waste!"
The Patriarch of the Chen Family said worriedly, "But the two deacons of the Cloud Sect reported that Luo Zheng went to the Cloud Hall a few days ago. Has Luo Zheng really returned?"
"Hmph! Luo Zheng? Even if he has returned, he might not be able to enter the Cloud Hall. That Sect Protecting Formation … "Another cultivator was halfway through his sentence when the Patriarch of the Chen Family interrupted him," The Sect Protecting Formation was set up by Luo Zheng himself. I presume Luo Zheng has a way to break it! "
At this moment, the Patriarch of the Wu Family said, "So what if he can break it? Mister Zhuo has led so many False Tribulation Realm experts. How can a mere Luo Zheng resist them? "
The Patriarch of the Chen Family shook his head, "I'm just slightly worried. I keep having a bad premonition."
"Don't worry. Once our Zhuo Family takes control of the Cloud Hall, our four great families won't be left behind!" A cultivator from the Zhuo Family said complacently. Once Mister Zhuo succeeded, his Zhuo Family would be the biggest beneficiary!
While they were speaking, the Cloud Hall had already floated to the sky above Cloud Sea City.
The Cloud Hall that had disappeared for a long time had returned. Many mortals walked out and looked up at it.
Even though these mortals lived in Cloud Sea City and there were countless cultivators around them, they didn't care much about the disputes, wars, and killings in the world of cultivators. In their eyes, the Cloud Hall would always be a lofty existence. Those who could enter the Cloud Hall were immortals who had been reborn and were worthy of their worship. As for the Cloud Hall itself, it was a miraculous existence and the most beautiful scenery in Cloud Sea City!
"Let's go up!" A cultivator from the Fei Family said with a smile.
"Let's go!"
"What's wrong with Mister Zhuo? Could it be that he doesn't even want to show his face after becoming the Hall Master?"
Swoosh! Swoosh! Swoosh!
Swoosh! Over 10 figures flew towards the Cloud Hall. But just as they were halfway there, they suddenly felt a dense cold energy envelop them.
That cold energy contained waves of killing intent, and it instantly caused their hearts to palpitate. Fear instantly transformed into sharp claws that grabbed their hearts.
Even those slow-witted mortals felt an extremely powerful aura emanate from the Cloud Hall.
There was an independent platform built at the edge of the Cloud Hall. That independent platform was extended by a stone pillar that was a few feet wide. Ning Yudie was standing on that small platform. She wore her Mystic Ice Armor, and her long Ice Phoenix Plume Pendant was about 10 meters long. It hung down from behind Ning Yudie and swayed in the air. Her straight and well-proportioned legs were pressed together.
Ning Yudie's cold gaze looked down at Cloud Sea City. At the same time, she suddenly released her aura. That extremely cold aura wasn't inferior to any male cultivator!
"This aura … It's a Life and Death Realm expert!"
"This is Ning Yudie's aura. She has succeeded in breaking through to the Life and Death Realm!"
"Oh no! Oh no! Did Mister Zhuo fail? Or did he not make a move? "

Chapter 703
As the Hall Master of the Cloud Hall, Ning Yudie already had absolute control over the Cloud Hall when she was at the late stage of the False Tribulation Realm.
The position of Hall Master was just an empty title. If she wanted to subdue the Elders, Deacons, and disciples of the Cloud Hall, she needed her own strength.
Ning Yudie's strength was only below Granny Yu's.
The great clans' calculations were very good. Taking advantage of Ning Yudie's tribulation to launch an attack, they chose the right time.
Because Ning Yudie chose to undergo tribulation at this time, the great clans even mocked Ning Yudie when they were plotting the rebellion. In the end, she was only a little girl. Although her talent was outstanding, she couldn't calculate the complexity of the human heart.
Unfortunately, luck wasn't on their side. Luo Zheng being able to appear at the right time was Luo Zheng's luck, and also Ning Yudie's luck!
Now that Ning Yudie had stepped into the Life and Death Realm, it was difficult for anyone in the Cloud Hall to have any thoughts of rebellion!
How complicated were the feelings of the great clans' Clan Heads at this moment? How fearful were they?
Ning Yudie's body gently tilted forward, her entire body falling head first towards the ground. Her long Ice Phoenix feathers trailed behind her, making her look like a Phoenix descending from the sky.
Soon after, Luo Zheng, Daoist Dameng, and the group of False Tribulation Realm Elders followed closely behind. They left the Cloud Hall one after another and flew towards the martial artists of the great clans.
After landing to a certain distance, Ning Yudie suddenly froze in mid-air, staring coldly at the Patriarchs and said, "Mister Zhuo has already been executed, I don't know what the Patriarchs have to say about the rebellion. What explanation do you have for the rebellion? Don't tell me that you didn't know about it? "
The Cloud Hall was above the Cloud Sea City, and it also protected the great clans' steady development. The two sides complemented each other, and now it had come to this.
The Family Heads of the third rank families all stared blankly at Ning Yudie. They were all speechless!
The heads of these aristocratic families all had families and mouths to feed. If it wasn't for the fact that they had absolute confidence, they wouldn't have tried to overthrow Ning Yudie. But now, they realized that this so-called "absolute confidence" was nothing more than a joke.
"I know that all of you feel that the Cloud Hall is in my hands, and it has always been a thorn in your hearts." Ning Yudie continued, "But today, I want to say that the Cloud Hall doesn't belong to your Fei Clan, nor does it belong to the Shen Clan. The Cloud Hall is the Cloud Hall. It's a fifth-grade sect in Cloud Sea City!"
In the eyes of these aristocratic families, the Cloud Hall had to belong to them. Indeed, in the early days of the Cloud Hall, these aristocratic families had made great contributions to the Cloud Hall. But when these aristocratic families promoted the Cloud Hall, they obtained considerable benefits as well!
However, they'd forgotten that they weren't the only people in the Cloud Hall. There were many cultivators from other regions as well. If a sect wanted to flourish, it absolutely couldn't rely on just a few aristocratic families. It had to rely on tens of thousands of cultivators, countless elites, and geniuses to develop bit by bit!
The expressions of the Family Heads dimmed. They didn't even have the chance to escape, and they could only be at Ning Yudie's disposal. They'd developed in Cloud Sea City for so many years, and their foundations were deeply rooted in the city. How could they leave so easily?
Not to mention that they were in troubled times now, and they could still preserve their lives under the protection of the Cloud Hall. If they left Cloud Sea City, it would be impossible for a third rank family to find a place to stay.
It didn't take long for the Family Heads to lower their heads. Among them, the Chen Family Head said, "I'm at your disposal, Hall Master."
Ning Yudie grunted coldly, "Since it's like that, then your four aristocratic families will be removed from the Cloud Hall! Exterminated!"
As soon as Ning Yudie spoke, the expressions of the cultivators from the Zhuo, Chen, Fei, and Wu Families changed drastically.
Not only that, but the expressions of Daoist Dameng, Luo Zheng, and the Void Tribulation Realm cultivators behind them changed drastically as well. Never had they imagined that Ning Yudie would actually be so ruthless!
The Chen Family Head knew that he would face severe punishment from Ning Yudie. Rebellion was unforgivable in any sect. However, he thought that since the four aristocratic families were the pillars of the Cloud Hall, they were inextricably linked to the Cloud Hall. Most of the cultivators from the four aristocratic families were in the Cloud Hall. From disciples to Deacons to Elders, there were people from the four aristocratic families!
Even among the Elders behind Ning Yudie, there were people from the four aristocratic families, such as Fei Han and the others. However, although Fei Han belonged to the Fei Family, he strongly opposed the rebellion. In the end, he chose a neutral position and didn't want to help.
Even if Ning Yudie wanted to punish them, it was impossible for her to truly exterminate the four aristocratic families. That would implicate too many people.
They never imagined that Ning Yudie would directly make such an announcement. Naturally, they were frightened.
Seeing the dark faces of these Family Heads, Ning Yudie sneered and said, "Now, do you know fear?"
"Hall Master, our four aristocratic families …" The Wu Family Head was about to speak.
Ning Yudie simply interrupted the Wu Family Head's words, "Shut up! When you rebelled, you naturally wanted to take my life, so why can't I take your lives today? Could it be … you think that because I, Ning Yudie, am a woman, you can bully me? "
Ning Yudie's words hit the nail on the head. They really thought that she was a little girl, too easy to bully!
Only now did they understand that Ning Yudie's methods weren't inferior to any of the previous Hall Masters.
"Hall Master, now that the Tian Xie Sect is closing in step by step, the Cloud Hall is in need of people. Isn't this too …" Daoist Master Dameng stood up and pleaded for leniency on behalf of the four aristocratic families.
There were too many people who wanted to move against the four aristocratic families. If Ning Yudie really did this, the entire Cloud Hall would be in chaos. He was afraid that Ning Yudie would really throw a tantrum and ignore the big picture.
Ning Yudie sneered and continued, "Big Dream is right. Since that's the case, your heads will be put on my tab. Also … those elders who were captured, your four aristocratic families can redeem them with ransom. One thousand top-grade true essence stones per person!"
Ning Yudie naturally wouldn't do such a stupid thing. She also calculated that someone would come out to stop her. Throwing out such ruthless words was just to scare people. When Daoist Master Dameng came out, she would use Daoist Master Dameng's words as a way to step down and then take the opportunity to extort them.
Under this kind of carrot and stick, the effect was really surprisingly good.
It had to be said that at this time, even Luo Zheng and Daoist Master Dameng admired Ning Yudie. They also admired Grandma Yu's vision even more. Ning Yudie indeed had the ability to take charge of the Cloud Hall. This ability wasn't just worthy of Ning Yudie's talent.
When the several family heads heard Ning Yudie's words, they immediately breathed a sigh of relief. The Hall Master intended to let them go, but when they heard that one thousand top-grade true essence stones would be used to redeem one person, their faces suddenly revealed a bitter expression.
Top-grade true essence stones were the largest currency in the Central Domain. Even when these Patriarchs passed through their hands, they would only use medium-grade and high-grade true essence stones to settle their transactions. A single top-grade true essence stone was equal to a million low-grade true essence stones, and a thousand top-grade true essence stones were equal to a billion low-grade true essence stones!
In the past, the Cloud Hall had spent several thousand top-grade true essence stones in order to cooperate with Luo Zheng in drawing up the Sect Protecting Great Array. It had almost taken away half of the Cloud Hall's wealth. The entire Cloud Hall only had a little over ten thousand top-grade true essence stones!
However, the four great families were different from the Cloud Hall. Each family had their own businesses and businesses. Under the protection of the Cloud Hall, their annual income was quite rich. However, if one person was worth one thousand top-grade true essence stones, then twenty Virtual Tribulation Realm martial artists would be worth over twenty thousand top-grade true essence stones!
Even if the four great families emptied their pockets, it would still be difficult to gather this much!
"Hall Master … this, this is too expensive! I'm afraid our Wu Family won't be able to gather this much! "The Wu Family Head said with a bitter expression.
Ning Yudie coldly snorted, "Whether you can gather enough or not, you can think of a way. If you can't gather enough, then kill the rest!"
The family heads exchanged glances, not knowing what to say. Ning Yudie's action seemed benevolent, but in fact, she was taking advantage of the opportunity to seize all of the savings of the four great families!
Martial artists also recognized money. After their family's wealth was emptied, the impact the four great families would suffer was unimaginable.
Seeing the bitter expressions of the family heads, Luo Zheng floated in front of Ning Yudie and said with a grin, "If you can't gather enough, I have a way to help you!"
"What way?" The Wu Family Head stared at Luo Zheng and asked.
Luo Zheng chuckled, "Pay in installments."
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 704
The Cloud Hall's main purpose was to nurture stronger martial artists, and its main source of income was the issuance of various missions.
Disciples would receive rewards after accepting missions, but the bulk of the rewards would fall into the hands of the Cloud Hall. However, this method of earning money was obviously not as fast as those aristocratic families.
The various clans and clans had their own mines, ticket companies, and even the teleportation formations in Ocean of Clouds City were built by them. For example, the teleportation formation between Ocean of Clouds City and Heavenly Awakening City had to pay a toll for every single person who entered it.
Moreover, Cloud Sea City had tens of millions of mortals. Although these mortals couldn't compare to martial artists, and the currency they used to trade with was gold and silver, it was still an astronomical figure. This gold and silver could be slowly converted into low-grade true essence stones during the process of trading with martial artists, and then converged into mid-grade true essence stones, and finally fell into the hands of the aristocratic families.
These aristocratic families really couldn't take out so many top-grade true essence stones in a single breath. Even the Cloud Hall couldn't take out 20,000 top-grade true essence stones in a single breath, but it didn't matter. Luo Zheng's plan was very simple. If they couldn't take it out, they would owe it and pay a portion of it every year.
"Remember, there's still interest!" Luo Zheng smiled faintly.
When the several Family Heads heard Luo Zheng's words, the corners of their mouths twitched. Ning Yudie was already fierce enough, and this Luo Zheng wasn't much worse. How could the younger generation be so fierce? Their cultivation speed was terrifying, their talent and strength were frightening, and all of them were ruthless masters!
But today, they had to accept it even if they didn't want to. How could they have a choice?
After reaching an agreement, these Family Heads hurriedly returned home to gather true essence stones. Luo Zheng didn't stay, but went straight to the Cloud Hall. Before this, he didn't have much time to gather with Hua Tianming and the others. A few days ago, he was worried about Ning Yudie, so he didn't say a word to Hua Tianming and the others. Now that he was back, he naturally had to have a good chat.
Ning Yudie originally wanted to call out to Luo Zheng, but when the words reached her mouth, she swallowed them back down.
When Daoist Big Dream saw Ning Yudie's expression, he naturally didn't dare to speak. Personal feelings, personal feelings, as subordinates, it wasn't good for them to get involved.
In a small pavilion in the Cloud Hall, Luo Zheng, Hua Tianming, Pei Tianyao, and Baili Hongfeng were gathered together. The table was filled with exquisite dishes and strong liquor. With their cultivation, even if they drank a hundred barrels of strong liquor, they would not get drunk, but they had not seen each other for a long time, so they had to drink to liven things up.
Hua Tianming downed the wine in his cup and wiped it with his sleeve. He smiled bitterly and said, "I thought I might be able to fight you after I opened my Sword Eye. Now, it seems like I was thinking too much."
This was Hua Tianming's personality, he would never admit defeat!
When he was in the Blue Cloud Sect, Hua Tianming treated Pei Tianyao and Luo Zheng as his biggest rivals. But, after he left the Blue Cloud Sect and entered the Cloud Hall, Hua Tianming gradually surpassed Pei Tianyao. But, during the Martial Dao Competition in Heavenly Awakening City, Hua Tianming realized that the gap between him and Luo Zheng was getting bigger and bigger. They were no longer on the same level.
Even so, Hua Tianming didn't give up. He still focused on comprehending the Heavenly Sword he was cultivating.
This continued until Hua Tianming awakened his Heavenly Eye, allowing him to gather the Force of the Heavens!
With this, Hua Tianming's overall strength had skyrocketed!
How many people in the Central Domain could kill three Void Tribulation warriors with a single strike? What was even scarier was that Hua Tianming was only at the Mid Divine Core realm! A Mid Divine Core warrior was equivalent to a fifth to sixth rank warrior. Even Luo Zheng couldn't do this when he was at his current level!
At this moment, Hua Tianming felt that he could fight Luo Zheng. But, Luo Zheng had been missing for a long time, and he had no idea what level Luo Zheng had reached.
But, two years later, Luo Zheng had reappeared, and his cultivation had reached the Void Tribulation realm. The gap between him and Luo Zheng wasn't shrinking, it was growing.
"Brother Hua Tianming, don't underestimate yourself. With your Heavenly Eye, as long as you have enough time, let alone a Void Tribulation warrior, even a Life and Death warrior won't be able to withstand it, right?" Luo Zheng said with a smile.
Hua Tianming shook his head, "I know my own strength. Besides, there are too many restrictions when using the Heavenly Eye, and I can only unleash a single strike. When fighting a warrior, I won't have enough time to unleash a second strike. You, can you dodge my first strike?"
There was a limit to everything in the world. Hua Tianming's Heavenly Sword was indeed a heaven-defying skill, but it was impossible for it to be perfect. If a Divine Core warrior could really kill a Life and Death warrior, then the world would be thrown into chaos.
As long as his opponent could dodge Hua Tianming's first strike, his strength would only be equivalent to an ordinary Divine Core genius. Hearing Hua Tianming's words, Luo Zheng nodded his head. It was useless to be modest with Hua Tianming, Luo Zheng was confident that he could dodge his first strike.
"I, I can dodge it too!" At this moment, Baili Hongfeng who was standing aside spoke. He was quite confident in his movement skill.
Hua Tianming gazed at Red Maple coldly. "Indeed, you can dodge my first strike, but so what?"
Red Maple's movement skill was indeed strange. Even some False Tribulation Realm warriors couldn't unleash such a powerful movement skill. However, rather than describing it as a movement skill, Red Maple's movement skill was more like an escape skill. His strength was far weaker than Hua Tianming's, so even if he could dodge Hua Tianming's first strike, he still had no chance of winning if he fought Hua Tianming head on.
Hearing Hua Tianming's words, Red Maple immediately shut his mouth. On the other hand, a faint smile emerged on Pei Tianyao's face, as if he was used to their conversation.
"Oh right, I have some gifts for you guys." Luo Zheng suddenly said.
"What is it? What is it?!" Hearing this, Red Maple immediately stared at Luo Zheng with his eyes lit up.
"You don't have a share!" Hua Tianming coldly said.
Red Maple revealed a pitiful expression. "Why don't I have a share …"
Luo Zheng immediately took out a longsword from his spatial ring. This was a low-grade saint rank longsword. This longsword wasn't the one Luo Zheng obtained in the True Dragon World, but rather from the Purple Heart Holy Land.
On the Sea God Continent, saint artifact level weapons weren't too rare. In the Sin Tower, almost all of the geniuses were equipped with saint artifacts. Of course, they were all low-grade saint artifacts. There were a few martial artists that were equipped with medium-grade saint artifacts, but high-grade saint artifacts were still very rare. In comparison, the martial artists of the Central Domain were a bit shabby.
In the entire Central Domain, there weren't many saint artifacts. There were only a few low-grade saint artifacts, but they were still treasures among treasures. They could almost become the most precious treasures of a sect. It was even believed that saint artifacts could bring great fortune to a sect.
So when Luo Zheng took out this low-grade saint rank longsword, Hua Tianming and Red Maple's eyes widened. Even the usually calm Pei Tianyao's eyes were wide open!
"Luo Zheng, you aren't planning on giving this sword to Hua Tianming, are you? Giving it to him is too wasteful! "Red Maple quickly said.
"What did you say!" Hua Tianming coldly glared at Red Maple, a faint sword shadow condensing in his eyes.
Red Maple's figure suddenly flashed and disappeared from his position, hiding behind Pei Tianyao. "That's right! Giving you a saint artifact is a waste! It's better to give it to me! "
"Luo Zheng, is this the gift you were talking about?" Pei Tianyao asked cautiously.
"Give this sword to Hua Tianming. What a swordsman needs the most is a good sword." Luo Zheng was also a swordsman, so he was very clear about the importance of a sword to a swordsman.
Hua Tianming took a deep breath, his eyes full of fanaticism. He firmly stared at the low-grade saint artifact, but in the end, he shook his head. "Luo Zheng, I can't accept it. A saint artifact is too precious!"
Even the Cloud Hall and the World Business Alliance only had one or two saint artifacts. But this saint artifact was going to be given to Hua Tianming. In Hua Tianming's eyes, this couldn't be described as precious, but he couldn't think of any other words to describe it.
Looking at Hua Tianming's eyes, he couldn't wait to swallow the sword. Luo Zheng smiled. "Don't refuse. A saint artifact may be precious in your eyes, but it's nothing to me."
"Nothing to you …" Red Maple felt his jaw drop to the ground. "Luo Zheng, where have you been for the past two years?!"
Pei Tianyao was also speechless.
After Luo Zheng took out the low-grade saint artifact, he also took out a pair of short swords. There were a total of four short swords, but they were a pair of mother and child swords. So normally, there were only two mother swords, and the other two child swords were sheathed in the mother sword.
"This pair of short swords is also a low-grade saint artifact." Luo Zheng finished speaking and glanced at Red Maple.
"It can't be, it can't be for me, right?" Red Maple looked at the pair of short swords, his whole body trembling.

Chapter 705
There were only three people in front of Luo Zheng. Hua Tianming was a swordsman, so he definitely didn't use this kind of short sword. Pei Tianyao didn't use a sword at all.
The only person who was suitable to use this kind of short sword was Red Maple.
How could he not be excited when he saw this pair of short swords?
Red Maple was most proud of his elusive movement technique. However, his strength wasn't strong enough. In the end, his elusive movement technique could only be used as a means to escape.
If he had a pair of sacred tools, he could make up for his weakness in attack.
Luo Zheng smiled and nodded to Red Maple.
Red Maple's hands stretched out like lightning. Even Luo Zheng didn't see it clearly before the pair of short swords fell into his hands. Such speed made Luo Zheng and the others feel ashamed. Hua Tianming stared at Red Maple.
Pei Tianyao was the most steady. He had a faint smile on his face. His cultivation technique focused on cultivating Buddha's nature. When he was in the Blue Cloud Sect, his strength was higher than Hua Tianming's.
However, the inheritance of Buddhism wasn't prosperous. Even the most famous Ten Thousand Law Temple could barely be considered a third grade sect. It lacked a way to improve, so Pei Tianyao could only rely on his Buddha's nature to comprehend. Relatively speaking, his speed of improvement was slower than Hua Tianming's.
Pei Tianyao didn't use any weapons. Instead, Luo Zheng had prepared a low-grade sacred artefact defensive artefact for him. This defensive artefact could be used in tandem with his Indestructible Body, allowing his defensive power to reach a terrifying level.
However, even if the three of them obtained low-grade sacred tools, they didn't dare to use them in front of others.
The Central Domain wasn't like the Sea God Continent. There were pitifully few sacred tools. Only Life and Death Realm experts or late False Tribulation Realm experts were qualified to possess sacred tools. If they took these sacred tools out to carry out missions, others would probably shift their attention to them.
While the 4 of them were having a small gathering here, a woman with an elegant expression was slowly descending into the city.
After Luo Zheng left Xi Youqin in Sunfire City, she hesitated for a long time before flying towards Ocean of Clouds City.
It just so happened that the world was in chaos. Along the way, people saw that she was a delicate girl who didn't have the slightest bit of Jianghu habits and didn't know much about the rules. Naturally, she was targeted by many martial artists.
If it was the previous Xi Youqin, then it would be practically impossible for her to arrive safely at Ocean of Clouds City. However, after she fully activated the Extreme Violet Yin Physique, her strength was extremely formidable.
Those warriors who had their eyes on Xi Youqin were out of luck. Initially, they thought Xi Youqin was an inexperienced warrior, and even if she was a Divine Core warrior, she wouldn't pose a threat to them. However, when she attacked, she immediately unleashed hundreds of gigantic swords, instantly turning them into minced meat. They didn't even have a chance to escape!
The most exaggerated thing was when she passed by Wild Goose City a day ago.
Wild Goose City was a very small city, and there wasn't even a single sect around it.
The situation in the Central Domain had been very unstable for the past two years, and some independent warriors didn't want to join any big sects or Seductive Sect. Therefore, they settled down in some small cities, and some even formed their own sects. However, these small sects weren't recognized by any of the big sects in the Central Domain.
These independent warriors weren't restricted by any sects, and most of them weren't good people. Instead of calling Wild Goose City a city, it was more accurate to call it a bandit's nest, and they specialized in robbing warriors who passed by.
If it was in the past, some fourth or even fifth grade sects would come and clean up these bandit's nests. With the overall strength of the Cloud Hall and Void Spirit Sect, cleaning up these bandit's nests was just a simple task. But now, these big sects couldn't even take care of themselves, so how could they have the time to do something like this?
Xi Youqin didn't know what was going on, so she just barged into the city. She wanted to take a short rest before continuing her journey to Ocean of Clouds City.
Just like before, no matter where she went, there would be people eyeing her. She instantly killed dozens of independent warriors in Wild Goose City!
After killing these independent warriors, Xi Youqin could only set off in a panic. Even though she had killed many people along the way, she was still not used to such slaughter. It was said that the world of warriors was cruel, and she was well aware of that. But after experiencing it for herself, she finally understood that this cruelty was practically unbearable for her.
However, Xi Youqin didn't expect that she would stir up a hornet's nest by killing those warriors. Most of the warriors in Wild Goose City were independent warriors who had gathered here. When they heard the commotion, they rushed over one by one. When she saw that more and more people were surrounding her, Xi Youqin had no choice but to summon over a million giant swords!
The might of a single giant sword was already so shocking. Over a hundred giant swords were enough to annihilate everything within 300 meters of her! The might of over a million giant swords that suddenly slashed down was almost unimaginable!
The entire Wild Goose City was obliterated by the giant swords she summoned, and the tens of thousands of independent warriors in Wild Goose City were obliterated along with them!
The Extreme Violet Yin Physique really did possess the might to destroy the world.
When she saw this scene, even Xi Youqin was shocked. She staggered out of Wild Goose City and ran madly towards Cloud Sea City. Her flying speed was too slow, so Xi Youqin only arrived at Cloud Sea City now.
After arriving at Cloud Sea City, Xi Youqin didn't really want to enter the city. She couldn't understand why every single warrior was targeting her.
After observing Cloud Sea City for a while, Xi Youqin discovered the Cloud Hall above the city.
"If Luo Zheng wants to return to Cloud Sea City, then he should naturally be in the Cloud Hall right now!" Xi Youqin's eyes flashed, and she circulated her True Essence and flew towards the Cloud Hall.
However, she had just flown halfway when she discovered that she seemed to be blocked by a transparent barrier.
"Is this barrier the Great Sect Protection Array set up by Luo Zheng?" Although Xi Youqin had never left Void Sky City, she was still quite knowledgeable. She could speak eloquently about the various Sects in the Central Domain, but she lacked the necessary experience.
When she thought of Luo Zheng, Xi Youqin's expression became complicated. Then, she circled around the barrier and arrived at the entrance of the Cloud Hall.
There were several guards standing at the entrance. When those guards discovered that there was someone outside the Cloud Hall, one of them flew over and asked in a stern voice, "Who is loitering outside the Cloud Hall? Leave immediately!"
Xi Youqin faintly felt wronged when she was scolded like this. Even when she went to the Void Spirit Sect, who would dare to scold her like this to her face? Ever since she left Void Sky City, she suddenly discovered that everything had changed.
It was just that along the way, Xi Youqin had suffered a lot. If it were in the past, she would have taught this guard a lesson on the spot. Naturally, there would be someone around her who would stand up for her. However, she was all alone now, and she didn't know what to do.
"I, I'm here to find Luo Zheng," Xi Youqin replied timidly.
"Looking for Luo Zheng? Hasn't Luo Zheng been missing for a long time? He's not in the Cloud Hall! "The guard replied.
Although the Cloud Hall was in an uproar during this period of time, most of it was concentrated in the middle and upper levels. This guard was just an ordinary warrior in the Cloud Hall. He didn't know that Luo Zheng had returned to the Cloud Hall. In the past two years, there were indeed many people who visited Luo Zheng, such as those from the Runic Tower. They were mainly rune masters who came because of Luo Zheng's reputation. This guard would always use the same excuse to drive them away.
"What? Luo Zheng isn't in the Cloud Hall? "Xi Youqin's expression changed, and she frowned deeply.
Luo Zheng had been pursued all along the way, and his objective was to return to Ocean of Clouds City. He'd even been able to escape Cui Xie's pursuit, so was there anything in the central territory that could obstruct him? It was impossible that Luo Zheng hadn't returned to the Cloud Hall. This guard must be deceiving her!
Thinking of this, Xi Youqin's voice gradually turned cold. "Don't lie to me. Tell Luo Zheng to come out!" However, she was wrong. She thought that Luo Zheng had told these guards that Luo Zheng didn't want to see her.
Why would Xi Youqin follow him all the way here?
Although she hated Luo Zheng to the core, she was still a traditional woman. Deep down, she felt that Luo Zheng owed her something. But, Xi Youqin couldn't figure out what she wanted from Luo Zheng.
"I told you, Luo Zheng is missing, he isn't in the Cloud Hall!" The guard didn't fall for Xi Youqin's trick. Although Xi Youqin was a Divine Core warrior, with this barrier, even a Life and Death warrior wouldn't be able to break in, let alone a mere Divine Core warrior. What can you do to me?
Xi Youqin approached the barrier and said with a gloomy expression, "I'll say it again, tell Luo Zheng to come out! Tell him to come see me! "

Chapter 706
Xi Youqin floated outside the boundary, her eyes constantly flashing, and she seemed to be in a daze. No one knew what she was thinking.
"Second Brother Zhao, that woman is so beautiful. Why don't you talk to her more?" A guard asked.

"Should we inform the higher-ups about this?" Another guard asked.
"I don't think so. The Hall Master is busy these days! Let's guard the entrance and not let anyone enter the Cloud Hall! "Another guard said.
The guards discussed in low voices, but their eyes were focused on Xi Youqin. Standing guard here was indeed a tedious job, and it wasn't bad to have such an elegant and clean beauty to feast their eyes on.
Xi Youqin lowered her head. After a while, she suddenly raised her head. With a wave of her hand, a crack appeared in the sky. A giant sword appeared from the crack and slashed towards the Cloud Hall's barrier!

When the huge sword hit the barrier, a muffled sound rang out, and then a layer of shallow water ripples appeared on the barrier of Abundant Light.

When these ten giant swords slashed down at the same time, the force of their attacks increased by tenfold. Not only were the guards frightened, it had also alerted everyone in the Cloud Hall.

"Stop!"
"You're courting death!"
"Do you know where you are?!"
Hearing the deacons' shouts, Xi Youqin didn't show any signs of stopping. This time, more than a thousand giant swords slashed down at the barrier!
When these thousands of giant swords slashed down at the same time, the force of their attack was extremely frightening. It was almost as powerful as Cui Xie's punch. However, Cui Xie's punch was focused on a single point, while these thousands of giant swords were scattered across the barrier in front of Xi Youqin!
However, the barrier was originally meant to disperse the force, allowing the entire barrier to bear the brunt of the attack. Therefore, Xi Youqin's attack wasn't any weaker than Cui Xie's punch. The entire barrier's' surface suddenly rippled with raging waves. At the same time, the top grade true essence stones maintaining the 'Abundant Light's' barrier were rapidly consumed.
Luo Zheng was originally chatting with Hua Tianming. In these two years, Hua Tianming and the others had experienced many things. Among them, they had narrowly escaped death twice. At this moment, Luo Zheng suddenly saw ripples appear on the barrier above the Cloud Hall!
To be able to make the 'Abundant Light's' ripples spread to such an extent, perhaps a Life and Death expert was attacking the barrier. Could it be Cui Xie?
Luo Zheng's expression changed, and he rushed towards the direction of the ripples.
At the same time Luo Zheng rushed over, Ning Yudie, Daoist Big Dream, and a group of Cloud Hall's elders also rushed over.
"Luo Zheng, what's going on?" On the way, Ning Yudie asked Luo Zheng.
"I'm also not sure. In the Central Domain, only a Life and Death expert could make the Abundant Light react this violently!" Luo Zheng said with a cautious expression.
There were only a few Life and Death Realm experts in the Central Region. In fact, one could count them with two hands. But, if it was Cui Xie, someone would have informed the Central Region a long time ago. But, who was the one who attacked the barrier?
Just as Luo Zheng arrived in front of the Cloud Hall's entrance, he saw over ten thousand huge swords appear out of thin air outside the barrier!
When Ning Yudie, Luo Zheng, and Daoist Big Dream saw over ten thousand huge swords, their complexions changed greatly. With so many huge swords falling at the same time, they might really be able to cut through the Abundant Light!
"Who dares to attack my Cloud Hall!" Ning Yudie said angrily with a sullen face. They had already seen a woman in plain clothes outside the barrier.
When Luo Zheng saw those huge swords, he immediately thought of Si Miaoling. In the blink of an eye, he also saw Xi Youqin, and his complexion changed greatly. At this time, he couldn't care less and immediately roared at Xi Youqin, "You, stop!"
When Xi Youqin heard Luo Zheng's voice, her face lit up with joy. However, when she saw Luo Zheng's angry appearance, her face immediately darkened. This man had never treated her well!
However, she still stopped. With a light flick of her finger, all the huge swords dissipated in the air.
Ning Yudie had already planned to attack. She just wanted to kill this woman before the huge swords fell! No matter who this woman was, she dared to destroy the Cloud Hall's barrier, she was courting death!
Although these huge swords were powerful and could destroy the Cloud Hall's barrier, they couldn't hurt Ning Yudie. At least she had enough time to go around these huge swords, find an opening, and kill this woman.
However, after hearing Luo Zheng's roar, the woman actually stopped. Ning Yudie was also somewhat surprised. Luo Zheng knew this woman?

Xi Youqin pointed at the guards. "They said you weren't in the Cloud Hall. I thought you were hiding and didn't want to see me …"

" These guards were scared to death just now. If this matter really caused the Cloud Hall's barrier to be destroyed, their sins would be great.
Luo Zheng snorted. Then, he stared at Xi Youqin and asked, "Why are you looking for me?"
Everyone was also curious. Why was this woman looking for Luo Zheng?
Xi Youqin thought for a moment, then she shook her head and said, "I don't know …"
Daoist Dameng and the other Sect Elders of the Cloud Hall had interesting expressions on their faces. They had all lived for many years, and they could roughly guess what was going on. Luo Zheng had been flirting with women, and now, his woman had come looking for him! Not long ago, Daoist Dameng had witnessed Luo Zheng hugging Ning Yudie, and he had no idea how this was going to end.
Of course, the one who had a headache wasn't them, it was Luo Zheng.
Ning Yudie looked at the woman with a complicated expression, then she asked, "Are you the young lady from the Xi family?"
The huge swords Xi Youqin summoned just now were exactly the same as when Si Miaoling fought with Luo Zheng a few years ago. Therefore, Ning Yudie had guessed that this woman possessed the Extreme Violet Yin Body. However, this woman was only an Early Divine Core warrior, but she was able to summon so many huge swords. She was much stronger than Si Miaoling.
Currently, there were only three people with the Extreme Violet Yin Body in the Central Domain. Two of them were in the hands of Cui Xie, and the last one was the great-granddaughter of Daoist Qing Xu. That was why Ning Yudie had made such a guess.
Hearing that Ning Yudie had guessed her identity, Xi Youqin quickly said, "That's right, I'm Xi Youqin!"
She thought by telling her name, everyone would believe her. However, Ning Yudie just looked at her with a strange expression and asked, "You don't know why you're looking for Luo Zheng, and why you're here in the Cloud Hall?"
