Chapter 707
As a woman, how sensitive was Ning Yudie in this aspect?
Actually, when she saw Xi Youqin's eyes, she already had some clues.
Because she was busy dealing with the Cloud Hall's affairs, she did not have time to talk to Luo Zheng in detail. Where did Luo Zheng go for the past two years? Why did he suddenly appear? What exactly happened in the Heavenly Mist Immortal Tomb?
She was prepared to have a good talk with Luo Zheng after she finished dealing with the matters.
Unexpectedly, at this time, the eldest daughter of the Xi Family suddenly came to the Cloud Hall to look for Luo Zheng. It seemed that something must have happened between the two of them …
Ning Yudie had more or less heard about the Xi Family's eldest daughter. Some time ago, she even suggested to the Void Spirit Sect to quickly let this woman get married. She had the Extreme Purple Yin Body. If she fell into Cui Xie's hands, the consequences would be unimaginable. Moreover, Luo Yan would probably suffer as well.
But why did Luo Zheng know this woman? And when Luo Zheng returned to the Cloud Hall, this woman followed Luo Zheng back …
Hearing Ning Yudie's words, Xi Youqin's face revealed a bashful expression. Although she had a bad temper and was willful, she was still a daughter of a respectable family. How could she say such things in front of so many people?
Xi Youqin's bashfulness deepened Xi Youqin's guess. Suddenly, Ning Yudie's voice turned cold. "Remove the barrier and let her in!"
Under Ning Yudie's command, the Cloud Hall's barrier was temporarily removed. After Xi Youqin entered the barrier, the barrier once again enveloped the Cloud Hall.
After Xi Youqin entered the barrier, she flew directly towards Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng's scalp instantly went numb. He did not know how to deal with this matter at all.
In the underground palace, Luo Zheng did such a ridiculous thing in a fit of anger! Moreover, at that time, he was being pressured by Cui Xie and Wu Zhanhe. He was afraid that Xi Youqin would fall into the hands of Cui Xie, and the consequences would be unimaginable. In the end, he chose to take away this woman's primordial yin.
Luo Zheng's motive was correct, but it was unfair to this woman.
What's more, Luo Zheng couldn't deny that Xi Youqin was Luo Zheng's first woman!
Xi Youqin didn't care about other people's expressions. She gently floated over and leaned against Luo Zheng's side. Her head was also lowered, like a child who had done something wrong.
When Ning Yudie saw this, a trace of anger suddenly emerged in her eyes, and her face was suddenly covered with a layer of frost!
It's just that as the Hall Master, she didn't dare to express her emotions too strongly. What's more, Ning Yudie had very little control over her emotions. She forcibly suppressed her unhappy mood. Instead, there was a faint smile on her face, but she seemed to be gritting her teeth as she asked, "Miss Xi, you still have to tell me, why did you follow me to the Cloud Hall?"
Daoist Master Dameng and the other Elders looked at each other. The expression on Ning Yudie's face changed unpredictably, but how could they not understand?
It was reasonable to say that Luo Zheng, as a member of the Cloud Hall, even if he brought Xi Youqin into the Cloud Hall, it wouldn't be considered breaking the rules. What's more, Ning Yudie had just asked. It turned out that this Miss Xi was the woman with the Extreme Violet Yin Body. No wonder she could summon so many giant swords.
Now that the Tian Xie Sect was wreaking havoc in the north, out of protection for Xi Youqin, it was normal to let Xi Youqin temporarily live in the Cloud Hall. Why did the Hall Master bother to ask again?
Needless to say, she was definitely jealous.
The Hall Master had now become a volcano, but she suppressed the fire in her heart. If this volcano erupted, they were afraid that they would be implicated. It was better to leave first, so a group of Cloud Hall Elders silently returned to the Cloud Hall.
"I, I have nowhere to go," Xi Youqin replied.
The Void Spirit Sect was being attacked by the Tian Xie Sect. Because the transmission array was destroyed, there was no news yet. But at this time, Xi Youqin's choice to return to Void Sky City was obviously not a good idea.
"Why do you want to follow Luo Zheng?" Ning Yudie stared at Xi Youqin and asked.
Xi Youqin blinked. Being stared at and asked by Ning Yudie again and again, she suddenly had a sense of vigilance. She didn't answer Ning Yudie's question and instead said, "You, you are the Hall Master of the Cloud Hall, right?"
"Yes, I am the Hall Master of the Cloud Hall," Ning Yudie replied.
Xi Youqin pursed her lips and then said, "I am willing to follow Luo Zheng. As the Hall Master … do you also have to ask?"
"…"
Hearing Xi Youqin's question, Ning Yudie was suddenly speechless …
Xi Youqin was right. Although she was the Hall Master and Luo Zheng was also a member of the Cloud Hall, she was meddling too much. Even the extremely strict Mysterious Yin Mansion wouldn't meddle to this extent, right?
Ning Yudie was stunned for a while, and a trace of loneliness appeared on her face. Then, she sighed, looked at Luo Zheng faintly, and said softly, "Yes, I meddled too much. Luo Zheng has the Extreme Purple Yin Body. She can't fall into the hands of Cui Xie. Let her stay in our Cloud Hall for the time being!"
After saying that, Ning Yudie turned around and left.
Hearing Ning Yudie's words, Luo Zheng felt as if there were countless little bugs crawling on his scalp. He was very uncomfortable right now. It seemed that Ning Yudie didn't know why he had already taken Xi Youqin away.
At this moment, a little red light escaped. It was Xun leaving Luo Zheng's body.
Xun had been feeling down for the past two days, so she didn't show up. After seeing what happened today, her mood suddenly seemed to improve a lot. After she appeared, she stared at the direction Ning Yudie left and said with a smile, "She already has such a big reaction. I wonder how she will react when she finds out about your matter."
Of course, Xi Youqin knew what Xun meant by "your matter". Her face turned red in an instant.
Luo Zheng's face was full of bitterness. For Luo Zheng, dealing with this kind of thing was no less difficult than dealing with Cui Xie. On the contrary, Xun was in a good mood. She just wanted to see how long Ning Yudie could suppress her anger!
When Luo Zheng left Xi Youqin in Huoyang City, he didn't think too much about it. Because of Xi Youqin, Luo Zheng did bring a lot of trouble. At that time, Luo Zheng was determined to get rid of Cui Xie's tracking. He just wanted to get rid of this trouble.
However, Xi Youqin came to the Cloud Hall. How should Luo Zheng deal with it?
"I'll arrange a place for you first. Come with me." Luo Zheng's face sank. Then he took Xi Youqin to find a deacon of the Cloud Hall to arrange a place for her.
There were many female disciples in the Cloud Hall. For example, the disciples who were promoted to the Cloud Hall in Caiyun Sect were all female warriors. It was not difficult to arrange a place for Xi Youqin. The problem was how to deal with Xi Youqin in the future. What kind of role would she play by Luo Zheng's side?
Luo Zheng himself couldn't figure it out. Luo Zheng may have planned his path well, but when it came to feelings, Luo Zheng was a mess. What's more, Luo Zheng and Xi Youqin had no feelings at all.
However, Luo Zheng didn't know, but Xi Youqin didn't think so.
Although the living conditions that Luo Zheng arranged for Xi Youqin were not bad, at least much better than the living conditions of the vast majority of female disciples, Xi Youqin was still not used to it.
Her environment since childhood was so superior. Even the size, height, and material of the bronze mirror hanging on the wall had to be carefully chosen. The position of the bed and the material of the pillow were also carefully selected. Even if this was the first time she could sleep soundly after leaving the Xi family, she still couldn't sleep.
Sitting on the bed, Xi Youqin hugged her knees and stared blankly at the deep dark sky outside the window. Her face suddenly revealed a smile. Even if she wasn't used to this kind of life, she should at least try to endure it. She should endure it for him!
This picky and stubborn young lady seemed to have found a noble reason to complete an arduous task …
If others knew that the slightly simple environment of the Cloud Hall made her feel so embarrassed, they would probably be speechless.
And this night, she wasn't the only one who couldn't sleep.
Ning Yudie sat upright in the ice palace and continued to consolidate her cultivation. She had just entered the Life and Death Realm and still needed time to cultivate her Zhen Yuan. After entering the Life and Death Realm, her Zhen Yuan began to slowly liquefy. The entire Life and Death Realm was a process of constantly liquefying Zhen Yuan. Only after reaching the Divine Sea would there be a space where she could transform her Zhen Yuan into a Divine Sea and create a world within her body!
Although Ning Yudie was cultivating, she felt incomparably restless. From time to time, Xi Youqin's words would appear in her mind.

Chapter 708
When True Essence was condensed to the extreme, it would liquefy little by little. For a Life and Death martial artist, this was a very smooth process.
But tonight, Ning Yudie had failed again and again, her state of mind was not stable.
Generally speaking, cultivating in this kind of situation was a very dangerous thing. It was very likely that one would suffer from Cultivation Deviation and damage themselves. Most martial artists would choose to temporarily stop cultivating.
However, Ning Yudie was incomparably stubborn. As her mind wandered, she suddenly felt a stabbing pain in her lower abdomen. She had lost control of a wisp of True Essence and was suddenly rushing upwards! As this wisp of True Essence went out of control, it also caused the other True Essences in her dantian to become violent.
Ning Yudie was suddenly startled. She calmed her mind and tried to stabilize her True Essence, but it was already too late.
The True Essence that had gone out of control shot up and penetrated her dantian!
"Puff!"
She spat out a mouthful of blood. Even though only a wisp of True Essence had penetrated her dantian, the damage it caused was very small. However, the dantian was a martial artist's core and also their most fragile place. Even if it was damaged, it would still cause a lot of damage.
Ning Yudie suppressed the True Essence in her dantian and swallowed a pill. After a while, when the pill completely dissolved, she slowly recovered, but her complexion was still deathly pale.
Ning Yudie, who had stopped cultivating, was still in a bad mood. She walked to the window. Tonight, the moon was bright and the stars were sparse. However, she was surprised to see a star on the horizon blooming with an orange light.
"What is that star?" Ning Yudie frowned. She felt the light of that star come from the distant horizon and directly shoot towards the Cloud Hall. It seemed that someone in the Cloud Hall had attracted the power of the star. How was this possible? Surprised, Ning Yudie's lithe figure gently floated and disappeared from the Ice Palace.
Luo Zheng didn't have as many complicated thoughts as Ning Yudie and Xi Youqin. At this time, he was still releasing his sense, rushing into the universe to communicate with the stars. Before this, Luo Zheng had tried to communicate with the stars many times. But besides the first time, he had failed almost every time.
The number of failures was too many, and even Luo Zheng felt a bit depressed.
Compared to other cultivation methods, the difficulty of the 'Transcendent Divine Might' was far greater than Luo Zheng had imagined.
But tonight, Luo Zheng's consciousness had successfully attracted the attention of a star. This was an orange star. This star was very beautiful, and the orange light on its surface was gentle and tranquil. More importantly, it didn't resist Luo Zheng at all!
With every additional star, the power of the 'Stellar Battle Body' would increase by a bit. Currently, there was still a certain disparity between Luo Zheng's strength and Cui Xie's. Although Luo Zheng hadn't directly fought with Cui Xie, he believed that the difference between them wasn't too great. If he could attract several more stars, then the day of killing Cui Xie wouldn't be too far off!
When Luo Zheng cultivated the 'Stellar Battle Body', his body would be bathed in a faint purple light. This was because Luo Zheng had established a connection with the first purple star. Now that Luo Zheng had successfully attracted the attention of a second star, there was a second orange light that covered Luo Zheng's body. These two different lights wove together, causing a layer of illusionary colors to appear on his body.
Ning Yudie looked at Luo Zheng on the roof from afar. "It really is him!"
Ning Yudie didn't know how Luo Zheng was able to attract the power of the stars. It must be an extraordinary cultivation method, so Ning Yudie guessed that it was most likely Luo Zheng. Now that her guess was confirmed, Ning Yudie was even more curious.
Looking at Luo Zheng's thin face from afar, Ning Yudie's heart was also moved.
In just two years, this youth had forcefully jumped from the Divine Reflection Realm to the False Tribulation Realm. Now, although Luo Zheng was only at the early stage of the False Tribulation Realm, even Mister Zhuo was easily killed by Luo Zheng. He was probably not much weaker than her.
These two years, he must have suffered a lot, right?
Ning Yudie wanted to go forward and have a long talk with Luo Zheng, but when she thought of the Xi Family's young miss, she finally suppressed her impulse. She sighed and murmured, "Let's talk about it later." Then she quietly retreated into the night.
Early the next morning, Xi Youqin was actually pacing back and forth in front of Luo Zheng's residence. When Luo Zheng appeared, she greeted him with a smile.
Now that Xi Youqin's state of mind had undergone a drastic change, she was like a completely different person from before.
Before, Xi Youqin was aloof and arrogant. She had an arrogant attitude towards everyone, but in front of Luo Zheng, she seemed to have removed all her arrogance.
Perhaps in her heart, she had already regarded Luo Zheng as her home. It was a pity that Luo Zheng had not clearly recognized this point. After seeing Xi Youqin, Luo Zheng just glanced at her indifferently, and then walked towards the depths of the Cloud Hall.
Xi Youqin was a bit troublesome for Luo Zheng. If it were not for the incident in the underground palace, Luo Zheng would definitely be determined to drive her away. However, in the Fire Sun City, he had already thrown her away once. Luo Zheng could not drive her away again.
It was just that at this stage, Luo Zheng could only temporarily not hate her.
Seeing that Luo Zheng ignored her, Xi Youqin pursed her lips, but her face did not show any anger. Instead, she smiled foolishly, and then silently followed Luo Zheng. She knew that Luo Zheng did not like her to talk too much, so she simply did not say a word.
As soon as they arrived at the meeting hall of the Cloud Hall, they heard many Cloud Hall Elders in the hall sighing.
"It's too miserable. Cui Xie is really crazy. He doesn't even let off those mortals who don't know how to cultivate!"
"Those independent warriors are not good people! They are just a bunch of mobs. Naturally, they will vent their anger when they have the opportunity! "
"It's just a pity for the warriors who stayed in the Virtual Sky City. They are completely implicated!"
While they were discussing, they saw Luo Zheng come in with Xi Youqin. Everyone's voices suddenly quieted down.
After Luo Zheng walked in, Daoist Master Dameng's gaze fell on Xi Youqin. He originally wanted to say something to Luo Zheng, but after seeing Xi Youqin, his face showed hesitation.
"What happened?" Seeing Daoist Master Dameng's expression, Luo Zheng asked.
Daoist Master Dameng then said, "The Virtual Spirit Sect has been captured by the Tian Xie Sect!"
Although this news didn't surprise Luo Zheng, Luo Zheng couldn't help but frown. When Xi Youqin heard this news, she was completely dumbfounded.
That day, Luo Zheng cooperated with the Seven Void Spirit Masters to set up a trap to kill Cui Xie. However, they didn't expect that Cui Xie's true strength was far beyond their imagination, leading to the annihilation of the Seven Void Spirit Masters. With two Life and Death Realm powerhouses, Cui Xie and Wu Zhanhe, it was only a matter of time before the Tian Xie Sect defeated the Virtual Spirit Sect. After all, Qing Xu Daoist was alone.
In fact, it had been some time since the Virtual Spirit Sect was captured. It was just that the transmission array was destroyed. It took some time for the news to be transmitted from the Virtual Sky City, so they only received the news today.
Xi Youqin stood in place and stared blankly at Daoist Master Dameng. "Then, then what about the Virtual Sky City? Is there any news about the Virtual Sky City? "
Daoist Master Dameng glanced at Luo Zheng again. Luo Zheng nodded. "Daoist Master, please speak directly."
"In order to repay those independent cultivators, the Tian Xie Sect massacred and looted the entire Virtual Sky City. The Seven Great Families were annihilated," Daoist Master Dameng said concisely.
"Seven, Seven Great Families? Are you sure it's the Seven … Families? "Xi Youqin asked with a trembling voice.
There were seven third-grade families in the Virtual Sky City. They were the Lin, Huang, Chang, Xu, Xiao, Chen, and Xi Families. Among them, the Xi Family was the leader. Xi Youqin didn't dare to ask what happened to the Xi Family, so she asked this question.
Unexpectedly, Daoist Master Dameng's answer was even more direct. "The Xi Family was also among them. Moreover, I heard that the Xi Family was the target of the Tian Xie Sect. The Xi Family was completely massacred and the entire Xi Family Castle was burned to the ground!"
Hearing Daoist Master Dameng's words, Xi Youqin's legs suddenly went soft and she knelt on the ground. Her gaze was unfocused as she stared ahead. Two lines of tears gushed out and flowed down her cheeks.
Although Xi Youqin was also worried in her heart, she never thought that such a big Xu Ling Sect would be so easily breached! In Xi Youqin's eyes, her great-grandfather, Daoist Master Qing Xu, was an invincible existence. However, this sect that had stood tall for tens of thousands of years, known as the number one sect in the world, was breached just like that …
Looking at this fair and clean little girl's miserable appearance, several Cloud Hall Elders couldn't help but shake their heads in their hearts. At this time, Ning Yudie also walked in and said coldly, "I just received news that there's a group of Xi Family people rushing towards my Cloud Hall."
Hearing Ning Yudie's words, a glimmer of hope flashed in Xi Youqin's eyes. "There's still someone from the Xi Family alive?"
Ning Yudie made an "En" sound and said, "They'll arrive today. I might need your cooperation."
"Cooperate with what?" Xi Youqin asked again.
Ning Yudie felt that this answer was a bit cruel. It was better to let the Xi Family's young miss face it herself, so she said faintly, "When they arrive, you'll know."

Chapter 709
It was bad news for all the sects in the Central Domain that the Void Spirit Sect was so easily defeated.
The most terrifying thing was that the Tian Xie Sect actually slaughtered the entire Void Sky City!
Generally, the battle between martial artists would not hurt civilians, so before the war began, most of the people who left the Void Sky City were martial artists, but the mortals who did not cultivate did not leave.
In the eyes of those mortals, whether it was the Void Spirit Sect or the Tian Xie Sect, it was the same. The change of the flag on the city wall had little to do with them. For those mortals, they were not sensitive to the war between sects. Instead, it was the martial artists who stayed in the Void Sky City who were worried.
But this time, they were wrong.
The Void Sky City had a very deep foundation, and so many independent martial artists followed the Tian Xie Sect. Since the Void Spirit Sect was defeated, the Void Sky City was naturally in their pocket. The purpose of slaughtering the city, burning and looting was also a kind of reward, and it was also a kind of deterrence to the sects in the world.
The elders who received the news in the Cloud Hall were also worried, and everyone's mood was not very good.
Luo Zheng looked at Xi Youqin, and a little pity appeared in his heart. It was just that she happened to have such a drastic change, so Luo Zheng did not know how to comfort her.
At this moment, a deacon entered the meeting hall and reported, "The Xi Family of the Void Sky City requests an audience!"
Ning Yudie responded faintly, "Let them enter the Cloud Hall and bring them to the meeting hall."
Xi Youqin wiped away her tears and stood up. She didn't know how many people of the Xi Family survived. What about Xi Xiaojie? Or her father?
After a while, several people walked in from outside the meeting hall. The person in the lead was wearing the black robe of the Void Spirit Sect. After entering the Cloud Hall, he cupped his hands towards Ning Yudie, "Greetings to the Hall Master of the Cloud Hall."
Xi Youqin's gaze swept over those people one by one, but she did not find the person she was looking for. She asked suspiciously, "Didn't you say that the people of the Xi Family would come? You … are just the elders of the Void Spirit Sect. Where are the people of my Xi Family? Where is my father? Second Uncle, Third Uncle, and the others! Qiu Cheng, how are Xi Xiaojie and the others?! "
These cultivators were not from the Xi Family. Almost all of them were from the Void Spirit Sect!
Qiu Cheng had a good relationship with the Xi Family and often traveled between the Void Spirit Sect and the Xi Family. He had already reached the False Tribulation Realm in his thirties, so it was only a matter of time before he entered the Elder Hall of the Void Spirit Sect.
Qiu Sheng was also one of Xi Youqin's suitors!
He was the one who had visited all the famous mountains and great rivers in the Central Domain and collected the seeds of the Radiance Grass before giving them to Xi Youqin.
However, Xi Youqin had received such a precious gift, but she had casually thrown it away. One could imagine how angry Qiu Cheng was, but because of the Sect Master's face, he could not flare up.
Cui Xie glared coldly at Xi Youqin and sneered, "Thanks to you, the Xi Clan was annihilated, and your father was killed by Cui Xie himself! Xi Xiaojie is also missing! "
Xi Youqin staggered. After a moment of silence, she stared blankly at Qiu Sheng and asked, "Make it clear! Why do you say it's thanks to me … "
"If it wasn't for you, the Tian Xie Sect might not have attacked the Void Spirit Sect! Cui Xie doesn't want the Void Spirit Sect's inheritance the most, but you! "Qiu Sheng snorted coldly.
"No, it's not like that …" Xi Youqin cried even harder, as if she was about to collapse.
"It's you. If it wasn't for the fact that you bitch relied on the Sect Master to protect you and refused to get married, the Tian Xie Sect might not have attacked the Void Spirit Sect!" Qiu Sheng did not care about Xi Youqin's emotions at all and continued to sneer.
Qiu Sheng looked at Xi Youqin's miserable appearance, and he enjoyed it very much.
He was also a heaven grade genius! Even in the Void Spirit Sect, how proud was he? Before Xi Xiaojie showed his amazing talent, Qiu Sheng was the number one genius of the Void Spirit Sect! However, this woman dared to humiliate him in public and destroyed all his face. He became the joke of the entire Void Spirit Sect for a while.
Now that the Xi family was in trouble and the Void Spirit Sect was destroyed, he wanted to see how this woman could continue to be arrogant in front of him!
"No, it's not because of me …" Because she alone harmed the Xi family, Void Sky City, and Void Spirit Sect. How could she bear this kind of pressure? At this moment, Xi Youqin's thoughts were not organized at all. She could only deny Qiu Sheng's words mechanically.
Luo Zheng looked at Xi Youqin from the side. He didn't know if he should say something …
At this moment, Qiu Sheng continued to sneer. "The Sect Master has fallen into Cui Xie's hands. He wants to use you to exchange!"
"Great-grandfather, great-grandfather didn't die … He wants me to exchange?" Xi Youqin asked.
"Yes! This is your last bit of value, and your only value! "Qiu Sheng sneered maliciously.
Although the Void Spirit Sect was destroyed, most of the disciples fled. As long as Daoist Qing Xu survived, he could rebuild the Void Spirit Sect with his rallying power. However, the sect's overall strength would naturally be greatly reduced. The Void Spirit Sect, which was originally a fifth-grade sect, would probably fall to fourth-grade!
However, the only way to exchange for Daoist Qing Xu was Xi Youqin. This was why Qiu Sheng and the others came to the Cloud Hall.
"Exchange for great-grandfather …" Xi Youqin nodded foolishly, but then she seemed to think of something. Her soft body suddenly trembled, and she quickly shook her head. "No, I can't!"
"Haha, you're still afraid even at a time like this!" Qiu Cheng pressed Xi Youqin further.
"I really can't, I can't …" Xi Youqin cried again. Her face was full of despair.
"At this time, it's not up to you to say no!" Qiu Sheng reached out to grab Xi Youqin. In fact, Qiu Sheng had another plan in mind. In Qiu Sheng's opinion, although Xi Youqin could be exchanged for the Sect Leader, the Void Spirit Sect's power was gone. So what if Cui Xie kept his promise and released Daoist Qing Xu? It would probably be the Tian Xie Sect's world in the future!
It would be a waste to give Xi Youqin to Cui Xie. He, Qiu Cheng, had been infatuated with this body for a long time. This time, he might as well go all the way. Wasn't the Extreme Purple Yin Body greatly beneficial to martial artists? The Central Domain was so big. No matter how far Qiu Sheng went, he would have a place to go if he took Xi Youqin away.
However, just as Qiu Sheng was about to grab Xi Youqin, Luo Zheng took a step forward and appeared in front of Qiu Sheng in a flash. Luo Zheng said indifferently, "I'm afraid it really won't work."
Qiu Sheng frowned and stared at Luo Zheng. "This is my Void Spirit Sect's business. Does the Cloud Hall want to interfere?"
Luo Zheng smiled faintly and said, "First, Xi Youqin is not from your Void Spirit Sect. She's only from the Xi Family. If I didn't hear wrong, your surname isn't Xi, right? Second, if Xi Youqin is given to Cui Xie, all the warriors in the world will suffer. Most importantly … this will indirectly harm my sister. "
"You're Luo Zheng?" Qiu Sheng guessed Luo Zheng's identity.
Luo Zheng nodded.
Qiu Sheng then said resolutely, "Even so, we must save the Sect Master!"
"Yes, we must save the Sect Master first! Luo Zheng, isn't our Sect Master's life more precious than your sister's? "
"Besides, that Luo Yan will only lose her own life. Cui Xie may not kill her!"
"Yes, the Heavenly Demon Joyous Union Formation is also very beneficial to women. She won't lose a piece of flesh!" a Void Spirit Sect warrior said.
As soon as the Void Spirit Sect warrior finished speaking, Luo Zheng's figure suddenly floated out like a ghost. He reached out with one hand and grabbed the warrior's neck. "To you, the Sect Master is very important. To me, my sister is the most important. Your Sect Master is worthless. Do you understand?"
After saying that, Luo Zheng punched out and hit the warrior's chest. This warrior had the cultivation of the early stage of the False Tribulation Realm, but he couldn't withstand a single blow from Luo Zheng. When this punch hit his chest, he instantly flew out and crashed into the wall of the Conference Hall. Half of the wall immediately collapsed.
The faces of the Void Spirit Sect's people darkened.
Qiu Sheng laughed wildly and said, "Haha, such a big Cloud Hall is too domineering and unreasonable! Is this how the Cloud Hall bullies people? "
Ning Yudie and the elders of the Cloud Hall didn't speak. Luo Zheng had a sneer on his face. It was related to his sister's safety. Luo Zheng didn't intend to be reasonable, and there was no reason to be reasonable.
Seeing Luo Zheng step forward at the critical moment, Xi Youqin suddenly felt a trace of warmth in her heart. She really had no reason to retreat now. With her eyes flickering, Xi Youqin said, "Qiu Sheng, in fact, Luo Zheng has more than two reasons. There is a third reason!"
Hearing this, Luo Zheng knew he was in trouble. Just as he was about to stop her, Xi Youqin said, "My Primordial Yin is gone!"
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 710
Admitting this kind of thing in public still made Xi Youqin blush instantly.
The expressions of the Cloud Hall elders became even more strange, and they all looked at Luo Zheng.
Qiu Cheng and the others were stunned. If Xi Youqin was telling the truth, then she was worthless to Cui Xie!
"Hahaha, Xi Youqin, you pretended to be as noble as a Saintess in the Xi Family! I didn't expect you to have already fooled around with another wild man! " As soon as he said this, Qiu Cheng's purpose was naturally ruined, but he felt very unbalanced in his heart. He boasted that he didn't lose to any man, but this woman didn't even look at him from beginning to end!
"He is not a wild man!" Xi Youqin stared at Qiu Cheng with burning eyes and replied.
"Who is it?" Ning Yudie suddenly asked.
Originally, this matter had nothing to do with Ning Yudie. This was a matter between the Xi Family and the Void Spirit Sect. Ning Yudie's inquiry seemed a bit gossipy.
However, Daoist Master Dameng and the other elders of the Cloud Hall had already seen through the clues. It was most likely Luo Zheng, so Ning Yudie was so concerned.
The elders of the Void Spirit Sect didn't know the inside story. They all looked at the master of the Cloud Hall with strange expressions, but they muttered in their hearts. Why is the master of the Cloud Hall asking about this?
At this time, Luo Zheng suddenly replied frankly, "It's me. Xi Youqin is already my woman. It's impossible for me to let you give her to Cui Xie."
When he said this himself, the entire Conference Hall suddenly quieted down.
Luo Zheng wasn't a dishonest person, but admitting it in front of Ning Yudie still made Luo Zheng a little embarrassed. However, things had already reached this point. He couldn't deny it. He had to bear the consequences of what he did!
Hearing Luo Zheng admit it himself, Ning Yudie's eyes flashed with a dim luster, and she turned her face to the side. She almost suffered from Qi Deviation while cultivating last night. Her face was already pale, and now there was no color on her face, just like a piece of white paper.
"So, please go back. My Cloud Hall doesn't have a good way to save your sect master," Luo Zheng said.
Qiu Cheng gritted his teeth and stared fiercely at Luo Zheng. At this moment, they were in someone else's territory. It was impossible for him to take Xi Youqin away by force..... not to mention the master of the Cloud Hall was here, Luo Zheng's strength was unfathomable............ they were....
......... of Luo Zheng........ of Luo Zheng.
The group of people from the Void Spirit Sect did not stay for long and left quickly.
After they left, the Conference Hall suddenly fell silent.
Xi Youqin lowered her head and slowly leaned against Luo Zheng. In her opinion, Luo Zheng might be her only pillar of support.
Luo Zheng looked at Ning Yudie and asked with concern, "Sect Master, are you not feeling well?"
"I'm fine," Ning Yudie replied coldly. She looked directly past Luo Zheng and asked the many elders of the Cloud Hall, "Elders, do you have anything else to report today?"
The group of elders secretly glanced at Ning Yudie's face. They were all well aware that if they spoke at this time, they would be looking for trouble. So, they simply remained silent.
In fact, the Cloud Hall had already received the news of the Tian Xie Sect's war today. Originally, they should have discussed the countermeasures of the Cloud Hall. There were too many things that needed to be formulated. However, looking at Ning Yudie's face, it was indeed not the time to discuss these things.
"If there's nothing else, we'll discuss it another day." After Ning Yudie finished speaking, she left the Conference Hall without looking back. It was as if she had forgotten the existence of Luo Zheng and Xi Youqin.
Many of the elders looked at each other and left the meeting hall as well.
On the contrary, Xi Youqin noticed that the atmosphere was not right. She carefully asked Luo Zheng, "Luo Zheng, your Cloud Hall Sect Master's face doesn't look good. What's wrong with her?"
Luo Zheng's expression was very interesting. How could he explain it to Xi Youqin? After taking a deep breath, Luo Zheng faintly looked at Xi Youqin in front of him. Suddenly, he reached out and touched her hair that was hanging on one side. He smiled and said, "It's nothing!"
Xi Youqin was like a docile kitten as she nestled next to him and let Luo Zheng stroke her hair. Although Luo Zheng said it was nothing, she still felt that something was wrong.
Luo Zheng returned to the Cloud Hall this time. He did not relax at all and seized the opportunity to cultivate.
For Luo Zheng, time was becoming more and more pressing. Not to mention Luo Yan, Luo Zheng and Cui Xie would have a battle in the end. Looking at the Tian Xie Sect's development momentum, it was very likely that they would attack either the Cloud Hall or the World Business Alliance.
For the Tian Xie Sect, in the entire Central Domain, only the Cloud Hall and the World Business Alliance could hinder their progress. After these two Sects were wiped out, the Mysterious Dark Mansion, Blood Wood Cliff, Black Mountain Sect, and other Sects were nothing to be afraid of!
Luo Zheng was not the only one who felt a sense of urgency. With the precedent of the Xu Ling Sect, many warriors in the Cloud Hall, from top to bottom, were surrounded by an atmosphere of urgency. During this time, many disciples rarely accepted the tasks distributed by the Cloud Hall. Most of them were in closed-door cultivation and began to break through their bottlenecks.
Once the Tian Xie Sect launched a Sect War against the Cloud Hall, they would at least have the ability to protect themselves.
In addition to this tense atmosphere, there was another strange atmosphere in the Cloud Hall. Of course, this atmosphere could only be felt by the Elders.
Ning Yudie's temperament suddenly became very strange. As long as Luo Zheng was in an occasion, Ning Yudie would either not say a word or turn around and leave. It seemed that she completely ignored Luo Zheng. The relationship between the two seemed to have fallen to a freezing point.
Many Elders were also discussing this matter behind their backs.
"Luo Zheng has made such a great contribution to our Cloud Hall. At this time, he has already stepped into the False Tribulation Realm. His strength is unfathomable. He is a sharp weapon for our Cloud Hall to fight against the Tian Xie Sect. Why are the two of them giving each other a cold war?"
"Alas, Hall Master is still too young. How can he let his emotions affect his decisions? Logically speaking, when Luo Zheng returns this time, he should be given the position of Elder! With his contributions, it's more than enough! "
"Young people can't escape the word 'love'. I think Hall Master is deeply in love. I'm afraid she is very sad. In fact, it's normal for a man to have three wives and four concubines. Even if she is the Hall Master, she can't deal with this matter like this!"
"Yes, yes …"
Just as several Elders were discussing this matter in private in a corner, Ning Yudie floated out like a ghost. Her face was as pale as a corpse, and her cold eyes fell on several Cloud Hall Elders. She asked, "What's right?"
Several Cloud Hall Elders were shocked. "Hall Master, no, nothing's right …"
Ning Yudie blinked her eyes. She had obviously lost a lot of weight in the past few days. Then she floated away like a ghost.
After the Hall Master left, several Elders stroked their chests and sighed. "This can't go on. Let's find Dameng and let him talk to Hall Master!"
In contrast, Xi Youqin's life seemed to be much better.
Although she couldn't help but feel sad when she thought of the Void Sky City, Xi Youqin finally found her home. Even if she knew in her heart that Luo Zheng might not like her that much!
Because of this, Xi Youqin's performance was completely different from before. Now, she almost didn't have any thoughts. She would even go to the market of the Cloud Hall to carefully buy some rare ingredients and borrow the kitchen of the Cloud Hall to show her exquisite cooking skills.
Xi Youqin was arrogant, but after all, she came from a big family. Since she was a child, in addition to practicing martial arts, she was also very proficient in playing the zither, flower arrangement, tea ceremony, calligraphy and painting, and cooking.
In the past, she was the Young Miss of the Xi Family. Everything was served by servants. How could she do it herself? But now, she had a place to use her skills. Every day, she would make exquisite dishes and wait for Luo Zheng outside the cultivation room.
However, Luo Zheng didn't cultivate for a day or two. He often went into closed-door cultivation for five or six days in a row. Those dishes were naturally eaten by her after the time limit, and the rest were thrown away. Even if she didn't see Luo Zheng for several days in a row, she didn't complain. She still made different dishes and waited for Luo Zheng at the door of the secret cultivation room.
Many disciples of the Cloud Hall were borrowing the secret cultivation rooms. Everyone who came and went was watching.
Now, in addition to the Elders, the entire low-level martial artists of the Cloud Hall more or less knew about it.

Chapter 711
Many disciples of the Cloud Hall were talking about this matter, but most of them were envious of Xi Youqin. When they walked past her, they would glance at her from time to time.
"Sigh, this Luo Zheng's luck is really good. After wandering outside for two years, his cultivation has improved by leaps and bounds, and he has brought back a woman who is so loyal to him …"
"Envious? If you're so capable, why don't you set up a Sect Protecting Formation as well? Let us be envious of him! That's called strength! "
"Have you all forgotten some rumors? I heard that there was a story between our Palace Master and Luo Zheng, I wonder how our Palace Master is feeling right now. "
"Keep dreaming! Who do you think our Palace Master is? How could he possibly fall in love with Luo Zheng?"
"Not necessarily, my source of information is much better than yours. This was admitted by one of the Sect Elders. Recently, the relationship between Luo Zheng and the Palace Master has been strained! I heard it's because of this woman. "
Some rumors would eventually spread through different channels, and for those well-informed warriors, it wasn't difficult for them to get hold of such information. Because of this, it had deepened everyone's speculations. If what the rumors said was true, how would Luo Zheng deal with this?
"Creak!"
Luo Zheng pushed open the door to the secret cultivation room. He glanced around with his bright eyes, then he saw Xi Youqin standing not far away from him. She was smiling brightly at him.
Xi Youqin wasn't a diligent cultivator. The reason she was able to break through to the Divine Core realm was because of pills and all sorts of resources. Therefore, she didn't spend much time on cultivation. However, she was very particular about her lifestyle.
As long as one was a martial artist, they would devote most of their time to cultivation, lest they fall behind and waste their time. Even female martial artists were no exception. But for Xi Youqin, an exquisite life was clearly more important than cultivation.
After settling down in the Cloud Hall, she would appear in front of Luo Zheng almost every day in a different outfit. Even if she was wearing ordinary home clothes, after a unique combination, it still had its own charm.
Putting aside her original disgusting personality, perhaps she is the perfect woman in the eyes of many martial artists. This is also the reason why many disciples of the Cloud Hall envy Luo Zheng.
"You're out," Xi Youqin said with a smile.
"Didn't you say that you don't have to wait here?" Luo Zheng had been in the secret cultivation room for three days, but she still spent most of her time here.
"Okay." Xi Youqin nodded meekly.
It sounded like she agreed. Luo Zheng also knew that she would still be waiting here in two days. This woman's stubbornness was engraved in her bones, but she didn't show the same arrogance as before in front of Luo Zheng.
She carried the fragrant dishes and held Luo Zheng's hand as they walked through the long corridor in the air. Then, she brought out the dishes bit by bit. The steaming hot dishes were placed on the table.
Days like this were hard to come by for Luo Zheng. After the meal, Luo Zheng asked around for information regarding the Central Domain. After gathering some necessary information, he returned to his secret cultivation room and began his endless cultivation. During this period of time, he had a feeling that he was about to break through to the Mid Divine Core realm, and perhaps it wouldn't be long before he broke through to the Mid Divine Core realm.
After he broke through to the Mid Divine Core realm, his chances of fighting Cui Xie would be higher.
However, Luo Zheng still needed to find an opportunity to fight Cui Xie, and he just needed to know when this opportunity would come. This was also the reason why Luo Zheng occasionally came out to gather information.
Aside from Luo Zheng who was busy with his cultivation, Ning Yudie was also busy with her cultivation.
Even though all kinds of news had piled up like a mountain in Cloud Palace, and Daoist Great Dream had passed them to her, she had thrown them all to one side, as if she wasn't too interested in learning about the movements in the Central Region.
As a Palace Master, this kind of action was very irresponsible.
After the Tian Xie Sect defeated the Void Spirit Sect, its influence was like the sun in the sky, and it was experiencing changes every single day. Even those independent warriors who had joined the other big sects had 'disappeared', and were now joining the Tian Xie Sect. In the hearts of these independent martial artists, it was impossible for them to have a sense of belonging to a sect. Whoever could give them benefits, whoever was strong, they would submit to that person. And the "Heavenly Evil Divine Kingdom" clearly had both of these conditions!
Even the number one sect in the Central Domain had been defeated by them, so which other sect could stop the Kingdom of Evil Deities?
Right now, the Kingdom of Evil Deities was slowly expanding towards the center of the Central Domain, and they had swallowed all the second and third ranked sects. How could those third ranked sects have the strength to resist? Without wasting a single soldier, they all declared their allegiance to the Kingdom of Evil Deities, and became the vassals of the Kingdom of Evil Deities.
The situation was getting more and more dangerous, and the pressure on the Elders of the Cloud Hall was getting greater and greater. But, as the Palace Master, Ning Yudie had no idea about this, and she didn't even leave her Ice Palace.
"This can't go on like this, the Heavenly Awakening City has sent people here, but none of them have met the shop owner!"
"Right now, the Cloud Hall can only join forces with the World Business Alliance to fight against the Tian Xie Sect, but the Palace Master hasn't made any moves, what should we do?"
"Sigh, although the Palace Master has outstanding strength, she is now troubled by her personal affairs, but the Cloud Hall can't … Big Dream, why don't you go and persuade the Palace Master?"
These days, Ning Yudie had announced that she was going into seclusion and wouldn't see anyone. If it was in the past, it wouldn't have mattered. In history, it was common for the Cloud Hall Palace Master to go into seclusion for eight to ten years, but it also depended on the timing. Now that the world wasn't peaceful, how could the Cloud Hall be without a leader?
Daoist Big Dream was the person closest to Ning Yudie, and at the same time, he understood Ning Yudie's feelings the most!
Daoist Big Dream was much older than Ning Yudie. Although Ning Yudie was the Palace Master, Daoist Big Dream still treated Ning Yudie as a little girl. He understood Ning Yudie's character, and if she was stubborn, no one would be able to persuade her. It was this stubbornness, combined with her talent, that allowed her to ascend to the position of Palace Master.
Under the advice of many Elders, Daoist Big Dream clenched his teeth and charged into the Ice Palace.
Inside the Ice Palace, there was an azure frost energy everywhere. This was condensed from the true essence that Ning Yudie released when she was cultivating. When Ning Yudie was still in the False Tribulation realm, the frost energy she released didn't have much of an effect on Daoist Big Dream. But now that Ning Yudie was a Life and Death realm master, even the frost energy she released was a great threat to Daoist Big Dream!
Therefore, after entering the Ice Palace, Daoist Big Dream had to carefully avoid the constantly flowing aura. Not only that, but he also had to circulate his true essence to resist the biting cold of the Ice Palace.
When he bypassed the layers of frost energy and found Ning Yudie, Daoist Big Dream's face revealed an expression of not knowing whether to laugh or cry. Ning Yudie was actually lying here, asleep!
When a martial artist's cultivation reached this level, it was very rare for them to be so tired that they fell asleep. They could only meditate to adjust their spirit. Daoist Big Dream didn't know how hard she cultivated to be able to tire herself to this extent.
There were only a few small Ice Phoenixes curled up beside her. When they saw Daoist Big Dream come in, those Ice Phoenixes spread their wings and vigilantly stared at Daoist Big Dream.
Then, Ning Yudie also vigilantly opened her eyes. She coldly glanced at Daoist Big Dream and spat out two words, "What's the matter?"
Daoist Big Dream hesitated for a moment, then said, "The World Business Alliance's Alliance Master, Shi Kefan, has already sent people to request an audience with Palace Master several times. They said they have important matters to discuss with my Cloud Hall!"
Ning Yudie slightly nodded and said indifferently, "I know." Then, she crossed her bare feet and raised her head to ask, "What does the World Business Alliance want to do now?"
"What they mean is that we can't just sit and wait for death." Daoist Big Dream had communicated with the World Business Alliance before.
In fact, after it escalated to a sect war, the situation was very clear. When large sects fought against large sects, there was only a fist to the flesh confrontation. Any schemes and tricks would be useless.
Now that the Tian Xie Sect was rapidly expanding, the ones who should be anxious shouldn't be the Tian Xie Sect, but the Cloud Hall and the World Business Alliance. Therefore, biding their time was the worst choice.
Ning Yudie thought for a moment, then nodded and said, "I understand."
"Then how does Palace Master plan to deal with this matter?" Daoist Big Dream asked.
"We'll discuss a strategy with the World Business Alliance. We'll join forces to suppress the Tian Xie Sect!" Ning Yudie seemed to have made some kind of decision.
Daoist Big Dream nodded, "I'll notify the World Business Alliance."
Right now, the one who was most anxious wasn't the Cloud Hall, but the World Business Alliance.
The Cloud Hall still had the Minor Heavenly Cycle Triple Kill Formation to rely on. Although the Tian Xie Sect would choose to attack Cloud Sea City and the Cloud Hall sooner or later, it was still much more difficult to deal with than Tianqi City. In comparison, the World Business Alliance was located in the richest city in the world, Tianqi City. It was very likely that they would choose to attack the World Business Alliance before then!
Although the World Business Alliance had that ancient giant and three fat alliance masters with unfathomable strength, the power hidden under the World Business Alliance was comparable to that of a Rank 6 Sect.
But when facing the Cui Xie and the Tian Xie Sect, it was still difficult to deal with them alone. It would be strange if they weren't anxious.
Fortunately, the teleportation formation between Cloud Sea City and Tianqi City was still in good condition. Although the two cities were thousands of miles apart, they were only separated by a single teleportation formation.
One month later …
Ning Yudie's figure floated past the right side of the Cloud Hall. Her gaze inadvertently fell on Xi Youqin.
Xi Youqin was still patiently sitting at the door of Luo Zheng's secret cultivation room. She supported her face with one hand and stared blankly at the gray door.
"Xi Youqin is my woman …" This sentence kept lingering in Ning Yudie's ears.
Ning Yudie's small face sank slightly. Then, her eyes revealed some envy. If she could put aside her identity as the Hall Master and wait bitterly for Luo Zheng, it would also be a great happiness.
At this moment, Xi Youqin felt the gaze from above. She raised her head slightly and saw Ning Yudie not far away.
When the two women's gazes met, Xi Youqin blinked her eyes and smiled at Ning Yudie, revealing her white teeth.
Ning Yudie felt uncomfortable in her heart. She snorted coldly and floated away.
Xi Youqin turned her head and continued to look at Luo Zheng's secret cultivation room. Her face revealed a faintly discernible smile. It was the smile of a victor.

Chapter 712
In the Conference Hall, many Elders had already gathered, waiting for Ning Yudie's arrival.
Ning Yudie hadn't even stepped into the Conference Hall when a fierce cold wind blew in. The Conference Hall suddenly became like a world of ice and snow, and a dense killing intent immediately enveloped everyone. Several Elders couldn't help but lift their lapels.
When Ning Yudie walked in with a dark face, several Elders looked at each other in dismay.
"What's wrong with the Hall Master?"
"Did she suffer some kind of shock?"
"Shh, everyone, lower your voices …"
During this period of time, Ning Yudie's mood wasn't good. No one dared to speak too much. Offending her wouldn't end well.
Daoist Master Dameng bowed to Ning Yudie and said, "Hall Master, the Cloud Hall is ready. Everyone can set off now."
Ning Yudie nodded and carelessly said, "Mm."
Seeing her worried expression, Daoist Master Dameng couldn't help but remind her, "Hall Master, Luo Zheng hasn't arrived yet."
Many Elders uniformly stared at Ning Yudie.
Ever since Luo Zheng returned to the Cloud Hall, many Elders in the Cloud Hall had a new evaluation of Luo Zheng's strength. Although Luo Zheng was only at the early stage of the False Tribulation Realm, he had easily killed Great Teacher Zhuo. His combat strength was probably above Daoist Master Dameng's. He was also an indispensable high-end combat strength for the Cloud Hall.
Although a Sect war involved millions of Martial Artists, it was often the top powerhouses who decided victory or defeat.
After a month of discussion, the Cloud Hall and Tian Qi Sect finally reached a consensus. As the two top Sects in the Central Domain, they couldn't sit still and wait for death. They decided to join forces and actively attack the Tian Qi Sect.
Daoist Master Dameng originally planned to inform Luo Zheng, but in the end, Ning Yudie said she would go by herself. However, after circling around, Ning Yudie returned to the Conference Hall with a murderous aura. Only Luo Zheng wasn't there.
Daoist Master Dameng naturally didn't understand. Many Cloud Hall Elders also didn't understand.
"No need to call him. Gather all Divine Realm Martial Artists and above. Let's set off!" Ning Yudie's aura suddenly skyrocketed. The Secret Art she cultivated was already extremely powerful, and after stepping into the Life and Death Realm, her strength had increased by several folds. As such, when her aura erupted, the hearts of many Elders trembled!
Countless disciples had formed independent groups in the sky above Cloud Hall, and they were flying towards Ocean of Clouds City.
When one looked up into the sky from afar within the city, one could see an expanse of dense black dots descending incessantly. Every single one of them was a cultivator, so how magnificent was such a scene in the eyes of the mortals of the city?
"I heard the Cloud Hall is about to set off!"
"Sigh, the Tian Xie Sect's influence is expanding. I think the Cloud Hall and the World Business Alliance are about to launch their final counterattack."
"I'm afraid this war will decide the ownership of the Central Domain! That Cui Xie is indeed an incredible man. If he really succeeds, he will be able to unify the entire Central Domain! "
Since ancient times, no single power had been able to unify the entire Central Domain. If Cui Xie succeeded, he would be the number one man in the history of the Central Domain.
After the Cloud Hall's Martial Artists entered the city, they all flew toward the Teleportation Formation. The first to enter were the Divine Realm Martial Artists, followed by the Divine Core Martial Artists, and then the False Tribulation Realm Elders in the Cloud Hall.
Ning Yudie, Daoist Master Dameng, and the others were the last to enter.
Standing next to the Teleportation Formation, Ning Yudie turned around and looked at the Cloud Hall floating in the sky.
At this moment, Daoist Master Dameng said. "Palace Master, we should bring Luo Zheng with us."
No matter what, Ning Yudie shouldn't leave Luo Zheng behind.
There was a huge grudge between Luo Zheng and Cui Xie. Even if it wasn't because of the Tian Xie Sect, Luo Zheng and Cui Xie would still have to fight. Furthermore, ever since Luo Zheng returned to the Cloud Hall, he hadn't shown his true strength. Luo Zheng had contributed a lot to the Cloud Hall, but he wasn't involved in the most important matters of the Cloud Hall. It was a bit too much.
"I think it's more suitable for him to stay behind in the Cloud Hall." Ning Yudie suddenly said with a smile. This time, the Cloud Hall didn't send out all its Martial Artists, they just left a small group of Martial Artists behind. "Besides, he seems to like this kind of life. He has beauties by his side every day, and he enjoys it very much. There's no need to bring him along!"
"This … Palace Master, I think we're facing a great enemy. Shouldn't we put these personal matters aside first?" Daoist Master Dameng's words were more straightforward. What he meant was, 'Are you jealous because of Palace Master? If you're jealous, then be jealous, but don't joke about the fate of the Cloud Hall. '
Ning Yudie snorted, turned around, and walked into the Teleportation Formation.
Daoist Master Dameng and the other Elders looked at each other and smiled bitterly. Then, they also entered the Teleportation Formation.
At this moment, Luo Zheng had already entered a meditative state. He felt that his cultivation was only a thin line away from the middle stage of the Void Tribulation Realm. As long as he broke through the last layer, he would be able to steadily break through!
Although Luo Zheng didn't know much about the situation in the Central Domain, he was able to analyze it thoroughly. The day of his battle with Cui Xie was getting closer and closer. Therefore, breaking through to the middle stage of the Void Tribulation Realm had become a matter of necessity for him!
In the secret cultivation room, Luo Zheng could no longer sense the passage of time. During this period of time, Qinglong and Chi Long rarely spoke. Previously, Xun would secretly leave Luo Zheng's body, but now, she seemed to be in a deep slumber and rarely appeared.
After all, it was Luo Zheng's time to break through a bottleneck. Although it shouldn't be too difficult for Luo Zheng to break through to the middle stage of the Void Tribulation Realm, breaking through any bottleneck was very important. It couldn't be affected in the slightest.
Xi Youqin was still sitting quietly at the entrance of the secret cultivation room. Occasionally, she would enter Cloud Sea City to collect some rare flowers and carefully trim them. Then, she would arrange them into exquisite shapes according to a certain pattern.
Right now, there were six to seven pots of flowers placed at the entrance of the secret cultivation room. The flowers were arranged in a row, and they were colorful and beautiful.
During this period of time, the number of warriors in the Cloud Hall had obviously decreased. In the past, there would always be groups of warriors entering or leaving the secret cultivation room. But now, there were only a few people in the secret cultivation room, and all of them were Xiantian realm warriors. There were very few Divine Reflection and Divine Core warriors in the secret cultivation room.
The entire Cloud Hall was deserted.
Although Xi Youqin could clearly sense the changes in the Cloud Hall, she was too lazy to think about it. In Xi Youqin's mind, Luo Zheng had become her world. As for what happened outside of this world, she didn't want to know.
Therefore, even those common people in Cloud Sea City knew about the Cloud Hall's expedition, but she, a Divine Core warrior, didn't know about it!
"I've been in secluded cultivation for so long!" Xi Youqin pouted as she stared at the entrance of the secret cultivation room. She broke off a green root with her fair fingers, then with a gentle twist of her fingers, she accurately inserted the root into the pot.
The number of flowers placed at the entrance of the secret cultivation room kept increasing. Every day, she would place a pot of flowers in the secret cultivation room. At first, there were only seven to eight pots of flowers, but it slowly increased to a dozen pots, then twenty pots …
Unknowingly, the number of flowers placed at the entrance of the secret cultivation room had increased to 35 pots.
On this day, a powerful aura suddenly erupted from the secret cultivation room! This secret cultivation room was the highest level secret cultivation room in the Cloud Hall. The walls of the secret cultivation room were made with a special metal, but they couldn't stop the aura from leaking out.
A faint smile emerged on Xi Youqin's face. "Has he broken through? Will he come out from secluded cultivation today? "
She looked at the entrance with an expectant expression. Her clean and elegant face suddenly blushed. When she thought about what she was going to say to Luo Zheng, she felt shy and conflicted.
"Creak …"
The door of the secret cultivation room was pushed open. Luo Zheng's figure flashed out with a faint smile on his face. At this moment, he had stepped into the middle stage of the False Tribulation Realm.
"Sure enough, he has broken through!" Xi Youqin stood up and walked towards Luo Zheng.
"Sorry to keep you waiting! How long has it been? "During this time, Luo Zheng was almost completely immersed in meditation. He had completely forgotten the passage of time.
Xi Youqin blinked her eyes and said gently, "You can count these flowers and you'll know how many days have passed."
Luo Zheng's eyes swept over the flowers, and his expression changed. "35 days. I've been in secluded cultivation for so long this time …"
"It doesn't matter!" Xi Youqin still had a faint smile on her face. She brushed her black hair to one side and suddenly whispered, "Luo Zheng, can I sleep at your place tonight?" When she said this, her voice was as soft as a mosquito, almost inaudible. Her cheeks were also flushed.
Luo Zheng stared blankly. He hesitated for a moment, but then he nodded.
Both of them were still young, and this was the first time they had experienced the intimacy between a man and a woman, so it was inevitable that there would be some longing for them, but Luo Zheng still had Ning Yudie in his heart. What he did not know was that Ning Yudie was no longer in Cloud Hall.
(Sorry, I was too busy at the end of the year! It's too late! I'm sorry, everyone!)

Chapter 713
Luo Zheng, who left the secret cultivation room, also felt that something was wrong. There were fewer disciples in the Cloud Hall, but Luo Zheng didn't think too much about it.
He couldn't imagine that no one in the entire Cloud Hall had informed him about such a big event.
How could Xi Youqin know what Luo Zheng was thinking? She had just said such shameful words. At this time, she was so embarrassed that she buried her whole head in his chest. She didn't even dare to look at Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng then took her hand and returned to his room.
The two of them, who had their first taste of being a man and woman, spent another night together. Last time in the underground palace, the two of them were chased by Cui Xie, so they didn't have time to stop and taste this wonderful feeling.
After a night of pleasure, the two of them had surprisingly good physical strength. They didn't fall asleep until the early morning of the next day.
It wasn't until the next day that Luo Zheng really felt that something was wrong in the Cloud Hall. The Cloud Hall seemed to be too deserted, as if there were a lot of people missing.
He wanted to find Hua Tianming and the others to ask about the situation, but he found that the three of them weren't there. Helpless, Luo Zheng went to the Conference Hall, but it was also empty. During this period of time, most of the elders gathered in the Conference Hall every day. How could there be no one?
Just as he left the Conference Hall, Luo Zheng bumped into Fei Han.
As an elder of the Cloud Hall, Fei Han was already old. This time, he was the one who stayed behind and didn't accompany Ning Yudie to the battle.
After all, several elders still needed to stay in the Cloud Hall to preside over the Cloud Hall's Minor Heavenly Cycle Triple Kill Formation. This was to prevent Cui Xie from bypassing the World Business Alliance and attacking the Cloud Hall.
However, when the elites of the Cloud Hall were all out, even if they really attacked the Cloud Hall from the sky, it wouldn't make much sense. After all, all of the main forces of the Cloud Hall had gone to Tianqi City. Even if the Tian Xie Sect really attacked the Cloud Hall, it would only be destroying an empty shell. The Tian Xie Sect wouldn't have much interest in tormenting the Cloud Hall.
"Elder Fei Han, why are there so many people missing in the Cloud Hall?" Luo Zheng asked with a face of suspicion.
When Luo Zheng asked this question, Fei Han was also baffled. "Didn't the Hall Master inform you? Now, most of the martial artists of the Cloud Hall have already entered Tianqi City and are cooperating with the World Business Alliance to fight against the Tian Xie Sect. They set off a month ago. "
As soon as Fei Han said this, Luo Zheng's face suddenly changed. "No one informed me."
Fei Han more or less knew about the cold war between Luo Zheng and Ning Yudie. Fei Han touched his nose and shook his head with a strange expression. "I don't know what the Hall Master is thinking."
"What's the situation now?" Luo Zheng asked with concern.
Fei Han nodded. Although he stayed behind to guard the Cloud Hall, news of the war between the World Business Alliance and the Tian Xie Sect was reported every day. Luo Zheng asked the right person.
It turned out that when Ning Yudie chose to go to war and entered Tianqi City, she immediately declared war on the Tian Xie Sect. At the same time, the World Business Alliance also declared war on the Tian Xie Sect!
With these two Sects joining hands, the Tian Xie Sect had no choice but to pay attention to them!
The Tian Xie Sect didn't fear the Cloud Hall at all. After all, in the eyes of Cui Xie and Wu Zhanhe, there was only one person who was difficult to fight against in the Cloud Hall, and that was Ning Yudie. She cultivated the 《Empyrean Fairy Heart Sutra》, which was several times more difficult than other cultivation methods. After entering Life and Death Realm, although she had only gone through one life and death calamity, she could fight against experts who had gone through two or even three life and death calamities.
However, the influence of the Cloud Hall was far less than that of the World Business Alliance!
The development of the Cloud Hall was exactly the same as that of the Void Spirit Sect. For the Tian Xie Sect, the Cloud Hall was at best another Void Spirit Sect. As long as they besieged the Cloud Hall, they would be able to take it down sooner or later.
However, the World Business Alliance was different. This alliance, which was originally formed by a group of businessmen, had now developed into a colossus. In terms of pure strength, the World Business Alliance was actually a quasi-sixth-grade Sect. However, the World Business Alliance was neither a Holy Land nor a Divine Nation. It was very low-key, but it was a hidden force. Even the Tian Xie Sect had to be careful.
After the Tian Xie Sect defeated the Void Spirit Sect, they only moved around the eastern part of the Central Domain. They didn't act too fiercely. It seemed that they wanted to rest and recuperate. When they grew to a certain extent, they would attack the World Business Alliance.
However, the World Business Alliance wasn't stupid. Those businessmen were the smartest. How could they just sit there and wait for their doom?
Since the Tian Xie Sect didn't declare war, the World Business Alliance took the initiative to do so. After communicating with the Cloud Hall, the two most powerful Sects in the Central Domain declared war on the Tian Xie Sect on the same day.
As a result, the biggest Sect war in the Central Domain in the past thousand years began.
More than a month after the declaration of war, both sides had their victories and defeats. However, the war only happened locally. The World Business Alliance destroyed two outpost cities of the Tian Xie Sect, while the Tian Xie Sect continued to slowly advance towards the Central Domain.
Everyone in the Central Domain understood that no matter if it was the Tian Xie Sect or the allied forces formed by the World Business Alliance and the Cloud Hall, there would definitely be a real decisive battle between the two sides. The result of this battle would directly determine the direction of the entire Central Domain!
Whether Cui Xie succeeded in the challenge and integrated the Central Domain into the Country of Evil Deities, or whether the World Business Alliance and the Cloud Hall won and became the Holy Land, it would all depend on the result of this battle.
However, no one knew when this battle would break out. However, looking at the current situation, both sides had tested each other to a certain extent. Eventually, one side would lose patience. Once that happened, the final battle wouldn't be too far away.
"In other words, Ning Yudie is still in Tianqi City?" Luo Zheng asked.
However, Fei Han said earnestly, "Luo Zheng, the Cloud Hall needs you."
"Yes, I understand." At this point, Luo Zheng naturally didn't have the slightest intention of shirking his responsibilities. However, Ning Yudie was too much. She actually isolated him from such an important matter!
"The Hall Master is in a bad mood recently. It's good," Fei Han said again.
How could Luo Zheng not understand what Fei Han was hinting at? He smiled helplessly, but he didn't know where to start talking to Fei Han.
Unexpectedly, Fei Han muttered, "It's normal for a man to have three wives and four concubines. In the past, I had more than twenty women. Women just need to be coaxed." Fei Han only dared to say these words to Luo Zheng alone. When Ning Yudie was in the Cloud Hall, he didn't dare to say these words.
Hearing Fei Han's words, Luo Zheng almost fainted on the ground. It must be said that Elder Fei Han was really a playboy when he was young.
However, for a martial artist, marrying a wife was a simple matter. In the Central Domain, the status of a martial artist was as high as that of an immortal. Even the poorest martial artist was countless times richer than a rich mortal! Therefore, no martial artist would lack women.
After Fei Han left, Luo Zheng stood in the corridor and looked at the rolling clouds above the Cloud Hall. He muttered, "Do women really need to be coaxed?"
After leaving the corridor, Luo Zheng returned to his residence. Xi Youqin had just finished bathing. Her black hair was wet and tied up behind her head. She was wearing undergarments and her body was brimming with a healthy luster.
"Come with me," Luo Zheng said after seeing Xi Youqin.
"Where to?" Xi Youqin asked.
"Tianqi City," Luo Zheng said again.
"Okay!" Xi Youqin smiled. She didn't even ask Luo Zheng why he wanted to go to Tianqi City. If it was in the past, she would definitely interrogate him in detail.
In fact, Xi Youqin's personality hadn't changed. It was just her state of mind that had changed.
No matter what, Xi Youqin had already regarded Luo Zheng as the only home in her life. Moreover, she was also clear that her position in Luo Zheng's heart wasn't high. In the underground palace, she and Luo Zheng had sex once, and it was even forced by Luo Zheng.
This man didn't love her.
Xi Youqin was very clear about this point in her heart.
The only reason he was with her was because of his inherent sense of responsibility.
Xi Youqin, who was aware of this point, carefully maintained this relationship. Therefore, she didn't have any requests for Luo Zheng. She didn't dare to have any requests. She was afraid that after Luo Zheng's sense of responsibility disappeared, she would lose everything.
Women were indeed strange creatures. In just a few short months, her relationship with Luo Zheng had undergone such a huge reversal. She was now treading on thin ice, but she still enjoyed it.
After the two of them made some preparations, they quickly left the Cloud Hall and entered Cloud Sea City. Then, they stepped into the teleportation formation in Cloud Sea City. Under the blazing white light, Xi Youqin held Luo Zheng's hand and the two of them instantly disappeared into the spatial tunnel.
The situation in Tianqi City wasn't optimistic.
Ever since the World Business Alliance and the Cloud Hall declared war on the Tian Xie Sect, merchants were leaving Tianqi City one after another.
There was no lack of merchants in Tianqi City who were as rich as countries!
These merchants weren't cultivators. Even if they were cultivators, they were only in the Xiantian Realm. However, the wealth they controlled was much richer than most cultivators. Even False Tribulation Realm cultivators weren't as rich as them.
The entire World Business Alliance was formed by these merchants.
These merchants did very well in business, but they were helpless against the Sect war. Now that the war had been declared, many merchants chose to temporarily leave Tianqi City and seek refuge in other cities in order to preserve their lives.
The World Business Alliance was happy to cooperate with them. For the World Business Alliance, money was their foundation. The World Business Alliance, which controlled the economic lifeline, knew very well that as long as there was money, there would be cultivators willing to risk their lives for the alliance. This was the fundamental reason why the World Business Alliance was able to become a Rank 6 Sect.
As the light of the teleportation formation continued to flicker, Luo Zheng and Xi Youqin appeared in Tianqi City.
At this time, many merchants were lining up to leave Tianqi City through the teleportation formation. Suddenly, they saw a group of talented men and beautiful women appear here. Many people's gazes gathered on them.
"Luo Zheng!"
Before Luo Zheng and Xi Youqin stepped out of the teleportation formation, a voice immediately sounded out.
The voice sounded familiar, but Luo Zheng couldn't remember who it was. He turned his head towards the source of the voice, and saw a handsome young man looking at him with a smile on his face.
"Ao Xiang?" Although it had been two years, Luo Zheng could still recognize the man in front of him.
This Divine Realm genius from the Hulun Grassland had participated in the Martial Dao Competition with him. Both of them had the same goal; to stop Cui Xie's Heavenly Demon Joyous Union Formation and kill Si Miaoling.
But in the end, Ao Xiang's efforts were in vain. He was defeated by Si Miaoling, and he was nearly crippled by her.
But now, Luo Zheng noticed that Ao Xiang had broken through to the Late Divine Core realm, and he was only one step away from the False Tribulation realm!
Ao Xiang's talent wasn't bad either. Although he was an independent cultivator, no matter if it was luck or opportunity, he was one of the top cultivators in the Central Domain. Perhaps he was only slightly weaker than Luo Zheng and Xi Youjie. If not for his sister, perhaps he would have become a powerful independent cultivator in the future.
"You've joined the World Business Alliance?" Luo Zheng asked.
Ao Xiang nodded his head, then he smiled and said, "You've broken through to the Mid False Tribulation realm!"
Up until now, both men still had the same goal. One was to save his sister from Cui Xie, and the other was to save his lover from Cui Xie.
After seeing Luo Zheng, Ao Xiang seemed to be in a good mood. He hurriedly said, "Come with me, you've just arrived in Tianqi City, so you don't know the current situation!"
Luo Zheng nodded his head, then he grabbed Xi Youqin's hand and left the Teleportation Formation with Ao Xiang. After that, they flew towards the Merchant Alliance's headquarters in Tianqi City.
"Are all the people from the Cloud Hall in the Merchant Alliance's headquarters?" Luo Zheng suddenly asked.
Although Fei Han had told Luo Zheng the general situation, Luo Zheng didn't know the details.
Ao Xiang's eyes lit up, then he said, "All the warriors from the Cloud Hall and the World Business Alliance have been mixed together. Only a few of them are still in the headquarters, and most of them have left the city."
This war between the sects was even more intense than Luo Zheng had expected. In fact, the battle between the upper echelons of the sects had yet to start, but the battles between the sects were extremely intense.
Just two days ago, in order to break free from Nine Tripod City, both the Tian Xie Sect and the World Business Alliance had lost several thousand warriors, and most of them were Divine Reflection warriors. Furthermore, the Cloud Hall and the World Business Alliance had lost a Void Tribulation Sect Elder!
"Aren't both sides fighting between the Divine Reflection and Divine Core warriors? Why did a Void Tribulation warrior die? "Luo Zheng furrowed his brows and asked.
A helpless smile emerged on Ao Xiang's face. "This is also the biggest problem that has been bothering us!"
Warriors were divided into different realms, and the gap between realms was huge. Therefore, during the war between the sects, Void Tribulation warriors would only die in the hands of Void Tribulation warriors. But this time, two Void Tribulation warriors from the Cloud Hall and the World Business Alliance had died in the hands of a group of Divine Reflection warriors.
Normally, a Divine Reflection warrior wouldn't pose much of a threat to a Void Tribulation warrior. Of course, except for an abnormal genius like Luo Zheng.
After all, no matter how many Divine Reflection Realm martial artists there were, it was very difficult for them to join forces and fight against a False Tribulation Realm martial artist. It might be very difficult for a False Tribulation Realm martial artist to kill a thousand Divine Reflection Realm martial artists, but if they couldn't win, a False Tribulation Realm martial artist could escape! Relying on the advantage of speed, he was able to forcefully wipe out the thousand Divine Reflection Realm martial artists one by one!
But this time, the Tian Xie Sect had invented a very strange tactic.
This kind of array was similar to a formation, but the Central Domain's warriors usually only had a few people working together to set it up. For example, the Void Spirit Seven had seven people working together to set it up.
However, the Heavenly Evil Sect had combined several hundred Divine Reflection Realm martial artists together. The power of this strange battle tactic was enough to instantly kill even a False Tribulation Realm martial artist if they were not careful.
If one were to simply count the number of peak experts in this sect war, the two sides were actually about the same. The World Business Alliance had that ancient giant whose strength was unfathomable, and now they had Ning Yudie. The Tian Xie Sect also had Cui Xie and Wu Zhanhe, two Life and Death warriors.
As for the number of Void Tribulation warriors on both sides, the difference was still about the same.
Originally, it was these few peak experts who decided the outcome of the sect war. But when the Tian Xie Sect had this strange tactic, the situation had been reversed!
Those Divine Reflection disciples, who were originally just bystanders, could erupt with a huge effect in the sect war, and this effect was enough to break the balance.
"This tactic is so mysterious. It's definitely not something Cui Xie could come up with. How did they do it?" After hearing Ao Xiang's words, Luo Zheng frowned slightly.
Ao Xiang sighed and said, "We also think so. This tactic is very mature. The Alliance Master is guessing that it might have been passed down from the four Divine Kingdoms."
"Four Divine Kingdoms!" Luo Zheng's eyes flashed. "Could it be …"
"Yes, it's very likely that there's a Divine Nation behind the Heavenly Evil Sect," Ao Xiang said with certainty.
Luo Zheng immediately thought of the Saintess and the Divine Kingdom Prince in the Heaven Mist Immortal Tomb. When Luo Zheng had killed the Divine Kingdom Prince, the Saintess had vowed to take revenge on him.
Moreover, the old man behind the Saintess had promised to give Wu Zhanhe the inner copy of the Five Poison Scripture because Wu Zhanhe had helped the Saintess dispel the curse on her.
The Central Domain and the four Divine Kingdoms were separated by the Chaotic Astral Ocean. Could it be that they really had people enter the Central Domain?

Chapter 714
From a geographical point of view, although the Chaotic Astral Ocean was like an insurmountable moat, the history of God Nation was much longer than that of the Central Domain, and there were countless powerhouses there.
From the trip to the Heaven Mist Immortal Tomb, it could be seen that Life and Death Realm powerhouses were the top existences in the Central Domain, but in God Nation, they were only followers of the Saintesses and Princes.
If God Nation really intervened, who in the Central Domain could resist the Tian Xie Sect?
Luo Zheng's face suddenly showed a cautious expression. "Did you come to this conclusion yourself?"
Ao Xiang shook his head. "Everyone in the World Alliance basically agrees with this point of view. Even the Hall Master of the Cloud Hall agrees!"
"Am I, the Hall Master of the Cloud Hall, still in Tianqi City?" Luo Zheng suddenly asked.
