Hearing Luo Zheng's sudden question, Xi Youqin's ears immediately perked up, and a faint smile appeared on her face. Of course, Luo Zheng and Ao Xiang were talking, so they didn't notice her expression.
Ao Xiang asked suspiciously, "Why don't you, Luo Zheng, know about the Cloud Hall's actions?"
In Ao Xiang's opinion, Luo Zheng was definitely a core figure in the Cloud Hall. Now that the Cloud Hall was cooperating with the World Business Alliance to attack the Tian Xie Sect, it was very strange that Luo Zheng didn't know about it …
Luo Zheng laughed dryly. How could he explain the matter between him and Ning Yudie to outsiders? Moreover, did he really have a conflict with Ning Yudie? It probably couldn't be considered a conflict. It was just that Ning Yudie's attitude suddenly became cold, and Luo Zheng was somewhat helpless about it.
This time, all the elites of the Cloud Hall had come out, and only Luo Zheng had been left behind. How could he not have some thoughts?
However, after experiencing the matter with Xi Youqin, Luo Zheng roughly understood that crazy women were completely unreasonable. Although he was annoyed by Ning Yudie's actions, he wouldn't bicker with her. There was a saying that was quite good: only women and villains were difficult to raise …
Ning Yudie wasn't difficult to raise, she was just difficult to serve.
"At that time, I was in seclusion and didn't know about this matter. I hope brother Ao Xiang can tell me," Luo Zheng said with a smile.
Ao Xiang also smiled and said, "The Hall Master of the Cloud Hall isn't in Tianqi City. During this time, she seems to have gone crazy, killing all the martial artists of the Tian Xie Sect. She rarely returns to Tianqi City …"
According to the original plan, the Cloud Hall and the World Business Alliance would join forces to declare war on the Tian Xie Sect. Ning Yudie, as the only remaining Life and Death Realm martial artist of the Cloud Hall, should have stayed behind to guard Tianqi City.
Tianqi City and the Tian Xie Sect would have a decisive battle sooner or later. Before that, the Life and Death Realm martial artists of both sides generally wouldn't make a move. Once the Life and Death Realm martial artists made a move, it meant that the real decisive battle had begun.
However, Ning Yudie broke this rule. From the beginning, she fought alone and slaughtered all the martial artists in the big cities occupied by the Tian Xie Sect. With regards to Ning Yudie acting alone, the Tian Xie Sect and the World Business Alliance were somewhat caught off guard.
The World Business Alliance was worried about Ning Yudie's safety. Ning Yudie's strength was unquestionable, but she couldn't resist both Cui Xie and Wu Zhanhe at the same time. If the Tian Xie Sect set a trap for Ning Yudie and killed her, it would be a devastating blow to the World Business Alliance and the Cloud Hall.
That ancient giant of the World Business Alliance would find it difficult to deal with the Tian Xie Sect alone. Their hopes of resisting the Tian Xie Sect would be even slimmer.
However, it was also because of Ning Yudie attacking everywhere, which gave the Tian Xie Sect a headache!
The Tian Xie Sect's battle strategy could gather hundreds and thousands of Divine Reflection Realm martial artists together. However, this battle strategy wasn't without flaws. As long as one's strength was strong enough, they wouldn't necessarily have the opportunity to join forces. This battle strategy was effective against False Tribulation Realm martial artists, but against Life and Death Realm martial artists like Ning Yudie, it wasn't enough.
Ning Yudie didn't abide by the rules. She took the lead and often took the lives of hundreds and thousands of martial artists. This greatly slowed down the pace of the Tian Xie Sect's expansion.
The Tian Xie Sect also didn't seem to be ready for a decisive battle. The two Life and Death Realm martial artists in the sect had been biding their time and didn't plan to attack Ning Yudie for the time being.
As a result, the situation between the two sides continued in this ambiguous manner …
Relatively speaking, the World Business Alliance and the Cloud Hall's worries were far greater than the Tian Xie Sect's. Ning Yudie's attack seemed to have taken a lot of advantages and defeated many of the Tian Xie Sect's forces. However, the martial artists at the bottom wouldn't affect the battle too much. The ones who really decided the outcome of this battle were those at the top.
Once the Tian Xie Sect began to counterattack, Ning Yudie would be in danger. Everything she had done before would not be worth it.
Because of this, many people came forward to persuade Ning Yudie, but she didn't listen at all.
The Elders of the Cloud Hall were her subordinates, so they naturally couldn't persuade her. The World Business Alliance was only her ally, so they couldn't order Ning Yudie around. She still did things her own way. She quietly left the Tian Xie Sect and stayed outside for five or six days. After killing several martial artists of the Tian Xie Sect, she returned to Tian Xie City and resupplied before leaving the Tian Xie Sect again. Over the past month, Ning Yudie had stayed in Tian Xie City for no more than four days …
After listening to Ao Xiang, Luo Zheng asked, "In other words, the Temple Master isn't in Tian Xie City?"
Ao Xiang faintly smiled, "It's hard to say, but she's most likely not in Tian Xie City …"
If a Life and Death Realm martial artist didn't deliberately inform others of their movements, who could track them?
"Too capricious," Luo Zheng shook his head.
Hearing Luo Zheng's evaluation, Xi Youqin, who was beside him, suddenly let out an "En" sound.
"You … en what?" Luo Zheng looked back at Xi Youqin and asked.
Xi Youqin blinked her eyes, and her lotus-like elegant face suddenly smiled and said, "Too capricious!"
How could Luo Zheng not understand that Xi Youqin's words actually had a hidden meaning? He could only sigh and follow Ao Xiang straight to the headquarters in Tian Xie City.
After entering the Merchant Alliance's headquarters, Ao Xiang had just reported the news of Luo Zheng's arrival in Tian Xie City. Immediately, someone came to invite Luo Zheng to the Merchant Alliance's Conference Hall.
In the past, the Merchant Alliance had a strong commercial aura, making it difficult for martial artists to associate it with the most powerful force in the world. Therefore, the World Business Alliance had always given people the impression that it was big but not strong. When some martial artists mentioned the World Business Alliance, they would use one sentence to describe it: After all, it was a group of profit-seeking businessmen.
But this time, after the World Business Alliance declared war on the Tian Xie Sect, the entire Tian Xie City had undergone earth-shaking changes!
The Merchant Alliance's headquarters was filled with a desolate aura, and the entire Tian Xie City was covered with hundreds of thousands of runes, forming a super large Spirit Array! This Spirit Array was far inferior to the Cloud Hall's Xiao Zhou Tai Energy Triple Kill Array, and it was even inferior to the Void Spirit Sect's Sect Protecting Array, but it won in terms of quantity!
After all, the Runic Tower in Tian Xie City was the gathering place of all the Rune Masters in the world. Moreover, with the wealth of the World Business Alliance, other Sects simply could not compare. These runes were set up as if they did not cost money, and their power would not be too small!
Under Ao Xiang's lead, Luo Zheng entered the Merchant Alliance's Conference Hall. As soon as Luo Zheng entered, he saw several familiar figures. In addition to Daoist Master Dameng and the other Elders of the Cloud Hall, there were also the three fat leaders of the World Business Alliance. As for the ancient giant in Tian Xie City, he was not among them.
"Luo Zheng! You're finally here! "Shi Kefan's round face was flushed red. After seeing Luo Zheng, everyone was pleasantly surprised.
"Greetings to the three leaders." Luo Zheng bowed to Shi Kefan, Yan Yueshan, and the others.
In fact, after the World Business Alliance knew that Luo Zheng had returned to the Cloud Hall, they had reminded Ning Yudie several times to let Luo Zheng come to Tian Xie City. However, every time, Ning Yudie would use the excuse that Luo Zheng was in closed-door cultivation as an excuse to refuse. After all, Luo Zheng was a member of the Cloud Hall. Although the two Sects had joined forces at this time, they could not directly intervene in the matters of the Cloud Hall.
After a simple greeting, Shi Kefan immediately said, "Luo Zheng, since you are willing to appear in Tian Xie City, I presume you will also fight against Cui Xie with our World Business Alliance, right?"
Shi Kefan's words were very clever. He knew that there was an irreconcilable conflict between Luo Zheng and Cui Xie, so after he spoke, he did not say that he would fight against the Tian Xie Sect, but directly said that he would fight against Cui Xie.
Luo Zheng nodded slightly. "Of course."
"Then please move to the Runic Tower. With the help of your runes, our chances of fighting against Cui Xie will increase by 20%!" Shi Kefan said quickly.
Increase by 20% …
Hearing Shi Kefan's words, everyone was shocked. However, on second thought, if Luo Zheng could arrange a formation more powerful than the Cloud Hall's Sect-Protecting Formation, coupled with the strength of the World Business Alliance, it was really possible!

Chapter 715
Everyone's gazes were focused on Luo Zheng, especially Shi Kefan, Yan Yueshan, and Mo Haishan.
Although Luo Zheng had entered the False Tribulation Realm and was a great supplement to the combat power of the Cloud Hall, Luo Zheng's rune technique was more outstanding compared to the strength he had shown. Therefore, everyone felt that Luo Zheng should join the Runic Tower!
Moreover, Luo Zheng had no reason to refuse. Everyone was on the same side now. The World Business Alliance might need Luo Zheng's help to fight against the Tian Xie Sect, but Luo Zheng also needed to rely on the World Business Alliance to fight against Cui Xie.
Just as everyone was looking forward to it, Luo Zheng shook his head and said, "Alliance Master Shi, I'm sorry, but I can't enter the Runic Tower now."
"Why?" Shi Kefan was also a little surprised by Luo Zheng's rejection.
"The current situation doesn't give me enough time to set up a large formation," Luo Zheng shook his head.
Luo Zheng was drawing divine patterns, not runes. The time and experience it took far exceeded that of ordinary runes. When Luo Zheng set up the Minor Heavenly Cycle Three Killing Formation for the Cloud Hall, it took Luo Zheng nearly two months.
Moreover, the Cloud Hall was merely equivalent to a tenth of the size of Ocean of Clouds City. As for Heavenly Awakening City, it was more than 3 times larger than Ocean of Clouds City. Setting up a grand formation to protect such an enormous city? There was no time at all.
Luo Zheng immediately said, "But don't worry, Alliance Master Shi. I will choose to participate in the battle."
It was time to resolve the conflict between him and Cui Xie. Before that, Luo Zheng still had one thing to worry about. Since the last Martial Arts Conference, Luo Zheng had not received any news from Luo Yan.
However, based on Cui Xie's judgment that Luo Yan should still be fine when he wanted to take Xi Youqin away. However, where was Luo Yan now? Where did Cui Xie hide it? Luo Zheng knew nothing about it.
Shi Kefan knew he couldn't change Luo Zheng's mind, but in the past, this young man was able to fight against a Divine Core warrior when he was just a Divine Reflection warrior. And now, Luo Zheng was already a middle stage False Tribulation Realm martial artist. Would he be able to cross a realm and fight against a Life and Death Realm martial artist?
If possible, then Luo Zheng's strength was likely to be above Shi Kefan and the others …
"Since Luo Zheng has made his own choice, I won't force you. You can form a small team with the others and listen to the orders of the allied forces," Shi Kefan said with a smile.
Luo Zheng nodded and prepared to register.
Unexpectedly, just as Luo Zheng left the chamber, he saw a white figure shoot down like a meteor and smash the dome of the chamber.
"Bam!"
Broken pieces of rock flew in all directions. Xi Youqin who had been quietly following behind Luo Zheng shrunk her head and hid behind him like a little kitten.
Actually, as a Divine Core warrior, she could easily block all the flying debris with her protective Zhen Yuan. However, she didn't have the intention to do so, she just hid behind Luo Zheng.
Everyone looked over and saw that the white figure was indeed Ning Yudie!
Ning Yudie looked really pitiful right now.
Her long black hair, which had always been draped behind her, was now tied into a ponytail. This outfit made her look much livelier, but at this time, half of the ponytail was burnt and scattered on her shoulders.
Ning Yudie's body was covered with wounds as well. A few parts of her clothes were burnt, and there were even a few bruises on her face.
"Palace Chief!"
"Palace Chief! Are you alright? "
Seeing Ning Yudie in such a state, Daoist Dameng and a few Sect Elders from the Cloud Hall immediately went up and surrounded her.
Shi Kefan asked with concern, "Palace Chief Ning, were you attacked by the Tian Xie Sect?"
Ning Yudie replied with a relaxed expression, "I'm fine, I accidentally walked into their trap and suffered some minor injuries!"
It turned out that Ning Yudie had decided to launch a sneak attack on the Tian Xie Sect's campsite, which was located a thousand miles away. There were over 4000 independent warriors gathered in this campsite, and the weakest of them was at least at the Divine Reflection realm. There was only one Void Tribulation warrior among them.
When these Divine Reflection realm warriors joined forces and used Cui Xie's battle techniques, their power was truly astonishing. However, if Wu Zhanhe and Cui Xie weren't there, Ning Yudie would still be able to take them down. However, this time, Ning Yudie had underestimated the enemy, because the only Void Tribulation warrior was called Xuan Chen.
From the start, Ning Yudie had planned to take the life of the only Void Tribulation warrior first, so that she wouldn't be restricted later. As long as she killed that Void Tribulation warrior, the rest of the motley crew would be like a dish on a plate to Ning Yudie.
However, what Ning Yudie didn't expect was that this warrior called Xuan Chen was a bait from the start!
Yes, Cui Xie and Wu Zhanhe still hadn't made a move against Ning Yudie, but they had sent Xuan Chen. This person was the chief military advisor of the Kingdom of Evil Deities! This Xuan Chen had always been resourceful. This time, he had purposely led thousands of Divine Reflection realm warriors to set up a trap here, waiting for Ning Yudie.
The result was very smooth. Ning Yudie had indeed pounced into the trap he had set up.
From the moment Ning Yudie had prepared to assassinate Xuan Chen, she had already walked into one trap after another that Xuan Chen had set up.
Under Xuan Chen's command, the more than four thousand Illuminating Spirit Realm cultivators worked together in an orderly manner. These Illuminating Spirit Realm cultivators were nothing more than ants in Ning Yudie's eyes, but when they merged into a single entity, even Ning Yudie could not avoid them!
So Ning Yudie immediately fell into this trap. That Xuan Chen didn't panic. On one hand, he blocked her attacks, and on the other hand, he directed those Divine Reflection realm warriors to strangle Ning Yudie.
It could be said that Ning Yudie's strength was outstanding. Even though she had fallen into the trap set up by Xuan Chen, under her full strength, she had forcibly torn open a gap and escaped!
This was also the reason why Ning Yudie looked so miserable. Thousands of Divine Reflection realm warriors had joined forces to attack. There were all kinds of methods, wind, fire, lightning, poison. They greeted her uniformly. How could she not be miserable?
"Hall Master, please don't act on your own again!"
"Hall Master, this time the Tian Xie Sect set up a trap. Next time, I'm afraid they will personally take action. Please reconsider!"
A few Sect Elders were trying their best to persuade Ning Yudie. But, they had repeated the same thing countless times, and it didn't work at all.
Right at this moment, Ning Yudie suddenly saw Luo Zheng. At the same time, she also saw Xi Youqin who was standing behind Luo Zheng. She immediately raised her brows. That woman was still lying on Luo Zheng's back? Looking at her posture, it seemed like she was just trying to avoid the stones that flew out from the roof. Why would a Divine Core warrior be afraid of these things?
This woman was really pretentious!
Ning Yudie commented in her mind. After venting her dissatisfaction, she let out a cold snort and prepared to leave.
Right at this moment, Luo Zheng stood in front of Ning Yudie. Fei Han's words suddenly echoed in his mind. Women were meant to be coaxed, but how?
"What are you doing?" Ning Yudie raised her head and stared at Luo Zheng. Her phoenix eyes were filled with a ferocious killing intent. During this period of time, Ning Yudie had killed too many people, and naturally, she had absorbed a lot of killing intent.
However, aside from the killing intent, there was also a trace of panic in her eyes as she stared at Luo Zheng.
Shi Kefan, Yan Yueshan, Mo Haishan, and everyone from the Cloud Hall were all looking at Luo Zheng. No matter what, if Luo Zheng could persuade Ning Yudie, it would be a good thing. But, they had no idea how Luo Zheng could persuade her to stop leaving Heaven Awaken City alone.
"Xiao Die, don't be so stubborn …" Luo Zheng stared at Ning Yudie and said with a gentle voice.
Hearing Luo Zheng's words, many Void Tribulation Realm warriors in the meeting hall nearly fell to the ground.
This Luo Zheng really didn't treat himself as an outsider, nor did he treat Ning Yudie as a Hall Master!
Hearing Luo Zheng's tone, Ning Yudie's heart rippled slightly. However, from the corner of her eyes, she suddenly glanced at Xi Youqin behind Luo Zheng. When she saw the faintly discernible expression on Xi Youqin's face, the ripples in her heart were immediately covered by anger. Then, her voice rose an octave. "What did you call me? Is Xiao Die something you can call? You are a member of the Cloud Hall, and I am the Hall Master of the Cloud Hall. Then, I am the ruler, and you are only the subject! Luo Zheng, please understand your position clearly! "
When Shi Kefan saw this situation, he knew it was bad. He wasn't a member of the Cloud Hall, so he didn't know much about the relationship between Ning Yudie and Luo Zheng. Although the Elders of the Cloud Hall knew about it, they couldn't spread this matter outside. Could it be that Daoist Master Dameng had told Shi Kefan that his Hall Master was being so stubborn because of jealousy?
So, Shi Kefan hurried over and prepared to mediate.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 716
However, before Shi Kefan could step forward, Luo Zheng got angry first.
Luo Zheng's personality had always been like this. Perhaps he didn't understand Elder Fei Han's words. Women were meant to be coaxed …
Originally, he wanted to gently persuade Ning Yudie, but he was slapped in the face by Ning Yudie. His face suddenly turned ugly.
Seeing the change in Luo Zheng's expression, Ning Yudie raised her head and sneered, "What? Am I wrong? "
"Right? That's right! "Luo Zheng chuckled, his face cold." You're right! You are the Hall Master of the Cloud Hall, a high and mighty figure. But now you are wrong! From now on, I am no longer a member of the Cloud Hall! "
This time, not only Shi Kefan felt bad, but everyone else also felt bad. It seemed like a fight was about to break out!
Ao Xiang couldn't get a word in this meeting hall, so he had been hiding in the corner and didn't speak. When he saw Luo Zheng daring to talk to the Hall Master of the Cloud Hall like this, he was stunned. This Luo Zheng was too fierce …
"Not a member of the Cloud Hall?" Ning Yudie sneered, "Do you think you can enter and leave the Cloud Hall as you wish? Those who leave the Cloud Hall without permission will be hunted down by the entire Cloud Hall! "
Shi Kefan, Yan Yueshan, and Mo Haishan finally squeezed in front of the two and separated them. Shi Kefan also made a "shh" gesture, indicating that the two should stop talking. Unexpectedly, it didn't work at all.
"I want to see how the Cloud Hall will hunt me down!" Luo Zheng didn't give in at all.
As soon as Luo Zheng spoke, he suddenly saw that Ning Yudie's eyes were filled with tears. Her face was full of sadness, and she was about to cry. She didn't quarrel with Luo Zheng anymore. Instead, she gritted her teeth and released her True Spirit. Her figure suddenly turned into an escape light, flying out of the Tianqi City!
"Hall Master!"
"Hall Master!"
When the several Elders saw this, they were shocked. Although Ning Yudie's injuries weren't serious and she only had some external injuries, she still needed to rest in Heavenly Awakening City for two days. How could she run away like this? As for Daoist Greatdream, he transformed into a streak of light, chasing after Ning Yudie.
Luo Zheng also seemed to be angry. He snorted coldly and left in a huff! Xi Youqin followed closely behind him.
As for Shi Kefan, Yan Yueshan, and the others, they exchanged glances.
"What do you think is going on with their quarrel? What kind of conflict is there between Luo Zheng and Ning Yudie? " Shi Kefan shook his head, puzzled. The dignified Hall Master of the Cloud Hall was actually crying in front of so many people. This was an inconceivable thing in itself.
"I don't feel it. I feel that the two of them are quarreling like children …" Yan Yueshan said.
Mo Haishan also didn't say anything. He stood to the side silently.
"Yes, you're right. The two of them aren't quarreling like children! It seems like they're quarreling with a couple! " When Shi Kefan said this, the three Alliance Masters' faces immediately revealed a certain expression. Shi Kefan's last guess seemed to be the most consistent with the truth of the matter.
This wasn't something that was very difficult to guess. It was just that no one in the Merchant Alliance knew about it. From the beginning, Ning Yudie didn't bring Luo Zheng with her. When the two of them met, they quarreled in a childish and boring tone. Other than this, there was no other possibility.
However, the three Alliance Masters shook their heads helplessly. Now that they were facing a powerful enemy, what were these two doing? Quarreling at this critical juncture was really too … capricious.
Luo Zheng walked in front, panting with rage. Xi Youqin followed closely behind him.
Before long, they arrived at the residence that the Merchant Alliance had arranged for Luo Zheng. It was an exquisitely decorated courtyard.
After entering the courtyard, Luo Zheng sat down angrily. Xi Youqin quickly helped Luo Zheng brew a pot of tea. She filled the teacup to the brim and placed it in front of Luo Zheng.
Seeing Luo Zheng's distressed appearance, Xi Youqin suddenly leaned over to Luo Zheng's back and shouted, "Luo Zheng!"
"Hm?" Luo Zheng stared blankly at the ceiling before responding.
"You're not magnanimous enough," Xi Youqin said.
Luo Zheng smiled helplessly and nodded silently.
Xi Youqin was right. He was indeed not magnanimous enough. Or rather, he was not magnanimous enough towards Ning Yudie.
In fact, Luo Zheng was only 20 years old. He had just passed the juvenile stage and entered the youth stage. Judging from the heart of martial arts, Luo Zheng was indeed a qualified warrior. It could even be said that he was a natural warrior!
But from another perspective, he was still a young man. When it came to relationships, his mentality was not good and he was not mature. Just like what Xi Youqin said, he was not magnanimous enough in front of Ning Yudie!
At this moment, Xun suddenly appeared beside Luo Zheng. She looked at Xi Youqin with a hint of ridicule. "Isn't this the result you wanted?"
Xi Youqin had always been afraid of Xun. What she was afraid of was not Xun's strength, but her words. She often made Xi Youqin feel at a loss.
But now, Xi Youqin nodded and said resentfully, "This is indeed the result I wanted, but Luo Zheng is not happy."
"How do you know if Luo Zheng is happy or not?" Xun asked again.
Xi Youqin shook her head and said, "He definitely won't be happy."
Indeed, just like what Xi Youqin said, Luo Zheng was very depressed for the next two days.
In the past, no matter what time it was, Luo Zheng would speed up his cultivation as long as he was free. But these two days, he spent all his time in a daze and sighed from time to time.
Sitting in the room, Luo Zheng stared blankly at the window. From time to time, Ning Yudie's face appeared in his mind. Does this woman like to cry so much? Isn't it just a sentence?
Thinking back and forth, Luo Zheng felt more and more irritable. He drank twenty jars of wine in one breath.
However, with his physique, let alone twenty jars, even if he drank two hundred or two thousand jars, he still wouldn't get drunk. Instead, the entire courtyard was filled with the smell of wine.
Xi Youqin was extremely fond of cleanliness. When she smelled the strong smell of wine, she frowned. She originally wanted to go to Luo Zheng's room, but in the end, she hesitated. After all, she wasn't the cause of his irritation. She also knew that she wasn't important to Luo Zheng. She couldn't untie the knot in his heart.
Two days later, Luo Zheng left Xi Youqin in Tianqi City. After registering with the World Business Alliance, they quickly formed a small team and accepted the mission issued by the Alliance. They left Tianqi City and went straight east.
The Tian Xie Sect expanded from east to west, step by step.
According to the previous Sect wars, the lower level warriors from both sides would fight each other until they gained the upper hand. Then, the middle level warriors would be dragged into the fight, and finally, the upper level warriors would be dragged into the fight. At that point, the Sect war would come to an end.
Of course, some Sects had conducted a 'blitzkrieg' before. When the Sect war had just begun, all the mighty warriors from the Sect would come out and 'kill' the top warriors from the opposing Sect.
Similar to the Sect wars, all kinds of strategies were used.
But this Sect war was different.
The Tian Xie Sect had used a strange strategy. They rarely used their top warriors, and even if they didn't use any of their top warriors, they would still be able to suppress the World Business Alliance and the Cloud Hall.
Therefore, Tianqi City had no choice but to use their top warriors to fight against the Tian Xie Sect's bottom warriors.
No matter if it was the Divine Core warriors, Void Tribulation warriors, or Divine Core warriors, all of them were divided into small groups and sent out in batches to ambush the Tian Xie Sect's army. They were constantly trying to break down the Tian Xie Sect's invasion. This was a method Tianqi City had come up with after they had no other choice.
In the beginning, this method was indeed effective. The top warriors were able to suppress the enemy, and the Tian Xie Sect was still in the stage of accumulating their strength. Therefore, they couldn't send out any more top warriors to fight back.
But recently, a few warriors from the Cloud Hall and the World Business Alliance had died one after another. Most of them were Mid Void Tribulation warriors, and some were even Late Void Tribulation warriors.
When Luo Zheng went to accept a mission, he bumped into Hua Tianming and the others who had just returned.
Half a month ago, Hua Tianming, Pei Tianyao, and Red Maple had all accepted a mission. But now, after completing their mission, they had to accept a new one.
Therefore, under Luo Zheng's request, the four of them formed a new team.
After learning that Luo Zheng had joined their team, Red Maple was so happy that he almost jumped up and down. Luo Zheng's heroic performance was still fresh in Red Maple's mind.
The three of them had come to a consensus. Luo Zheng's current strength was equivalent to an ordinary Life and Death warrior. With such an abnormal monster in their team, perhaps any problem they faced would be easily solved! Perhaps, their team's survival rate had also increased by a lot! How could they not be happy?
"Now that Luo Zheng has joined us, our team's name has to be changed! I think we should be called the Red Maple Team! "Red Maple said with a proud smile on his face.
Hua Tianming glared at Red Maple and said with a cold voice, "I don't agree, it sounds awful."
"Awful? How is that possible? Since we don't want to be called the Red Maple Team, then let's call it … the Bai Li Team! "Red Maple said with a grin on his face.
"It's still awful!" Hua Tianming shook his head.
The four of them kept flying at a low altitude, but Luo Zheng suddenly interrupted and said, "If that's the case, why don't you say it's called the Jia Lan Team?"
"Jia Lan … good name," Pei Tianyao suddenly nodded and praised. These two words were originally taken from Tantric Buddhism, meaning a guardian deity. However, Red Maple was still somewhat unhappy, "This name is inexplicable, I don't understand it at all. It's obviously not as domineering as I was before …"
Before he could speak, he heard a "whoosh" sound. Hua Tianming had already unsheathed his sword, so Red Maple tactfully shut his mouth.
Three days later, the Jia Lan Team appeared one thousand four hundred li east of Apocalypse City.
The Tian Xie Sect had originally started to expand from the east, but mainly from the northeast. However, since they had broken through Void Sky City, the entire east of the Central Domain had now been included in their expansion.
The four of them stood on a hillside and looked at a city not far away.
Hua Tianming took out the map in his hand and said while comparing it, "This city is called Tian Yun City. It's just a small city with a population of less than four hundred thousand."
The population of the central territory was truly too huge. For example, an extraordinarily large city like Heavenly Awakening City had a population that was counted in the hundreds of millions. On the other hand, even large cities like Void Sky City and Ocean of Clouds City had a population of a few tens of millions.
Therefore, a city with a population of only a few hundred thousand was indeed a small city.
"Although the population is small, this is a strategic military location. The Tian Xie Sect is building a Transmission Array here because it is located in a strategic location," Pei Tianyao said immediately.
Their mission this time was not to defeat the Tian Xie Sect's army, but to destroy the Transmission Array in Tian Yun City. Building a Transmission Array was not something that could be completed in a day or two. However, since the Tian Xie Sect planned to build a Transmission Array, there must be a lot of powerful martial artists guarding it.
"Only the four of us, can we complete it?" Red Maple's face showed a timid expression.
Pei Tianyao and Hua Tianming seemed to have gotten used to Red Maple's appearance and simply ignored him. This kid was naturally timid, but he still kept accepting missions. They didn't know if this kid was pretending to be timid or not!
"Let's go!" Hua Tianming ordered. Luo Zheng and the others had already rushed out.
Although this Sky Rhythm City was under the control of the Heavenly Evil Sect, most of the population were mortals. Basically, it was rare to see any cultivators. The construction of the Transmission Array was probably done in secret. The Heavenly Evil Sect couldn't send too many cultivators into the Sky Rhythm City with great fanfare. After all, that would arouse the suspicions of the Heavenly Awakening City.
However, what the Tian Xie Sect didn't know was that their news had already spread. Therefore, Luo Zheng led everyone to complete the mission of destroying the Transmission Array.
As soon as they entered the small city, someone tried to sell some specialties to Luo Zheng's group. Luo Zheng's group wasn't here for a tour, so they didn't care about these mortals. They directly explored the interior of Tian Yun City.
Luo Zheng suddenly spread out his perception. Tian Yun City wasn't big, so Luo Zheng used his perception to explore. If he could detect spatial fluctuations, he would naturally be able to find the location of the Transmission Array.
The problem was that the four of them had completely explored Tian Yun City, but there were no spatial fluctuations at all. This made Luo Zheng very puzzled!
"Something's wrong," Luo Zheng suddenly said vigilantly.
"I also feel that something's wrong!" Red Maple quickly said. Since entering Tian Yun City, he had been very nervous. It was said that timid people had an innate ability to escape. Red Maple was very timid, and his innate ability to escape was also very amazing. As long as Red Maple felt that something was wrong, there would always be a big crisis.
Hua Tianming looked back and forth. When they had just entered Tian Yun City, they could still see many mortals on the streets.
These mortals were still indifferent to the Sect wars between martial artists. Whether it was the Tian Xie Sect, the Cloud Hall, or the World Business Alliance, who controlled this small city had nothing to do with them.
"The people have disappeared," Hua Tianming's face showed a cautious expression.
Pei Tianyao also looked around vigilantly. He suddenly pointed at the city wall not far away. On the originally low city wall of Tian Yun City, a light blue barrier suddenly began to appear. It was this barrier that enveloped the entire small city!
At this time, the mortals who had disappeared opened their doors one by one and walked out.
"These people, what's wrong?" Red Maple looked at the mortals and asked strangely.
More and more people appeared on the streets of this small city. These people's eyes were lifeless, but they kept walking back and forth. Although they still had vitality, they looked like zombies without the slightest spirit.
"Trouble is coming," Luo Zheng's face sank and said to Hua Tianming, "You guys stay here!"
Although their team didn't appoint anyone as the leader, it was obvious at this time that only Luo Zheng was the core existence.
Hearing Luo Zheng's order, Hua Tianming drew his sword, sat cross-legged, hugged his sword, closed his eyes tightly, and began to accumulate power.
Hua Tianming was very clear that with Luo Zheng's current strength, if he was cautious, it was definitely an unprecedentedly strong enemy. Of course, Hua Tianming didn't dare to be negligent.
Like Hua Tianming, Pei Tianyao and Red Maple also had cautious expressions. They vigilantly stared at the mortals around them, even though these mortals were no threat to them.
Luo Zheng suddenly soared into the sky, turned into an escape light, and rushed towards the blue barrier. Before Luo Zheng could get close, he heard a faint laughter.
"Ahahaha! I didn't expect my luck to be so good today. The cage I set up in Tian Yun City actually captured a Middle Stage False Tribulation Realm cultivator! "At this time, a sharp voice suddenly sounded.
The voice even had a trace of childishness, like a three or four year old child. The voice was loud and sharp.
"Who are you?" Luo Zheng stared at the blue barrier and asked.
After a while, a faint silhouette appeared above the blue barrier. The silhouette gradually became clearer, revealing the appearance of a real person. However, Luo Zheng felt that this wasn't a real person, but a soul, or a projection of a soul.
Even on the Sea God Continent, Luo Zheng had never seen such a secret technique that could project one's soul.
Of course, in the Heavenly Feather Holy Land, Xun once talked to the Demon Night Clan's King. That was also a kind of soul projection, but it was beyond the scope of the Sea God Continent.
"Who are you? Answer me! "Luo Zheng frowned and asked.
As the silhouette became clearer, Luo Zheng saw a small boy. The boy looked delicate and cute, but his eyes were filled with ruthlessness.
"You don't need to know who I am. Once you enter my Mental Goblet, only death awaits you. Just like those guys, obediently die!" The boy's face was filled with childishness, but his words were filled with viciousness.
"Mental Goblet?" Luo Zheng asked unhurriedly.
The boy sneered, "There's no harm in telling you. I only need to gather the soul energy of the several hundred thousand mortals in this small city to project my soul! As for me, I have a Battle Soul Realm soul! "
Hearing the boy's words, Luo Zheng was speechless. It seemed like most of the False Tribulation Realm Elders who had died recently had died at the hands of this boy. Not to mention False Tribulation Realm Elders, even Life and Death Realm experts would find it hard to resist the soul of a Battle Soul Realm expert.
"You're not from the Central Domain, right?" Luo Zheng suddenly asked.
"So what if I am, so what if I'm not? You should die first! I'll tell you later! " The boy sneered and suddenly drilled into Luo Zheng's mind. Obviously, he wanted to use the advantage of his Battle Soul Realm to directly erase Luo Zheng's soul.

Chapter 717
Luo Zheng didn't know what method the boy used to project his soul into this small city.
But when he connected the dots to the strange scene in this small city with a population of several hundred thousand, and the boy mentioning something like the Spirit Cup, Luo Zheng guessed that he must have controlled the souls of these several hundred thousand mortals to allow the boy's soul to descend here.
Not only was this method inconceivable, but it was also extremely cruel.
Although the Sect Wars in the Central Domain were huge, and once a Sect War broke out, millions of disciples from both sides would be involved. It was truly a disaster with corpses strewn all over the place. However, it was rare for the common people to be involved.
Firstly, the combat strength of ordinary mortals in front of martial artists was close to zero. Secondly, the meaningless slaughter of mortals would attract too much baleful aura. The gains did not make up for the losses.
Moreover, these mortals were the foundation of the entire Central Domain. Countless geniuses were slowly born from these mortals. They were the cornerstone of the entire Central Domain's development. After all, the Central Domain didn't have a super cultivation holy land like the Sin Tower like the Sea God Continent. It could only rely on the accumulation of time and steadily develop.
If it weren't for the Seductive Sect, in another hundred years, one or two holy lands might have been born in the Central Domain. In another thousand years, there might be a seventh-rank holy land and several almighty Divine Sea Realm experts …
But this time, the Seductive Sect had broken the bottom line. They had no scruples at all.
Seeing the boy enter his mind, Luo Zheng didn't resist at all. Instead, he allowed the boy's soul to enter his mind.
"Have you given up resisting? The False Tribulation Realm martial artists of the Central Domain are too weak. Not only are they weak, but their souls are also weak beyond my imagination! " The boy sneered. The other False Tribulation Realm martial artists he had killed before had more or less put up some resistance. However, this martial artist in front of him didn't put up any resistance. According to the boy's estimation, Luo Zheng's soul was even weaker than the previous False Tribulation Realm martial artists.
Luo Zheng's soul floated quietly in his mind, its entire body glowing with a green luster. Luo Zheng's soul was only a step away from the Blue Soul Realm, but Qinglong had asked Luo Zheng to stop cultivating his soul and wait for his soul seed to be completed before he could break through to the Blue Soul Realm.
Creating a Soul Seed seemed to be a very complicated task. Even with Qinglong's vast knowledge, the progress was extremely slow.
But even so, the strength of Luo Zheng's soul far surpassed other warriors. Whether it was the Sea God Continent or the Central Domain, Luo Zheng had never met a second warrior with a Battle Soul Realm soul. Similarly, there were no Life and Death Realm warriors or Battle Saints on the Sea God Continent.
Luo Zheng estimated that only the Divine Sea Realm warriors from the Purple Heart Sacred Land and the Heavenly Feather Sacred Land could possess a Battle Soul Realm soul.
As for the cultivation of this boy's body, Luo Zheng didn't know, but it shouldn't be too far off …
Soon, the boy appeared in Luo Zheng's mind.
When the boy saw Luo Zheng, the sneer on his face suddenly froze. When the boy saw Luo Zheng's body glowing with a green luster, his voice trembled, "Blue, Blue Soul Realm?"
Luo Zheng smiled faintly, "My soul is indeed weak, but your soul is even weaker, just like an ant!"
As soon as Luo Zheng finished speaking, the boy flew out of Luo Zheng's mind with a "swoosh"! A battle between souls often happened in an instant. When the boy saw that Luo Zheng's soul was close to the Blue Soul Realm, how could he stay any longer?
"Since you're here, don't think you can leave!" Luo Zheng smiled faintly.
Then, from Luo Zheng's mind came a roar full of majesty!
"Roar!"
It was Qinglong's roar.
"What's that!"
The boy's face showed an extremely frightened expression. He saw that in the depths of Luo Zheng's mind, there seemed to be a huge dragon appearing. From the dragon's mouth, a ray of light rolled out. The ray of light emitted a great attraction, firmly nailing the boy's soul to the ground, unable to move at all.
Luo Zheng's soul floated in the void. He didn't intend to attack, but said to the boy indifferently, "I forgot to say, your opponent is not me, but it."
As soon as he finished speaking, the huge ray of light suddenly engulfed the boy, and then rolled him away …
Even if it was the powerful Evil Spirit of the Owl Beast, Qinglong had a way to capture it, not to mention that the boy was just a War Soul. How could he be Qinglong's opponent?
After Qinglong took the boy's soul away, he said to Luo Zheng, "This person's soul is very mixed. It should be formed by the souls of countless people gathered together. There's only a wisp of his soul in it."
"A wisp? Then the other souls must be the souls of the hundreds of thousands of ordinary mortals in this Sky Charm City. "Luo Zheng frowned.
"Yes, this boy seems to have used a kind of Soul Extraction Technique. He extracted a wisp of each person's soul and gathered it through his own soul," Qinglong said slowly.
Luo Zheng nodded, then opened his eyes and slowly descended towards Hua Tianming and the others.
"Luo Zheng, what happened? I think I just saw a soul enter your mind. "Hua Tianming's eyes were the sharpest, and there was a faint trace of worry on his face. A battle on the soul level was extremely dangerous, and he didn't know if Luo Zheng could deal with it properly.
"I've already killed it," Luo Zheng replied with a faint smile.
At this moment, the mortals in this small city opened their eyes one by one. However, most of them were holding their heads, and their spirits were dispirited. Some of them had even fainted on the ground.
A wisp of their souls had been extracted and used by the boy. After being swallowed by Qinglong, their souls were damaged at the same time, so they were naturally in pain.
Fortunately, Luo Zheng arrived in time. If this continued for a long time, the boy would have extracted the souls of these hundreds of thousands of mortals bit by bit. At that time, these hundreds of thousands of mortals would have become a group of walking corpses in their muddle-headedness.
At the same time, in the Void Spirit Sect, 10,000 kilometers away from Sky Charm City.
After the Tian Xie Sect conquered the Void Spirit Sect, they set the Void Spirit Sect as the temporary headquarters of the Tian Xie Sect!
In the center of the Void Spirit Sect's main hall, there was an eight-immortal offering table, and in the center of the table was a small Eight Trigrams Mirror. At this moment, a cracking sound came from the Eight Trigrams Mirror, and a crack appeared in the center of the Eight Trigrams Mirror!
At the same time, a painful scream came from the Eight Trigrams Mirror.
What appeared in the Eight Trigrams Mirror was the boy's face!
"Heartless Master! What happened to you? "
"Heartless Master, wait a moment, I'll go and invite the Monarch here!"
Several Virtual Tribulation Realm warriors who were waiting in the hall hurriedly flew away. This Heartless Master was extremely strange. Even though he was only projecting a soul in the hall, with such a perverted guy around, every breath was more dangerous. These Virtual Tribulation Realm warriors were nominally going to find Cui Xie, but in fact, they were taking the opportunity to escape.
After a while, Cui Xie's figure appeared in the hall.
Seeing a crack on the Eight Trigrams Mirror on the table, Cui Xie's face revealed a strange expression. He cupped his hands towards Heartless Master and asked, "Master, what's wrong?"
"Hmph, the wisp of soul I projected through the Eight Trigrams Mirror was destroyed!" The boy in the Eight Trigrams Mirror said coldly.
Cui Xie's face also revealed a surprised expression. He then asked, "Even if it's just a projected soul, it still has the strength of a Battle Soul Realm warrior. Who in the Central Domain can destroy your soul?"
"This person's soul is not only extremely powerful, but I also discovered a great secret!" The boy's face revealed a cold smile.
"Oh? What secret did you discover? "Cui Xie also revealed a look of interest.
"You don't need to know this. When the Astral Sea Storm is over, I will personally come to the Northern Heaven Region! Leave that boy to me! " The boy's face revealed a trace of an evil smile, and then his face gradually disappeared from the Eight Trigrams Mirror.
Hearing Heartless Master's words, Cui Xie's face darkened. Cui Xie could guess who Heartless Master was referring to. In the younger generation of the Central Domain, only Luo Zheng had such a powerful soul.

Chapter 718
Cui Xie was very clear about the origin of this Heartless Sweetheart.
On the other side of the Chaotic Astral Sea was a vast continent. There used to be seven Holy Kingdoms on this continent, as well as some other races.
Compared to that continent, the vast Central Domain could only be regarded as a large island.
Three of the seven Holy Kingdoms had been destroyed because of the war with the Dragon Bloodline Clan. Thus, the current situation of the four Holy Kingdoms was formed.
This Heartless Sweetheart was from the same Holy Kingdom as the Saintess he had met in the Heavenly Mist Immortal Tomb, Yu Da's Holy Kingdom!
After the last trip to the Heavenly Mist Immortal Tomb, the Life and Death Realm "Elder Gong" from Yu Da's Holy Kingdom had indeed fulfilled his promise. He had passed the inner jade slip of the Five Poison Scripture to Wu Zhanhe through a secret method. Over the past two years, Wu Zhanhe had spent most of his time comprehending the inner jade slip of the Five Poison Scripture.
As for the Saintess, although she had vowed in the Heavenly Mist Immortal Tomb that she would lead the experts of the Holy Kingdom to crush the Central Domain, it was impossible.
However, it was extremely difficult to cross the Chaotic Astral Sea.
In the sky above the depths of the Chaotic Astral Sea, there was an unusually violent Astral Sea Storm all year round. The Northern Heaven Domain and the four Holy Kingdoms were separated by this storm.
However, according to the Heartless Sweetheart, although this storm was continuous, it would weaken every few years. As long as the Astral Sea Storm weakened, the Saintess of Yu Da's Holy Kingdom would definitely go out and crush the Northern Heaven Domain.
From this point of view, Cui Xie and Yu Da's Holy Kingdom were not on the same side. With Cui Xie's character, he would never be willing to be a vassal. His ambition was not just the Central Domain. Even the four Holy Kingdoms were not his end. He, Cui Xie, was destined to set foot on a higher level!
However, for now, Cui Xie was cooperating with the Great Yu Divine Nation. Both sides were getting what they needed. Cui Xie had benefited a lot from the Great Yu Divine Nation. Therefore, he wouldn't refuse their help.
Earlier, Cui Xie had speculated that Luo Zheng's body must be hiding a huge secret. Unfortunately, Luo Zheng was too cunning. Last time, Luo Zheng was trapped in the Yunmeng Swamp, but he still managed to slip away. What was even more infuriating was that he even took away the Xi Family's young miss!
After that, Cui Xie and Wu Zhanhe joined forces and attacked the Void Spirit Sect. After that, they sent their men to the Cloud Hall and exchanged the Void Spirit Sect's Sect Chief, Daoist Qing Xu, for the young lady from the Xi family. In Cui Xie's opinion, this was a deal that was set in stone. The Void Spirit Sect's Sect Chief would be exchanged for a Divine Core junior, not to mention the young lady from the Xi family was the great granddaughter of Daoist Qing Xu.
What was even more unexpected was that Luo Zheng had actually taken away the young lady's Primordial Yin.
Because of this, Cui Xie's plan to set up the Heavenly Fiend Joyous Union Formation had failed. He had no idea how many years it would take before the next Extreme Purple Yin Body appeared and was found by Cui Xie.
Luo Zheng was too ruthless, as if he was Cui Xie's nemesis. Not only did Cui Xie want to get rid of that young man, he also wanted to take away the secret in Luo Zheng's body, and take away Luo Zheng's luck!
Besides, he couldn't afford to be slow. If those mighty warriors from the Great Yu Divine Nation arrived here, Cui Xie wouldn't have the chance to do so.
Thinking of this, Cui Xie also felt that he had to speed up his plan. As for Wu Zhanhe, it would be best if he told him not to go into seclusion!
In Sky Rhythm City, Luo Zheng and the others had completed their first mission. However, they didn't find the so-called "Transmission Array" here. This Sky Rhythm City was originally a trap set up by the Tian Xie Sect to lure and kill the False Tribulation Realm warriors of the alliance.
Luo Zheng didn't care if he could complete the task or not. In fact, he had rushed out in such a hurry because he was worried about Ning Yudie.
Although Ning Yudie had already broken through to the Life and Death Realm, when she went out alone, no one could find her. That day, Daoist Big Dream rushed out with Ning Yudie, but with Daoist Big Dream's speed, how could he track her? In the blink of an eye, they had disappeared into the horizon. Until now, there had been no news of Ning Yudie.
What if she became dizzy and directly charged into the headquarters of the Tian Xie Sect? With her strength alone, she definitely wasn't a match for Wu Zhanhe and Cui Xie …
"Since we didn't find the Transmission Array, we have to go back and report," Hua Tianming said to Luo Zheng.
Although their trip this time seemed to be very smooth, and they even broke the Spirit Cup set up by the boy, if it weren't for Luo Zheng, how could Hua Tianming, Red Maple, and Pei Tianyao break the Spirit Cup? Facing such a powerful soul attack, they would have most likely fallen by now.
"Wait," Luo Zheng's face showed a trace of hesitation.
"What's the matter?" Hua Tianming and Pei Tianyao's faces were suddenly filled with vigilance. Could there be something else in Sky Rhythm City?
At this time, Luo Zheng suddenly took out a white nameplate in his hand. This nameplate originally belonged to Ning Yudie. She had given it to Luo Zheng in the Road of Smelting Trial. This way, the two of them could directly use the nameplate to transmit sound.
Generally speaking, there were three ways for a martial artist to transmit sound. The first way was to directly transmit sound through True Essence. This method of transmitting sound was suitable for relatively short distances, at most a few hundred feet. The second way was to use a Sound Transmitting Talisman. The range of a Sound Transmitting Talisman was relatively long. There were Sound Transmitting Talismans hundreds of miles away, but the cost of making them was relatively expensive.
As for a Sound Transmitting nameplate, it had a range of about one or two hundred miles. Within this range, one could directly use the nameplate to transmit sound.
Holding Ning Yudie's nameplate, Luo Zheng poured a wisp of True Essence into it and tried to transmit sound, but the nameplate didn't respond in the slightest …
Luo Zheng's face showed a dim luster. It seemed that Ning Yudie wasn't within a hundred miles.
"Let's go back. We'll take another mission!" Luo Zheng faintly smiled.
Seeing Luo Zheng's neurotic appearance, Hua Tianming and the others looked at each other in dismay. They had no idea what Luo Zheng was up to.
On the way back, Luo Zheng poured True Essence into the nameplate every once in a while. The white nameplate didn't respond, but Luo Zheng didn't give up.
Halfway back, Luo Zheng once again poured True Essence into the nameplate. At this time, he suddenly discovered a trace of fluctuation coming from the nameplate!
Luo Zheng's eyes lit up. He hesitated for a moment, then asked, "Little Die … is that you?"
There was no response from the other side of the nameplate.
"She heard me." Luo Zheng was absolutely certain. Ning Yudie should be within a hundred miles of him, but in which direction?
Luo Zheng suddenly stopped.
"Boss, what's wrong?" Red Maple looked at the neurotic Luo Zheng and couldn't help but ask. Ever since Luo Zheng gave him a pair of low-grade sacred tools, Red Maple had been calling him boss non-stop.
"Are there any big cities around here?" Luo Zheng asked.
Hua Tianming also approached Luo Zheng with a strange expression and said, "To the southeast is River Wave City, but it's a stronghold built by the Tian Xie Sect. Most of the Tian Xie Sect's forces are gathered there, but it's easy to defend and hard to attack. Therefore, the Alliance has no plans to take River Wave City for the time being."
The Alliance and the Tian Xie Sect's forces were intertwined like canine teeth, and River Wave City was the Tian Xie Sect's sharpest tooth, ruthlessly nailed there. It was also an extremely important outpost of the Tian Xie Sect. It was said that the unpredictable military commander of the Tian Xie Sect, Xuan Chen, was guarding River Wave City!
Hearing Hua Tianming's words, Luo Zheng's eyes suddenly lit up. "It's there!"
Then, Luo Zheng turned into a streak of light and rushed towards River Wave City.
"Boss, what's going on?" Red Maple shrugged his shoulders.
"Follow him!" Hua Tianming and Pei Tianyao didn't hesitate and rushed forward. Now, they had almost unconditional trust in Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng maintained a high speed in the sky. At the same time, he used the sound transmission nameplate to communicate with Ning Yudie. However, Ning Yudie didn't say a word …
"Hall Master!"
"Ning Yudie!"
"Xiao Die!"
"Can you say something?"
No matter what method Luo Zheng used, the other side still turned a deaf ear.
This deepened Luo Zheng's worries. When she was angry, who knew what unwise things she would do?
So what if she had the strength of a Life and Death Realm expert? Since Cui Xie had the support of the four Divine Kingdoms, their methods weren't something the Cloud Hall could resist. Even a Life and Death Realm expert wouldn't be enough in front of a Divine Kingdom.
After rushing for sixty to seventy li, Luo Zheng stared at River Wave City in front of him, his eyes suddenly bursting with rage!
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 719
Below the River Wave City, several tens of thousands of Divine Reflection warriors were sitting together neatly. All of them were wearing the Lascivious Sect's uniform, and they were sitting in meditation while constantly releasing their True Yuan.
Many of them would occasionally take out low grade True Yuan Stones and crush them, absorbing the True Yuan contained within. After that, they would release the True Yuan through their own bodies.
Above these tens of thousands of Divine Reflection warriors, there were several hundred Divine Core warriors flying back and forth. They gathered all their True Yuan together, forming a few thick True Yuan beams and sending them upwards.
Above these Divine Core warriors, there were six Void Tribulation warriors wearing green outfits. These six Void Tribulation warriors were the ones controlling the True Yuan beams and forming a huge rune. Within the rune, there was one person, Ning Yudie herself!
No one in River Wave City was a match for Ning Yudie.
If it was in the past, no Sect would have been able to gather all the cultivators in their Sect together and display their full strength. Under such circumstances, Ning Yudie alone could slaughter all the cultivators in River Wave City.
But now, this Heavenly Evil Sect had such a strange battle tactic. The Zhen Yuan of tens of thousands of Divine Illumination Realm disciples were gathered together, forming a great formation that could cover the sky and cover the sun. This great formation was controlled by six False Tribulation Realm cultivators, and it actually trapped the Life and Death Realm Ning Yudie in the air above River Wave City!
A tired expression emerged on Ning Yudie's face, but she didn't give up. Instead, she kept attacking the huge rune.
No one dared to underestimate the strength of a Life and Death warrior. Every time Ning Yudie started accumulating her True Yuan, a frightening cold energy would shoot out from Ning Yudie's body. Under the extreme cold energy, the rune that trapped Ning Yudie suddenly dimmed down.
But, she still couldn't escape from the rune.
When the True Yuan in the rune became scarce, the Divine Reflection warriors took out True Yuan Stones to replenish their True Yuan, then continued sending their True Yuan up.
These Divine Reflection warriors didn't need to be responsible for attacking, they only needed to use their True Yuan.
From this point of view, this tactic was similar to the Minor Universal Triple Slaughter Formation deployed by Luo Zheng. This was the principle behind the Minor Universal Triple Slaughter Formation. It used more than a thousand small runes to provide energy to a huge rune, and with a clear division of labor, the efficiency was greatly increased.
The six Void Tribulation warriors kept controlling their True Yuan and using the rune to attack Ning Yudie.
The space within the rune was about 300 meters. Ning Yudie was dodging their attacks, trying her best to avoid them. It wouldn't be easy for them to kill Ning Yudie.
For a moment, the two sides actually confronted each other like this.
Luo Zheng didn't know how long they had been facing each other, but now that he had suddenly rushed over and saw Ning Yudie's miserable state, how could he not feel heartache?
From the bottom of his heart, how could Luo Zheng not care about Ning Yudie?
Compared to Xi Youqin, Ning Yudie's position in Luo Zheng's heart was much more important. However, Luo Zheng was angry and contradicted Ning Yudie. How could he have known that she would run away in anger?
Not only did she run away, she even dared to rush into such a dangerous place by herself. This River Wave City was almost half of the Heavenly Evil Sect's forces. Even the entire Alliance avoided River Wave City. Did Ning Yudie really intend to take River Wave City by herself? Wasn't she taking her own life as a joke?
After a while, Hua Tianming, Red Maple, and Pei Tianyao also rushed over. Seeing the scene in front of them, their expressions changed greatly.
"Hall Master! The Palace Lord was actually trapped inside! Hurry back to Heaven Awaken City and call for reinforcements! "Seeing this, Red Maple immediately revealed his true nature … …
"It's too late." Hua Tianming said with a serious expression.
Pei Tianyao's eyes lit up as well. As members of the Cloud Hall, how could they just watch their Hall Master die in front of them?
Right at this moment, Luo Zheng turned to Hua Tianming and the others and said, "Don't attack them for now, just wait here."
These warriors from the Tian Xie Sect were using a unique combat tactic, and the force they unleashed was enough to trap even Ning Yudie. If Hua Tianming and the others rushed forward, they would just be cannon fodder. Luo Zheng's mind was spinning rapidly. How should he deal with the current situation?
Without saying anything, Hua Tianming hovered in the air and drew his longsword. He closed his eyes and began accumulating strength.
Although his combat strength was weak right now, when facing these disciples from the Tian Xie Sect, he could only barely suppress a Late Divine Core warrior. As for ordinary Early False Tribulation warriors, Hua Tianming was no match for them.
Unless he could use the Sword Eye to accumulate strength, only then would he have the strength to fight them. As for Pei Tianyao and Red Maple, they could only stand behind Hua Tianming and protect him. A battle at this level was not something warriors at their level could interfere with.
Luo Zheng hovered in the air and slowly approached River Wave City. He was only a few miles away from River Wave City. At the same time, he raised the message token and said with a low voice, "Xiao Die, I can see you."
While speaking, he stared at Ning Yudie who was trapped in the rune. She was trapped in the huge rune, and she had just dodged a scorching flame. Then, she raised her head and looked in Luo Zheng's direction. However, she didn't say anything, she just continued attacking the rune.
"You can hear me." Luo Zheng said again.
It looked like Ning Yudie could hear Luo Zheng's voice from the very beginning, but she didn't want to respond.
"Still not saying anything? If you don't want to talk, I'm leaving. "Luo Zheng suddenly said in a half-joking manner.
Right at this moment, Luo Zheng noticed that Ning Yudie had started attacking the rune. However, the rune was very stable, and Ning Yudie's attack didn't affect it at all. At the same time, Ning Yudie said, "You can leave now. You're no longer a disciple of the Cloud Hall. My life and death has nothing to do with you."
Hearing Ning Yudie's words, Luo Zheng sighed in his mind. This woman was really stubborn. After thinking for a moment, Luo Zheng decided to restrain his temper. Then, he suddenly said, "I'm sorry, Xiao Die."
"Sorry for what?" Ning Yudie replied with an expressionless face.
"I shouldn't have spoken to you like that." Luo Zheng suddenly began to admit his mistake.
Ning Yudie pursed her lips. The zhenyuan in her hand turned into a blue frost wall, blocking in front of her. Suddenly, a circle of raging fire appeared in the rune. As soon as the circle of raging fire appeared, it tried to bind Ning Yudie.
Although the raging fire was powerful, it still couldn't break through Ning Yudie's frost wall.
"You shouldn't have done anything wrong. Aren't all the things you men do right?" Ning Yudie suddenly asked.
Luo Zheng's face revealed a trace of a bitter smile. It looked like Ning Yudie still cared about Xi Youqin's matter …
At this critical moment, Ning Yudie still had the interest to bring up this matter. Luo Zheng really admired her.
"I admit that I was wrong, okay?" Luo Zheng suddenly said pitifully.
"Too late," Ning Yudie replied.
"Then how do you want to forgive me?" Luo Zheng asked again.
"I'll forgive you if you die." The frost wall around Ning Yudie melted bit by bit. She suddenly sent out a stream of zhenyuan and began to reinforce the frost wall.
"Then I'll go die." Luo Zheng smiled and suddenly flew towards River Wave City!
"Ah!"
Seeing Luo Zheng madly rushing down, Ning Yudie couldn't help but cover her mouth and cry out softly. Was this guy crazy?
Although Ning Yudie was in a difficult situation, it wasn't certain that she would die. Even if others didn't act, she might still be able to escape!
She was trapped inside and was also looking for a way to break out. This kind of battle method that combined the power of tens of thousands of people was not without flaws. The zhenyuan gathered together was very heterogeneous. Not to mention the zhenyuan of tens of thousands of people, even the movements of six False Tribulation Realm martial artists could not be unified. As long as Ning Yudie had enough time, she believed that she could find a flaw using this.
If it was in the past, Ning Yudie would definitely warn Luo Zheng not to come over. Even if she couldn't break out of this battle method, it wouldn't be difficult for her to escape unscathed by using her trump card.
Now that Luo Zheng suddenly joined in, things became much more troublesome. Luo Zheng didn't know how terrifying this battle method was. How could Ning Yudie not be nervous now?
Xuan Chen, who was the most important person in River Wave City, suddenly revealed a cold smile when he saw a middle stage False Tribulation Realm martial artist rush out from not far away. "Another headless fly has entered the birdcage?"
Birdcage …
This kind of battle method that combined the power of tens of thousands of people was indeed passed down from the Great Yu Divine Nation. However, it seemed like Cui Xie and Wu Zhanhe weren't good at commanding. On the contrary, Xuan Chen was a natural commander. Under his command, the actions of tens of thousands of people were highly unified.
This kind of battle method had its own name, but Xuan Chen liked to call it the 'Birdcage'. Setting up a cage and waiting for the birds outside to break in, using the word 'Birdcage' to describe it was a more appropriate way to describe it.
Even the Palace Master of the Cloud Hall was trapped by Xuan Chen. As for that False Tribulation Realm martial artist, he should just kill him! Xuan Chen thought for a moment, then he extended his hand and took out a black war flag. After that, he waved it in the air and drew a special shape.
The Divine Reflection Realm martial artists on the ground didn't do anything, they were still sending their True Yuan up. On the other hand, the Divine Core martial artists in the middle started flying back and forth, changing the direction of their True Yuan and shooting it towards one of the False Tribulation Realm martial artists.
Originally, there were six False Tribulation Realm martial artists who had formed the huge talisman and trapped Ning Yudie within it. But now, three of them had split up and used the True Yuan sent up from the ground to attack Luo Zheng.
In other words, among these tens of thousands of martial artists, about half of them had turned their attention to attacking Luo Zheng.
With this, the pressure on Ning Yudie was greatly reduced, but Luo Zheng didn't show any fear on his face.
If Xuan Chen knew that this man was Luo Zheng, he wouldn't have just sent half of his strength to Luo Zheng. In Xuan Chen's opinion, this was more than enough. With the combined strength of tens of thousands of Divine Reflection Realm martial artists, there was no way a False Tribulation Realm martial artist could resist them!
Seeing this, Ning Yudie became anxious. Although she could protect herself within the talisman, it would take her a long time to break through it.
"Die!" Xuan Chen let out a cold laugh. He waved the black war flag in his hand once again, and at the same time, a yellow war flag appeared in his other hand. He lightly twirled it around his hand.
Zzz zzz zzz zzz …
The True Yuan of tens of thousands of Divine Reflection Realm martial artists turned into a huge blue electric net, wrapping around Luo Zheng. At the same time, the electric net started shrinking violently. The power it unleashed was far stronger than any ordinary False Tribulation Realm martial artist's attack.
In the distance, Hua Tianming's eyes were tightly shut. He knew that Luo Zheng was in great danger, so he was rapidly accumulating power. However, although the power of the "Sword Eye" was terrifying, it still needed a long time to activate.
As for Pei Tianyao and Red Maple …
Apart from acting as Hua Tianming's guards, the two of them could only watch from the sidelines!
"Boom!"
When the electric net shrank to its limit, it suddenly burst out with a blazing white light. After it completely condensed, its aura and power were comparable to a Life and Death Realm martial artist's full strength attack!
Ning Yudie's heart suddenly trembled, and a strong sense of regret spread through her heart.
"Luo Zheng!"
Even if it was Ning Yudie herself, if she was hit by this attack head-on, without any defense, she wouldn't be able to escape.
Although she knew that Luo Zheng's physique was extraordinary, and that he had already broken through to the False Tribulation Realm, could he really block it?
"You really want to die because of a sentence? Do you have to be so stupid?" Ning Yudie stared at the ball of light, her face full of worry!
After a while, the blazing white light slowly dissipated. Many martial artists' eyes were focused there. Xuan Chen's face had a cold smile. He should have turned into ashes, right?
However, when the light completely dissipated, he saw Luo Zheng's figure standing there unscathed, with a faint smile on his face. Seeing this, Xuan Chen's face suddenly became gloomy.
"Am I stupid?" Luo Zheng said to the Sound Transmission Nameplate in his hand.
Seeing that Luo Zheng was safe and sound, Ning Yudie's hanging heart immediately relaxed, but then her face darkened, and she said in a cold voice, "You'd better die!"
To be honest, the True Essence of tens of thousands of martial artists in the Divine Reflection Realm was indeed terrifying. Even if Luo Zheng had the body of a High-Grade Immortal Weapon, he still couldn't resist such a terrifying power. If Luo Zheng had taken that blow with his body, he would have been seriously injured even if he didn't die.
However, before the power suddenly erupted, Luo Zheng took the initiative to operate the power of the Law of Space, allowing himself to float outside the space!
This was the horror of the Law of Space. The Law of Space and the Law of Time were the two most mysterious laws among all the laws. It was not easy to control these laws, but once it was mastered, it had a very terrifying effect.
Now that Luo Zheng had comprehended the second level of the Law of Space, he was like a fish swimming in the space. No matter how stormy the waves were, Luo Zheng could be like a fish in water. It was very difficult to injure him unless the opponent was also proficient in the Law of Space!
Facing this brat who had suddenly appeared, Xuan Chen realized that he was in big trouble.
Previously, Xuan Chen relied on the "birdcage" to guard River Wave City safely. Occasionally, he would even lead thousands of martial artists out to gain experience. Ordinary martial artists in the False Tribulation Realm were no match for Xuan Chen. He only needed to wave the battle flag to easily kill those martial artists in the False Tribulation Realm.
This time, as soon as Xuan Chen returned to River Wave City, he suddenly encountered the Hall Master of the Cloud Hall. That woman attacked River Wave City like a madman.
In a hurry, thousands of people in River Wave City died in her hands. Even Xuan Chen himself almost lost his life in front of Ning Yudie. Fortunately, he ran fast and quickly organized all the martial artists in River Wave City to quickly set up his "birdcage"!
This time, Xuan Chen realized that he had caught a big bird. A Life and Death Realm expert was actually going to be killed by him. How could Xuan Chen not be excited?
It was only a matter of time. As long as Ning Yudie could not break out of the "birdcage", her Zhen Yuan would eventually be exhausted. At that time, wouldn't she be at the mercy of others?
Moreover, Xuan Chen had other plans!
That woman was the Hall Master of the Cloud Hall. She was probably the most powerful woman in the Central Domain. What was commendable was that she was still astonishingly young. She should not be married yet! If he could capture this woman alive, Xuan Chen had already begun to fantasize.
However, Xuan Chen was very confident that he could turn his fantasies into reality. As long as he exhausted this woman's Zhen Yuan, he had many methods in his hands to make her lose the ability to resist. He could even make her lose her spirit and become a slut …
Therefore, Xuan Chen was very patient in this battle. Under that patience, there was a soul that was as hungry as a wolf.
He never expected that a False Tribulation Realm martial artist would appear at this time.
River Wave City was the outpost of the Tian Xie Sect. For the time being, Heaven Awakening City would not send troops to attack River Wave City. However, why did this guy suddenly appear?
When Xuan Chen saw this False Tribulation Realm martial artist, he immediately used the power of the "birdcage" and prepared to kill him instantly.
However, he never expected that the False Tribulation Realm martial artist's strength would be far beyond Xuan Chen's imagination. With Xuan Chen's experience, he did not know what method Luo Zheng had used to avoid his attack.
He only knew that he was in danger now.
However, at this juncture, Xuan Chen would not easily retreat, let alone escape. River Wave City was a solid fortress. How could he escape? Could he outrun the Hall Master of the Cloud Hall?
Right now, Xuan Chen could only fight to the death.
Whoosh! Whoosh!
Xuan Chen quickly waved the flag. The six False Tribulation Realm elders also quickly changed their positions. On one hand, they extracted Zhen Yuan to support the rune in the sky. On the other hand, they formed another huge rune at the same time. As soon as the rune appeared, it suddenly enveloped Luo Zheng.
How could Luo Zheng do as Xuan Chen wished? He did not stop at all. He flew directly towards the Divine Reflection Realm martial artists below River Wave City. As long as these Divine Reflection Realm martial artists continuously supported the False Tribulation Realm martial artists above, this formation could not be broken.
Luo Zheng saw the problem at a glance. He sneered at the Divine Reflection Realm martial artists. Since these independent martial artists had chosen to rely on the Tian Xie Sect, they had to be prepared to die.

Chapter 720
All the Divine Reflection Realm warriors were sitting upright in rows. When they saw Luo Zheng diving down from the sky, their expressions changed dramatically.
They didn't know who Luo Zheng was, but just now, three Void Tribulation warriors and over ten thousand Divine Reflection Realm warriors couldn't do anything to Luo Zheng, so how could they possibly fight back?
Furthermore, Xuan Chen had given out a strict order, no one was allowed to retreat without permission. Even if they had to die, they had to die on the spot, and those who disobeyed the order would be killed without mercy!
Therefore, with Luo Zheng's incredible speed, these Divine Reflection Realm warriors became Luo Zheng's targets. With Luo Zheng's current strength, these Divine Reflection Realm warriors were no different than ants.
Luo Zheng was in River Wave City. Wherever he went, broken limbs and blood would fly all over the sky, forming a clear trail of blood.
At first, these Divine Reflection Realm warriors were able to hold their ground, as the Tian Xie Sect's deterrence was still very strong for these temporary independent warriors. But, as Luo Zheng kept slaughtering, the murderous aura unleashed from his body became stronger and stronger. Finally, these Divine Reflection Realm warriors who were sitting still couldn't hold on any longer.
None of them were willing to be killed like ants by Luo Zheng.
"That guy is crazy!"
"Staying here will only lead to death!"
"Run!"
At first, there were only a few warriors running for their lives. The Military Governors who were standing on both sides of the street were waving the huge blades in their hands, stopping these warriors from running away. Those who dared to run away were all killed by the Military Governors.
However, the number of warriors who chose to run for their lives was like an avalanche. More and more warriors were running away, and eventually, all the warriors in River Wave City were flying away in all directions …
Tens of thousands of Divine Reflection Realm warriors were running away in all directions. Even Xuan Chen couldn't control them all. There was no way he could kill all these Divine Reflection Realm warriors. Besides, Xuan Chen himself didn't have the ability to do so.
When the middle ranked Divine Core warriors saw that the situation was getting worse, they didn't want to stay put any longer. They immediately joined the fleeing army.
These independent cultivators did not have much loyalty to begin with. They chose the Tian Xie Sect because the Heavenly Evil Deities Nation was powerful. In the future, when the Heavenly Evil Deities Nation ruled the Central Domain, they would be able to get a share of the profits. However, to independent cultivators, their own lives were the most important.
Without a supply of true essence, how could the six Nihility Tribulation Realm cultivators trap Ning Yudie?
The huge rune covering Ning Yudie gradually began to dissipate. Then, Ning Yudie slightly raised her hand, and a little bit of snow-white light condensed in her hand, quickly forming a Profound Ice Halberd.
She looked coldly at Xuan Chen and said coldly, "Die!"
When Xuan Chen saw that the situation was getting worse, he immediately retreated out of River Wave City. But, how could Xuan Chen escape from Ning Yudie?
Ning Yudie gently waved the Profound Ice Halberd, and with a sway of her body, a long Ice Phoenix feather was dragged behind her, and it kept fluttering in the air.
"Huff … huff … huff …"
When Ning Yudie was a few dozen meters away from Xuan Chen, she immediately felt that her movements had slowed down. Not only was her flying speed slowed down, even the rotation of her true essence within her Dantian had slowed down.
As Xuan Chen's speed slowed down, Ning Yudie arrived in front of him in an instant. She stared at Xuan Chen with her cold eyes, then she gently blew at him.
In the blink of an eye, Xuan Chen's body was covered by a layer of frost, and his entire body was frozen into a pile of ice!
Before Xuan Chen could fall to the ground, Ning Yudie had already waved the Profound Ice Halberd in her hand and smashed it onto the human-shaped pile of ice.
"Clang!"
Xuan Chen's entire body was instantly smashed into pieces of ice.
On the other side, Luo Zheng was constantly chasing after the six Virtual Tribulation Realm warriors. When the six Virtual Tribulation Realm warriors saw that the situation was not good, they complained incessantly in their hearts. Naturally, they also chose to escape, and they fled in different directions. They flew in six different directions.
After Luo Zheng chased after two people, he stopped. He was afraid that Ning Yudie would run away again. Otherwise, with Luo Zheng's current speed, he could at least chase after three or four Virtual Tribulation Realm warriors.
Sure enough, just as Luo Zheng expected, after Ning Yudie killed Xuan Chen, she glared fiercely at Luo Zheng in the distance, then turned into a white light and flew towards the south!
Seeing this, the vital essence in Luo Zheng's body suddenly erupted, and he also increased his speed to the extreme, chasing after Ning Yudie.
