As for Hua Tianming and the others, they could only stare blankly at the two people flying away, looking at each other in dismay.
"What should we do?!" Red Maple asked with his eyes wide open.
"No need to chase. Let's go back and report. The fall of River Wave City is probably the best news in a while!" Hua Tianming immediately said. It seemed that there was some conflict between the Palace Master and Luo Zheng, but the situation should not be too bad. Besides, with their speed, it was impossible for them to catch up with Luo Zheng, so they might as well go back and report truthfully.
Although Ning Yudie's speed was also extremely fast, Luo Zheng was not slow at all. The two lights, one in front and one behind, drew an arc on the boundary of the Central Domain.
Seeing that she could not get rid of Luo Zheng, Ning Yudie suddenly stopped in mid-air and pointed her Profound Ice Halberd at Luo Zheng, pointing it directly at Luo Zheng. "Why are you following me?"
Seeing that Ning Yudie had stopped, Luo Zheng immediately approached her slowly and said indifferently, "As a Palace Master, you don't have the slightest awareness and act so recklessly on the battlefield! Have you ever thought about the Cloud Hall? "
Faced with Luo Zheng's questioning, Ning Yudie gritted her teeth. "What does it have to do with you what I do?"
Luo Zheng smiled faintly and continued to approach Ning Yudie.
Ning Yudie's Profound Ice Halberd shook. "Don't come any closer!"
How could Luo Zheng listen to Ning Yudie's warning? He continued to walk slowly towards Ning Yudie, and his eyes gradually became gentle.
Seeing that Luo Zheng and Ning Yudie were getting closer and closer, she suddenly waved the Profound Ice Halberd in her hand and fiercely smashed it on Luo Zheng's body.
"Bang!"
When the Profound Ice Halberd smashed on Luo Zheng's body, Luo Zheng immediately smashed down like a meteor.
Even though Ning Yudie did not use her Quintessential Essence in this attack, the power of a Life and Death Realm warrior's subsequent attack was not the slightest bit inferior.
"Boom!"
Luo Zheng's entire body plunged into the ground.
If other False Tribulation Realm warriors were hit by Ning Yudie's attack, even if they did not die, they would probably be seriously injured.
With the strength of Luo Zheng's body, apart from the strands of Primordial Chaos Dipper, there was naturally no harm done to him.
After Luo Zheng climbed up from the big pit, he immediately flew towards Ning Yudie again with a very persistent expression on his face. This time, just as Luo Zheng approached, Ning Yudie suddenly waved the Profound Ice Halberd in her hand and smashed Luo Zheng down again!
"Don't come any closer!" Ning Yudie sternly shouted at Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng remained unmoved and continued to rush towards Ning Yudie.
In this way, Luo Zheng was repeatedly smashed into the ground by Ning Yudie. Gradually, one big pit after another appeared on the spacious and flat ground around him.
After repeating this more than ten times, more than ten big pits appeared on the ground …
In the end, Ning Yudie could no longer smash Luo Zheng down. The Profound Ice Halberd hung weakly to the side as she stared blankly at Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng continued to fly towards Ning Yudie unhurriedly, slowly passing by her Profound Ice Halberd. This time, Ning Yudie finally did not smash Luo Zheng down.
The distance between the two grew closer and closer. Then, Luo Zheng reached out towards her and hugged her in his arms. He said faintly, "Don't be willful anymore."
Ning Yudie's petite body suddenly tensed up and a loud explosion sounded in her head. Her pair of beautiful eyes faintly glistened with tears. Then, she heard Ning Yudie nod and let out a soft "En".
The two of them did not say anything else and just hugged each other like this.
Three days later, Ning Yudie finally returned to Tianqi City.
The news of Jiangbo City being destroyed by Ning Yudie and Luo Zheng had already spread throughout Tianqi City. Ever since the Tian Xie Sect started fighting, Jiangbo City had been like a wedge nailed to a strategic location. Tianqi City was not willing to send troops to Jiangbo City because of various considerations. The biggest concern was that they were afraid that Cui Xie would directly start a decisive battle with the Alliance.
Now, the Tian Xie Sect was very powerful. Even if the Cloud Hall and the World Business Alliance joined forces, their strength was still slightly inferior. Both sides were currently accumulating their strength.
However, after Jiangbo City was forced to retreat, this concern finally came true.
As expected, Cui Xie lost his patience. He led the Tian Xie Sect's army out in full force and headed straight for Tianqi City.
In the eyes of the World Business Alliance, the main reason why Cui Xie chose to make a move at this time was probably because Jiangbo City had been destroyed.
What they did not know was that Cui Xie chose to make a move in advance because of Luo Zheng. He wanted to make a move before Heartless Sweetheart crossed the Chaotic Astral Ocean and intercept Luo Zheng's secrets for himself.

Chapter 721
The entire Apocalypse City was making preparations for this decisive battle.
Every day, hundreds of runes would be sent out from the Runic Tower and placed in every corner of Apocalypse City.
All the martial artists had already given up on cultivating. With the decisive battle before them, no matter how crazily they cultivated, it would be useless and wouldn't play a decisive role.
Every day, over a hundred scouts from the General Commerce League would return from the frontlines. The Heavenly Evil Sect had chosen to march into Tianqi City with great momentum. They didn't care about their whereabouts being exposed at all. Cui Xie was very confident in this battle. What he wanted to do was to crush them, to crush Tianqi City completely!
As for the people from the Cloud Hall … If Ning Yudie and Luo Zheng were holed up in the Cloud Hall, it would be troublesome for Cui Xie. Since they had chosen to join forces with the World Business Alliance, then the Tian Xie Sect would destroy them all in Apocalypse City!
As long as he defeated the World Business Alliance and the Cloud Hall, no one in the entire Central Domain would be able to stop Cui Xie. Fourth grade sects like the Mysterious Yin Mansion, Black Mountain Sect, and Blood Wood Cliff could only bow their heads and let Cui Xie slaughter them. They had no other choice!
"Reporting! The Tian Xie Sect's army has appeared 1,800 li south of Apocalypse City! "
"Reporting! The Tian Xie Sect's army has appeared 1,000 li south of Apocalypse City! "
"Reporting … 600 li south of Apocalypse City!"
"Reporting …"
The Tian Xie Sect's army didn't advance quickly. However, as they slowly approached Apocalypse City, the pressure on the World Business Alliance and the Cloud Hall was increasing!
In the past few days, Ning Yudie's mood had calmed down a lot.
On the way back to the city with Luo Zheng, the two of them were deeply entangled with each other. Luo Zheng told Ning Yudie that if he could survive this battle and save Luo Yan, he would hold a Dual Cultivation Ceremony with Ning Yudie.
Ning Yudie coquettishly refused. How could she, a Hall Master, marry a disciple of the Cloud Hall? Wouldn't that mess up the seniority?
Luo Zheng was still a disciple of the Cloud Hall, but how could Luo Zheng let her do as she pleased?
After returning to Apocalypse City, the two of them rarely met. However, when the Elders of the Cloud Hall saw Ning Yudie's condition, they gradually relaxed. The final battle was imminent. The Alliance's side was originally inferior to the Tian Xie Sect. As the Hall Master, Ning Yudie couldn't be in a situation like this.
One day later, Luo Zheng left the courtyard. The Tian Xie Sect was approaching Apocalypse City. If there were no accidents, today would be the day of the battle.
Xi Youqin was wearing casual clothes. She calmly looked at Luo Zheng, but there was a faint trace of worry on her face. Like an ordinary woman, she helped Luo Zheng tidy up his clothes and then warned him, "Be careful …"
"En!" Luo Zheng nodded and immediately rushed towards the Conference Hall in Apocalypse City.
After Luo Zheng left, Xi Youqin blinked her eyes and faintly looked in the direction Luo Zheng left. "Don't worry, I won't let you die …"
Soon, the tenderness in her eyes disappeared and was replaced by a serious expression. Soon, she took out a black Taoist robe from her Sumeru Ring.
This Taoist robe was the disciple robe of the Void Spirit Sect. She wasn't a disciple of the Void Spirit Sect. When she was fourteen years old, she argued with her father and insisted on this robe. After a few years, her figure had become much slimmer and straighter. Now, it didn't fit her well, especially when her chest was tight, which made her feel a little uncomfortable.
But today, she also had to fight!
Perhaps it was for Luo Zheng, for her Xi Family, and for the destroyed Void Spirit Sect.
After a while, Xi Youqin, who had changed into the Void Spirit Sect's disciple robe, quietly rushed towards the side door of the Void Spirit Sect.
The army of the Tian Xie Sect had appeared directly opposite Void Sky City. The black mass of independent martial artists formed one square formation after another. In addition to the numerous independent martial artists, there were many Nihility Tribulation Realm martial artists in the sky, as well as two Life and Death Realm martial artists, Wu Zhanhe and Cui Xie!
The army quietly stood ten miles in front of the Apocalypse City Gate.
Aside from the martial artists, there were also some strange things in the army. For example, there was a huge lampstand held up by more than a hundred Divine Core warriors. In the center of the lampstand was a huge wick. The wick was dark, and it was extinguished.
On the other side of the lampstand were two huge coffins. One was black, and the other was white. No one knew what was inside the coffins.
As for the army of the Tian Xie City, Shi Kefan, Yan Yueshan, and Mo Haishan were standing at the top of the city wall. The three fat men had solemn expressions on their faces. As for the tycoon of the World Business Alliance, he was sitting cross-legged on a rattan chair, meditating with his eyes closed.
Ning Yudie led all the Nihility Tribulation Realm martial artists in the Cloud Hall and stood on the other side, looking at the Tian Xie Sect with an indifferent expression.
Not long after that, a beam of light suddenly flew over from the Tian Xie City. It was Luo Zheng.
As soon as Luo Zheng arrived, he exchanged a glance with Ning Yudie. Both of their eyes were filled with tenderness. Luo Zheng shifted his gaze and stared straight at Cui Xie in the distance. Even if the two of them were separated by ten miles at this moment, they still quickly noticed each other.
Cui Xie's figure suddenly moved towards the Tian Xie City. Facing all the martial artists in the city, he let out a wild laugh and said, "The Heavenly Kingdom of Evil Deities will unify the Central Domain! As the saying goes, the Heavenly Dao is vast, and its power is limitless! Those who follow it will prosper, and those who go against it will perish! The Heavenly Kingdom of Evil Deities represents the Heavenly Dao. If any martial artist is willing to submit to the Heavenly Kingdom of Evil Deities, I, Cui Xie, will not refuse them. Those who resist are destined to bring about their own destruction! "
Cui Xie poured his Quintessential Essence into his voice and spread it towards the Tian Xie City. His voice was as loud as thunder, and the entire Tian Xie City, from the martial artists to the commoners, felt their ears hurt from the vibrations!
Many of the martial artists in the Tian Xie City fell silent. At this point in time, naturally, no one would really submit to Cui Xie. However, Cui Xie did not expect his words to be useful. It was just that before the start of the war, his words could greatly suppress the enemy's morale and increase his own.
"Ha!"
"Ha!"
"Ha!"
As soon as Cui Xie's voice fell, the independent martial artists behind him suddenly roared.
Facing Cui Xie's provocation, Shi Kefan was about to speak when Luo Zheng spoke first. He also poured his Quintessential Essence into Cui Xie and coldly said two words to him. "Bullshit!"
His two words were like a clap of thunder, no less loud than Cui Xie's. Although these two words were vulgar, they were simple and powerful. They completely despised the Heavenly Kingdom of Evil Deities!
Hearing Luo Zheng's words, Cui Xie's face flashed with anger. However, that anger instantly dissipated and was replaced by a trace of greed. In Cui Xie's eyes, Luo Zheng was like a moving treasure trove!
In just a few years, this boy had risen to his current level. If Cui Xie were to obtain all of Luo Zheng's secrets, who in this world would be able to stop him?
"Luo Zheng! Do you dare to fight me head-on? Last time, I chased you all the way across half of the Central Domain. Do you still want to be a coward this time? "Cui Xie sneered at Luo Zheng, his body shrouded in a black aura.
Before the Sect War started, it was very common for experts from both sides to challenge each other to a duel.
This kind of thing often happened even among mortals. When the generals of both sides challenged each other to a duel, the winning side's morale would naturally soar, while the losing side's morale would plummet!
From Cui Xie's tone, it was obvious that he wanted to challenge Luo Zheng …
After all, Cui Xie was a Life and Death Realm martial artist. Moreover, he was the strongest person in the Central Domain with the highest cultivation base. Even if he were to challenge Luo Zheng, he would usually choose Ning Yudie or that ancient tycoon of the World Business Alliance.
At this time, challenging Luo Zheng to a duel seemed somewhat shameless.
The ancient tycoon opened his eyes and gently floated up from the rattan chair. He said to Cui Xie with an extremely old and hoarse voice, "The dignified number one person in the Central Domain is challenging a junior to a duel. You really don't have a bottom line. How about this old man play with you?"
The ancient tycoon's voice was hoarse, but just like Luo Zheng and Cui Xie, everyone inside and outside the Heaven Awakening City heard him clearly.
Ning Yudie also floated up and faintly smiled at Cui Xie, "Cui Xie, I, the Hall Master, am here, yet you're challenging a disciple of my Cloud Hall. Aren't you afraid of lowering your status?"
Cui Xie coldly snorted. His gaze was like a knife as he stared at Luo Zheng and asked, "Luo Zheng, do you dare?"
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 722
Luo Zheng clenched his fists tightly. Although he maintained a calm expression, he was extremely excited in his heart.
When he was still in the Blue Cloud Sect, Luo Zheng had listed Cui Xie as one of his must-kill targets. Even if he managed to save Luo Yan, he would still kill Cui Xie. This man had become an obstacle in Luo Zheng's path of cultivation, and the only way to destroy this obstacle was to kill Cui Xie!
After waiting for so long, Luo Zheng had finally arrived.
Luo Zheng moved his feet and stepped on the edge of the city wall. His body moved without any wind, and he slowly drifted away from the city wall. The more excited he was, the calmer his expression became.
Seeing Luo Zheng leaving the city wall, Ning Yudie shouted, "Luo Zheng? You want to fight? "
Luo Zheng turned around and smiled at Ning Yudie, "Don't worry!"
"But …" Ning Yudie furrowed her brows, and was about to say something.
Luo Zheng immediately said, "Don't worry, I won't die, I still want to marry you!"
His voice wasn't loud, but everyone in the Cloud Hall, including Daoist Dameng and the Void Tribulation Warriors and Divine Core warriors, could clearly hear what he said. Daoist Dameng and a few other Sect Elders who knew what was going on had a knowing smile on their faces. As for those Divine Core warriors who didn't know what was going on, such as Hua Tianming, Red Maple, and a few others, they had strange expressions on their faces.
Especially Red Maple, his eyes were wide open as he poked Hua Tianming who was standing next to him. "Tianming, am I hearing this right?"
Hua Tianming had his eyes closed. His eyes were slightly bloodshot. For the past two days, he had been using the Sword Eye to accumulate the Force of the Heavens. Only by doing so could he unleash all of Hua Tianming's strength.
"Is it strange?" Hua Tianming hugged his sword and replied with his eyes closed. A faint smile emerged on his face.
"Of course it's strange!" Looking at Hua Tianming's confident expression, Red Maple said disapprovingly.
Hua Tianming shook his head, "It's not strange for something like this to happen to Luo Zheng …"
As for Ning Yudie, when she heard Luo Zheng mention this in front of everyone, her face immediately blushed.
After Luo Zheng finished speaking, he turned his head and stared at Cui Xie. Using his Quintessential Essence, he said slowly and clearly, "Two years ago, when I was still in the Divine Reflection Stage, I warned you that I would spare your life if you let Luo Yan go. Unfortunately, you didn't take advantage of this opportunity. Now … your chance is gone!"
After he finished speaking, Luo Zheng slowly pulled out a long sword. The long sword blossomed with a faint light. He gently waved the long sword, and a sharp sword hum was transmitted. The sword hum was very soft, but it was very pleasing to the ear.
"It's a high-grade sacred artifact!" Shi Kefan's eyes flashed as he suddenly shouted.
"Where did Luo Zheng get a high-grade sacred artifact?"
"A high-grade sacred artifact doesn't exist in the Central Domain, and no refiner can forge one. Where did this boy get it?"
In fact, most people couldn't recognize the grade of Luo Zheng's long sword. Only Shi Kefan and the others could. Of course, the two sides were about to fight, and Luo Zheng seemed to intend to accept Cui Xie's challenge. The battle between the two of them would directly determine the morale of the martial artists on both sides.
Since Luo Zheng had obtained a high-grade sacred artifact, Shi Kefan naturally wouldn't let go of this opportunity. He had to let everyone know!
"Hahahaha!" At this time, Cui Xie suddenly burst into wild laughter, "Luo Zheng, two years ago, I had a taste of your mouth! I didn't expect that after two years, you still haven't made any progress. Your mouth is still so fierce! Unfortunately, it's a pity that you forgot that when martial artists fight, it's not just their mouths that matter, but their fists and their own strength! "
Luo Zheng faintly looked at Cui Xie and took a deep breath. Faint starlight began to appear on the surface of his body. Two different colors of starlight shone upon his body, shrouding him in a special luster.
"Hey, everyone look! Two stars have appeared on the horizon!"
"It's daytime, how can there be stars?"
"Those two stars seem to be shining on Luo Zheng. How did he do it? He's directly borrowing the power of the stars! "
Even during the day, the stars could still be seen. It was just that the light of the sun completely covered the starlight. However, after Luo Zheng activated the Stellar Battle Body, the purple star and the orange star suddenly burst out with light, shooting towards Luo Zheng, who was hundreds of millions of miles away. The sun couldn't cover the light of the stars, so everyone could see the two stars on the horizon during the day.
After the Stellar Battle Body was condensed, Luo Zheng said coldly, "You're right. I'll show you what a fist is and what strength is!"
After noticing the starlight on Luo Zheng's body, Cui Xie's face revealed a trace of surprise, but it was quickly replaced by a serious expression. Indeed, the cultivation method that Luo Zheng practiced made Cui Xie feel unimaginable. Since ancient times, the stars had been an extremely mysterious existence for human beings.
Even if you opened your mind and visualized, it was difficult to obtain the power of the stars. On the other side of the distant starry sky, the stars were completely incomprehensible to human beings. If you couldn't even understand them, how could you visualize them?
However, Luo Zheng seemed to be directly absorbing the power of the stars! What kind of cultivation method was this?
With a serious expression, Cui Xie also took out the nameless Buddhist Commandment Blade in his hand. In the past, this nameless Buddhist Commandment Blade had been the best weapon in the Central Domain. Among the few remaining low-grade sacred weapons in the Central Domain, this nameless Buddhist Commandment Blade was the most suitable for fighting enemies.
However, compared to Luo Zheng, Cui Xie now looked a little shabby.
In terms of value, a high-grade sacred weapon could be exchanged for many low-grade sacred weapons …
Luo Zheng waved his longsword and didn't say anything else. With a sway of his body, he unleashed his Vital Energy and turned into a beam of light, shooting toward Cui Xie who was a few miles away.
All the Divine Illumination warriors could only see a blur in front of their eyes. In an instant, Luo Zheng and Cui Xie had turned into two beams of light. The two beams of light kept crossing each other, colliding into each other, separating from each other, then colliding into each other again …
As for the Divine Core warriors, their eyesight was slightly better, but they still couldn't see their movements clearly. They could only vaguely distinguish them.
As for the Void Tribulation warriors from both sides, all of them had nervous expressions on their faces. This fight was too important for both sides.
Ao Xiang stared into the distance. His face was filled with excitement. Compared to the others, he had a much more complicated feeling right now. Luo Zheng's victory not only meant that the morale of Heavenly Awakening City had increased, his lover and Luo Zheng's younger sister were going to share the same fate!
Both men were moving at high speed, and Ao Xiang couldn't see them clearly. But, he still clenched his teeth and stared at both men, trying to identify Luo Zheng!
Luo Zheng's eyes were filled with killing intent!
He had been waiting for this fight for so many years, and he had to win no matter what. Therefore, right from the beginning, Luo Zheng didn't hold back at all. While charging toward Cui Xie at high speed, his longsword had already pierced toward Cui Xie.
"Formless!"
Luo Zheng's sword seemed crooked, but his target was Cui Xie's chest. He was trying to pierce through Cui Xie.
Cui Xie held the nameless Buddhist Commandment Saber horizontally. Luo Zheng's sword was a high-grade sacred weapon after all. Relying on the strength of the weapon, Cui Xie was indeed at a disadvantage. At least, he didn't dare to use the nameless Buddhist Commandment Saber to meet Luo Zheng's sword head-on!
Clang!
Cui Xie's Buddhist Commandment Saber gently brushed Luo Zheng's longsword, using a skillful force to push Luo Zheng's longsword away.
However, just as Cui Xie pushed Luo Zheng's sword away, Luo Zheng said faintly, "It's useless!"
The tip of the sword casually drew a circle in the air and still pierced toward Cui Xie's chest like a shadow. Formless wasn't really without moves. This was a move created by Luo Zheng himself. There was only one move, but it could evolve into countless moves!
However, Luo Zheng was more willing to call this move an unbreakable move!
Seeing that he couldn't break Luo Zheng's sword, Cui Xie's expression suddenly changed, and he was about to retreat.
Luo Zheng had a strange smile on his face. He gently thrust the sword in his hand forward, and the originally light sword suddenly underwent an earth-shattering change!
The sword suddenly pierced out, followed by a swift and fierce sword light. The tip of the sword extended and continued to shoot toward Cui Xie's chest. Luo Zheng's Formless was a combination of all the basic swordsmanship, and it had reached the point where it could be used at will.

Chapter 723
A joyful expression emerged on the faces of all the warriors in Heavenly Awakening City.
Before Luo Zheng entered the battlefield, everyone had their own concerns, and the biggest concern was the gap between their cultivation bases.
Cui Xie was now a fourth life and death tribulation warrior. Every time he went through a life and death tribulation, it was the same as escaping from death, and his overall strength would become stronger!
As for Luo Zheng, although he was a Mid Void Tribulation warrior, there was still a huge gap between them.
Indeed, when Luo Zheng was still a Divine Reflection warrior, he was able to challenge Divine Core warriors, and even Mid Divine Core geniuses.
However, the gap between the Divine Reflection realm and Divine Core realm was far smaller than the gap between the Void Tribulation realm and Life and Death realm!
This was also the reason why when Cui Xie challenged Luo Zheng, Ning Yudie and that ancient tycoon stepped forward to stop him.
Only Life and Death warriors were qualified to fight with Life and Death warriors.
In the Central Region, only martial artists who had stepped into the Life and Death Realm could be considered peak martial artists! Below the Life and Death realm, there were countless Void Tribulation warriors, and none of them could fight with a Life and Death warrior.
This was the reason why everyone was worried about Luo Zheng. They were worried that he would be killed by Cui Xie right away, and that would be the worst case scenario.
Of course, that ancient tycoon and Ning Yudie wouldn't let this happen either. Once Luo Zheng was in a disadvantageous position, they would definitely take action. Although it was a one on one duel, since both sides had declared war, they were now mortal enemies, and there was no reason for them to be reasonable.
In fact, this was how most wars between sects usually unfolded. Both sects would send out a warrior to fight, and as the fight went on, everyone would join the fight … this tradition had been maintained for tens of thousands of years, and it was still the same today.
If Cui Xie was in a disadvantageous position, Wu Zhanhe would still lead his men and attack Luo Zheng.
Now that Luo Zheng's first attack had hit Cui Xie, the World Business Alliance's people were naturally happy. This sword attack was effective, which meant that Luo Zheng still maintained his strength, the strength to challenge those of a higher realm!
"This Luo Zheng is too abnormal. Two years later, not only did he break through to the middle stage of False Tribulation Realm, but he was also able to fight against a Life and Death Realm expert!"
"Don't forget, Cui Xie is not an ordinary Life and Death expert. This means that a martial artist who has just broken through Life and Death realm might not be his opponent!"
"You're right. I think that the Hall Master of the Cloud Hall is not as good as Luo Zheng!"
"If this kid breaks through the Life and Death Realm, he will be able to fight against Spirit Sea Realm warriors!"
Hearing these voices, Ning Yudie's gaze was very complicated. Although she was not convinced, she had to admit that she was not Luo Zheng's opponent.
As for the many martial artists from the Country of Evil Deities, they were still standing in place. Wu Zhanhe turned a blind eye to Cui Xie's wound. Such a small injury was nothing to Cui Xie. 'But Old Cui seems to be a bit careless. This is not a good sign,' Wu Zhanhe thought indifferently.
Wu Zhanhe did not want Cui Xie to obtain the Extreme Violet Yin Body, which was why he helped Luo Zheng escape. However, he also did not want Cui Xie to be defeated. If Cui Xie was defeated, Wu Zhanhe's own plans and ambitions would also be in danger.
Cui Xie looked at the wound under his ribs, then exerted some strength. Under the pressure of the muscles on his back, the wound instantly healed. "Not bad, you can actually injure me. This is worthy of praise, but it's far from enough!"
Luo Zheng smiled faintly. "Just injure you? Aren't you afraid of being poisoned? "
Hearing Luo Zheng's words, Cui Xie's face suddenly changed. Except for Luo Zheng's abnormal physique, which martial artist was not afraid of poison? If it was an ordinary poison, it probably wouldn't affect Cui Xie at all. However, it was hard to say for Luo Zheng. He could even get his hands on a high-grade saint artifact. Who knew if he would smear some unimaginable poison on his sword?
Seeing Cui Xie's nervous expression, Luo Zheng sneered, "I lied to you! The dignified number one martial artist in the Central Domain turns out to be a cowardly rat! "After saying that, Luo Zheng suddenly flashed, waved his long sword again, and slashed at Cui Xie.
Having suffered a loss in front of Luo Zheng's strange swordsmanship once, how could Cui Xie be willing to suffer a loss a second time?
He also flashed, and the momentum in his body suddenly skyrocketed. He held the nameless Buddhist Commandment Blade in one hand and clenched his other hand into a fist. Under the flickering dark golden fist radiance, he grinned hideously, "It's over, boy!"
After he finished speaking, Cui Xie's fist suddenly slammed down heavily!
At the same time, a huge golden fist also appeared in the sky very suddenly. This golden fist was as tall as a city wall, and the golden light it emitted was like a huge sun. Most people could not even open their eyes at this moment.
"Human Emperor's Ultimate Fist!"
Cui Xie's fist smashed down fiercely at Luo Zheng, as if an entire city wall was smashing down.
However, with Luo Zheng's current strength, even if he was crushed under a city wall, he would not be injured at all. However, the power of this "Human Emperor's Ultimate Fist" was many times greater than that of a section of the city wall!
"Boom!"
Fortunately, the area at the gate of Tianqi City was spacious. When the fist smashed down, a huge pit several miles wide was formed in the ground in front of the city gate. This pit was also more than 30 meters deep!
As for Luo Zheng's small figure, he was lying in the middle of the deep pit, deeply embedded in the soil. From afar, he was just an inconspicuous little black dot.
"Mighty, our Emperor!"
"Mighty, our Monarch!"
"Mighty, our Emperor!"
Many warriors of the Tian Xie Sect began to clamor.
In fact, only those who had reached the Divine Sea Realm were qualified to call themselves Emperors. However, Cui Xie had established his Divine Kingdom in advance, so he could be regarded as an Emperor. After all, his strength was not qualified yet.
Ning Yudie stood on the city wall, but there was a faint trace of worry on her face. Facing Luo Zheng's terrifying body, Cui Xie should be able to withstand this blow. However, she was not sure whether he would be injured or not.
At the west corner of Tianqi City, Xi Youqin looked at the huge pit in the distance. Her expression could no longer remain calm. Cui Xie was so powerful. Could Luo Zheng … withstand it?
"Cui Xie's punch is probably able to smash a mountain into a flat cake …"
"I don't know if Luo Zheng can withstand it. I think it's a bit difficult!"
"Under this punch, everything will be smashed into powder. It's said that Luo Zheng's body is special and almost invulnerable. However, I don't believe that he can withstand it!"
"With the cultivation of the middle stage of the False Tribulation Realm, he can injure Cui Xie. I'm afraid this is already his limit. It's normal that he can't defeat Cui Xie!"
The warriors on both sides were also discussing in low voices.
However, Luo Zheng quickly pulled himself out of the huge pit. He shrugged his shoulders and raised his head. A trace of blood flowed out of the corner of his mouth. He sneered at Cui Xie and said, "You're just so-so!"
Seeing Luo Zheng get up, the warriors who were discussing immediately shut up. They suddenly realized that their judgment was pale and powerless in front of Luo Zheng …
Cui Xie coldly snorted and said, "Stubborn! Then let's do it again! "
After saying this, Cui Xie once again waved his fist and smashed it towards the ground. At the same time, a huge fist appeared in the air and smashed towards Luo Zheng's head.
This time, Luo Zheng did not allow this fist to smash down. He waved his hand, and the Vital Spirit in his body suddenly burst out. The starlight on the surface of his body suddenly circulated rapidly and slowly flowed towards Luo Zheng's hand.
"I've already said that you're just so-so. You don't know your own limits. Hahaha!" Luo Zheng sneered and punched towards the sky.
"Stars Sink and Earth Quake!"
"Boom!"
Under the surging starlight, the huge fist began to shatter layer by layer. Luo Zheng's figure turned into a stream of light and shot towards Cui Xie along the shattered fist radiance.
While Cui Xie was still in shock, Luo Zheng was already in front of him. Luo Zheng said coldly, "Now, it's your turn to go down!"
After saying this, Luo Zheng's fist also smashed down towards Luo Zheng's head!

Chapter 724
Facing Cui Xie, Luo Zheng didn't show any mercy, nor did he have much killing intent.
In order to fight against Cui Xie, Luo Zheng had adjusted his mentality to the best state. He was neither excited nor sad.
His fist suddenly smashed out, and the power of Vital Spirit and dragon scales instantly broke out.
Compared with Cui Xie's "Emperor's Ultimate Fist", Luo Zheng's fist didn't seem to have much power, but it was much more deadly than Cui Xie's!
Just as Luo Zheng's fist hit Cui Xie, black smoke suddenly emerged from Cui Xie's body and wrapped him up.
"Bang!"
The moment Luo Zheng's fist landed on Cui Xie's body, he felt as though he was not punching a human body. Instead, he felt as though he was punching a thick layer of cotton wool. This unstoppable punch was like a stone ox that fell into the sea, disappearing without a trace!
"What's going on?"
Luo Zheng frowned slightly.
The black smoke that emerged from Cui Xie's body was very strange. Last time in Virtual Sky City, the Seven Voidspirits used Dragon-trapping Rope to bind Cui Xie, and then used their strongest attack, but it didn't cause any harm to Cui Xie!
Now Luo Zheng understood why the Seven Voidspirits were so shocked at that time.
However, Luo Zheng still had no expression on his face. Facing Cui Xie, strange things could happen. What he needed to do was to stabilize his mind and exert his strongest combat strength!
After being shrouded by the black smoke, Cui Xie seemed to be unharmed, but Luo Zheng didn't stop there. The second round of attacks followed!
"Get down!"
"Bang!"
Luo Zheng grabbed Cui Xie in the black smoke, and once again broke out the Vital Spirit in his body, pressing Cui Xie down!
"Boom!"
In this way, Luo Zheng pressed Cui Xie from a high altitude all the way to the big pit on the ground. Even so, Luo Zheng didn't stop, and kept pressing Cui Xie all the way to the surface of the big pit.
Hundreds of thousands of warriors from both sides saw Luo Zheng rubbing Cui Xie on the ground. Smoke and dust billowed all the way, and the two figures were completely buried in it.
One lap, two laps, three laps …
Luo Zheng still pressed Cui Xie on the ground and kept running, plowing gullies after gullies in the big pit!
In the end, Luo Zheng stopped, and a faint killing intent flashed in his eyes. He once again waved his fist at Cui Xie, and then everyone heard a dense burst of explosions!
"Boom, boom, boom, boom, boom …"
It was as if many children had lit firecrackers at the same time to welcome the New Year. In the corner of the big pit, smoke and dust sputtered up like mushroom clouds, blooming one after another. How could anyone see the situation of the two people clearly?
On the city wall, when the people from the World Business Alliance and Cloud Hall saw this scene, they all felt their blood boil!
Why was the Tian Xie Sect so powerful? It wasn't because of the hundreds of thousands of independent warriors, nor was it because of Wu Zhanhe. It was because of Cui Xie, who was truly the number one person in the Central Domain.
In recent decades, no one had been able to shake Cui Xie's position, and no one had been able to test out Cui Xie's true strength.
Therefore, Cui Xie was like a nightmare, floating above the many Sects in the Central Domain. His deterrence alone was stronger than that of the Cloud Hall and the Void Spirit Sect. It was almost on par with the World Business Alliance!
This was also why the Minor Heavenly Cycle Three Kills Array that Luo Zheng had set up in the Cloud Hall had caused a commotion in the entire Central Domain!
The Cloud Hall had such a terrifying array that even Cui Xie could block it. Who else in the Central Domain could shake the Cloud Hall? The Sect Protection Array had already placed the Cloud Hall in an invincible position.
However, the Sect Protection Array was ultimately only a "shield" of a Sect. All along, everyone had thought that the Cloud Hall's overall strength was still inferior to the Void Spirit Sect and the World Business Alliance. Therefore, the Void Spirit Sect was still the number one Sect in the world at that time.
Now, everyone discovered that Luo Zheng had once again subverted their understanding. A disciple of the Cloud Hall had actually beaten Cui Xie to the ground. Wasn't this too exaggerated?
"Cui Xie doesn't seem to be able to fight back at all! This Luo Zheng's true strength is probably already above the Cloud Hall's Hall Master and the ancient giants! "
"Two years ago, he was still a Divine Reflection Realm warrior. The speed of his rise … is hard to accept!"
"This is too demoralizing! However, watching him beat up Cui Xie like this, I feel very good! "
This scene greatly boosted the morale of the World Business Alliance and the Cloud Hall's warriors! On the contrary, the morale of the more than 100,000 warriors of the Tian Xie Sect began to plummet.
"Can the King really do it? He's the number one person in the Central Domain, but he's being beaten up by a junior with a bone age of only 20 years. If I didn't see it with my own eyes, I wouldn't believe it even if you beat me to death!"
"Bone age of only 20 years old? You've underestimated that Luo Zheng! When he was just a Divine Reflection Realm warrior, he was able to defeat all ordinary Divine Core geniuses! I'm afraid a man like this has gone beyond the concept of geniuses! There's no place for a man like this in the Central Domain! "
"If the King is really defeated, or even killed by that young man, what should we do?"
"Hmph, not necessarily! If the King only has this little strength, our Kingdom of Evil Deities will become a joke! Don't worry, the King won't lose!"
Many warriors of the Tian Xie Sect had worried expressions on their faces. As their leader, Cui Xie was being beaten up like this, it was hard for them to accept. After all, most of them had joined the Tian Xie Sect because of Cui Xie's name. They had never expected that they would be beaten up by Luo Zheng in just a single round …
Although Luo Zheng's attacks were concentrated, and his strength was pouring onto Cui Xie's body, it was as if every single punch had landed on a piece of broken cotton. Cui Xie's body was shrouded by the black smoke, and it was as if he had entered a strange state. All the attacks that landed on his body were completely neutralized!
In the end, Luo Zheng decided to stop. He was panting heavily as he stared coldly at the black smoke in front of him.
In this round, Luo Zheng had used almost all of his strength. If he had concentrated all of his strength, he might have been able to destroy ten giant mountains.
However, the Cui Xie in front of him was still shrouded by the black smoke. He lay motionless on the ground, and his face and expression couldn't be seen clearly.
"Hahahaha …"
After a while, Cui Xie's muffled laughter came out from the black smoke. His laughter went from low to high, from gentle to hurried, and became louder and louder!
Hearing Cui Xie's laughter, Ning Yudie's expression turned solemn. She shook her head and said, "Cui Xie isn't injured."
"Under such an attack, he's actually unscathed. This Cui Xie … is really unfathomable!" Daoist Master Dameng frowned as he looked at the movement below the city wall.
As for the ancient giants and the three leaders of the World Business Alliance, although they knew that Luo Zheng's attack wouldn't be able to kill Cui Xie, at this moment, their expressions were still somewhat unnatural. Cui Xie's strength was still beyond their imagination.
On the other side, Wu Zhanhe's face was expressionless. Wu Zhanhe's mood was rather conflicted. Cui Xie's strength was too strong for him, and too weak was also not a good thing for him.
As for the many independent warriors of the Tian Xie Sect, their originally unsightly expressions slowly recovered. The person they worshipped as their king couldn't be so weak!
The black smoke surrounding Cui Xie's body gradually dissipated, and Cui Xie's tall and sturdy figure slowly appeared. Now, Cui Xie's entire body was covered in a layer of black scales, and a slender horn grew out of his head!
"It's really admirable that you can threaten my existence in just two years. It turns out that there is such a genius in the Central Domain!" Cui Xie said, and his body slowly floated up again. His eyes revealed a trace of danger. "However, it's still not enough!"
Luo Zheng's eyes were as calm as water. Instead, he asked, "Not enough? How much do you need? "
"Far from enough!" Cui Xie roared, and the bones in his body made cracking sounds. It was as if his bones had shifted positions in an instant. His entire body underwent an astonishing change. For example, the lower half of his legs protruded forward, like the legs of some kind of animal. The muscles on his body began to expand, and his entire body swelled to twice its original size!
At this moment, Cui Xie didn't look like a human at all. His entire body was as big as the Demon Race on the Sea God Continent.
Watching the changes in Cui Xie's body, Luo Zheng's eyes revealed a trace of surprise. "This Cui Xie seems to have a special bloodline. What race is it? The Demon Race? Or even the Dragon Race? Or other races … "
At this moment, a faint voice sounded in Luo Zheng's mind. It was Qinglong. "You guessed right, but Cui Xie didn't awaken the bloodline of the Hundred Races of the Upper Realm. This is the bloodline of the Underworld Devil."
"Devil!" Luo Zheng's heart skipped a beat, and his calm eyes stared at Cui Xie not far away.

Chapter 725
Hell, the Yellow Springs, demons, ghosts …
There were many records of these things in the Central Domain, but they were all legendary existences, and no one had ever seen them with their own eyes.
It was precisely because of these legends that they were described in all kinds of strange stories, gossip, and anecdotes that people added all kinds of unimaginable conjectures and conjectures.
Whether or not these things really existed was another matter, but now, according to Qinglong's tone, Cui Xie indeed had the bloodline of a demon. In other words, the current Cui Xie was a living demon!
Luo Zheng naturally didn't know how Cui Xie had obtained the bloodline of a demon. However, the low-grade sacred tool in his hand had the ability to summon a Yaksha from the Yellow Springs. Perhaps Cui Xie had obtained it through the nameless Buddhist Commandment Blade, or through other means. Luo Zheng could only guess.
No matter what, the current Cui Xie that Luo Zheng was facing could only be more terrifying than before!
If it was the Luo Zheng of the past, his state of mind would have been greatly affected. But now, there wasn't much of a change in his expression. Not to mention that Cui Xie was a demon, even if he were a god or a Buddha, Luo Zheng would still risk his life to kill him!

When the demon bloodline in his body was stimulated, a steady stream of Demon Force crazily flowed through his body!

That thing was extremely special. It only stayed in this world for a breath's time, but the hearts of all the warriors, mortals, and peerless experts in Tianqi City beat wildly for a while. Everyone was stunned in an instant!
Even the warriors who were fiercely fighting each other stopped. It was as if they would be the next to die if they did not stop. This was an instinctive fear that came from life!
Luo Zheng and Ning Yudie were no exception. Their hearts seemed to be grasped by a strange little monster. What was hidden in the nameless Buddhist Commandment Blade? How terrifying was Cui Xie's attack?

Chapter 726
However, just as Cui Xie was about to swing his saber, a great power came from above the city wall, and an old voice was heard.

. A faint sneer appeared on Cui Xie's face. "Don't worry. I'll send you to hell one by one …"

What kind of person was the ancient magnate?

Qinglong did not directly answer Luo Zheng's question. However, it could be considered as an answer. Qinglong did not have a way to break it.
Luo Zheng and Ning Yudie were shuttling back and forth in Tianqi City. Cui Xie was too lazy to pay attention to the battle on the city wall. It was as if the outcome of the battle had nothing to do with him!

Indeed, Cui Xie had always been on top of him. When he was in the Blue Cloud Sect, he had a huge impact on Luo Zheng's heart of martial arts. Then he went to the Cloud Hall, and then to Tianqi City. In the end, after Luo Zheng returned from the Sea God Continent, Cui Xie continued to pursue him.
Today, Luo Zheng finally made up his mind to fight Cui Xie head-on and kill him. However, Cui Xie's methods once again suppressed him, forcing him to retreat!
Even though Luo Zheng had mentally prepared himself and stabilized his heart of martial arts to the extreme, he still could not help but feel agitated. Could it be that Cui Xie was his nemesis?
In fact, from Cui Xie's point of view, Luo Zheng was also his nemesis. Ever since he, Cui Xie, became the strongest independent warrior in the Central Domain, Cui Xie had never felt so troubled by anyone. Luo Zheng had made him suffer again and again, even making him a laughingstock in the eyes of the Central Domain's warriors. It was all thanks to this brat in front of him. He had never been so eager to kill someone because he could always kill the person he wanted to kill. Only Luo Zheng was an exception.
Therefore, this time, he was determined not to let Luo Zheng go!
Ning Yudie could also feel Luo Zheng's agitation. This little guy, who was always calm at any time, was finally agitated …
The most important thing for a warrior was to maintain their state of mind. However, Ning Yudie understood Luo Zheng very well. If he could control his state of mind to be as calm as water at this time, then he was not a human, but a statue, a dead object.
After all, humans were animals with emotions. With emotions, there would be fluctuations in their state of mind. They just needed to learn to control their own fluctuations.
Thinking of this, Ning Yudie's body slightly tilted, and one of her lily-white hands drilled into Luo Zheng's robes, covering Luo Zheng's chest.
At the same time, her true essence circulated, and an ice-cold aura spread out.

Luo Zheng felt the cooling sensation, and his thumping heart gradually slowed down. His originally tense face gradually relaxed, and that agitated look disappeared without a trace.
"Yes, calm down!"
A good state of mind had always been Luo Zheng's pride. But today, he had almost lost it.
Luo Zheng had encountered countless dangers, but he had always been able to maintain a steady state of mind and calmly escape danger. He never thought that today, when he faced Cui Xie, his state of mind would almost fall apart!
At this time, Luo Zheng softly called out, "Xun!"
A little red light instantly condensed around Luo Zheng. Then, Luo Zheng stretched out his hand, and a light erupted from his spatial ring. The medium-grade saint artifact spear spun twice before falling into her hand.
"Do you want me to kill him?" Xun smiled.
"You are not his match." Luo Zheng shook his head. "Stall him!"
Xun nodded, "No problem."

Chapter 727
Because Smog was Luo Zheng's Sword Spirit, Smog's strength increased along with Luo Zheng's strength. If Smog were to fight alone, even a warrior who had just stepped into the Life and Death Realm wouldn't be her opponent. However, it would be too difficult for her to fight against Cui Xie alone.
Of course, Luo Zheng didn't expect Cui Xie to defeat Smog. Even if Smog could really defeat Cui Xie, Luo Zheng wouldn't let Smog fight!
Luo Zheng pursued the Ultimate Martial Dao all his life. He already had a perfect start, stepping into the middle stage of the False Tribulation Realm when his bone age was only 20 years old. However, Cui Xie had now become his archenemy.
If he wanted to go further on the Martial Dao, he had to personally eliminate Cui Xie. Otherwise, Cui Xie would very likely become his inner demon. In the future, he would never be able to go far on the Martial Dao, and it would be even more impossible for him to climb to the peak of the Martial Dao!
When he returned from the Sea God Continent, the simplest way was to ask Situ Haotian or even Liu Yu, who was a powerful expert from the deep sea, to personally make a trip!
Since Luo Zheng had spoken, neither Situ Haotian nor Liu Yu could refuse!

However, in the end, Luo Zheng chose to fight against Cui Xie himself. His purpose was to personally eliminate this obstacle.
Now, it was the most critical moment.
The moment Smog entangled Cui Xie, Luo Zheng circled around Tianqi City with Ning Yudie, then rushed towards the city wall again.
At this time, the battle between the two sides was in full swing!
Although the Tian Xie Sect's army was powerful, the World Business Alliance and the Cloud Hall's allied forces were not weak either. Especially the World Business Alliance, which was known as a sixth-grade sect. Its foundation was not something that the Cloud Hall could match!
Hundreds of gigantic puppets appeared on the battlefield. These puppets were made of steel and were as large as mountains. Under the control of the Puppetry Technique, they charged toward the Lascivious Sect's phalanxes. Each puppet could take away thousands of lives!
On the Tian Xie Sect's side, two huge coffins, one black and one white, had already been opened. Two huge zombies came and went like the wind from the coffins. In addition to these two zombies, the huge lampstand had also been ignited. Under the raging flames, a phoenix formed from raging flames was born!
However, this phoenix was not a true Super Divine Beast. It was similar to Ning Yudie's Ice Phoenix, which was also formed from visualization. However, this Fire Phoenix's might was not ordinary. As soon as it was born, it let out a sharp phoenix cry and flew towards Tianqi City. The Fire Phoenix's strength was far beyond that of ordinary Void Tribulation Realm experts. Ordinary warriors could only dodge.
As for the three fatties of the World Business Alliance, although they were extremely sad because of Old Qin's death, they did not have much time to spare in the face of the World Business Alliance's life and death battle. At this time, the three fatties were fighting with another Life and Death Realm expert of the Tian Xie Sect, Wu Zhanhe!
Overall, Tianqi City's side was slightly weaker. However, the most crucial battle was between Cui Xie and Luo Zheng.
After Luo Zheng returned to the battlefield, many people's gazes fell on Luo Zheng.

Against Cui Xie, I'm afraid I can only use that move, and the chance … is only one!

Just by relying on the power of the stars, one could erupt with extraordinary power. According to the 《Stellar Battle Body》, after communicating with the second star, one could begin to cultivate the second move.
Luo Zheng had just successfully communicated with the second star. During this period of time, he had been meditating in seclusion. He also had some comprehension of the second move, but he was not proficient in it …
However, the power of the second move was far stronger than "Sinking Stars and Shaking the Earth". Now, he had no choice but to use this move.

The Wind of Life and Death was extremely peculiar. Any life form would instinctively fear the wind. Therefore, every time the Wind of Life and Death appeared, everyone's hearts almost stopped. Everyone stared blankly at the two bright yellow winds.
Daoist Master Dameng's face suddenly sank. The faces of many martial artists in the Cloud Hall also sank.

As for Wu Zhanhe, there was a trace of a sneer on his face. Luo Zheng was destined to not be able to escape death …
At this moment, the light in Luo Zheng's hands began to shine. He put his hands together and the two starlights of completely different colors suddenly merged into one. In an instant, the entire world suddenly underwent a great change!
It was as if day and night had instantly changed in the sky. In the dark sky, everyone could only see two bright stars. One was orange and the other was purple. The two stars bloomed with a strange light. This light bloomed between Luo Zheng's hands!
"The Glory of Two Stars!"
Luo Zheng's expression was calm. His hands, which were originally together, instantly separated as he spoke indifferently.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 728
The Stellar War Body was a transcendent divine might. Whether it was practicing it or not, it was far more difficult than an ordinary martial skill!
Just having one's perception roam the great void was enough to stop 99% of martial artists. Then, communicating with the stars was even more difficult.
But once one succeeded in practicing it, they could use the power of the stars to fight in conjunction with astral essence. The power they could erupt with far surpassed the imagination of an ordinary martial artist.
In the past, Empyrean Stellar had relied on this Stellar War Body to become nearly invincible in the upper realms.
However, the first style of the Stellar War Body, Sinking Stars and Shaking the Earth, was only the most basic application of the Stellar War Body. It was the most primitive level. Even so, Luo Zheng was able to deal with those peak war saints when he was at the general level.
The second style was much more complex. It required the user to completely fuse the power of several stars together in order to erupt with an even greater strength. But, the characteristics of many stars were completely different. Some stars were abnormally gentle, and some stars were wild and restless. When these strengths fused together, they would repel each other. But once one succeeded in practicing it, the power would multiply!
Currently, Luo Zheng had only fused two stars together. In the future, the more stars Luo Zheng opened, the greater the power of the second style would be.
In truth, just the second style alone was enough to become an independent martial skill. Thus, just the first half of the Stellar War Body was enough for Luo Zheng to practice for a long time.
After Luo Zheng separated his hands, the purple and orange starlight intertwined. The two different powers of the stars were like boiling water, forming a small luminous sphere that emitted a faint multicolored smoke. The strength contained within seemed as if it would erupt at any moment!
When Luo Zheng cultivated, he had superimposed these two powers together. But at that time, the power of the stars Luo Zheng extracted was miniscule, only the size of a grain of rice. Even so, the power that erupted from this tiny grain of rice was hard to imagine. Luo Zheng found it difficult to fully control this power, and several times he had almost injured himself in the explosion.
But this time, the two types of starlight merged together, forming a small ball that was constantly boiling. It was the size of a fist! If it exploded in his hands, even if Luo Zheng had the body of a low-grade celestial artifact, he would probably be blown into nothingness in an instant!
Overall, the success rate of the "Glory of Two Stars" was only about 40%. Luo Zheng hadn't completely mastered it, so this was a risk for him.
Luo Zheng carefully stabilized the starlight in his hand, while the two bright yellow winds of life and death slowly blew towards him.
Luo Zheng's eyes flashed, and he suddenly threw the fist-sized ball at Cui Xie.
"Huff huff huff …"
Time seemed to have stopped at this moment. The light in the sky dimmed, and all the battles stopped at this moment.
Everyone watched as the small ball instantly intersected with the two bright yellow gusts of wind without the slightest hindrance!
Cui Xie and Luo Zheng's complexions changed greatly at the same time!
Luo Zheng was afraid of the Wind of Life and Death. He didn't know what kind of strange thing this Wind of Life and Death was. It didn't seem to have great power, but with a gentle blow, it could cut off all life!
Cui Xie was afraid of the small ball that was rapidly flying towards him. The small ball that Luo Zheng had thrown out contained a power that Cui Xie couldn't imagine. Even if Cui Xie had transformed into a demon and his body was extremely tough, he still wouldn't be able to resist it.
If he was hit by this small ball, he would probably die!
When this thought flashed through Cui Xie's mind, how could he dare to stay in the slightest? He began to retreat rapidly!
After three breaths of time, the small ball finally split open …
Several years later, the cultivators of the Central Domain would call this battle the Battle of Heavenly Awakening City. Any cultivator who participated in this Sect War would inevitably mention the moment when Luo Zheng and Cui Xie fought. This was a moment that hundreds of thousands of cultivators couldn't erase. Even time couldn't forget this moment. This was because this moment had become a legend that had been passed down in the Central Domain for tens of thousands of years!
Everyone watched as the small ball that was emitting a dual-colored light suddenly split open. Two different kinds of starlight soared into the sky. It was as if a pot of boiling oil had been poured into a pot of boiling water. A huge ball of fire appeared in the pitch black world!
With Cui Xie as the center, everything within several thousand feet of him disappeared, including a section of Heavenly Awakening City's city wall!
It was as if a giant had suddenly drilled out of a different space and erased a piece of space out of thin air.
When this horrifying scene entered everyone's eyes, everyone heard a dull sound transmitted over. Almost all the martial artists at the same time had blood flowing out of their ears!
Compared to the terrifying power of Cui Xie's side, almost no one noticed Luo Zheng's side …
The two gusts of Wind of Life and Death were like real death gods, blowing in front of Luo Zheng and Ning Yudie. These gusts of Wind of Life and Death couldn't be blocked, couldn't be erased, and could penetrate everything, including space and time …
At this moment, thousands of thoughts flashed through Luo Zheng's mind. What should he do? How could he break this Wind of Life and Death? Did Xun have a solution? Did the knowledgeable Qinglong have a solution?
Amidst these chaotic thoughts, the two gusts of Wind of Life and Death entered Luo Zheng and Ning Yudie's bodies.
"Pu, pu," the tiny sound was almost negligible.
There was no solution …
When the gusts of Wind of Life and Death entered Luo Zheng's body, he suddenly felt a deep sense of powerlessness. He turned his head and looked at Ning Yudie, only to see that Ning Yudie was also staring at him in a daze.
Luo Zheng felt his vitality drain away at an alarming rate. Ning Yudie was the same.
She was no longer able to maintain the dignity of a Temple Master. Her face was full of panic, like a lost little girl looking for her home. She staggered towards Luo Zheng.
At this moment, a faint voice was transmitted into Luo Zheng's mind. Qinglong suddenly said, "I've thought about it. This Wind of Life and Death contains a trace of the Law of Karma. That's why it's so powerful. I wonder if the Heavenly Dao fragment can unravel it."
Qinglong was clearly speaking in his mind, but to Luo Zheng, it sounded so distant, as if it were on the horizon.
"What is the Law of Karma …"
Luo Zheng silently asked this question. But he found that his consciousness could not be transmitted to Qinglong. He could only stand there in vain, watching Ning Yudie throw herself into his arms. The two of them then fell to the ground.
"The Law of Karma. If there is a cause, then there is an effect. Life is the cause, death is the effect. So when the Wind of Life and Death touches you, you die." Xun's voice also floated out. Then she said, "Luo Zheng, Luo Zheng, you need to use your true essence to open your Sumeru Ring … take out the Heavenly Dao fragment …"
Xun's voice was still incomparably distant, and it sounded a bit unreal. Luo Zheng felt his body become numb and rigid. He should be slowly "dying".
Xun could not use her Sumeru Ring because Luo Zheng's mark was on it. If she forcibly opened it, the entire Sumeru Space would explode. But how difficult was it for Luo Zheng to revolve his true essence at this time? He just felt sleepy and tired, and wanted to fall into a deep sleep.
His father gave him the single name "Zheng", which meant that the journey of his life was endless. Could it be that his life's journey had come to an end?
He struggled to open his eyelids, holding Ning Yudie in his arms. She had closed her eyes, and her face was full of serenity, as if she had fallen asleep. Luo Zheng's face showed a trace of relief. Maybe this was good?
"True essence … just a little bit!" Xun's voice came again. "Do you want to die with Ning Yudie? Where is your sister? She is still in Cui Xie's hands! Even if Cui Xie dies, others will still use her. Do you have the heart to … "
Luo Yan!
Luo Zheng's heart had already stopped beating, but when he heard Xun's voice, it suddenly twitched, and then weakly began to beat again. In the end, there was still someone he couldn't let go of!
At the same time, a wisp of true essence escaped from Luo Zheng's dantian, followed Luo Zheng's meridians, and directly poured into the Sumeru Ring. It erased the mark on the Sumeru Ring, and the Sumeru Ring became an ownerless ring.
Xun quickly took out a small fragment from the Sumeru Ring engraved with a phoenix, and cautiously stimulated the power of the fragment.
The law of cause and effect was a power above the Laws. Compared to the various Origin Laws of this world, the law of cause and effect was even more unreasonable and overbearing.

Chapter 729
Everything in this world had a cause and effect.
Because walking in this world was destined to be difficult …
Because of hard work in cultivation, there would be success in martial arts.
Because …
Compared to the power of laws, the law of karma was purer and more direct.
However, all karma was built around the Heavenly Dao. When a Heavenly Dao was formed, all the karma had already been set. Therefore, the power of the law of karma was the top power of the Heavenly Dao.
No one in this world could fully comprehend all the laws of karma, unless they became the Heavenly Dao itself.
Xun looked at Luo Zheng nervously. Although the fragment of the Heavenly Dao contained a lot of karma, it was only a fragment of the Heavenly Dao. Even she and Qinglong didn't know much about the law of karma, so they didn't know if the fragment of the Heavenly Dao would have any effect.
If Luo Zheng died, the Sword Spirit Body would gradually dissipate, and the last wisp of Xun's soul in this world would lose its place to rely on. It would also dissipate between heaven and earth.
As for Qinglong and the others, they would probably be sealed in the mysterious furnace again.
However, at this time, everyone's attention wasn't focused on Luo Zheng and Ning Yudie. More people were looking at Cui Xie on the other side!
The power of Luo Zheng's attack had exceeded everyone's imagination.
The power of a middle stage False Tribulation Realm martial artist was much stronger than that of a Life and Death Realm martial artist …
Under this attack, how could Cui Xie survive?
As long as Cui Xie died, the Tian Xie Sect would only have Wu Zhanhe left. As a Life and Death Realm martial artist, Wu Zhanhe was difficult to deal with, but the Tian Xie Sect alone couldn't do much. The other martial artists weren't worth mentioning. The World Business Alliance and the Cloud Hall might be able to turn the tables!
Both sides were aware of the importance of Cui Xie. Their fight was meaningless. Cui Xie's life or death directly affected the overall situation!
The two intertwined starlights gradually dissipated. The rocks, yellow soil, trees, and the city walls were also blown away by the wind.
As the smoke and dust gradually dissipated, everyone saw a person lying in the huge crater …
That person's entire body was pitch black, like a sweet potato that had been completely charred. His entire body was wrapped in charcoal, and he lay on the ground, motionless.
"It's Cui Xie!"
"He should be dead, right?"
"Lying there motionless, he's most likely dead. It's impossible for him to survive that kind of attack!"
Many martial artists discussed in low voices.
Many warriors on the city wall naturally hoped that Cui Xie would die. If Cui Xie died, it would mean that they had won this battle.
However, under everyone's watchful eyes, Cui Xie's body suddenly moved.
After seeing Cui Xie move, everyone from the World Business Alliance and the Cloud Hall felt their hearts twitch. Everyone's hearts sank.
Immediately after, Cui Xie moved his limbs and stood up from the ground!
Although Cui Xie was in a sorry state, he was still alive …
"This, this … Luo Zheng's terrifying attack was already unimaginable. However, this Cui Xie is even more exaggerated. He took that attack head-on and still didn't die!"
"This is too exaggerated. This Cui Xie's life is actually so tough!"
The warriors from Apocalypse City were in low spirits, but the warriors from the Tian Xie Sect were in high spirits. Cui Xie was indeed a powerhouse worthy of their trust!
"Our King is mighty! Our King is mighty! Our King is mighty! "
Many independent martial artists roared once again. Their roars shook the heavens and the earth.
"He should be almost there! Kill him while he's down! I'll kill him! "Daoist Master Big Dream looked at Cui Xie in the crater and suddenly charged toward him!
Daoist Master Big Dream naturally understood that only by killing Cui Xie could they end this war. After taking such a heavy blow, Cui Xie was now an arrow at the end of its flight. Daoist Master Big Dream didn't believe that Cui Xie still had room to resist.
Just as Daoist Master Big Dream charged toward Cui Xie, Cui Xie's originally sluggish body suddenly became nimble. His charcoal-like arm suddenly punched toward Daoist Master Big Dream.
Daoist Master Big Dream's expression changed. How could he dodge when he was caught off guard?
"Bang!"
This punch directly smashed Daoist Master Big Dream out of the crater. It was unknown whether he was dead or alive.
Everyone saw Cui Xie's charcoal-like face split open and reveal a sinister smile. Two rows of white teeth were exposed on his pitch-black face, looking extremely terrifying.
"It's over …"
Everyone understood that this sect war had most likely ended prematurely.
Cui Xie was still alive. It looked like he had suffered a very serious injury, but his combat strength didn't seem to be affected too much …
Where was Luo Zheng? Where was Ning Yudie?
Only at this moment did everyone remember the two of them.
The two of them were already hugging each other and lying on the ground.
When Luo Zheng and Cui Xie had fought, the two bright yellow winds were still fresh in everyone's minds. Those two winds hadn't been extinguished by Luo Zheng's attack. Most likely, they had already blown Luo Zheng and Ning Yudie away.
They didn't know what those winds were, but that ancient powerhouse of the World Business Alliance had been blown to death by them. How could Luo Zheng and Ning Yudie resist?
Cui Xie slowly walked out of the crater. The edges of the crater were covered in fine dust. With every step he took, a cloud of dust rose up.
He quickly walked out of the crater and walked towards Luo Zheng and Ning Yudie.
Originally, Xun had activated the Heavenly Dao fragment and placed it beside Luo Zheng. When she saw Cui Xie walk over, she took out a spear from her spatial ring and pointed it at Cui Xie!
Xun's expression was incomparably serious. She pointed her spear at Cui Xie and coldly said, "Stop."
Cui Xie once again grinned and said, "I don't know what kind of existence you are. Perhaps you are Luo Zheng's greatest secret? Luo Zheng is already dead. How about you follow me! "
"Hehe." A contemptuous smile appeared on Xun's face.
"What? Am I not qualified? In the future, I will be the strongest in the Central Domain. The entire Central Domain will be under my control! I will be the ruler and king here! "Cui Xie shouted.
His words were not only for Xun to hear, but also for all the martial artists present.
"What a joke … a small Central Domain, a small lower realm, an ant-like existence, yet you are so arrogant." Xun stared at Cui Xie contemptuously and continued, "A Life and Death Realm martial artist is no better than a speck of dust in my eyes. Back then, with just a glance, I only needed a millionth of my strength to kill you. You want me to follow you? Do you think you are qualified?"
Xun's words stunned everyone.
What kind of existence was this Sword Spirit beside Luo Zheng? A millionth of its strength was enough to kill Cui Xie?
If Xun was not bragging, then she was probably an existence that they could not understand …
Xun's words made Cui Xie's expression freeze. He knew that this foreign race woman had an extraordinary background, but he did not expect to hear such words from her. What level of strength did this woman once possess? What kind of power did she come from?
Cui Xie's gaze continued to shift to Luo Zheng, who was lying on the ground, and said, "It's strange. Logically speaking, Luo Zheng's vitality should have been completely extinguished, but now he seems to be hanging on by a thread?"
After saying this, he ignored Xun's spear and continued to walk toward Luo Zheng.
Seeing Cui Xie move, Xun did not hesitate. With a shake of her spear, an invisible wave of energy fiercely pierced toward Cui Xie.
"Puff!"
The power of Xun's spear was actually comparable to Luo Zheng's all-out attack, but when it pierced Cui Xie's body, it was as if it had pierced a broken piece of cotton, and was distorted by a strange power on the surface of his body.
Then Cui Xie grabbed Xun's spear, and with a shake of his hand, immediately scattered Xun into countless Sword Spirits. These Sword Spirits immediately gathered inside Luo Zheng's body.
As for the spear in Cui Xie's hand, after appreciating it for a moment, he stuffed it into his spatial ring and continued to walk toward Luo Zheng.
Ten zhang …
Fifty zhang …
Thirty zhang …
Cui Xie's gaze fell on a fragment on Luo Zheng's chest, and his eyes revealed a strange luster.
"This fragment …"
Of course, he did not know the origin of this fragment, nor did he know how much it weighed. However, he discovered that Luo Zheng's life was being continued by this fragment, so he naturally believed that this fragment was not ordinary!

Chapter 730
Cui Xie didn't understand the principles behind the Wind of Life and Death, but that didn't mean he couldn't use the Wind of Life and Death to kill.
In fact, using the Wind of Life and Death was very risky for Cui Xie, and it would have a negative effect on his body. But, for the sake of this battle, he had no choice but to fight with everything he had!
The shard in front of him flashed with a color that made his heart skip a beat. With just a glance, Cui Xie was sure that this was no ordinary item. But, he didn't know if this item was the 'great secret' that Master Heartless had mentioned.
In Cui Xie's opinion, Luo Zheng had too many secrets. Perhaps, killing this guy would bring him unimaginable benefits!
Just as Cui Xie continued walking towards Luo Zheng and was about to take the shard, an extremely sharp sword beam suddenly shot out from the city wall!
"Swoosh!"
The sword beam contained an extraordinary aura, as if it represented the entire heavens, and it was here to punish Cui Xie!
The speed of the sword beam was so fast that no one had time to react. Even Cui Xie was slower by a beat!
"Puff!"
The sword beam accurately cut through Cui Xie's wrist, cutting off Cui Xie's hand!
Then, a young man holding a sword in his hand fell onto a green brick on top of the city wall. Next to him, there were two dark bloodstains.
In order to accumulate enough energy for this sword beam, Hua Tianming had nearly overdrawn his own life.
"Too bad … I didn't manage to hit his vital points." Hua Tianming thought to himself regretfully as he fell onto the green brick.
Even though he had only cut off one of Cui Xie's hands, it was enough to make Hua Tianming proud. After all, he was only a Divine Core warrior. The power of the Heavenly Sword was comparable to that of a transcendent divine might!
In fact, this Heavenly Dao was the Heavens itself! The Heavenly Sword that Hua Tianming cultivated also secretly catered to the power of the Heavens itself, and it followed the Heavenly Dao! If he followed the Heavens, Hua Tianming's future achievements might not be any lower than Luo Zheng's.
"That sword beam just now came from a young Divine Core warrior …"

"What a pity, there are so many amazing geniuses from the World Business Alliance and the Cloud Hall, but they're all going to be destroyed. Losing one of Cui Xie's hands won't help him."
Cui Xie looked at his severed arm, then glanced at Hua Tianming, who was on the city wall. A trace of killing intent appeared on his face. At his level, there was indeed a way to regenerate a severed limb. However, the regenerated limb often had to be cultivated again. Otherwise, it would become a flaw in his body.
But, Cui Xie didn't have the time to find trouble with Hua Tianming. The most important thing right now was to find out Luo Zheng's secret!
Cui Xie stretched out his hand and pointed at the wound, stopping the blood from flowing out. After that, he stretched out his other hand.
Right at this moment, he suddenly sensed a black shadow flying above his head. He stretched out his hand and grabbed it. What he saw was a thick and heavy sword! It was a sword from the Extreme Purple Realm!
After that, huge swords kept appearing in the sky, forcing Cui Xie to back off!
A figure as elegant as a lotus flower rushed over from the edge of the battlefield. Xi Youqin was wearing a white dress. Her skin was as white as snow. She pounced on Luo Zheng like a leopard. She touched Luo Zheng's skin and felt a chill coming from his body. She couldn't feel the life force in Luo Zheng's body …
Cui Xie was forced back several feet by the huge swords. He felt agitated for no reason. Luo Zheng was almost completely dead. Why were there still so many irrelevant people coming to kill him?
Luo Zheng's luck was so heaven-defying! Even if he died, he wouldn't let him kill him so easily!
Xi Youqin suddenly raised her head. Her eyes were slightly red. Compared to the others, she didn't know much. She couldn't tell that Luo Zheng had suffered many injuries. She also didn't know what the fragment in Luo Zheng's chest was for or whether Luo Zheng was still alive …
She only knew that the person in front of her was the one who caused Luo Zheng to become like this.
Since the Xi Family was destroyed, Luo Zheng had become her sky. Xi Youqin understood that perhaps Luo Zheng didn't value her too much. She didn't even have the thought of asking for a status from Luo Zheng …
However, she still regarded Luo Zheng as her husband and the person she could rely on in this life.
Xi Youqin's eyes slowly became cold. Her eyes were filled with killing intent. She was like an angry leopard, staring at Cui Xie who wasn't far away.
Cui Xie glanced at this woman indifferently. He didn't care about her eyes. If it were before, Cui Xie might have been interested in Xi Youqin. However, this young lady of the Xi Family had lost her Primordial Yin. She was of no use to Cui Xie.
As for this woman's strength, Cui Xie wasn't afraid of her at all …
Si Miaoling's Extreme Violet Yin Body was awakened by Cui Xie using a secret technique. Although the Extreme Violet Yin Body was powerful, it was still limited by one's cultivation base. This young lady of the Xi Family was only an Early Divine Core warrior, there was nothing to be afraid of.
"There are many things between you and me that need to be settled." Xi Youqin suddenly said, "My Xi family, my great grandfather, and the millions of warriors in the Void Spirit Sect!"
A faint smile emerged on Cui Xie's face, then he casually asked, "What are you going to do?"
Xi Youqin's eyes became misty, then she said with a cold voice. "Of course I'm going to kill you!"
"With just you?" After saying that, Cui Xie glanced around. There were still many warriors from the Cloud Hall and the World Business Alliance, but none of them dared to charge at her. Those Void Tribulation warriors still had some self-awareness, but this stupid woman in front of him didn't know anything …
"That's right, with just me." After saying that, Xi Youqin suddenly waved her hand.
In the next moment, Cui Xie's expression changed, and his expression became gloomy.
Numerous huge swords suddenly appeared in the sky …
One, ten, a hundred, a thousand, ten thousand, a million …
These huge swords covered the entire sky, and once again, the outside of Heaven Awakening City was plunged into darkness.
When the people from the World Business Alliance and the Cloud Hall saw this, their eyes went wide!
Many people had witnessed the might of the Extreme Violet Yin Body. When Luo Zheng fought against Si Miaoling, huge swords appeared one after another. The battle was even more dazzling, too much for the eyes to take in.
But, from the beginning to the end, there were only a few dozen huge swords!
How many were there in front of them?
They were densely arranged in the sky, as if the entire sky was made up of these huge swords!
How many people from the Tian Xie Sect would be able to survive if these huge swords slashed down?
Only then did Cui Xie realize that he was really wrong. He stared at Xi Youqin and asked, "You, how did you do it?"
Xi Youqin smiled brightly and shook her head. With a light wave of her finger, millions of huge swords slashed down from the sky at the same time.
She had the best physique in the Extreme Violet Realm, the first-grade Extreme Violet Yin Body. The huge swords she could use were far from what an ordinary Extreme Violet Yin Body could compare to …
Millions of huge swords were not her limit! If she could, as long as she paid a certain price, she could summon all 99 billion huge swords. With so many huge swords, it was enough to destroy the entire Central Domain.
Of course, this was not the result she wanted.
"Retreat!"
"Run!"
"We can't run away. There are huge swords everywhere!"
"Pfff, pfff, pfff, pfff, pfff …"
One by one, the huge swords slashed down, turning all the warriors below into minced meat.
Over a hundred thousand independent martial artists belonging to the Heavenly Evil Sect had been annihilated by Xi Youyou's zither in the span of a few breaths … Only a few Hollow Tribulation Realm martial artists and Wu Zhanhe had survived under the giant swords.
The battlefield outside Heaven Awakening City was like hell on earth. After the huge swords disappeared, the ground was covered in brown mud, which was a mixture of flesh and blood …
At this moment, Cui Xie's expression was as ugly as it could be.
