Chapter 731
The ones who decided the victory of a war between the sects were usually the few top cultivators.
As long as Cui Xie didn't die, he alone would be able to win this war between the sects.
But, winning was one thing, controlling the city was another …
Even if he killed Luo Zheng, Ning Yudie, and those ancient giants, who was going to help him take over Heaven Awakening City?
Besides, there were hundreds of thousands of cultivators in Heaven Awakening City, was he going to kill them one by one?
Top cultivators against top cultivators, middle cultivators against middle cultivators, and bottom cultivators against bottom cultivators …
Cui Xie had won the battle between the top cultivators, but now, a strange woman had appeared out of nowhere and slaughtered all the middle and bottom cultivators from the Kingdom of Evil Deities. Cui Xie's mood was as bad as it could get!
"Woman, you're courting death!" Cui Xie's face was filled with anger.
This time, he didn't hesitate. He reached out his hand and grabbed Xi Youqin. This fair and clean woman could be crushed into pieces with just one hand!
However, Xi Youqin blinked her eyes and furrowed her brows. Underneath her angry expression, there was a hint of disdain. She reached out her hand and grabbed Luo Zheng and Ning Yudie, then all three of them disappeared in an instant.

Cui Xie was the one who helped Si Miaoling activate her Extreme Purple Yin Body. However, the huge swords Xi Youqin summoned were completely out of Cui Xie's expectation.
After all, Si Miaoling's limit was only a few hundred huge swords. But, this woman was only an Early Divine Core warrior, and she was able to summon huge swords that were several tens of thousands of times stronger than Si Miaoling's. This was something that Cui Xie didn't expect!
After Cui Xie finished speaking, he reached out to grab the place where Xi Youqin had disappeared. According to Cui Xie's judgment, Xi Youqin should be hiding in the Purple Extreme Realm. No matter what, there was still a special node between her and this world. As long as he attacked that node, this woman would have nowhere to hide!
During the Martial Arts Conference, Luo Zheng was also forced by Si Miaoling to hide everywhere. In the end, it was because Luo Zheng relied on the Law of Space to find the location of the node that Si Miaoling was chased out of the Extreme Purple Realm.
Cui Xie originally had a hundred percent confidence, but with this grab, he frowned. His hand was empty!
"How is this possible?!"
Cui Xie clearly saw Xi Youqin disappear into the void. The node must be here. How did this woman do it?
Xi Youqin's faint voice drifted over. "Didn't you say that I was courting death? Haha … Then let me die! "
At this time, how could Cui Xie give up? He had to take down Tianqi City, but his Tian Xie Sect's army had been completely destroyed by this woman. More than a hundred thousand independent warriors, and they were the best among the independent warriors in the Central Domain. This was not a force that could be gathered in a short period of time!
He had paid such a huge price to attack Tianqi City today. How many independent warriors would dare to seek refuge with him in the future?
This time, he could not return empty-handed. Luo Zheng was his biggest gain. How could Cui Xie be willing to let Luo Zheng be taken away like this? He would never allow it!
"Hmph!"
Cui Xie snorted coldly and shot out a punch. With a wave of spatial fluctuations, his sharp eyes searched for the node.
It was still empty …
Then, Cui Xie threw out dozens of punches in one breath. Suddenly, his eyes lit up. He finally found a spatial node in the front left!
But just as he rushed over, the node suddenly disappeared.
"Idiot," Xi Youqin sneered.
Cui Xie did not say a word. He did not want to bicker with this woman at all. It was just that he could not find the node in front of him, and his mood gradually became impetuous.
Extreme Purple Realm …
This was a grotesque and variegated realm.
Dense giant swords were suspended in the sky. They were arranged neatly one by one, and they looked endless, extending to the edge of the world …
Compared to the True Dragon Realm, the Extreme Purple Realm was indeed very different. After all, the True Dragon Realm was a complete realm, and it was one of the largest independent realms among the hundred thousand realms.
The Extreme Purple Realm, on the other hand, was a broken realm with its own set of rules that were completely different from the outside world.
Xi Youqin put Luo Zheng to the side and pulled Ning Yudie away from Luo Zheng. She was not a generous woman. Even though Ning Yudie's body was cold, when she saw the two of them hugging each other, jealousy slowly rose in her heart.
After laying Luo Zheng flat on the ground, Xi Youqin knelt beside him. She held Luo Zheng's cold hands, but there was nothing she could do.
"That … Xun …" Xi Youqin awkwardly called out, "Sister Xun."
After a while, spots of red light began to condense. Xun coldly glanced at Xi Youqin, then turned to look at Luo Zheng. Her face was full of concern, and she immediately asked, "What is it?"
"What happened to Luo Zheng?" Xi Youqin murmured, anxiety appearing on her face.
"He's dead," Xun coldly replied.
Hearing Xun's straightforward answer, Xi Youqin opened her mouth slightly. She wanted to say something, but she stuttered and didn't know what to say. She was so sad that she was in a mess.
Seeing Xi Youqin's expression, Xun snorted and said, "I lied to you. It's hard to say now. We can only wait."
Xi Youqin held Luo Zheng's hand tightly and continued to ask, "Luo Zheng won't die, right? Sister Xun! "
Xun shook her head. "I'm not sure either. I hope so …"
The Heavenly Dao fragment released a strange light. Even after it was activated, the power it released only occupied a very small part of it. The Heavenly Dao fragment was not a panacea. It just happened that the wind of life and death contained a trace of the law of karma.
If one wanted to break the law of karma, they had to use another law of karma to fight it!
Because of life, death …
For many ordinary people, this law of karma was indeed very powerful.
However, it was not the most powerful law of karma.
For example, another law of karma could be easily broken.
"Because of death, nirvana rebirth."
This sort of law of karma could only be controlled by the legendary super God Beast, the Phoenix!
The Phoenix was a Super Mythological Beast, but it was not its attack power that was feared. In comparison, the True Phoenix Clan was generally weaker than the True Dragon Clan, but the Phoenix's life was controlled by the Karma Law of "Nirvana". Death was Nirvana, the beginning of their new life. Therefore, the True Phoenix Clan was almost an indestructible existence. Only by using a more powerful Karma Law could they be truly exterminated.
Compared to all sorts of karma laws, these were all relatively low level karma laws.
Now, they could only rely on Luo Zheng's good fortune. They didn't expect Luo Zheng to comprehend any sort of law of karma. They only needed Luo Zheng to use some sort of law of karma to break the law of karma in the wind of life and death!
A faint trace of energy from the Heavenly Dao fragment flowed into Luo Zheng's body. Luo Zheng's consciousness gradually came into contact with this energy …
At this time, Luo Zheng was immersed in a special state. He was analyzing the information within this energy.
For Luo Zheng, this information was too massive.
The Heavenly Dao recorded the vicissitudes of 3.6 billion years, the vicissitudes of an era. There were 100,000 great worlds, millions of boundless worlds, the stars of the heavens, and the various foreign domains.
Too many things could happen in 3.6 billion years. Even a boundless world couldn't record the events that happened in 3.6 billion years.
Take Luo Zheng's boundless world as an example. The history of this boundless world was only tens of thousands of years old. The history before that was completely blank. This was only tens of thousands of years, let alone 3.6 billion years.
However, the Heavenly Dao fragment wasn't the entirety of the Heavenly Dao. Not to mention, Luo Zheng had only absorbed a trace of energy from the Heavenly Dao fragment.
What do I need to comprehend?
Why do I need to comprehend?
With no one to guide Luo Zheng, he couldn't communicate with anyone, including Qinglong and Xun. He could only rely on himself.
As he continued to analyze, he gradually realized the emotions hidden within the Heavenly Dao fragment.
The Heavenly Dao had emotions?
An inexplicable thought suddenly popped into Luo Zheng's mind …
Xun once told him that this fragment wasn't a fragment of the firmament, but a fragment left behind by the previous Heavenly Dao, "Ying Tian".
From this small fragment, Luo Zheng felt a trace of helplessness and grief from Ying Tian.

Chapter 732
The Heavenly Dao also had emotions. Could it have originally been transformed from a living being?
The Heavenly Dao was the ruler of the universe. If it could transform itself into the entire Heavenly Dao, just what kind of powerful being was it?
The emotions that escaped from this fragment made Luo Zheng understand the helplessness of Ying Tian when it was shattered!
After Ying Tian was shattered, a new Heavenly Dao had to be established. As Xun had said, the current Heavenly Dao was Heaven. In the universe, after countless martial artists broke through to the Life and Death realm, they would encounter life and death tribulations. In other words, minor heavenly tribulations and major heavenly tribulations were sent down by Heaven.
In other words, after Ying Tian was shattered, it was completely replaced by Heaven. Then, how did Heaven accomplish this?
To Luo Zheng, these things were unsolved mysteries. But since he had already obtained a fragment of the Heavenly Dao, he might have a chance to participate in this in the future. It was just that Luo Zheng didn't know just what sort of magnificent secret he was about to face.
As Luo Zheng analyzed the fragment, besides the memories and emotions of the fragment, Luo Zheng also discovered some special things.
Those things were reflected in Luo Zheng's mind. They were made up of characters, arranged in rows and densely packed together.
Above each row were two characters. The upper characters were golden and the lower characters were dark gold. It seemed to be a question and an answer …
Luo Zheng didn't know that these characters were the Karma Laws recorded in the Heavenly Dao fragment!
The first row of characters was cause and the second row of characters was effect.
In the entire universe, if a martial artist wanted to cultivate Karma, they could only choose the Karma Laws that existed in the Heavenly Dao. Thus, these characters were the general outline of the universe. All the Karma Laws were written in them!
Because a seed was planted, flowers and trees could grow.
Because a person worked hard to irrigate and irrigate, they could harvest …
Because a person cultivated a special cultivation method, they could make a breakthrough at a specific time …
Every living being, every martial artist, and even every plant were included in the Karma Laws. Thus, the Karma Laws were the superior Laws of the universe!
As for the so-called inferior Laws, such as the Fire Laws, Water Laws, Time Laws, and Space Laws, they were all inferior Laws.
The operation of the entire universe was supported by the Superior Laws and the Inferior Laws. Only then could it operate freely.
Of course, the laws recorded in the Heavenly Dao fragment of Ying Tian were not the laws below the firmament. The current Heavenly Dao was the ruler of the firmament. This fragment of Ying Tian contained the laws of the Heavenly Dao from 3.6 billion years ago.
Just as Luo Zheng scanned the rows of characters, one of them suddenly shone brightly with golden light!
Luo Zheng's consciousness seemed to have understood the row of characters!
"Because of the eternal cycle of reincarnation, it will never perish …"
Eternal reincarnation, eternal immortality …
Luo Zheng was completely immersed in these words. He was even more unaware that his body was undergoing an astonishing change!
Xi Youqin accompanied Luo Zheng and quietly looked at Luo Zheng's face. This guy who had created countless miracles wouldn't die so easily, right?
At this moment, her heart was filled with regret.
In fact, because she had a First Grade Extreme Purple Yin Body, she had paid a price. Not only did she have the ability to destroy the world, but she could also summon the infant true spirits of the Extreme Purple Realm.
Although those true spirits were only in their infancy, they should have been more than enough to deal with Cui Xie.
However, Luo Zheng had told her more than once that he didn't need anyone to interfere in his battle with Cui Xie. He needed to resolve it by himself. This was his destiny.
This was also why Xi Youqin hadn't made a move when she saw Luo Zheng retreating step by step. Luo Zheng's personality was too stubborn. If she rashly made a move, she would instead anger Luo Zheng. Xi Youqin was now afraid of offending this arrogant guy.
Seeing Luo Zheng's current state, Xi Youqin was naturally filled with regret. If she had another chance, she wouldn't hesitate to sacrifice her lifespan and summon her true spirit to kill Cui Xie.
Even if it had an impact on Luo Zheng's heart of martial arts, it was still much better than his current half-dead state.
Of course, Xi Youqin didn't understand that if Luo Zheng wanted to kill Cui Xie, he didn't even need to use Xi Youqin's ability. He only needed to invite the Divine Sea Realm experts of the Sea God Continent.
Luo Zheng was now walking on an impartial and brilliant path of martial arts. He was moving forward in an almost perfect manner towards the peak. On this path, he didn't allow any flaws. It wasn't that Luo Zheng's mentality couldn't get over Cui Xie, but if he used external forces, Cui Xie would definitely become his inner demon in the future. This demon would erupt at a critical moment and bring him unpredictable results.
"Eh?"
Xi Youqin suddenly had a strange expression on her face.
Next to her, Xun coldly stared at this woman and asked, "What's the fuss about?"
"Luo Zheng, Luo Zheng's body seems to have become smaller!" Xi Youqin blinked. Her clear eyes were filled with disbelief.
"What nonsense are you talking about!" Xun suddenly looked at Luo Zheng and was shocked. "It has indeed shrunk!"
Luo Zheng's body suddenly became smaller. His appearance changed as well. The vicissitudes and murderous aura on his fair face were gone. Instead, he looked more childish. His hair also became shorter. It was as if he had suddenly turned from 20 years old to 14 or 15 years old!
"What on earth happened?" Xi Youqin asked curiously.
Xun shook her head. Even Xun had never seen such a strange thing. She had heard of things like regaining one's youth, and it wasn't difficult for the Yao Ye Clan to do so. However, it definitely wouldn't happen under such circumstances!
The two women stared at Luo Zheng. His body continued to shrink. Soon, he turned from 14 or 15 years old to 11 or 12 years old.
Luo Zheng's green clothes also became loose. He looked like a child wearing clothes that didn't fit his body!
"He became smaller again!" Xun said in surprise.
In the blink of an eye, Luo Zheng turned back to 7 or 8 years old.
3 or 4 years old …
1 or 2 years old …
In the end, Luo Zheng turned back into a baby …
Xi Youqin's eyes flickered. She looked at the baby on the ground and suddenly said, "Did Luo Zheng look like this when he was a child?"
The baby looked very cute. He had a round face and long lush eyelashes. If it weren't for the fact that she was worried about Luo Zheng's safety, Xi Youqin would have hugged him and kissed him.
Xun didn't have the same thoughts as Xi Youqin. She was also deep in thought. This was the first time she had seen such a strange thing. If she guessed correctly, it should be because of the Heavenly Dao fragment that caused Luo Zheng to undergo such a shocking change.
What did Luo Zheng comprehend from the fragment? Or did he trigger some kind of karmic law?
While Fuming was pondering, Luo Zheng's body suddenly began to expand. He gradually grew from the appearance of a baby to that of a boy!
"This is!" Xun's eyes suddenly flickered. A strange idea suddenly flashed in her mind. "Is this the reconstruction of the body or the reincarnation of the body?"
Even Xun and Qinglong knew very little about the karmic law!
The karmic law was the purest rule of the Heavenly Dao. Even Celestial Masters couldn't control it. The Celestial Masters who could control the karmic law were the best of the best.
In Xun's impression, the reconstruction of the body and the reincarnation of the body could achieve this effect. However, Xun couldn't tell. After all, she hadn't seen this kind of karmic law with her own eyes!
"Does it matter which kind it is?" Xi Youqin asked with concern.
Xun smiled, shook her head, and said, "It doesn't matter. If it's the reconstruction of the body, it can make Luo Zheng's body purer. If it's the reincarnation of the body, it won't have any effect on him. But I believe he can wake up!" Xun thought of Luo Zheng's body, which had been soaked in the Hong Meng Dipper, and was much purer than ordinary warriors. His body was a freak in the whole universe. The reconstruction of the body was a great adventure for other warriors, but for Luo Zheng, it might not be a good thing.
The reconstructed body might not be stronger than his current body.

Chapter 733
Luo Zheng's body was expanding at a visible speed, but Xi Youqin had already put away his clothes when he became smaller. Now Luo Zheng was naked and smooth.
Xun looked at the scene in front of her expressionlessly, while Xi Youqin glanced at Xun quietly and blushed slightly. Although Xi Youqin was no stranger to Luo Zheng's body, she was still not used to admiring her man's body with another woman.
But on second thought, Xun was originally in Luo Zheng's body, so she probably knew Luo Zheng better than she did.
Xi Youqin had not thought of this aspect before. In this way, didn't Xun hear the words she said when she and Luo Zheng were entangled? Thinking of this, Xi Youqin's face became even redder.
Seeing Xi Youqin like this, Xun felt puzzled. But this woman was strange, so Xun did not bother to pay attention to her.
As Luo Zheng's body continued to grow, he quickly returned to a seven- or eight-year-old child, an eleven- or twelve-year-old teenager …
Slowly, Luo Zheng's body shape completely recovered, and he returned to the appearance of a 20-year-old young man.
Both women looked at Luo Zheng on the ground anxiously. Would he wake up?
After waiting for a long time, Luo Zheng's body suddenly trembled, and he opened his eyes abruptly.
The sky in the Purple Extreme Realm was dark purple, and purple-black auroras were constantly circulating in the sky. A huge purple sun in the sky was emitting a charming purple light.
Luo Zheng looked at this strange environment, and only then did he notice that Xun and Xi Youqin were beside him. "Where is this?"
Xun immediately replied, "The Purple Extreme Realm. Xi Youqin pulled you in."
Xi Youqin ignored everything and threw herself into Luo Zheng's arms, not hiding the joy on her face.
Luo Zheng hugged Xi Youqin and continued to ask, "Where's Cui Xie?"
Cui Xie's life was very important, and Luo Zheng was most concerned about it.
"He's not dead," Xun replied simply.
"He's not dead …" Luo Zheng lowered his head and murmured, showing a trace of regret. Out of the corner of his eye, he glanced at Ning Yudie next to him, and his face suddenly changed. He pushed Xi Youqin aside and stretched out his hand towards Ning Yudie's small nose.
Cui Xie shot two gusts of the Wind of Life and Death, one, hit Luo Zheng, and the other hit Ning Yudie.
Luo Zheng knew very well how dangerous the Wind of Life and Death was. Even that ancient giant had no power to resist the Wind of Life and Death. He had relied on the Heavenly Dao fragment to recover, but what about Ning Yudie?
Luo Zheng could feel Ning Yudie's slightly cold breath, which meant that Ning Yudie's body was still "alive", but the life force coming from Ning Yudie's body was very weak …
Seeing Luo Zheng's gradually darkening face, Xun said, "The Wind of Life and Death directly devours life force, and it contains the law of karma. Touching it means certain death, so there's no way to resist it. Her life force is now almost exhausted …"
Luo Zheng nodded silently. Even that ancient giant had died immediately after being hit by the Wind of Life and Death. He and Ning Yudie could survive mostly because of the Heavenly Dao fragment, which offset part of the law of karma between life and death. However, Ning Yudie was now in a state of near death, so how could she recover?
He had used the law of karma of reincarnation in the Heavenly Dao fragment to fully recover to his current state.
The reincarnation of his physical body was equivalent to him skipping 20 years of his life, allowing him to grow to his current state.
But what about Ning Yudie? Should he let her comprehend the laws in the Heavenly Dao fragment?
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng did not hesitate and placed the Heavenly Dao fragment on Ning Yudie's chest.
The three of them waited quietly for a while, but Ning Yudie did not respond at all. The Heavenly Dao fragment also did not respond …
"Is it useless?" Looking at Ning Yudie's serene face and her petite body lying on the ground as if she had fallen asleep, Luo Zheng's heart suddenly ached.
Xi Youqin and Xun were also silent. Although Luo Zheng did not speak, they were very clear about Ning Yudie's position in Luo Zheng's heart. He would never allow Ning Yudie to die like this.
"Vitality … Xun, when you went to the Heavenly Feather Sacred Land, didn't the Sacred Tree inject vitality into you?" Luo Zheng asked, a glimmer of hope in his eyes. Even if he could not awaken Ning Yudie now, as long as he could get enough vitality, he might be able to save Ning Yudie.
When Luo Zheng and Xun went to the Heavenly Feather Sacred Land, the Heavenly Feather Sacred Land had injected the vitality of the entire Sacred Tree into Xun. If that vitality could save Ning Yudie, Luo Zheng could take Ning Yudie to the Sea God Continent.
However, Xun shook her head and said, "It's useless. The Sacred Tree's vitality is very special. That Sacred Tree is connected to the hearts of all the members of the Demon Night Clan. The vitality of the Sacred Tree can only be used by members of the Demon Night Clan. Other clans cannot bear it. If Ning Yudie was a member of the Demon Night Clan, this method would work."
After Xun finished speaking, Luo Zheng fell silent again.
After a few breaths of time, Luo Zheng's face was filled with killing intent. He suddenly stood up and clenched his fists tightly. The veins on the back of his hands bulged as he said coldly, "I will think of other methods. No matter what method, Ning Yudie cannot die! However, Cui Xie … I must kill him! Xi Youqin, take me back! "
Seeing Luo Zheng's murderous expression, Xi Youqin's heart began to beat rapidly. At this moment, Luo Zheng felt like a complete stranger to her.
She nodded slightly. She felt a bit sad in her heart. He was willing to be so angry for Ning Yudie. What if it was her?
Xi Youqin didn't have much confidence in facing the question that suddenly popped up in her heart. She had always believed that she wasn't that important in Luo Zheng's heart …
Cui Xie was still searching the surroundings!
The person who understood you the most was often your enemy. Cui Xie also regarded Luo Zheng as his most important enemy. Therefore, he was very clear about how powerful Luo Zheng's fate was. As long as he didn't kill Luo Zheng with his own hands, this brat would always have a heaven-shaking fate to reverse his predicament.
Although the hundreds of thousands of independent martial artists of his Tian Xie Sect had been killed, those martial artists were nothing more than cannon fodder. Even if he was alone, he could still crush the entire Tianqi City. Not to mention that the Tian Xie Sect still had Wu Zhanhe, but there wasn't a single Life and Death Realm martial artist in Tianqi City!
The key problem was to kill Luo Zheng. However, that damned woman was actually hiding in the Extreme Purple Realm. He had been searching for so long, but he still couldn't find that node!
It was true that one wrong step led to trouble at every step!
If he had taken the initiative to snatch Xi Youqin, this wouldn't have happened today, and his Heavenly Demon Joyous Union Array would have been completed.
What Cui Xie didn't expect was that when this Xi Youqin was forcibly taken away by Luo Zheng, he had chased her all the way without success. Later, Luo Zheng had even taken away that woman's Primordial Yin. But today, this woman had taken such a great risk to save Luo Zheng …
This Luo Zheng's fate was much stronger than his. Cui Xie was also incomparably depressed. If it weren't for this series of karma, Luo Zheng would have already been a dead man. Moreover, the hundreds of thousands of disciples of his Tian Xie Sect wouldn't have been killed by that woman's giant sword.
On the city wall, many False Tribulation Realm warriors coldly watched this scene. As for the three fat alliance leaders, they were confronting Wu Zhanhe. At this time, they no longer had the heart to continue fighting.
In the eyes of the many warriors in Tianqi City, as long as Luo Zheng was still alive, then all hope still existed. If Luo Zheng really died, then everything would be over …
"I think it's better to surrender," a False Tribulation Realm warrior said indifferently as he sat powerlessly on the broken city wall of Tianqi City.
"We are warriors of the World Business Alliance. We would rather die than submit. Why should we surrender to the other party?" A stubborn warrior replied unwillingly.
"But facing Cui Xie, we don't even have the strength to fight back. Why should we throw away our lives for nothing?"
"Rather than drag out an ignoble existence, it's better to fight it out with him …"
Every warrior had a different mentality at this time, and their reasons were also different. However, the vast majority of warriors didn't express their opinions. In their hearts, there was still a last glimmer of hope.
At this time, Cui Xie finally discovered that a node had appeared in the air. His face immediately revealed a happy expression as he grinned maliciously, "Luo Zheng, let's see how you dodge!"
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 734
Everyone was paying close attention to Cui Xie's movements. When the martial artists in Tianqi City heard Cui Xie's words, their hearts sank.
Cui Xie must have discovered Luo Zheng's hiding place!
This meant that their last hope was about to be extinguished.
Cui Xie didn't hesitate at all. He took a step forward, and his fist flashed. He punched out with unparalleled power and hit the spatial node!
"Bang!"
The moment Cui Xie's fist hit the spatial node, his expression suddenly changed!
A sharp sword suddenly appeared from the spatial node and instantly shattered Cui Xie's fist. The tip of the sword pierced deeply into Cui Xie's fist!
Luo Zheng was holding a high-grade sacred artifact! Even though Cui Xie's fist was covered in a layer of sturdy black scales, it still couldn't block the sharpness of the sacred artifact.
The long sword pierced through Cui Xie's fist and embedded itself into the bones of Cui Xie's hand. A huge force immediately pushed Cui Xie back!
The long sword completely left the spatial node. Next, Luo Zheng's right hand that was holding the sword was pushed back, followed by his entire body!
The ten fingers were linked to the heart. The pain in the hand was the most real and intense. Even Cui Xie's charred face showed a pained expression …
His other hand had been cut off by Hua Tianming's sword. At this moment, he could only continuously retreat.
Luo Zheng didn't give Cui Xie any chance. He firmly held the sharp sword in his hand and pushed forward. He wanted to use the long sword in his hand to nail Cui Xie to the city wall!
After Luo Zheng appeared, Xi Youqin and Ning Yudie also returned from the spatial node. However, Ning Yudie was carried out by Xi Youqin.
"It's Luo Zheng! He really didn't die! "
"I already said that this brat's luck is heaven-defying. No matter how dangerous the situation is, he can still escape with his life. I really don't know how he recovered. It seems like he didn't suffer any injuries!"
"But the Hall Master of the Cloud Hall seems to still be lying on the ground without any movement. Could he have died?"
Atop the broken walls of Heavenly Awakening City, numerous martial artists began to seethe with excitement!
They had already given up hope just now. There was no one in the Central Domain who could stand up to Cui Xie.
Previously, Luo Zheng's "Glory of Two Stars" had completely convinced them. If there was anyone who could defeat Cui Xie, then that person must be Luo Zheng!
But now, Luo Zheng had indeed returned, and he looked like a dragon and a tiger, as if he hadn't suffered any injuries at all.
"Kill Cui Xie! Luo Zheng! Good luck! "
"Luo Zheng, we support you!"
"Luo Zheng, I support you spiritually …" This was shouted by Red Maple.
Hearing this, Pei Tianyao smiled helplessly. Fortunately, Hua Tianming had fainted, otherwise, he was afraid that he would have to teach this brat a lesson.
"Whoosh!"
Luo Zheng and Cui Xie, one in front and one behind, flew towards the city wall. The two of them were separated by a long sword, and the upper half of the long sword was still firmly nailed to Cui Xie's arm. Cui Xie's blood splattered all the way.
"You're really lucky. Even the Wind of Life and Death can't do anything to you!" Cui Xie said coldly.
In fact, the Wind of Life and Death was something that even Cui Xie himself couldn't completely control. Today's battle was not only related to the fate of the Cloud Hall and the World Business Alliance, but also the fate of Cui Xie and the Tian Xie Sect. That was why he used such a terrifying thing!
Cui Xie had never heard of the law of causality. Even Cui Xie himself couldn't figure out how the Wind of Life and Death could kill the opponent. He only knew that the creatures that were blown by the Wind of Life and Death would undoubtedly die. There were no exceptions. No matter how powerful the creatures were, they would take a detour.
Although Cui Xie was desperately looking for the space node, he thought that Luo Zheng's luck was too heaven-defying. As long as he didn't see Luo Zheng die in his hands, he wouldn't feel at ease until he confirmed that his vitality was completely extinguished!
But in fact, Cui Xie thought that Luo Zheng was 99% dead.
The result was completely different from what he had imagined. The Wind of Life and Death didn't seem to cause any harm to Luo Zheng. Looking at Luo Zheng's vigorous appearance, how could Cui Xie not be shocked? It could be said that he was extremely shocked!
What kind of freak was this guy! How could he not die like this?
A sense of powerlessness rose from Cui Xie's heart. He, Cui Xie, was able to develop to this point as an independent warrior. His martial arts heart was as strong as an iron wall. If he couldn't achieve his goal, he would never give up. He was cold-blooded, cruel, decisive, and never hesitated!
But at this moment, he suddenly found that he seemed to have lost his confidence in facing Luo Zheng.
When martial artists fought, especially martial artists with similar strength, it was a competition of one breath. As the saying goes, when two rivals meet on a narrow path, the brave will win. The best mentality for a martial artist was to always maintain an unbelieving belief!
Previously, when Luo Zheng was being chased by Cui Xie, his martial arts heart was somewhat shaken. It was Ning Yudie who used her frost aura to calm Luo Zheng down. Under Ning Yudie's comfort and encouragement, he quickly recovered. But at this time, who could help Cui Xie?
Cui Xie had prepared many methods for Luo Zheng. The Wind of Life and Death was the enemy of all life, but it couldn't do anything to Luo Zheng.
Now, Cui Xie couldn't figure out how he could kill Luo Zheng!
How sharp was Luo Zheng's perception?
The trace of sadness that flashed through Cui Xie's eyes was caught by Luo Zheng's sharp eyes.
Cui Xie was Luo Zheng's greatest enemy and also someone he had to kill. Any flaw that Cui Xie revealed would be grasped by Luo Zheng!
Now that a flaw had appeared in Cui Xie's martial arts heart, Luo Zheng naturally wouldn't let it go!
"Cui Xie, you're not qualified to kill me!" Luo Zheng sneered.
"Is that so? You're just a False Tribulation Realm martial artist. Do I need to be qualified to kill you? "Cui Xie wasn't a fool. At this time, he quickly adjusted his mentality.
But Luo Zheng immediately said, "That's right! It's a pity that people like you are too arrogant. You want to call yourself an emperor and establish a Divine Kingdom, but you don't know that both a sacred ground and a Divine Kingdom need a Divine Sea Realm cultivator to guard it. What do you have to open the Divine Sea and call yourself an emperor with your talent? "
In order to build a Divine Kingdom or Sacred Land, it required a Divine Sea warrior to guard it. Just like the Purple Heart Sacred Land on the Sea God Continent. Although it was ranked at the bottom of the Sea God Continent, it still had Situ Haotian and two other Divine Core warriors guarding it.
Of course, if the entire Central Domain was united, they might be able to build a Divine Kingdom. But, as the King, he wasn't qualified to build one!
As long as a Divine Realm martial artist didn't die, he or she would be able to steadily break through to the Divine Sea realm!
Divine Realm geniuses weren't rare in the Sea God Continent, but in the Central Domain, their numbers were very small. However, in recent years, Cui Xie's son, Cui Yun, Xi Xiaojie from the Void Spirit Sect, and a few others had shown up.
As for Cui Xie, his talent couldn't be rated as a Divine Realm warrior. At most, he could only be rated as a Heavenly Realm warrior.
Of course, it was possible for him to break through to the Divine Sea realm with a Heavenly Realm warrior's talent, but it would be much more difficult. That was why Cui Xie's talent had always been his sore spot. When he found out that his son had a Divine Realm warrior's talent, he had spent a lot of effort in training him!
"It's just talent, I'm not limited by such a trivial matter. Once I set up the Great Heavenly Devil Joyous Union Formation, I'll definitely …" Before Cui Xie could finish his sentence, Luo Zheng clenched his fist, and the tip of his sword rotated in Cui Xie's hand. Cui Xie's face instantly turned pale.
"You won't have the chance. I've said it before. Since you dared to take Luo Yan away, you must be prepared to die!" Luo Zheng's voice had just faded away when the two finally crashed into the city wall.
"Boom!"
A large part of the city wall had already collapsed. Under this collision, a large hole was smashed open, and bricks one zhang wide fell down one after another.
At the moment of impact, Luo Zheng suddenly pulled his long sword out of Cui Xie's hand, and once again fiercely stabbed towards Cui Xie's chest!
Now, one of Cui Xie's hands had been chopped off, and the other hand had been crushed by Luo Zheng's high-grade saint artifact. He had completely lost the ability to fight back. Facing Luo Zheng's long sword, he could only choose to retreat. He shot backwards, and escaped along the inside of the city wall!
The inside of the city wall was built with all kinds of boulders and beams. Under this collision, the row of city walls began to collapse along the direction of Cui Xie's escape.

Chapter 735
The warriors outside could see that the ten li wide city wall was quickly collapsing like tofu.
The warriors watching the battle on the city wall quickly left to avoid being caught in the crossfire.
"Pu!"
In the process of dodging, Luo Zheng stabbed his sword again. Cui Xie couldn't dodge in time, and the sword directly cut a slender wound on his chest, causing blood to flow out.
If it was before, when Cui Xie stimulated his demon bloodline and a layer of special scales appeared on his body, Luo Zheng wouldn't have been able to do much to him.
After all, Luo Zheng exhausted all his means of attack and still couldn't break Cui Xie's defense!
But now … …
Luo Zheng's "Glory of Twin Stars" directly carbonized Cui Xie's skin. Under the charred skin, there was only new flesh that had just grown out. If he wanted to completely recover his previous defense, he would need time to recuperate. How could a layer of newly grown flesh block Luo Zheng's high-grade Sage Grade sword?
If Cui Xie knew that Luo Zheng was still alive and could still chase after him, he would've left early and hid in a corner to recuperate. He would've waited for his body to completely recover before coming back.
But now, he had to fight against Luo Zheng with his incomplete body, which made him complain incessantly.
"Rumble … …"
Luo Zheng chased him from one end of the city wall to the other. From time to time, the figures of the two would appear from the broken walls. Everyone who saw this scene was also stunned.
"This Luo Zheng is really a fierce person. At the beginning, he was pressing Cui Xie down and beating him. Seeing that he was about to be killed by Cui Xie, he suddenly jumped up and pressed him to the ground again … …"
"This guy is already an existence that we can't understand. His strength can't be measured by his cultivation base. It can't even be measured by his eyes!"
"He's going to win! I can't believe that he can really kill Cui Xie! "
Seeing this inconceivable scene, the morale of many martial artists in Heavenly Enlightenment City soared. From the looks of it, the World Business Alliance and the Cloud Hall should be saved!
"Boom!"
Cui Xie finally made it out of the city wall and dashed towards Wu Zhanhe. "Old Wu, help me!"
As the dignified number one master of the Central Domain, he had been chased down by a 20 year old youth and had even called out to other martial artists for help. This loss of face was far too great. It was easy to imagine how this matter would spread throughout the entire Central Domain, to all the tea houses and wine shops, to the point where it would be repeated over and over again. It would become the topic of conversation for many martial artists and even mortals. It would even become a story told by storytellers!
When Wu Zhanhe heard this, he naturally wanted to help. Cui Xie's death was not a good thing for Wu Zhanhe!
However, how could the three leaders of the World Business Alliance allow Wu Zhanhe to help Cui Xie? The moment the three fatties saw Wu Zhanhe move, the three of them surrounded Wu Zhanhe.
Who knew that Wu Zhanhe would give Shi Kefan a strange smile, "Do you really think that you can stop me?"
Previously, Wu Zhanhe was indeed entangled with Shi Kefan, Yan Yueshan, and Mo Haishan. However, at that time, Wu Zhanhe was waiting for Cui Xie to make a move. He did not put in any effort, preserving his own strength!
Even if the Kingdom of Evil Deities was really taken down, it would belong to Cui Xie. Wu Zhanhe was a Life and Death Realm martial artist. He was not so magnanimous as to be willing to hand over the Central Domain to Cui Xie.
He was also preserving his strength. If he chose the right time, he would naturally turn against Cui Xie and seize everything he had!
However, right now, Cui Xie could not die …
"Si si …"
After Wu Zhanhe revealed a strange smile, traces of pitch black Quintessential Qi swirled out. The Quintessential Qi transformed into hundreds of small black snakes that shot towards the three Merchant Union Heads.
Those small snakes were like real snakes, coiling in the air. Revealing their poisonous fangs, they bit towards Shi Kefan and the rest.
Transforming True Yuan into snakes was something that any martial artist who had stepped into the Xiantian realm could do. They only needed to catch a snake and visualize it. Most martial artists could do it vividly.
However, the danger of these small snakes was not their fangs, but the poison they contained!
Wu Zhanhe could be called the "Poison King". His standard of using poison was something that ordinary martial artists could not hope to reach. Furthermore, he had cultivated the inner version of the "Five Poison Scripture", and his standard of using poison had improved greatly.
The three leaders carefully dealt with these black poisonous snakes. As for Wu Zhanhe, he rushed towards Cui Xie. Colorful lights began to emit from his body. At this time, the colors on Wu Zhanhe's body were abnormally bright!
"Careful! Luo Zheng! Don't come into contact with this guy! "Shi Kefan was unable to get rid of these small black snakes and could only warn Luo Zheng.
The brighter the color, the stronger the poison!
Luo Zheng glanced at Wu Zhanhe indifferently. His gaze was still fixed on Cui Xie. Wu Zhanhe's "Five Poison Scripture" was an absolutely fatal existence to other martial artists. Even Cui Xie would be wary of it. However, to Luo Zheng, it was completely futile.
His high-grade Immortal tool body was not invulnerable to poison because he would not react to any poison at all!
With a wave of Wu Zhanhe's hand, a huge colourful net flew towards Luo Zheng's head. Before the net could cover Luo Zheng, there was already a fishy smell wafting over.
"Let's see if you'll retreat or not!" Wu Zhanhe stared at Luo Zheng with a sinister smile.
His poison net was made from 81 types of poisonous substances in the Central Domain. Moreover, it was refined using the secret method in the "Five Poison Scripture". Not to mention the False Tribulation Realm Luo Zheng, even Cui Xie would not dare to be enveloped by this poison net. Moreover, according to the "Five Poison Scripture", even if a Divine Sea Realm expert was enveloped by this poison net, they would still die.
This was the terrifying part of cultivating poison techniques. As long as one could collect fatal poisons, they could still kill those experts above their realm!
In the end, Wu Zhanhe saw Luo Zheng crash into the poison net. Moreover, it seemed that Luo Zheng did not hesitate or fear. His gaze was fixed on Cui Xie and he did not even look at his poison net …
"Is this guy crazy?!" Wu Zhanhe's expression changed. Then, a large hole appeared in his poison net. Part of the colorful poison net was still hanging on Luo Zheng's body.
"Ignoring my poison net, you will pay a painful price!" Wu Zhanhe stared at Luo Zheng. The poison of this poison net acted up very quickly. Moreover, when Luo Zheng circulated his True Essence, the poison could quickly spread throughout his body. If nothing unexpected happened, Luo Zheng could only live for another ten breaths of time!
"Fall for me!"
"Fall!"
"That's true!"
"Fall …"
Ten breaths, twenty breaths, thirty breaths …
The corner of Wu Zhanhe's mouth twitched. Only now did he unwillingly admit that his poison net seemed to have no effect on Luo Zheng!
What kind of monster was this kid! Wu Zhanhe lamented in his heart. After all, in his heart, Wu Zhanhe's poison net was on par with Cui Xie's Wind of Life and Death. But now, it had no effect at all. The degree of shock in his heart was almost the same as that of Cui Xie.
Shi Kefan, Yan Yueshan, and the others were resisting the small black snakes while paying attention to the movements below. They looked at each other and saw smiles in each other's eyes!
Luo Zheng really had the ability to turn the tide!
"Pu!"
Luo Zheng stabbed his sword into Cui Xie's back.
Cui Xie was an arrow at the end of its flight, while Luo Zheng had just undergone a physical reincarnation and obtained a new life. Whether it was in terms of mentality or body, the two were completely different.
When the sword pierced through, Luo Zheng's Vital Spirit suddenly erupted and pushed the long sword down. The long sword directly pierced through Cui Xie's chest and nailed him to the ground. The sword hilt was still shaking on the ground.
How could Cui Xie be willing to be tied up without a fight?
The moment he was nailed to the ground, he was about to turn over and get up. Luo Zheng had already taken a step forward and stepped on Cui Xie's chest, stomping him into the mud.
"It's over." Luo Zheng pressed his hand on the hilt of his sword and stared indifferently at Cui Xie.
Cui Xie raised his head to look at Luo Zheng, a look of panic finally appearing in his eyes. To him, this war between the sects was almost a sure-win battle, and it was also a crucial moment for him to establish his Divine Kingdom. But thanks to this boy, the situation had taken a sudden turn. Hundreds of thousands of independent martial artists had been wiped out, and he had fallen into this predicament in an instant!
How could Cui Xie be willing to accept this?
At this moment, a strange smile suddenly appeared on Cui Xie's face. "Over? Hahahaha! It's still far from over! "

Chapter 736
Luo Zheng looked at Cui Xie coldly with calm eyes. In fact, Luo Zheng had already guessed what was going on.
"The Central Domain will be purged by the Yu Dynasty sooner or later, and you … Hahahahaha!" Cui Xie's smile was crazy and ferocious. His scorched face looked as abominable as a demon's.
As Cui Xie spoke, he took out an Eight Trigrams Mirror from his Sumeru Ring with trembling hands and threw it at Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng casually blocked the Eight Trigrams Mirror. The Eight Trigrams Mirror rolled a few times on the ground and faced up. The broken mirror slowly condensed into a human figure.
Everyone's eyes were focused on Luo Zheng. The battle between Luo Zheng and Cui Xie was what they were most concerned about. They all wanted to know what tricks Cui Xie, an ambitious and ruthless person, had up his sleeve when he was at the end of his rope.
The human figure slowly condensed. It was the boy that Luo Zheng had encountered before.
Luo Zheng looked at the boy indifferently with contempt on his face. He asked Cui Xie, "Is this your last trick? You're just a defeated opponent. "
The boy suddenly spoke, "You're Luo Zheng, right?"
"So what if I am?" Luo Zheng replied coldly.
"Yes, I did lose to you in Tianyun City last time, but I only lost a wisp of my soul. The Astral Storm has already stopped. Hahaha, you just need to sit and wait for your death. When the time comes, the entire Central Domain will be my, Heartless Sweetheart's, fiefdom! As for the secret in your mind, it belongs to me as well! "The boy laughed wildly.
Cui Xie also laughed, "Luo Zheng, I advise you to give up resisting! There are countless warriors of the Divine Sea Realm in the Yu Dynasty. They can totally crush you. If you choose to surrender now, I can consider leaving your corpse intact! "
Hearing Cui Xie and Heartless Monk's words, the hearts of many martial artists in Heavenly Enlightenment City trembled. Now, the Central Territory had truly fallen into troubled times. They had finally forced Cui Xie into a dead end and victory was right in front of them. But now, the Great Yu Divine Kingdom had suddenly appeared!
Divine Sea Realm warriors …
With the strength of the warriors in the Central Domain, a Divine Sea Realm warrior could sweep the entire Central Domain!
There was a huge Astral Storm in the sky above the Chaotic Astral Ocean, so it was very difficult to travel between the Divine Kingdoms and the Central Domain.. Only a few warriors had been to the Divine Kingdoms, but there was not much communication between them..
For the Central Domain, the four Divine Kingdoms were too powerful. The Astral Storm was also a big barrier to protect the Central Domain.
For the four Divine Kingdoms, the Central Domain could only be regarded as a barren land. The four Divine Kingdoms were not very interested in the Central Domain. Therefore, the two continents had maintained peace for so many years and minded their own business.
Judging from Heartless Sweetheart's tone, what he cared about was not the Central Domain, but the secret in Luo Zheng's mind. What on earth existed in Luo Zheng's mind? Even the warriors of the Divine Kingdoms were so interested in it?
"The secret in my mind?" Hearing Heartless Sweetheart's words, Luo Zheng's face darkened.
The secret of the mysterious furnace and the nine real dragons in Luo Zheng's mind had never been known to outsiders. Last time, when Luo Zheng fought against Heartless Sweetheart's soul in his mind, Qinglong directly swallowed his soul. He did not expect Heartless Sweetheart to know his secret!
Seeing Luo Zheng's face change, Cui Xie thought Luo Zheng was scared. He sneered and said, "Have you thought it through? If you are willing to give me all your secrets, I will consider sparing your life! "
Luo Zheng's eyes flashed. He said coldly, "Childish!"
Then he pulled out the long sword in his hand. A sword light shot out and immediately cut the Eight Trigrams Mirror into pieces.
The moment the Eight Trigrams Mirror was cut into pieces, Heartless Sweetheart's shadow slowly became blurred. Finally, his whole figure gradually disappeared, leaving only a strange smile.
The moment Luo Zheng pulled out his sword, Cui Xie suddenly rose from the ground. With his character, even in this situation, he would not give up.
However, Luo Zheng did not give Cui Xie any chance. He slashed out with his long sword. The sword light was like a silver snake, entangling Cui Xie's body. In an instant, his body was cut into pieces. His demon-like head rolled on the ground. In addition to unwillingness in his eyes, there was also a smile on his face. He wanted to say something, but how could he speak when his head was separated from his body? His shriveled mouth opened and closed weakly a few times. His vitality completely dissipated.
Luo Zheng suddenly felt relaxed after killing his old enemy with his own hands.
Since the beginning of the Blue Cloud Sect, Cui Xie's shadow was like a brand on his martial heart. And now, Luo Zheng had finally expelled this brand, removing the biggest stone in his heart!
Many martial artists in Tianqi City heaved a sigh of relief in their hearts. Even though the words of the boy had made them feel even more pressured, today's battle should be over. The only troublesome one was Wu Zhanhe!
Wu Zhanhe, who was floating in the air, saw this scene. His face suddenly changed. His black Zhen Yuan suddenly revolved, turning into a stream of light and speeding away!
Although Wu Zhanhe was a Life and Death expert, he mainly cultivated poison arts. His poison arts were incomparably sharp. Even if Cui Xie was stronger than him, he was still wary of Wu Zhanhe. The problem was that he had seen Luo Zheng break through his poison net with his own eyes. He did not put his poison arts in his eyes. How could he resist Luo Zheng?
This kid was completely immune to poison!
What's more, Cui Xie was already dead. His defeat was already set. If he continued to fight, he would only lose his life. There was no other meaning!
It seemed like he would have to live a life of concealing his identity again …
As for the remaining False Tribulation Realm martial artists of the Tian Xie Sect, they also scattered and fled. Some of them were chased by the martial artists of Apocalypse City, but the three Alliance Masters and Luo Zheng did not chase after them.
Although Wu Zhanhe was a False Tribulation Realm martial artist, he was only a loner. In the Central Domain, he did not have much fame. The backbone of the Tian Xie Sect was Cui Xie. Now that the number one person in the Central Domain was dead, the entire Tian Xie Sect was scattered. These independent martial artists could only live a life of concealment in the future. Otherwise, if they went to any other sect, they would be punished!
With a long sword in hand, Luo Zheng slowly walked towards Ning Yudie and Xi Youqin, who were not far away.
Within a radius of dozens of li, Ning Yudie's huge sword had plowed through the ground. The ground was filled with a mixture of mud and blood. As he walked forward, he left behind footprints.
The setting sun was dyed red with blood, and the rays of sunlight reflected on the broken tiles of Apocalypse City. Every martial artist felt a sense of sadness and joy. They had won this battle by a great margin, but they had still won in the end!
When Xi Youqin saw Luo Zheng walking towards her, a trace of joy appeared on her face. She pursed her lips and smiled. When she smiled, her faint appearance gave people a gentle and tranquil feeling. She reached out to hold Luo Zheng's arm, but Luo Zheng gently avoided her and continued walking towards Ning Yudie.
Xi Youqin's hand froze in mid-air. She looked at Luo Zheng's back in disappointment. However, her bright eyes turned and she immediately cheered up. She followed Luo Zheng. She knew that Luo Zheng was in a bad mood. Now was not the time to throw a tantrum.
Walking to Ning Yudie's side, Luo Zheng held her petite body in his arms and then flew towards the sky. After floating in the air, he glanced indifferently at the remaining martial artists.
The martial artists of Apocalypse City hadn't suffered too many losses. Of the hundreds of thousands of martial artists, only about a third had been lost. After all, almost all of the martial artists of the Tian Xie Sect had been killed by Xi Youqin's giant sword …
All of the martial artists' eyes were shining. It wasn't known who shouted first, "Luo Zheng! Mighty! "
The shouting wasn't loud, but it was very loud. Soon, more than a dozen martial artists joined in.
"Luo Zheng! Mighty! "
Immediately after, hundreds, thousands, tens of thousands …
All of the martial artists followed the rhythm and roared towards Luo Zheng in the sky!
Today, Luo Zheng had become the savior of Apocalypse City. Or rather, the savior of all the sects in the Central Domain …
As long as they poured true essence into their voices, their voices could be transmitted far away.
All of the martial artists held their breaths. Now that they had won, how astonishing would their roars be? Hundreds of thousands of martial artists roared together with their true essence. That sound was terrifying to the extreme.
In the entire Apocalypse City, tens of millions of mortals could hear it clearly. That sound drifted far, far away.

Chapter 737
Looking at Ning Yudie's serene face, Luo Zheng's face revealed a trace of sadness.
He wasn't a pretentious person. Most of the time, he could control his emotions. However, the sadness he felt at the moment made it difficult for him to control himself.
The battle with Cui Xie ended with his victory. However, he paid such a painful price. Most of Ning Yudie's vitality was taken away. She was like a dead person.
This was also a result that Luo Zheng didn't expect.
Many warriors were roaring and shouting Luo Zheng's name. However, Luo Zheng was moving forward slowly, flying towards Tianqi City.
The three leaders of the World Business Alliance looked at each other and followed Luo Zheng. In fact, the three of them were most concerned about what the boy said. Would the Yu Da Divine Kingdom really send people to the Central Domain?
If so, this would be the biggest crisis in the history of the Central Domain!
Throughout the history of the Central Domain, there were constant battles. This time, the Sect War launched by the Tian Xie Sect swept across more than half of the Central Domain. However, it wasn't unprecedented in the history of the Central Domain. During the most chaotic period of the Central Domain, all the Sects fought for themselves and attacked each other. It was completely chaotic.
However, these Sect Wars were ultimately internal conflicts within the Central Domain!
On the other hand, the Divine Kingdom of Yu the Great had come from another continent, and it had entered the central territory as an invader!
As long as a powerful warrior of the Divine Sea Realm entered the Central Domain, it was enough to sweep the entire Central Domain.
Luo Zheng was powerful, but he was only a warrior of the False Tribulation Realm. It was impossible for him to fight against a powerful warrior of the Divine Sea Realm. By then, who could resist the Yu Da Divine Kingdom?
This wasn't a hidden worry. Just now, everyone heard clearly that the Astral Sea Storm in the Chaotic Astral Sea had stopped. Those powerful warriors of the Divine Sea Realm could easily pass through the Chaotic Astral Sea and arrive in the Central Domain.
However, this imminent crisis didn't have the slightest possibility of turning into a crisis …
Three days later.
Luo Zheng stood at the entrance of a palace like a statue, quietly looking at the sky.
Heavenly Awakening City was the most prosperous city in the Central Domain. It was home to all the artificers, alchemists, runemasters, and of course, all the famous doctors in the world.
An old man in a white robe came out of the palace and sighed towards Luo Zheng. There was a look of regret on his face.
This old man's name was Xue Xi. Even in the Central Domain, where famous doctors gathered, he was considered a first-rate miracle doctor.
"Doctor Xue, how is it?" Luo Zheng asked softly.
In fact, Luo Zheng already knew the answer from Xue Xi's expression.
Even the knowledgeable Qinglong was helpless. How could the doctors in the Central Domain compare to Qinglong's knowledge?
However, people who were about to drown would always grab onto some life-saving straws. Luo Zheng couldn't tell right now, so he could only try.
Xue Xi was indeed a knowledgeable man. He said, "The Hall Master of the Cloud Hall isn't sick!" Xue Xi didn't know anything about the law of causality, nor did he know that Ning Yudie was hit by Cui Xie's Wind of Life and Death. He said according to his own understanding, "Since we were born, when we absorbed the first breath of Innate Fetal Qi, the life force in our body is constant! That's why the little guy gives people a feeling of vitality! "
"Our fleshly bodies are like buckets filled with vitality. However, as time passes, the vitality in the buckets will be slowly depleted! When a warrior cultivates, he will constantly expand the bucket, increasing the life force in the bucket by ten times, a hundred times, a thousand times. Therefore, a warrior will have a lifespan that is ten times, a hundred times, a thousand times longer than a mortal! "
"As a person gradually grows older, the bucket will slowly decay, and the life force in the bucket will gradually dry up. For example, when a mortal reaches the age of eighty or ninety, even if they don't suffer from any pain or illness, they will still die. This is the so-called dying without illness! In fact, the life force in the bucket will dry up … "
"The Hall Master of the Cloud Hall is like this. 99% of the life force in the bucket has already disappeared. Only a faint trace of life force is hanging on to her body. Fortunately, the bucket of the Hall Master is much larger than the other warriors. Gradually, the remaining trace of life force is enough to keep her body from dying. However, her current state, to put it bluntly, is not much different from a dead person!"
Xue Xi was very patient as he explained to Luo Zheng.
In Tianqi City, Xue Xi was considered a very old and famous doctor. In addition to his great fame, his temper was also very bad. As the saying goes, a person with talent is arrogant. Even if he was the Alliance Master of the World Business Alliance, Xue Xi would rarely give him a good face.
However, today, he was very patient and patiently explained so much to Luo Zheng.
"Is there a solution?" Luo Zheng asked.
In fact, Luo Zheng knew what Xue Xi said. He didn't want an explanation, but a solution. No matter how much he said, it was useless to Luo Zheng.
"No," Xue Xi replied firmly, "In the Central Domain, there are indeed many medicinal pills that can prolong life. However, these medicinal pills don't really replenish life force! Those medicinal pills only repair the 'bucket' and slow down the loss of life force. However, the Hall Master of the Cloud Hall is not facing this problem! "
Luo Zheng frowned, but in the end, he nodded. "I understand!"
After Xue Xi left, Luo Zheng entered the palace. Several maidservants were waiting on the side. Ning Yudie was lying on a brown wooden bed. She still hadn't changed at all. It was as if she had fallen asleep and stubbornly refused to wake up.
Luo Zheng quietly looked at her for a long time. He remembered that on the way back from River Wave City, Luo Zheng had vowed that if he survived this crisis, he would hold a Dual Cultivation Ceremony with her.
The crisis had passed, and Cui Xie had been killed by him. However, he still ended up in this situation.
When Luo Zheng walked over, the maidservants obediently stepped aside. He originally had some words to say, but when they reached his mouth, he swallowed them back down. A firm look appeared in his eyes again. However, in his heart, he said, "I won't let you die."
Then, Luo Zheng turned around and walked out of the palace. He arrived at the main entrance of the World Business Alliance's headquarters. The three Alliance Masters and many False Tribulation Realm martial artists had been waiting here for a long time.
On the side of the main entrance, Ao Xiang was dressed in martial arts attire. An emerald green scimitar was strapped to his waist. He was ready to go.
Although Cui Xie was dead, the remnants of the Tian Xie Sect had not been completely eradicated. In the past few days, the martial artists of the World Business Alliance and the Cloud Hall had spread to the eastern cities one after another to clean up the independent martial artists.
However, most of the martial artists of the Tian Xie Sect had already scattered like birds and beasts. Their defeat was like a landslide. The backbone of the Tian Xie Sect was dead. The other martial artists simply had no power to resist.
As for this group of people, they were heading to the Void Spirit Sect. It had become the temporary headquarters of the Tian Xie Sect. All the important things of the Tian Xie Sect had been moved to this place.
On the day Cui Xie was killed, Ao Xiang couldn't wait to go to the Void Spirit Sect. He had heard that after Cui Xie made the Void Spirit Sect his headquarters, he had imprisoned his lover there.
Although Cui Xie was dead, there should still be many False Tribulation Realm martial artists guarding the Void Spirit Sect. With his Divine Core cultivation base, it was impossible for him to save Cui Xie's life if he went there alone. He had no choice but to wait for the main group in Tianqi City.
After this group of people gathered together, they started marching towards the Void Spirit Sect.
A few days later, Luo Zheng and his group appeared in the sky above the Void Spirit Sect. When they arrived at the Void Spirit Sect, all the defensive formations in the sect had been activated.
Although the Void Spirit Sect had been occupied by the Tian Xie Sect, the defensive formation left behind by Feng Guanyu wasn't completely destroyed. Although Cui Xie was dead, there were still many martial artists with great ambitions in this world.
Obviously, these martial artists hadn't given up yet. They wanted to rely on the defensive formation of the Void Spirit Sect to resist.
In the eyes of some False Tribulation Realm martial artists of the Tian Xie Sect, the Tian Xie Sect's backer was the Yu Divine Kingdom. Once the people of the Yu Divine Kingdom arrived, the entire situation would be reversed.
This was also the reason why they still wanted to protect the Void Spirit Sect.
"Although Feng Guanyu's Thousand Illusionary Formation is very exquisite, it can only defend against False Tribulation Realm martial artists. Break the formation." Luo Zheng said with an indifferent tone.
The strength of the three Alliance Chiefs of the World Business Alliance was comparable to a Life and Death Realm martial artist. With the addition of Luo Zheng and many other False Tribulation Realm martial artists, it wasn't difficult for them to break this defensive formation.
Furthermore, many False Tribulation Realm martial artists in the Tian Xie Sect were either dead or injured. Most of them had fled in all directions and concealed their identities. There weren't many martial artists who were willing to stay in the Tian Xie Sect and fight to the death.
* Boom * * Boom * * Boom * * Boom * *
Under the continuous attacks of the group, the Thousand Illusionary Formation was finally broken.

Chapter 738
The Void Spirit Sect was in chaos right now.
Back then, when they attacked the Void Spirit Sect, just how arrogant were they?
In front of the Void Spirit Palace, the plaque with the words' Void Spirit Sect 'was destroyed by them, and replaced with a plaque made from pure gold. The words' Tian Xie Sect 'were engraved on the plaque. This plaque made from pure gold weighed over 5,000 kilograms, and it shone brightly as it hung in front of the Void Spirit Palace. It was an extraordinary sight.
But too bad, this plaque had only been hung for a few months …
These independent warriors thought that if they followed the Tian Xie Sect, they would be able to secure their position in the entire Central Domain. But in the end, the Tian Xie Sect rose like a shooting star, and fell like a shooting star …
These independent warriors weren't trained by the Tian Xie Sect, and they weren't loyal to the Tian Xie Sect. During this period of time, more than one or two warriors had fled from the Void Spirit Sect. In the beginning, the Sect Elders of the Tian Xie Sect had tried to restrain them, but soon, even those Sect Elders had fled, leaving only a small number of warriors behind.
Within the Void Spirit Palace, Cui Yun was alone. Next to him were two girls who had sealed their senses. One of them was Luo Yan, and the other was Ao Xiang's lover. Both of them possessed the Extreme Violet Yin Body.
Because there weren't enough women, Cui Xie had sealed their senses. Through this secret cultivation method, their cultivation had reached the Mid Divine Core realm.
Cui Yun was lying on the spacious Dragon Throne without any energy left in his body. He stared blankly at everything in front of him, and there was a crazed look in his eyes.
A few months ago, he was still having a beautiful dream. When the Kingdom of Evil Deities reclaimed the entire Central Domain, his father would become the King of the Kingdom, and he would become the Crown Prince. Once the Kingdom of Divine Deities was established, he would unify the entire Central Domain, and no one in the Central Domain would be able to fight against him.
Unfortunately, this dream was too short, as if it was really a dream. After waking up, he was shocked to realize how childish he was.
At this moment, Cui Yun had also comprehended something. He realized that everything was destined. Luo Zheng wasn't just his nemesis, he was his father's nemesis as well ….
But so what if he comprehended it? The Central Domain was huge, but he had nowhere to go. Those independent warriors could still hide their identities. After all, there were too many independent warriors in the Tian Xie Sect. It would be difficult for the World Business Alliance and the Cloud Hall to find them one by one.
On the other hand, Cui Yun was Cui Xie's son. As a God-level genius, everyone understood the principle of eliminating the source of trouble. Not to mention that Luo Zheng would not let him off, those fatties from the World Business Alliance looked simple and honest, but they were all extremely shrewd. They would probably dig three feet into the ground to find him.
He no longer had a place to hide, so he might as well face it head-on.
He had to avenge his father's death.
"Luo Zheng, since you killed my father, I'll kill your sister in front of you." Cui Yun's face revealed a trace of ruthlessness. If he lost this war, he would lose everything. However, since you made me suffer, I won't let you have an easy time either!
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh whoosh …"
Several escape lights appeared at the entrance of the Spirit Void Hall at the same time. Luo Zheng was in the lead, and Ao Xiang was more anxious than anyone else. He also followed closely behind Luo Zheng and entered the Spirit Void Hall.
When Cui Yun saw Luo Zheng, she couldn't help but feel afraid. That kind of fear came from instinct, like a mouse meeting a cat.
During the fight for the Rising Dragon Platform, Luo Zheng shattered Cui Yun's martial arts heart, leaving Cui Yun with an indelible memory for the rest of her life.
However, Cui Yun had already thrown caution to the wind. She forcibly suppressed her fear and pretended to be calm as she sneered at Luo Zheng. "Luo Zheng, you're here!"
Luo Zheng looked at Cui Yun coldly. This person in front of him was not qualified to say this to him. He looked at Luo Yan and the other woman next to him, and then said coldly, "Let them go, and I'll leave your corpse intact!"
"Let them go?" Cui Yun's face revealed a crazed look. "In your dreams!"
Cui Yun suddenly stood up from the wide dragon throne. Streams of True Essence flickered in her hands, and the True Essence transformed into thin threads that surrounded Luo Yan and the other woman's necks.
The thin threads of True Essence were extremely sharp. As long as she pulled them gently, she could separate the two women's heads!
Luo Zheng was still able to keep calm, but Ao Xiang couldn't stand it anymore. "Stop!" Ao Xiang roared and suddenly rushed toward Cui Yun.
Cui Yun smiled evilly. She gently twisted her fingers, and the thin threads suddenly tightened a little. They immediately cut the skin on Ao Xiang's lover's neck, and a trace of blood immediately flowed down from the wound.
Seeing this scene, Ao Xiang immediately stopped in his tracks.
"Come on, I promise I can separate her head from her body before you come here!" Cui Yun smiled faintly, her eyes full of madness. Since she was going to die anyway, she wanted to make herself happy.
Ao Xiang gritted his teeth and clenched his fists so tightly that blue veins were exposed. He stared at Cui Yun and asked, "What do you want?"
Cui Yun smiled faintly and said, "I don't want anything. I just want you … to pay a little price."
Luo Zheng stared coldly at Cui Yun and suddenly said, "Let them go, and I'll spare your life!"
"Hahahaha," Cui Yun suddenly sneered again, "Just now you wanted to leave my corpse intact, but now you want to spare my life! You gave in so quickly. Luo Zheng, you disappoint me! "
Luo Zheng didn't speak. He didn't completely figure out Cui Yun's thoughts.
In fact, during this period of time, if Cui Yun had enough time to kill the two women, and only took Luo Yan hostage after Luo Zheng's arrival, what was his purpose?
"What on earth do you want?" Luo Zheng asked coldly.
"I'm a god-level genius. If you just spare my life, how will you compensate me for everything I've lost?" Cui Yun continued, the madness in her eyes growing stronger and stronger, "Let me tell you, Luo Zheng, I don't need anything! I only want their lives! "
"My father is dead, and the Tian Xie Sect has been defeated. I have nothing, and it's boring to live." Cui Yun pointed to the distance, "All of this is thanks to you! Thanks to you, Luo Zheng! "
Luo Zheng's eyes were cold, and he didn't speak.
The faces of Shi Kefan, Yan Yueshan, and the others also darkened. They knew very well that they had encountered the most difficult situation now.
They were not afraid of Cui Yun's request. As long as they could do it, they could promise to spare Cui Yun's life. After all, the Tian Xie Sect had lost its power. Leaving him alone couldn't make any waves, and it was impossible to reverse the situation.
However, Cui Yun was almost crazy now. He didn't even care about his own life. Everything was for revenge.
As the saying goes, one can be strong without desires. This kind of crazy guy was the most troublesome. The most troublesome thing was that Luo Zheng's sister was still in his hands. What should they do?
Luo Zheng's mind was also spinning rapidly. If Shi Kefan and the others could understand, Luo Zheng could also understand that the guy in front of him simply wanted revenge. He wanted to kill Luo Yan in front of him!
How could he save Luo Yan's life?
Kill Cui Yun as quickly as possible?
Luo Zheng didn't have the confidence to do so. Even if his speed was extremely fast, and if he used his astral essence to erupt, combined with the innate talent of the Cloud Chasing Lion, he could take Cui Yun's life in the blink of an eye. Hearing Cui Yun's words, Luo Zheng suddenly smiled faintly and continued, "You are right! I am indeed scared. I am afraid that you will kill my sister, but … "
"But what?" Cui Yun asked.
"But …" The moment Luo Zheng spoke, he was about to take a step forward. His Vital Spirit had already been activated.
However, Cui Yun was more vigilant than Luo Zheng imagined. Luo Zheng's figure only swayed for a moment. His fingers suddenly tightened slightly, and the Zhenqi filament directly strangled the two women's necks. In the Zhenqi filament, a trace of blood seeped out, and the two women's necks were blood-red.

Cui Yun had a faint smile on her face, "Do you think I am a fool? Luo Zheng! "
Luo Zheng gritted his teeth and stared at Cui Yun. It seemed that attacking the Void Spirit Sect this time was a mistake. Along the way, he was worried about the safety of his sister, so he could only break into the Void Spirit Sect first.
In contrast, Cui Yun was waiting for Luo Zheng to come and planned to kill Luo Zheng in front of Luo Yan.
This was the effect Cui Yun wanted.

Chapter 739

Under the seemingly endless deep sky, there was a desolate mountain. Strange birds were circling around the mountain.

Chapter 740
Luo Zheng didn't know what this power was formed from, and he couldn't even feel the fluctuations of true essence within it.

Seeing him break the seal on the six senses so easily, the three leaders couldn't help but raise their eyebrows.
[This technique is too unimaginable …]
The Great Seal of Six Senses was a secret cultivation skill. Cui Xie had mastered this secret skill, and so did the World Business Alliance. However, this secret skill would have an unexpected effect on the cultivator's heart of martial dao, and it was very troublesome to use. Therefore, the World Business Alliance rarely used it.
Using this method to bring a few Divine Core warriors to the World Business Alliance was meaningless. However, because of the Heavenly Fiend Joyous Union Formation, this secret skill was very suitable for Cui Xie.
The process of sealing the six senses was extremely troublesome, and breaking the seal on the six senses was extremely complicated as well. If they were the ones who brought Luo Yan back, they would have to spend a lot of effort, and it would take at least a few months before they could break the seal.
This young man had just casually waved his hand, and easily broke the seal. This was incredible.
Luo Yan's eyelids twitched, then she suddenly opened her eyes. A puzzled expression could be seen within her pupils.
She had been sealed for more than two years. Although she had been constantly cultivating during these two years, her entire body and mind had been sealed. If it had been a cultivator who was over a hundred years old, it probably wouldn't have affected her too much. After all, in the long years of a cultivator's life, it was very common for them to cultivate in seclusion for ten years or even a hundred years.
But, Luo Yan was still young, and in her short life, she had spent nearly one-ninth of her life in a seal. This would definitely have some negative effects on her body and mind.
She was startled with a puzzled expression. Looking at the unfamiliar environment in front of her, as well as the unfamiliar faces around her, her mind was filled with questions.
All of a sudden, a familiar figure entered her sight. Immediately after, her small oval face was filled with pleasant surprise. Finally, she opened her mouth and called out softly, "Brother …"

Chapter 741
This brother and sister pair's short life could also be considered full of misfortune.

It was just like what those people said, Luo Yan was their guide, as well as the pointer in their words. Every step they took in life always had an ultimate goal in mind.
When he was in Luo Family, Luo Zheng's goal was to head to Blue Cloud Sect to find his younger sister. After finding his younger sister, his goal was to rescue his younger sister. In the end, Luo Zheng relied on his stunning achievements to bring his younger sister away from Purgatory Mountain.
Who would have thought that after this short reunion, they would be separated for several years, only now did they meet again.
Because her six senses were sealed, Luo Yan couldn't sense the passage of time. Her memories were still from two years ago.

Luo Yan pursed her lips. Her eyes revealed a trace of disbelief.
As Luo Zheng's sister, she had unconditional trust in her brother's words. However, Luo Zheng's words were too shocking and hard to believe.

In comparison, those warriors who had lived for hundreds of years, or even hundreds of years, had all their time been wasted? As for that ancient tycoon of the World Business Alliance, had he also lived for more than a thousand years?
This was the reason why Luo Yan didn't dare to believe it.
This was the Central Domain, not the Eastern Domain!

He didn't hide or hide anything from Luo Yan.

Chapter 742
