At this time, Luo Zheng suddenly thought of something. Others might not be able to solve this problem, but with the ability of the young man in front of him, he should have a way!
Ning Yudie's life force was exhausted, so it shouldn't be too difficult to restore her life force, right? It should be just a matter of raising a hand.
Luo Zheng had never been willing to open his mouth to ask others for help, but this matter was related to Ning Yudie, so he had to swallow his pride no matter what!
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng suddenly said to the young man: "I have one more thing to ask."
"Speak," the young man said with a faint smile. His patience was indeed excellent.
Then, Luo Zheng told him about Ning Yudie.
"The wind of life and death, the law of karma, hehe," the young man said with a smile that deserved a beating, "I can indeed resolve this level of karma easily, but … I won't help you."
"Why?" Luo Zheng frowned and continued to ask.
"Because this is also your fate!" The young man said faintly: "I can't interfere too much. This is already against the rules. You have to work hard. As for Luo Yan, I'm taking her away!"
This is also my fate …
Luo Zheng lowered his head and pondered for a while. At this time, he felt a pair of cold little hands suddenly cover his neck. Luo Yan had kissed him on the face.
After understanding the fate of her brother and her brother, Luo Yan suddenly seemed to become sensible. Or rather, she had already understood her fate.
She knew that her brother had already spent too much energy and didn't want to bring any psychological burden to Luo Zheng, so she showed her positive side to Luo Zheng. She smiled and said: "Brother, you can do it!"
Looking at Luo Yan's confident smile, how could Luo Zheng not see through her thoughts? Although he was also depressed, he also smiled and nodded. Since Luo Yan wanted him to be happy, he had to be happy!
Just as the young man was about to leave with Luo Yan, Ao Xiang suddenly said: "Senior, can you help Xiao Yu unseal her six senses?"
Ao Xiang's lover's six senses were still sealed. This method of sealing six senses was very troublesome to unseal, and even had some risks. If the young man could unseal Xiao Yu, it would save them a lot of trouble. What's more, Xiao Yu could wake up a day earlier.
The young man didn't refuse Ao Xiang's request. He didn't want to interfere with Luo Zheng's fate. He didn't care about the others, so he waved his hand and unsealed Xiao Yu.
Seeing this scene, Ao Xiang was overjoyed and almost knelt down to the young man.
The young man didn't say anything. He grabbed Luo Yan's hand and smiled at Luo Zheng, then said: "Work hard, little guy. Don't let me down."
A moment later, Luo Yan and the young man disappeared from the hall of the Void Spirit Hall. The two disappeared in such a strange way. There was no spatial tunnel, no tearing of space, and not even a trace of the fluctuation of the Law of Space. It was even more unclear how the young man did it.
After the young man left, Luo Zheng still stood in the hall for a long time. Not far away, Ao Xiang was talking to his lover with a face full of joy.
As a god-grade genius, Ao Xiang's fate was also full of misfortune. Back then, he almost died at the hands of Si Miaoling at the Martial Arts Conference. Now, his wish was fulfilled and he was able to save his lover.
He had god-grade talent. In the future, he would be put in an important position by the World Business Alliance. Now that the hidden danger of the Tian Xie Sect had been removed, with his talent, it was very likely that he would become an important member of the World Business Alliance.
On this trip to the Void Spirit Sect, he saved his sister, but he still had to face separation. On the way back, Luo Zheng's mood was abnormally low.
The matter of the Tian Xie Sect had come to an end.
The sect war that had ravaged more than half of the Central Domain had also come to an end.
However, the crisis was actually not over. The threat from the Yu the Great Divine Kingdom was like a sharp sword hanging over everyone's head. Now, this sharp sword could fall at any time.
Before going to the Void Spirit Sect, Shi Kefan knew that Luo Zheng missed his sister, but it was not convenient to discuss this matter.
After returning to the World Business Alliance, Shi Kefan discussed this matter with Luo Zheng.
At that time, according to Luo Zheng's tone, he seemed to have a way to deal with it. However, Shi Kefan could not figure out how Luo Zheng would deal with the Divine Sea Realm experts of the Yu the Great Divine Kingdom.
After learning about Shi Kefan's secret concerns, Luo Zheng smiled faintly and said, "Leader Shi, I naturally have a way to deal with this matter. The World Business Alliance's contacts are all over the Central Domain. Please help me look out for a miraculous person who can cure Ning Yudie."
Looking at Luo Zheng's confident expression, the three leaders were even more confused. Previously, he had spent so much effort fighting against Cui Xie. Now, he was going to face Divine Sea Realm experts, but he was still so confident. What was Luo Zheng up to?
Could it be that Luo Zheng still had the strength to fight against Divine Sea Realm experts?
That was impossible!
If that was the case, Luo Zheng would not have let Cui Xie use the Wind of Life and Death, which caused Ning Yudie to be in this state!
However, apart from this, what else could explain Luo Zheng's confidence?
The three leaders were puzzled. However, thinking that Luo Zheng had never let them down, maybe he really had a way?

Chapter 743
Even so, Luo Zheng felt that there was little hope.
The World Business Alliance had originally gathered many famous doctors from the Central Domain. The famous doctor named Xue Xi had already clearly analyzed Ning Yudie's problem. Ning Yudie wasn't injured. She didn't have any illness, but her life force was exhausted. She was like a withered flower. How could they bring it back to life?
Even if there was little hope, Luo Zheng didn't give up.
Ning Yudie was also struggling to hold on. Luo Zheng didn't have any reason to give up.
Many warriors from the Cloud Hall had already returned to the Cloud Hall through the transmission array. Ning Yudie couldn't preside over the affairs of the Cloud Hall. Many elders agreed to recommend Luo Zheng to take charge of the Cloud Hall.
This suggestion was actually very suitable. After Luo Zheng killed Cui Xie, he was now considered a person in the Central Domain. His strength was above all the warriors in the Central Domain. For the Cloud Hall to have such a person in charge, it was a great blessing for the Cloud Hall!
However, Luo Zheng refused.
He didn't want to take charge of the Cloud Hall. Now that the Tian Xie Sect had been destroyed, there were no forces in the Central Domain that could threaten the Cloud Hall and the World Business Alliance. Not to mention, he had other important matters to attend to.
However, it was impossible for the Cloud Hall to be without a leader. In the end, they needed a backbone.
In the end, Luo Zheng recommended Daoist Master Dameng.
On the day of the battle with Cui Xie, Daoist Master Dameng took a punch from Cui Xie. After recuperating for a few days, his injury was almost healed. Daoist Master Dameng had always been Ning Yudie's confidant. Therefore, he could rest assured that he would take charge of Luo Zheng.
After everyone from the Cloud Hall left, Luo Zheng stayed by Ning Yudie's side.
Ning Yudie was like a sleeping beauty. She was in a deep sleep and showed no signs of waking up.
Most of the time, Luo Zheng stayed by Ning Yudie's side to cultivate. The rest of the time, he just stared at Ning Yudie's face in a daze.
During this time, the World Business Alliance had already spread the news to the entire Central Domain!
The World Business Alliance's influence was very widespread. Even in remote areas, there were business alliance accounts. The entire Central Domain's true essence stone trade and circulation was completely controlled by them.
This news spread out, and within a few days, all the cities and teahouses in the Central Domain were discussing this matter …
"I heard that the reward is a low-grade saint artifact and a thousand top-grade True Origin Stones!"
"This reward is too terrifying. With these two items added together, I'm afraid it's even richer than an ordinary third-ranked faction!"
"The strength of a third-grade sect? Hehe, are you dreaming? Even a fourth-grade sect doesn't have a saint artifact. Spending so much just to save a woman? Is this woman worth so much? "
"What a joke, the Cloud Hall's Hall Master! A Life and Death Realm cultivator, this price shouldn't be too high, right? "
"My family has an ancestral pill recipe. It's said that it can bring the dead back to life and even extend one's lifespan by several decades. I want to try it …"
"Bah, don't take out your family's rubbish to show off, it's shameful! The World Business Alliance gathers alchemists from all over the world. Do you think your lousy alchemist will take a fancy to it? "
With the generous reward, all kinds of people flocked to the World Business Alliance. For a time, all kinds of "eccentrics" appeared and ran over to show off their treasures.
However, how many capable people were there in the World Business Alliance?
The vast majority of the so-called "eccentrics" were nothing more than charlatans. Even if they really had some miraculous pills and secret techniques, in the eyes of the World Business Alliance, they were nothing more than child's play. They were completely useless.
Of course, among these eccentrics, there was no lack of people with real ability. However, in the face of Ning Yudie's problem, they were still helpless and resentfully returned.
Half a month later, a beggar dressed in rags entered Heavenly Awakening City.
When the beggar entered the World Business Alliance's headquarters, he explained his purpose in coming to the entrance. He had a way to cure Ning Yudie.
During this time, all kinds of eccentrics came to give advice and offer treasures. They were like carps crossing the river, but not a single one was useful. When the people of the World Business Alliance saw this beggar, how could they show any kindness? Naturally, they waved their hands and drove him away.
However, this beggar didn't leave Heavenly Awakening City. Instead, he patiently inquired about Ning Yudie's residence.
This was really inquired by the beggar. The matter of Luo Zheng temporarily bringing Ning Yudie to Ning Yudie wasn't a secret. The World Business Alliance had specially allocated a palace to Luo Zheng.
After the beggar inquired about the location, he went to look for it. He found that there were guards at the entrance of the palace.
Helpless, he climbed over the wall and entered.
This beggar also had a cultivation base in the Xiantian realm. No matter how poor a cultivator was, it was impossible for them to be poor to this extent. Perhaps roaming around as a beggar was also a method of cultivation.
Not long after the beggar climbed into the palace, he was quickly discovered by other cultivators.
The palace was covered in runes, especially a kind of one-star rune. This rune didn't have any offensive properties, but when it was touched, it would erupt with a sharp cry. After the beggar trespassed, he naturally triggered these runes.
When the runes cried out, they immediately attracted a large number of guards!
Soon, the guards surrounded the beggar.
Luo Zheng was currently accompanying Ning Yudie in the palace. Ever since the World Business Alliance spread the news, there were probably tens of thousands of Eccentrics entering Heaven Awaken City. Although the World Business Alliance helped Luo Zheng screen and block 99% of the Eccentrics, there were still many people who entered the palace.
Every Eccentrics that entered the palace gave Luo Zheng a glimmer of hope, but in the end, it was an even greater disappointment.
However, Luo Zheng couldn't refuse. What if a blind cat bumped into a dead mouse? Luo Zheng didn't dare to take Ning Yudie's life as a joke. Even if the chances were small, he would still try.
"It's a pity that I disturbed your sweet dream," Luo Zheng looked at Ning Yudie with an apologetic smile. If he could really disturb Ning Yudie's sweet dream and wake her up, Luo Zheng was willing to give it a try. However, this was just Luo Zheng's own wishful thinking.
At this time, Luo Zheng suddenly heard a clamor coming from the palace.
"En? What happened? "Luo Zheng frowned slightly. He didn't know who dared to intrude here.
After thinking for a while, Luo Zheng's figure flashed and disappeared from the palace. Just as he left the palace, he heard a guard shout, "Intruding into the Heavenly Palace, you're courting death! Arrest this person and send him to prison to await trial! "
He saw a group of guards surrounding a beggar.
The beggar was only at the Xiantian realm. Suddenly being surrounded by so many guards, he stammered, "I, I came here to present a treasure to the Cloud Hall's Hall Master!"
"Present a treasure! You? What a joke! "A guard coldly said," Take him away! "
Seeing that there was no other way, the beggar took out a tattered sackcloth bag and took out an ancient jade. He immediately said, "This ancient jade was left behind by my family's Immortal. It can rejuvenate a person's vitality and restore a certain amount of life force. I heard that the Cloud Hall's Hall Master's life force has dried up. This ancient jade will definitely be useful!"
"Scram, scram, scram!" How could the guard listen to the beggar? Recently, there were too many people like this. Some "eccentrics" couldn't enter the World Business Alliance, so they came here to try their luck. Even these guards were tired of listening to this kind of talk.
However, most of the eccentrics lingered at the entrance of the Heavenly Palace. No one dared to sneak into the palace!
At first, Luo Zheng didn't care about this matter. He didn't intend to intervene. However, after seeing the ancient jade in the beggar's hand, Luo Zheng's heart suddenly jumped.
The beggar was right. Luo Zheng did feel the life force in the ancient jade. Although he was holding an ancient jade in his hand, when Luo Zheng scanned it with his perception, he found that the ancient jade was like a living thing!
"Take him away! Put him in prison! See if he has any other intentions! "At this time, many guards had already grabbed the beggar and were ready to take him out of the palace.
At this time, Luo Zheng appeared in front of everyone and lightly said, "Wait!"
Seeing that Luo Zheng was alarmed, the guard leader's face revealed an embarrassed expression. He carefully apologized, "I'm sorry, Lord Luo Zheng, we …"
"It's none of your business," Luo Zheng waved his hand, but his eyes didn't leave the ancient jade in the beggar's hand. He then said to the beggar, "Take your ancient jade and follow me!"

Chapter 744
Luo Zheng didn't know whether this ancient jade was useful or not. Desperate times call for desperate measures.
At least, Luo Zheng could feel the life force from this ancient jade. Whether it was useful to Ning Yudie or not, he had to try it to find out.
Ning Yudie's life force was originally very vigorous. However, because of the Wind of Life and Death, her life force was now in a state of near exhaustion. Luo Zheng didn't know how long the remaining life force could last Ning Yudie. If she could absorb the life force from this ancient jade, that would be the best news.
He led the beggar into the palace. Then, Luo Zheng stretched out his hand to the beggar and said coldly, "Give it to me!"
The beggar glanced at Luo Zheng and said, "If it's effective, the reward …"
"I won't give you less!" Luo Zheng replied indifferently.
Who knew that the beggar would shake his head and say, "I'm just a Xiantian warrior. Even if you give me the reward, I'm afraid I can't keep it. I want to join … the Cloud Hall or the World Business Alliance."
Luo Zheng coldly stared at the beggar and said, "If it's effective, I can help you. However, you have no room to bargain."
In the world of warriors, how could there be any consideration for feelings? Luo Zheng could directly snatch the jade from the beggar's hands. He could even kill the beggar to keep his mouth shut. Many warriors would do the same!
It was just that Luo Zheng wouldn't do that.
As a warrior from the Central Domain, the beggar certainly understood this. Since he chose to come and offer the treasure, he was prepared to gamble.
Therefore, he nodded and handed the ancient jade to Luo Zheng.
It was unknown whether this ancient jade was originally so warm or because of the life force, or because it had been on the beggar's body for a long time. When Luo Zheng touched it, it didn't have the cold feeling of ordinary ancient jade. Instead, it made him feel warm.
Luo Zheng didn't think too much about it. After picking up the ancient jade, he walked towards Ning Yudie. He was a bit hesitant. How could he inject the life force into Ning Yudie?
Seeing Luo Zheng's hesitation, the beggar said, "My ancestors said that this ancient jade can only be used if it is crushed. The life force inside will automatically dissipate."
More than one piece of ancient jade? Hearing the beggar's words, Luo Zheng's heart skipped a beat. He glanced at the beggar. This guy wasn't a fool. He probably didn't bring all the ancient jades.
"Let's see if it's useful." Thinking of this, Luo Zheng placed the ancient jade above Ning Yudie's head. Then, he gently pinched it with his fingers, causing a crack to appear on the surface of the ancient jade like a broken egg. At the same time, wisps of milky white light escaped from the crack.
"This is the life force!" Luo Zheng's heart skipped a beat. He didn't know that this life force could be absorbed by Ning Yudie.
This life force was indeed very strange. After it dissipated, it actually drilled into Luo Zheng's hand. Seeing this, Luo Zheng felt as if he had been bitten by a scorpion. He quickly retracted his hand.
However, he was afraid that he would absorb the life force.
After Luo Zheng removed his hand, the life force hovered above Ning Yudie's head for a while. Then, it drilled into Ning Yudie's body!
"It worked?" Luo Zheng's eyelids twitched a little.
Then, Luo Zheng felt that Ning Yudie's aura seemed to have strengthened a little! Although it was only a little, Luo Zheng saw great hope. It was indeed useful!
This ancient jade could really replenish Ning Yudie's life force!
Luo Zheng suddenly turned his head. He stared at the beggar and asked, "Tell me, how many pieces of ancient jade do you have?"
"Many, many …" The beggar seemed to be a little nervous.
He did indeed have a lot of ancient jades. To be precise, he had seven pieces. This beggar's surname was Li, and his name was Li Hetai. His Li Family was also a third-grade aristocratic family. However, martial aristocratic families would eventually decline one day. His Li Family had declined three hundred years ago. After that, each generation was worse than the previous one. In his generation, his Li Family had directly collapsed.
However, these seven pieces of ancient jade had been preserved until today. They were the most precious treasures of his Li Family.
This time, the World Business Alliance announced to the world that Li Hetai had heard about the Cloud Hall's Hall Master's matter. In the announcement, life force was clearly stated. Li Hetai wanted to try his luck.
After all, according to his family's ancestral records, this ancient jade did indeed contain life force.
It seemed that it was indeed useful!
However, Li Hetai wasn't a fool. If he took them all in one go, he wouldn't be able to do anything if the World Business Alliance snatched them away.
Therefore, when he entered Tianqi City, he found several places and buried the remaining seven pieces of ancient jade in seven different places. However, Li Hetai didn't expect that Luo Zheng would see through his plan at a glance. At this time, he was a little flustered.
Seeing Li Hetai's hesitation, Luo Zheng was even more certain. He said in a stern voice, "How many pieces of ancient jade are left? Where are they?"
"I, I …" Li Hetai originally wanted Luo Zheng to swear on his Martial Heart that he would fulfill his promise if he took out the seven pieces of ancient jade. However, under Luo Zheng's stern voice, his tongue couldn't help but tremble. How could he speak?
This time, Luo Zheng was even more anxious. "I will definitely fulfill my promise! In the future, you will be a member of my Cloud Hall. The Cloud Hall will protect you. There will be no changes! "
Luo Zheng didn't want to dawdle with this guy. What he cared about the most was how many pieces of ancient jade this guy still had.
"Seven pieces. There are still seven pieces of ancient jade. I buried them outside Tianqi City!" Li Hetai quickly said.
Seven pieces …
Luo Zheng's eyes revealed disappointment. The amount was too little.
There was only a trace of life force in one piece of ancient jade. Seven pieces wasn't much. It couldn't replace the life force that Ning Yudie had lost.
However, Luo Zheng naturally wanted as much as there was. He grabbed Li Hetai's shoulder and said in a cold voice, "Take me there!"
After grabbing this person and flying out of Tianqi City, it only took an incense stick's worth of time. Under Li Hetai's guidance, Luo Zheng quickly dug out the seven pieces of ancient jade one by one. Then, he took Li Hetai back to the Tianchen Hall.
Then, Luo Zheng crushed the pieces of ancient jade one by one. A trace of life force was injected into them by Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng nervously looked at Ning Yudie. This life force was too little. Could it awaken Ning Yudie?
After waiting for a long time, Ning Yudie still didn't move. She was still like a sleeping child, lying there motionless.
Luo Zheng stood there in a daze for a long time. Then, he faintly sighed, "It's too little …"
Hearing Luo Zheng's words, Li Hetai suddenly became nervous. He also knew that his ancient jade didn't have any effect. However, he still asked, "You, you will fulfill your promise, right?"
"The World Business Alliance will pay you a thousand top-grade true essence stones." Luo Zheng lightly said, "However, this thing of yours didn't awaken Little Die. I can't give you a low-grade sacred tool."
As soon as Luo Zheng finished speaking, Li Hetai's face immediately lit up with joy!
A thousand top-grade true essence stones. Even if his Li Family was at its peak, they still wouldn't be able to escape. For any third-grade family, this was a large sum. Besides the third-grade families in the Cloud Hall and the World Business Alliance, the third-grade families in the other cities didn't have much savings!
This sum of top-grade true essence stones was enough to make him a "nouveau riche" amongst martial artists! Li Hetai was only a Xiantian realm master. How could he dare to beg for a low-grade sacred tool? Even if that thing was given to him, it would only bring death upon him. Therefore, from the very beginning, Li Hetai had been aiming for a thousand top-grade true essence stones.
"However, you must tell me, where did this ancient jade come from?" Luo Zheng stared at Li Hetai and asked. This was the question that Luo Zheng was most concerned about.
During this period of time, Luo Zheng had seen many rare treasures of the Central Domain. However, this was the first time that Luo Zheng had seen this ancient jade that contained the power of life. If he could obtain some clues from Li Hetai, Luo Zheng would do everything in his power to find it.
"Speak." Luo Zheng stared at Li Hetai. "Give me some clues and the thousand top-grade true essence stones will be yours. You will also become an elite disciple of the Cloud Hall."
Now that the Tian Xie Sect and the Void Spirit Sect had been destroyed in the Central Domain, besides the World Business Alliance, the Cloud Hall was the largest sect in the Central Domain. Even when the Cloud Hall was only a fourth-grade force, all the martial artists in the Central Domain had scrambled to join, much less now.
Li Hetai was a Xiantian realm master with mediocre talent. In this lifetime, he had no hope of entering the Cloud Hall. However, now was his chance to change his destiny. How could Li Hetai hide anything?
