Chapter 745
Li Hetai hurriedly nodded and said, "This, this ancient jade was left behind by my ancestor."
This guy in front of him was talking nonsense. Luo Zheng was also impatient and impatient.
"So I don't know where this ancient jade came from …" Li Hetai continued.
Hearing this, Luo Zheng's face suddenly filled with killing intent. He stared at Li Hetai and said coldly, "You tricked me!"
Shrouded by Luo Zheng's killing intent, Li Hetai was dumbfounded. His face was ghastly pale. His legs gave way and he actually knelt on the ground. "I don't dare. Please listen to me first!"
"Speak!" Luo Zheng stared at him coldly.
"This ancient jade was indeed left behind by my ancestor. However, our Li Family of the Western Desert saved a person 700 years ago. That person … was an expert from the four Divine Kingdoms!" Li Hetai hurriedly said, "Our Li Family was able to develop thanks to this expert from the Divine Kingdom! Although our ancestors didn't tell us the origin of this ancient jade, I guess that this ancient jade should have been left behind by that expert from the Divine Kingdom! "
"From the Divine Kingdom!" Luo Zheng's eyebrows suddenly twitched.
Li Hetai's guess was actually based on some evidence. This ancient jade contained such a miraculous life force. It had never been heard of in the Central Domain.
If this ancient jade was produced in the Central Domain, with the ability of the Central Domain, it would have been found long ago.
No matter what, now that Luo Zheng had found a clue, the life force in this ancient jade was far from enough to awaken Ning Yudie. Therefore, Luo Zheng had to go to the Divine Kingdom!
While Luo Zheng was thinking carefully, he saw Li Hetai looking at him eagerly. He naturally knew what this person was thinking, so he said to Li Hetai, "Follow me."
Li Hetai was delighted. He followed Luo Zheng and the two went straight to the World Business Alliance.
The atmosphere in the Merchant Union was still tense. Although Luo Zheng had promised that he could deal with the Divine Sea Realm warriors of the Yu Dynasty, Shi Kefan and the others were still preparing for this.
It was just that no matter how much the World Business Alliance thought about it, they couldn't think of a way to deal with it.
The Central Domain was not on the same level as the four Kingdoms. So far, the Central Domain had never given birth to a real Holy Land, while the four Kingdoms had a long history. Even the number of Spirit Sea Realm warriors was not small. They should be stronger than the Seagod Continent.
Cui Xie, who was only at the Life and Death Realm, almost destroyed the World Business Alliance and the Cloud Hall. It was all thanks to Luo Zheng alone that the situation was reversed. How could a powerful warrior at the Divine Sea Realm be mentioned?
There was a huge disagreement within the World Business Alliance on this matter.
Shi Kefan was inclined to trust Luo Zheng.
Although this kind of trust was somewhat blind, Shi Kefan believed that since Luo Zheng had agreed, he naturally had his own way to deal with it.
On the other hand, Yan Yueshan, Mo Haishan, and some other elders were not willing to pin their hopes on Luo Zheng. Yan Yueshan believed that the target of the Yu Dynasty wasn't the Central Domain, but Luo Zheng!
The words of the Heartless Sweetheart's projection that day were clear. He seemed to know that Luo Zheng had a huge secret. It was this secret that attracted the warriors of the Yu Dynasty to the Central Domain.
Otherwise, why didn't the four Kingdoms show any interest in the "Tianbei Domain" after so many years?
Since it was Luo Zheng who caused this trouble, the World Business Alliance didn't need to share the burden with Luo Zheng.
It couldn't be said that Yan Yueshan and the others were burning bridges after crossing the river. People had the instinct to seek benefits and avoid harm. This time, Luo Zheng killed Cui Xie, which was indeed a great favor to the World Business Alliance. However, that was another matter. When they felt that they couldn't defeat such a powerful opponent, they would inevitably choose a way to avoid it.
While many people were arguing, someone in the Conference Hall announced, "Luo Zheng requests an audience!"
Shi Kefan nodded and said, "Quickly, let him in!"
Luo Zheng led Li Hetai into the Conference Hall. When Shi Kefan saw the beggar behind Luo Zheng, he was slightly surprised. "Brother Luo Zheng, this is …" Now that Luo Zheng's strength was not weaker than anyone in the Central Domain, the three Alliance Masters and Luo Zheng addressed each other as equals.
"One thousand top-grade True Meta Stones. This money will be paid by the World Business Alliance," Luo Zheng said with a faint smile.
Hearing this, the three fat men were shocked. The one thousand top-grade True Meta Stones were the reward for curing Ning Yudie's illness. Luo Zheng's words, could it be …
"He cured Ning Yudie?" Shi Kefan glanced at the ordinary-looking Li Hetai behind Luo Zheng and asked.
In the past few days, in addition to plotting against the Great Yu Divine Kingdom, the most important thing for the World Business Alliance was to help Luo Zheng screen various eccentric people.
Eccentric people emerged one after another. Although most of these people were swindlers, in order to cure Ning Yudie, they would not let go of even the slightest hope!
There were many martial artists in the General Commerce League, but there were only a few hundred of them who could discern the situation. Many of the elders were extremely busy as well.
Unexpectedly, they could not find a way to cure Ning Yudie, but Luo Zheng found it himself?
Luo Zheng shook his head and said with a smile, "Yes, and no."
"Brother Luo Zheng, what do you mean?" Yan Yueshan asked quickly.
When Luo Zheng saw the puzzled expression on everyone's faces, he told them about how Li Hetai had offered up the ancient jade. Only then did everyone's doubts were cleared.
"In that case, Brother Luo Zheng does not plan to go to the Divine Kingdom?" Shi Kefan guessed Luo Zheng's thoughts.
"Yes," Luo Zheng nodded without hesitation, "After dealing with those guys, I will go to the Divine Kingdom and look for this ancient jade!"
Deal with those guys …
Everyone's eyes flashed. This Luo Zheng was so arrogant! How could the Divine Sea Realm experts of the Great Yu Divine Kingdom be so easy to deal with?
But Luo Zheng had never let them down!
Why was Luo Zheng so confident?
Everyone looked at Luo Zheng in confusion.
Yan Yueshan had a hot temper and could not restrain himself, "Brother Luo Zheng! No matter what method you have, can you tell us first? "
"Yes, we are indeed curious. Does Brother Luo Zheng really have the strength to defeat a Divine Sea Realm expert?" Mo Haishan, who had always been a man of few words, asked.
Shi Kefan smiled faintly. In fact, his two brothers' doubts were reasonable. No matter how powerful Luo Zheng's trump card was, it was impossible for him to be a match for a Divine Sea Realm expert.
This was like a mystery that no one could figure out, and it was related to everyone's life and death. It would be unreasonable for Luo Zheng to keep silent!
Luo Zheng smiled faintly and did not keep them in suspense. He said directly, "It's very simple! I don't have the strength to fight against a Divine Sea Realm expert, but since the Divine Kingdom of the Great Yu Divine Kingdom has sent a Divine Sea Realm expert, then we can also fight with a Divine Sea Realm expert! "
"What?" The faces of everyone in the meeting hall changed abruptly.
A Divine Sea Realm expert? Since when did the Central Domain have such a powerful expert?
"Brother Luo Zheng, are you kidding?" an Elder of the World Business Alliance said, shaking his head. He did not believe Luo Zheng's words at all.
"I'm not kidding," Luo Zheng said calmly.
Everyone looked at each other and felt that Luo Zheng's logic was completely wrong. Yan Yueshan was the first to question, "Since Brother Luo Zheng has the help of a Divine Sea Realm expert, why didn't you see him when you faced Cui Xie?"
Luo Zheng was almost killed by Cui Xie in the battle with the Tian Xie Sect. Ning Yudie was still in a coma because of Cui Xie.
Luo Zheng's eyes flashed. He said loudly, "Because Cui Xie is someone I must kill! In order to stabilize my heart of martial arts, Cui Xie must be killed by me! This is my test, and also my first step to the whole world! "
Luo Zheng's words were powerful and resonant. An invisible majesty bloomed. This was Luo Zheng's domineering aura.
His domineering aura was completely different from that of Cui Xie. Cui Xie's domineering aura was very rude and unreasonable. He would not reason with anyone and would crush anyone who did not agree with him. However, Luo Zheng's domineering aura was very serious, to the extreme!
Who would have thought that Luo Zheng would choose to fight with Cui Xie with his life in order to stabilize his heart of martial arts? He obviously had a better choice, but he chose the most difficult path at the risk of his life and pressed forward with indomitable will?
Everyone was silent for a long time.

Chapter 746
Everyone looked at Luo Zheng.
Shi Kefan's question was also what everyone was most concerned about.
Since Luo Zheng said that we have the help of Divine Sea Realm experts, then where are they?
There were many hermits in the Central Domain.
There were some hermits who had never entered the world. The General Commerce Alliance knew quite a lot about some of these hermits. There were even two or three Life and Death Realm experts. It was just that these experts were not known to the public.
But there were absolutely no Divine Sea Realm experts!
Where was Luo Zheng going to find a Spirit Sea Realm warrior?
"Alliance Master Shi, I'll give you an answer in seven days. Please wait patiently," Luo Zheng said, cupping his hands.
Shi Kefan nodded. He trusted Luo Zheng the most. Besides, Luo Zheng had no reason to lie, because it wouldn't do him any good. Moreover, those experts of Yu the Great Divine Country were targeting Luo Zheng!
As for the others, they still didn't believe it. But seven days would pass quickly. At that time, they would be able to prove whether Luo Zheng was telling the truth or not!
Li Hetai followed Luo Zheng to get his reward. But after he followed Luo Zheng, he heard them talking about Divine Sea Realm experts. He suddenly felt dizzy.
But in this Conference Hall, almost all the people were False Tribulation Realm experts. He was just a Xiantian Realm expert. He would tremble in front of any of them. How could he interrupt?
Seeing that everyone had finished their discussion, he said in a low voice, "Well, my reward …"
Shi Kefan coldly glanced at Li Hetai. Then, he ordered someone to take Li Hetai away to get the top-grade True Meta Stones.
Li Hetai didn't dare to ask Luo Zheng about joining the Cloud Hall. But he was smart enough to understand the situation. Since Luo Zheng had agreed, he would go to the Cloud Hall and say Luo Zheng's name. The Cloud Hall wouldn't refuse him.
After Luo Zheng returned to the Heavenly Palace, he summoned Xun.
Smoky had been lurking in Luo Zheng's body, so she naturally understood why Luo Zheng summoned her.
When he had left the Sea God Continent, Liu Yu had connected a voice transmission talisman to Xun's king statue.
Xun's soul was too weak. He was unable to directly communicate with the Demon Night Clan's Sacred Land through the King Statue like the other Kings.
However, with the help of the special Sound Transmission Talisman and the King Statue, it was possible!
After Luo Zheng burned the Voice-transmitting Rune, Xun also closed her eyes. Her fine eyelashes quivered slightly as she soon entered a certain state …
Hundreds of thousands of miles away …
Crossing the boundless sea, the Sea God Continent, the Heavenly Feather Holy Grounds.
The Yao Ye clansmen of the Heavenly Feather Holy Land would pay their respects to the statue of the supreme king every day. This was something that they couldn't do in their entire lives.
To them, the King Statue was like a spiritual totem.
Liu Yu led the Yao Ye clansmen to kneel in front of the King Statue. Their faces were filled with sincerity. This kind of worship ceremony would last for the time it took three incense sticks to burn.
Today, during the worship ceremony, a voice transmission talisman suddenly burned on the third King Statue. This was the statue that controlled slaughter. Soon after, Xun's deep voice faintly rang out.
"Liu Yu," Xun said calmly. Although Xun appeared to be very clever and quick-witted in front of Luo Zheng, Xun was a supreme existence in front of the Yao Ye clansmen!
When they heard Xun's voice, all of the Yao Ye clansmen were shocked. Joy appeared on their faces.
"King!"
Liu Yu knelt on one knee and called out excitedly.
"I need all of you," Xun said calmly.
"Yes, King. Liu Yu will arrive today!" Liu Yu didn't hesitate at all. He didn't even think about it. All of the Yao Ye clansmen obeyed the King unconditionally.
However, Xun said coldly, "If you leave, what will happen to the Heavenly Feather Holy Land? What will happen to the Purple Heart Holy Land? "
The situation on the Sea God's Continent wasn't as stable as expected. If Liu Yu left the Sea God's Continent, the Twin Devil Holy Land would most likely become restless. That would be the best time for them to counterattack.
However, the Yao Ye clansmen viewed serving the King as the highest honor. In comparison, their Holy Land wasn't ranked first.
Xun naturally knew what Liu Yu was thinking. As such, she said calmly, "There's no need for you to come. Liu Yu, you have to guard the Heavenly Feather Holy Land. The Heavenly Feather Holy Land and the Purple Heart Holy Land can each send a few Divine Sea Realm experts."
A trace of disappointment appeared in Liu Yu's eyes.
However, how could he disobey the King's orders? He could only acquiesce.
Then, Xun gave instructions on how to get to Heavenly Awakening City through the teleportation formation.
After that, Liu Yu suddenly stood up. With his back to the three King Statues, he glanced at the Yao Ye clansmen and said calmly, "Who is willing to use the teleportation formation to go to the Central Domain and serve the King?"
Serving Xun was the highest honor for Liu Yu. How could it not be the same for the other Yao Ye clansmen?
"Liu Yu! I'm willing to go! "The one who spoke was another Divine Sea Realm expert of the Yao Ye clan.
"I'm willing as well," another woman in black said.
"Liu Yu, give me a chance!"
"Liu Yu …"
The Heavenly Feather Holy Land had the most Divine Sea Realm experts out of all the Holy Lands on the Sea God's Continent.
For example, the Purple Heart Holy Land only had three Divine Sea Realm experts. The Heavenly Feather Holy Land had seven, and Liu Yu was one of the top Divine Sea Realm experts!
Now that Liu Yu had spoken, all of the Divine Sea Realm experts were willing to go!
Apart from the six Divine Sea Realm experts, many Life and Death Realm experts also wanted to go …
Life and Death Realm experts were the top experts in the Central Domain. However, there were many of them in the Sea God's Continent.
Liu Yu thought for a moment. Xun didn't want Liu Yu to leave the Heavenly Feather Holy Land, but she didn't limit the number of people. Since the Battle-Saints of the Holy Land were willing to go, she had no reason to stop them.
Liu Yu nodded. "Apart from you, the humans of the Purple Heart Holy Land will go as well …"
The Holy Land's efficiency was extremely high. In just a day's time, a huge group of people had set off for the Heavenly Eagle Grassland!
The Twin Devil Holy Land naturally received news of the Heavenly Feather Holy Land and Purple Heart Holy Land's movements. However, with Liu Yu present, the Twin Devil Holy Land didn't dare to act rashly. However, once Liu Yu left, things would be different. Although the Twin Devil Holy Land couldn't fight against the Battle-Saints who broke through in the Sin Tower, they could directly attack the Holy Land. Those old monsters of the Demon Race wouldn't stay put.
This was also why Xun refused Liu Yu.
Three days later, in the skies above the Chaotic Astral Sea to the east of the Central Domain, a massive spatial storm suddenly appeared!
Following this, a massive transmission array appeared in the skies.
Pipa pipa …
Such a massive spatial storm was enough to crush an ordinary martial artist to pieces!
However, a group of over a hundred people appeared in the middle of this spatial storm. These people seemed to completely disregard the spatial storm, as if they had forcefully rushed out!
This group of over a hundred people was led by nine Divine Sea Realm powerhouses. The rest were all Battle-Saints.
"This is the Central Domain? Luo Zheng's hometown? "Situ Haotian looked down at the distant coastline of the continent, his heart filled with emotion.
Before Luo Zheng came to the Sea God Continent, the humans of the Purple Heart Holy Land were like orphans. Who would have thought that the continent Luo Zheng came from would be completely dominated by humans?
"Let's go." A Divine Sea Realm powerhouse from the Fiend Night Clan glanced over indifferently. She didn't feel as emotional as Situ Haotian. The only thing that drove the Fiend Night Clan was Xun, their king.
Thus, these hundreds of cultivators rushed forward in a mighty manner.
"Sect, Sect Master, something bad has happened!" In the easternmost part of the Central Domain, in the Fish Dragon Sect, a sharp cry suddenly rang out.
"What are you panicking for?" Sect Master Xiao's face darkened. He sternly shouted at the person who had shouted, "We cultivate the heart of martial arts. The most important thing is our temperament. You will never be able to handle anything if you panic like this!"
This Sect Master Xiao was quite shrewd!

Chapter 747
"But, but …" The messenger stammered.
"You can't even speak properly. What a piece of trash!" The messenger was one of Sect Master Xiao's favored subordinates. He was usually calm, but today, he was panicking. Sect Master Xiao was furious and stood up to reprimand him.
However, Sect Master Xiao knew that something was wrong. He asked, "What happened?"
"Outside, there are many people outside. Many martial artists," the man stammered.
"Many warriors? How strong are they? "Sect Master Xiao asked again.
"I don't know. Sect Master, you should go out personally …" The messenger suggested.
Sect Master Xiao coldly snorted. He was even more confused. What kind of warriors could scare him like this?
Sect Master Xiao then walked out of his residence and looked up. He saw over a hundred people floating in the sky above him …
"What level of strength …" Sect Master Xiao checked for a moment and inhaled a breath of cold air. This breath of cold air caused him to cough violently.
"Cough, cough, cough …"
Sect Master Xiao was a middle stage False Tribulation Realm warrior, but when he saw the scene in the sky, he couldn't help coughing. His body didn't seem to listen to him.
"Life and Death Realm warriors …"
"Life and Death Realm warriors …"
"There are hundreds of Life and Death Realm warriors, right?"
"No, the ones in the front aren't Life and Death Realm warriors. They are much stronger than Life and Death Realm warriors! Could they be the legendary Divine Sea Realm warriors? " After Sect Master Xiao finally finished coughing, his eyes widened like copper bells. His gaze was filled with disbelief.
Oh my god …
After his mind went blank for a moment, Sect Master Xiao's brain began to work quickly.
These warriors definitely didn't come from the Central Domain. There was only one possibility. They came from the four Divine Nations on the other side of the Chaotic Astral Ocean.
The news of the Tian Xie Sect joining forces with the World Business Alliance and the Cloud Hall in Tian Qi City had spread throughout the Central Domain.
The news of Yu Da Divine Nation invading the Central Domain had also spread.
However, Sect Master Xiao wasn't in a hurry. He was just a third-grade sect. Even if the Da Yu Divine Nation really occupied the Central Domain, he could just choose to submit.
When the Tian Xie Sect was wreaking havoc, the Fish Dragon Sect didn't have the slightest intention of resisting. Instead, they became a member of the Tian Xie Sect. Even he himself said that he wanted to join the Kingdom of Evil Deities and become a member of the Divine Kingdom.
When the Tian Xie Sect attacked Tian Qi City, Sect Master Xiao used sect affairs as an excuse and did not participate.
Cui Xie did not take his third-grade force seriously at all.
After the Tian Xie Sect was destroyed, the Fish Dragon Sect was not affected at all.
It was because of this that the Fish Dragon Sect was safe and sound. Therefore, this time, when the Yu Dynasty attacked, he did not panic.
However, he was still surprised by the strength of the four Divine Kingdoms. How could the Yu the Great Divine Kingdom send out more than 10 Divine Sea Realm warriors to battle so casually? How terrifying was the background of the four Divine Kingdoms?
Luo Zheng was probably going to be miserable. Last time, the World Business Alliance and the Cloud Hall escaped a disaster. This time, they might not be able to escape!
Sect Master Xiao had already determined their fate.
In the sky, hundreds of people stood there. Situ Haotian looked at the many humans below and smiled faintly. "Let me go and ask for directions! After all, they are of the same race as us! "
After that, Situ Haotian flew towards the bottom of the Fish Dragon Sect.
The Fish Dragon Sect also had its own Sect Protection Array. However, in the face of a Divine Sea Realm warrior, how could they resist?
Situ Haotian flew towards Sect Master Xiao casually and then asked lightly, "You, tell me, which direction is Tian Qi City?"
Sect Master Xiao's face was immediately filled with smiles. Sure enough, his guess was right. These fellows came from the four Divine Kingdoms and were heading towards Tian Qi City. He hurriedly said, "Tian Qi City is southwest!"
After he finished speaking, Sect Master Xiao's hand moved as fast as lightning as he took out a map from his spatial ring. This map was Sect Master Xiao's personal map. The map was very detailed, and the names of various places were even drawn with golden lines.
"Sir, this is a map of my Central Domain. If you follow the markings, you will be able to find Tian Qi City!" Sect Master Xiao said with a smile.
"Mm." How could Situ Haotian be polite to a War Reverend? Moreover, this fellow in front of him was only a fifth-order War Reverend. How could he enter his eyes? After putting away the map, Situ Haotian didn't even bother to say another word. He casually threw down an object and flew towards the sky.
The hundreds of people immediately turned into streaks of light and flew towards the southwest.
After the hundreds of people left, Sect Master Xiao immediately felt much more relaxed. The aura of the hundreds of experts was like a huge mountain, almost causing Sect Master Xiao to be unable to breathe.
After making sure that they were far away, Sect Master Xiao finally had the mood to look at the object. When he saw the ordinary-looking dagger, his eyes began to shine with a fierce light. Even his voice began to tremble as he said, "This, this, this … this is a Saint, Saint artifact!"
Hearing the Sect Master speak like this, the martial artist that informed him revealed an indignant expression. The Sect Master had even lectured him before. Didn't one have to pay attention to one's temperament when cultivating martial arts? If the Sect Master was so excited, how good could his temperament be …
However, he didn't dare to voice this thought out loud. He could only stare blankly at the ecstatic Sect Master.
Among these martial artists, the weakest was a Life and Death powerhouse. With over a hundred people flying together, just how fast was their speed?
However, with over a hundred streaks of light moving at high speeds, it caused a great stir in the Central Domain. There were even some martial artists that thought that a heavenly phenomenon was descending from the heavens and that some strange treasure was being born.
A day later, the hundreds of people finally arrived at Apocalypse City.
On top of Apocalypse City's huge city walls, countless craftsmen were working day and night to repair them. In the previous battle, the city walls had been completely destroyed. Repairing them like this was a huge project.
"Look, what's that!" On top of the city walls, a craftsman suddenly discovered that over a hundred streaks of light were shooting towards them from the horizon. At a glance, it was as if over a hundred meteors were falling towards them.
Hearing the craftsman's words, many martial artists raised their heads and looked towards the horizon.
When a city guard saw this scene, his expression changed. His figure flickered and he rushed towards the city gate tower. There was a large bell on top of the city gate tower. After the guard went up, he began to rapidly ring the bell!
This large bell was used as an alarm. Under the ringing of the bell, the other bells also began to ring. As the bells passed down one after another, the entire Apocalypse City was filled with alarm bells.
"What's going on?"
"It can't be those powerhouses from Scandinavia, right? So fast! "
"How should my Apocalypse City deal with them this time?"
All the martial artists in Apocalypse City were in a panic.
A Divine Core warrior stood on top of the city wall and looked at those martial artists in the sky. In the end, he murmured, "Over a hundred Life and Death warriors, what do we have to deal with them?! How are we going to deal with them?!"
Right when the alarm bells were ringing, the three Alliance Chiefs of the World Business Alliance, and over twenty Void Tribulation Elders flew out from Apocalypse City. They gathered together and stared at those mighty warriors in the distance. Their expressions were extremely unsightly.
Although Luo Zheng did say that he could ask for help from a Divine Sea warrior, it was still a fact.
But, Shi Kefan and the others had a preconceived notion, and they felt that it would be great if Luo Zheng could ask one or two Divine Sea warriors to help him.
After all, how much would it cost to ask for help from a Divine Sea warrior?
No matter what, just like what Luo Zheng had said, with a Divine Sea warrior in Apocalypse City, they would have more hope in dealing with Yu Da Divine Nation.
After all, Yu Da Divine Nation wouldn't send too many Divine Sea warriors to the Central Domain. Although the overall strength of the Divine Nation was much stronger than the Central Domain, Divine Sea warriors weren't as common as cabbages. They wouldn't send all their mighty warriors just to deal with a tiny Central Domain.
But, the Divine Sea warrior that Luo Zheng mentioned didn't come, but the mighty warriors from the Divine Nation did.
"Nine Divine Sea warriors, ninety Life and Death warriors, hehe …" Shi Kefan said with a bitter expression, "It doesn't matter whether the reinforcements Luo Zheng is waiting for are here or not …"
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 748
In the Central Domain, any one of these super warriors would be an overlord!
Life and Death Realm warriors were equivalent to Battle-Saints on the Deep Sea Continent.
For example, Ning Yudie had just broken through the Life and Death Realm, so she was equivalent to a Battle-Saint.
Regardless of whether they were Life and Death Realm warriors or Battle-Saints, they all had to go through the Life and Death Realm, which was also known as the minor Heavenly Tribulation.
The minor Heavenly Tribulation was not only a life and death tribulation for warriors, but also a test.
After entering the Life and Death Realm, one could reach the Divine Sea Realm after going through several minor Heavenly Tribulations. The number of tribulations varied for every warrior, but to reach the Divine Sea Realm, one had to go through at least five or six minor Heavenly Tribulations.
Moreover, the higher the talent of a warrior, the more minor Heavenly Tribulations he had to go through, and the more powerful he would be after entering the Divine Sea Realm!
Therefore, the number of minor Heavenly Tribulations was also a measure of a warrior's future.
There were many Battle-Saints on the Sea God Continent, and this group of Battle-Saints was the elite among them. They were only one step away from reaching the Divine Sea Realm! Therefore, they were basically sixth or even seventh level Battle-Saints.
These Battle-Saints had already gone through six or even seven Life and Death Realms.
Any one of them was much more powerful than Cui Xie, who had gone through four Life and Death Realms!
In other words, any one of them could sweep through the Central Domain!
However, there were more than a hundred of such terrifying existences!
How frightened were the warriors of the World Business Alliance when they saw this scene?
Shi Kefan, Yan Yueshan, and Mo Haishan suddenly felt their scalps go numb and their backs turn cold. Last time, when they faced the Tian Xie Sect, they still had the courage to fight, but now, facing these super warriors, they didn't even have the courage to resist!
The difference in strength was too great, and any resistance was futile.
Even so, Shi Kefan still braced himself and flew forward, bowing to the many super warriors. "I am the leader of the World Business Alliance. Greetings to all the warriors from the Divine Nation!"
The leader, Situ Haotian, glanced at the fat man in front of him and asked, "Where is Luo Zheng?"
Although Shi Kefan's strength was good, that was only in comparison to the powerhouses of the Central Domain. In Situ Haotian's eyes, he was nothing more than a peak War Reverend. For a martial artist like Shi Kefan, the only use he had on the Sea God Continent was to fight for a seat at the top of the Sin Tower.
Furthermore, Situ Haotian silently evaluated Shi Kefan's strength in his heart. Even among the Peak War Reverends, this person's strength was considered to be at the bottom. He was probably inferior to Zhao Fenqin and Zhou Zhuohe, much less the Peak War Reverends of the Demon Night Clan and the Demon Clan.
Hearing this mighty figure in the Spirit Sea Realm ask about Luo Zheng, Shi Kefan hesitated. To be honest, as the leader of the Business Alliance, he should give Luo Zheng away without any hesitation. For the survival of the World Business Alliance, this was the right thing for him to do. He should draw a clear line between the Business Alliance and Luo Zheng.
These powerhouses of the Divine Kingdom shouldn't be interested in a mere Business Alliance.
However, Luo Zheng had made great contributions to the World Business Alliance. Although Shi Kefan flaunted himself as a businessman, it was difficult for him to sell out Luo Zheng like this.
However, as the leader of the Business Alliance, he couldn't let down the World Business Alliance!
"Big Brother!"
Yan Yueshan roared at Shi Kefan.
How could they not see why Shi Kefan was hesitating?
In Yan Yueshan's opinion, these powerhouses weren't people they could resist. Even if they wanted to protect Luo Zheng, it would be like throwing an egg against a rock!
Shi Kefan sighed inwardly. He thought, "Forget it, forget it. Luo Zheng probably won't blame me. Besides, Luo Zheng was originally in Tianqi City. Even if he wanted to escape now, it would be impossible …"
However, before Shi Kefan could speak, a beam of light shot out from Tianqi City. This newcomer was Luo Zheng.
The many Elders of the World Business Alliance and the three fat Alliance Masters turned their heads to look. As they saw Luo Zheng's calm and composed appearance, they all revealed astonished expressions.
How could this fellow be so indifferent? It was as if he didn't place these hundreds of Divine Kingdom powerhouses in his eyes at all!
Did Luo Zheng really have a way to deal with them?
It wasn't just the upper echelons of the World Business Alliance that were astonished. In Tianqi City, countless Business Alliance Deacons and martial artists were secretly observing the situation in the sky. Every martial artist's face was covered in a layer of frost. Seeing this scene, even a blind man would know that Tianqi City was in grave danger!
Under the watchful eyes of everyone present, Luo Zheng floated in front of Situ Haotian. He faintly smiled and said, "The last time we parted at the Heavenly Eagle Prairie, I thought that it would take forever, but I never imagined that I would be able to summon all of you to help me. Luo Zheng is deeply ashamed!"
Situ Haotian faintly smiled. "Young Hero Luo Zheng's words are too exaggerated. The environment that you have created for our Human Race will likely be blessed for generations to come. Such a trivial matter is not worth mentioning."
Luo Zheng had single-handedly reversed the situation in the Tower of Sin. This had a profound and profound impact on the Purple Heart Holy Land. As long as the Human Race was given enough time, they would surely become one of the greatest clans of the Sea God Continent in the future. How could the Purple Heart Holy Land not be grateful to Luo Zheng?
It was also for this reason that Situ Haotian had personally come here.
Luo Zheng faintly smiled. Specks of red light scattered from his body. These specks of light intertwined and condensed into a slender figure. It was Luo Zheng's Sword Spirit, Xun.
The moment Xun appeared, the six Divine Sea Realm experts and dozens of Life and Death Realm experts of the Fiend Night Clan all knelt on one knee. They faced Xun and said in unison, "Greetings, King!"
Dozens of Life and Death Realm experts and several Divine Sea Realm experts were respectfully paying their respects … to Luo Zheng's Sword Spirit?
In Heavenly Awakening City, Shi Kefan, Yan Yueshan, Mo Haishan, many Elders, and countless Merchant Union cultivators were all dumbfounded. For a moment, they couldn't wrap their heads around what was happening …
This scene had completely overturned their worldview …
What the hell is going on?
These people didn't seem to be experts of the Great Yu Divine Kingdom.
Were these people the reinforcements that Luo Zheng had mentioned?
It was fine if they were reinforcements, but why were they so respectful to Luo Zheng's Sword Spirit? Was there a need to be so respectful?
There was no Divine Kingdom in the Central Domain. As for the cultivators, strength was the only thing that mattered.
Even Cui Xie didn't have the qualifications to make others kneel and pay respect to him.
After all, they were all cultivators. No one was willing to submit to another. Cui Xie was indeed powerful, but he wasn't powerful to that extent!
Making a group of False Tribulation Realm cultivators kneel and pay respect to Cui Xie? No way, not to mention the other Life and Death Realm experts …
Only after establishing a Divine Kingdom and establishing a monarch system would it be possible to make all the cultivators in the Central Domain submit to him. However, this would take a long time to consolidate the imperial power and make all the cultivators in the Central Domain recognize him as their monarch. He was someone who was qualified to be called an emperor …
Even so, a truly powerful expert still wouldn't be so courteous to you.
However, these Divine Ocean Realm experts were truly kneeling in front of Xun. The sincerity on their faces came from the bottom of their hearts. It wasn't an act!
Shi Kefan was speechless. If he could speak, he really wanted to shout, "Oh heavens, oh heavens, can someone tell me what is going on?"
Even if they racked their brains, they wouldn't be able to figure out that the Sword Spirit beside Luo Zheng was the king of the Demon Night Clan. He was the spiritual symbol of the Demon Night Clan. In the upper world, not to mention the Divine Ocean Realm experts, even the Realm Lords who were much stronger than Xun had to pay respect to him. In their eyes, this was something that couldn't be more normal!
Xun glanced at the Spirit Sea Realm warriors and said softly, "No need for formalities."
Only then did the Yao Ye clan experts straighten their backs and respectfully retreat to the side.
Shi Kefan hesitantly floated over to Luo Zheng's side and asked in a trembling voice, "Brother Luo Zheng, what, what is going on?"
Situ Haotian had addressed Luo Zheng as Young Hero Luo Zheng. Luo Zheng and Situ Haotian had addressed each other as equals. Shi Kefan suddenly felt that calling him "Brother Luo Zheng" was a bit overboard.
However, Luo Zheng was only 20 years old. Was Shi Kefan supposed to call him "Lord Luo Zheng"? That didn't seem right. Even Cui Xie wasn't worthy of Shi Kefan calling him that!
Shi Kefan was really conflicted.

Chapter 749
How could Luo Zheng know that Shi Kefan was so entangled in his heart?
However, when he saw Shi Kefan's stuttering appearance, Luo Zheng knew that Shi Kefan was shocked by Situ Haotian and the others.
So, Luo Zheng comforted him, "Alliance Master Shi, don't worry. These people are friends that we have made in the past two years."
"Friends …"
Shi Kefan was indeed not worried. Not only was he not worried, but he was also overjoyed. With such a group of people, what was the Great Yu Divine Country?
But where had Luo Zheng been in the past two years? How could he have made such a group of "friends"?
How much face did he have to have to invite such a group of friends?
Yan Yueshan had always been impatient. He couldn't wait to grab Luo Zheng and ask Luo Zheng to tell him the whole story. However, with over a hundred powerhouses present, how could Yan Yueshan dare to speak?
As for Mo Haishan, he was a man of few words. His white and chubby face didn't have much expression. Now, he looked the most normal, but the degree of shock in his heart was no less than anyone else's.
"Friends, good, good friends!" Shi Kefan froze for a moment. Then, with a smile on his face, he said, "Since you are Luo Zheng's friends, the World Business Alliance will host a banquet to entertain you! Quickly, please enter Heavenly Awakening City! "
At this time, how could Shi Kefan dare to show the slightest neglect?
However, when Shi Kefan spoke, Situ Haotian and the others didn't have any expression on their faces. They didn't even smile politely.
Shi Kefan might be a famous figure in the Central Territory, and as the Alliance Master of the Merchant Union, his influence wasn't small. However, in the eyes of Situ Haotian, Shi Kefan was nothing more than a minor character that wasn't worth mentioning.
These hundreds of powerhouses of the Sea God Continent only wanted to give face to Luo Zheng and Xun.
Seeing their indifferent appearance, Shi Kefan scratched his head awkwardly. He didn't know what to say. He could only look at Luo Zheng with pleading eyes.
Luo Zheng didn't want to embarrass Shi Kefan too much, so he faintly smiled and said to Situ Haotian, "Senior Haotian, since you've come from afar, I, Luo Zheng, naturally have to entertain you well. Please come into the city for a chat."
Situ Haotian nodded. Many powerhouses followed Luo Zheng and flew towards Heavenly Awakening City.
In a dazzling hall in Heavenly Awakening City, over a hundred wide tables were arranged in a row. All kinds of beautiful women were shuttling back and forth, and exquisite dishes were hurriedly served.
How could the General Commerce League treat these experts with the slightest neglect? It could be said that this was the highest standard of reception in the history of the business league!
All kinds of exquisite food were served up like flowing water.
Most of these ingredients were treasures from the Merchant Union's warehouse. Some of them were the most precious parts of Level Seven and even Level Eight demonic beasts.
Heavenly Awakening City was filled with talented people. Not only were there alchemists and weapon refiners, but even the chefs were the best in the entire Central Domain. The quality of the dishes they produced was naturally top-notch.
However, a scene that made Shi Kefan and the others' jaws drop suddenly appeared.
Many of the Central Domain's martial artists didn't expect that these powerhouses' table manners would be so unsightly. On one side, they were praising all kinds of dishes, while on the other, they were gulping down huge amounts of food.
Shi Kefan didn't feel distressed. The World Business Alliance's wealth far surpassed any sect. How could they eat so much?
However, Shi Kefan could never have imagined that these powerful warriors of the Life and Death Realm and Spirit Sea Realm would be like farmers entering a city when they saw these dishes. It was as if they had never tasted such delicious food in their lives, which indeed confused them.
While Shi Kefan couldn't figure it out, Luo Zheng was very clear about the reason.
Situ Haotian looked down on the strength of the Central Domain's martial artists. In the Central Domain, it was already extremely difficult for a Life and Death powerhouse to be born. But in the Sea God Continent, Life and Death powerhouses were only Battle Saints. They couldn't even stand on their own. The two continents were simply not on the same level of strength.
However, the entire Sea God Continent had always been in chaos. More than a dozen Holy Lands attacked each other, and all kinds of conflicts erupted around the Tower of Sin. Every race faced the crisis of survival.
In such an environment, whether it was the Purple Heart Holy Land or other Holy Lands, the first priority was to develop martial skills and cultivate powerhouses. How could there be a culture of eating and drinking? For martial artists, as long as the food wasn't disgusting, it was fine. Even if it was disgusting, they had to force it down in order to survive.
In fact, apart from having many powerhouses, the Sea God Continent was far inferior to the Central Domain in other aspects, such as literature, music, and so on. Their first priority was survival and strength!
Compared to the Central Domain, the Sea God Continent was much luckier. The other races had no space to develop. After the Human Race occupied the Central Domain, at least there was no survival crisis. If there was, it would only be disputes between humans. Therefore, a rich and colorful culture could be born in the long river of history.
As the saying went, if one ate someone's food, one's mouth would be soft. As the powerhouses of the Sea God Continent ate happily, the atmosphere also became lively. Situ Haotian and Shi Kefan chatted and laughed, learning about the situation and development of the Human Race in the Central Domain.
Shi Kefan was also a good talker. Naturally, he answered all of Situ Haotian's questions.
After eating and drinking, another few hundred beauties wearing long dresses entered the hall. Along with the sounds of various instruments, the group of beauties also began to dance gracefully …
On this night, the people of the Commercial Union were extremely busy. In order to entertain these powerhouses, the Commercial Union had to exert all of their strength.
Although Luo Zheng sat in the seat of honor, he still had a smile on his face. His mood wasn't too high.
The one he was most concerned about was Ning Yudie. Although there were maids taking care of her, she was still lying quietly on the bed, unable to move.
After dealing with the powerhouses of the Great Yu Divine Kingdom, he had to personally go to the four Divine Kingdoms to investigate the origin of the ancient jade. But before that, Luo Zheng first asked Situ Haotian and the others to help him. He wanted to see if they had a way to treat Ning Yudie.
A night of music and music.
For people with their cultivation, even if they didn't sleep for several months, their spirit would still be full.
In the Heavenly Palace Hall, after Situ Haotian reached out to take Ning Yudie's pulse, he shook his head and came to a similar conclusion. "This woman isn't seriously ill. It's just that her life force has been completely exhausted. If we want to treat her, we must find something to replenish her life force."
"Does such a thing exist on the Sea God Continent?" Luo Zheng eagerly asked.
Situ Haotian shook his head, "I haven't seen it on the continent, so I don't dare to say whether it exists in the Mysterious Underworld Cave. However, if such a thing exists in the Mysterious Underworld Cave in the future, my Purple Heart Holy Land will send someone to deliver it."
Luo Zheng reluctantly smiled. Although he believed that Situ Haotian would do as he said, Luo Zheng didn't know whether or not the Mysterious Underworld Cave really had such a treasure. He couldn't wait for too long.
After all, a person's life force was constantly being consumed. Although he had used Li Hetai's ancient jade to replenish Ning Yudie's life force, it was just a drop in the bucket. Who knew how long Ning Yudie could last?
It seemed that Luo Zheng still had to go to the four Divine Kingdoms to find clues about the ancient jade.
After these hundreds of powerhouses settled down in Tianqi City, Luo Zheng eagerly looked forward to the arrival of the Great Yu Divine Kingdom's Master Heartless in the Central Domain.
Only after solving this problem could Luo Zheng set off.
But after waiting for nearly a month, there was still no trace of the Master Heartless. Luo Zheng became somewhat anxious. He even regretted smashing the Bagua Mirror. Otherwise, he could have used the Bagua Mirror to provoke the Master Heartless, allowing him to cross the Chaotic Astral Ocean faster.
Luo Zheng waited anxiously, but Situ Haotian and the others weren't.
The Central Domain might not be suitable for cultivation, but it was very suitable for living. During their time in Tianqi City, their days were very leisurely. They also fully appreciated the Central Domain's delicious food and customs. After all, they were all from the Human Race, but their tastes and aesthetics were basically the same.
As for the powerhouses of the Demon Night Clan, they were even less anxious. Being able to accompany their King was their greatest honor. They were afraid that the smell would drive them away!
Just after Situ Haotian led the hundreds of powerhouses to the Central Domain 33 Heavens …
In the skies above the Chaotic Astral Ocean, six streaks of light were rapidly advancing.
Crossing the Chaotic Astral Ocean wasn't an easy task. Even after the Astral Ocean Storm stopped, they still had to face the endless Vicious Beasts of the Chaotic Astral Ocean.
Among them, there were some level 10 and even level 11 Vicious Beasts whose strength was comparable to Divine Sea Realm powerhouses!

Chapter 750
Among the six escape lights, two were in the Divine Sea Realm, while the other four were in the Life and Death Realm.
Just as the warriors in the Central Domain had guessed, the four Divine Kingdoms did not take the Central Domain seriously.
In the eyes of the four Divine Kingdoms, the Divine Kingdoms were the center of the Great Thousand World. The opposite side of the ocean was almost a barren land.
As for the "North Heaven Territory", many warriors from the four Divine Kingdoms had gone there in the Middle Ages and Ancient Antiquity. However, there were no opportunities in that desolate place. Therefore, few warriors from the four Divine Kingdoms were willing to risk their lives to enter the North Heaven Territory, let alone pay a huge price to conquer the North Heaven Territory.
Therefore, since ancient times, the warriors who came to the Tianbei Domain were those who had offended the Divine Kingdoms and chose to flee. In desperation, they could choose to live in seclusion in the Central Domain.
But it was different this time.
Because of the Saintess's order, the Heartless Sage had to flatten the so-called "Cloud Hall", a small fourth-grade Sect …
Previously, the Heartless Sage did not understand why the Saintess of the Divine Kingdom of Yu the Great would be angry with a small Sect in the Tianbei Domain, which was tens of thousands of miles away.
Later, the Heartless Sage understood that the guy named Luo Zheng had offended the Saintess.
If it was only for this reason, the Heartless Sage would not have come in person. As a Divine Sea Realm expert, he did not directly take orders from the Saintess.
The reason he came to the Tianbei Domain was naturally because of the secret in Luo Zheng's mind!
The moment the Heartless Sage's soul was devoured, he saw clearly what was in Luo Zheng's mind. It was a great temptation!
Every time he thought of this, the Heartless Sage could not suppress his excitement. If he took this secret for himself, his future would be bright. Even the Divine Kingdom of Yu the Great would be trampled underfoot by him.
The four Divine Kingdoms were too far away from the Tianbei Domain, and they had to avoid some important places on the way. They tried not to provoke the powerful Fierce Beasts in the sea.
Several days later, the six warriors of the Divine Kingdom of Yu the Great finally appeared in the east of the Tianbei Domain.
"Heartless, there seems to be a small sect there. Let's … go and have a look." Another powerful warrior in the Divine Sea Realm who came with Heartless Monk was called Star Sword Venerable Master. He was a close friend of Heartless Monk. This time, he came with Heartless Monk because of Heartless Monk's invitation.
In the eyes of the warriors of the Great Yu Divine Kingdom, the trip to Tianbei Region was not dangerous at all. A place where even the Life and Death Realm warriors could dominate, they only needed to close their eyes and crush it. In comparison, the Chaotic Astral Ocean was much more dangerous than Tianbei Region.
The small sect that Master Xingjian had mentioned was naturally the Fish Dragon Sect, a third grade sect located in the easternmost lands.
Heartless Monk sneered and suddenly rushed into the Fish Dragon Sect. His strong body directly stepped on the open space of the main hall of the Fish Dragon Sect.
"Bang!"
Heartless Monk looked like a child on the surface, but in fact, he was an old monster who had lived for thousands of years. He was a veritable giant. As he stomped down, the open space in front of the main hall of the Fish Dragon Sect suddenly cracked with him as the center. Countless cracks appeared on the ground within a radius of a hundred acres!
"Who is it? Who broke into the Fish Dragon Sect!"
Sect Master Xiao of the Fish Dragon Sect was furious.
When Sect Master Xiao rushed out of the hall and glanced around, the angry expression on his face suddenly disappeared, and an extremely polite smile appeared on his face …
But Sect Master Xiao was cursing in his heart.
"Damn it, why is there another Divine Sea Realm warrior here?
Since when did the Divine Sea Realm warriors in the Central Domain become like cabbages, popping up every now and then? "
Although he was cursing in his heart, the smile on Sect Master Xiao's face was extremely warm. After encountering hundreds of super strong masters, Sect Master Xiao found that his heart had become stronger. At least when he met the six strong masters in front of him, Sect Master Xiao did not lose his composure.
"Master, I wonder what brings you to the Fish Dragon Sect?" Sect Master Xiao asked Heartless Monk with a bright smile.
The Heartless Sage glanced at Sect Master Xiao indifferently and said coldly, "How do I get to Tianqi City?"
Going to Tianqi City again!
Could it be that these people were also going to find Luo Zheng?
Some time ago, hundreds of super strong masters had already gone to the Divine Kingdom. Why was it still not enough?
But speaking of which, why was there no news of the hundreds of strong masters after they borrowed the path from the Fish Dragon Sect to Tianqi City? Sect Master Xiao was puzzled, but he did not dare to ask.
Sect Master Xiao knew very well that curiosity killed the cat. If he asked too much, he was afraid that he would be killed, or even destroyed. How could he dare to ask?
So Sect Master Xiao quickly took out a map and respectfully handed it to the Divine Sea Realm warrior. Then he said with a smile, "This is the map of the Central Domain. Tianqi City has been marked on the map. You can find it by following the map …"
The Heartless Sage took the map, shook it, and memorized it in his mind. Then he snorted. The six strong masters stopped talking and turned into escape lights, heading straight for Tianqi City.
This time, the Heartless Sage did not leave any reward …
After the six left, Sect Master Xiao spat at them. Last time, the hundreds of strong masters rewarded him with a low-grade holy weapon. These two Divine Sea Realm warriors were really stingy!
In less than a day, the six escape lights quickly approached Tianqi City.
Standing at the gate of the city wall, the Heartless Sage glanced indifferently. He had memorized the surroundings of Tianqi City in the Eight Trigrams Mirror. Although the World Business Alliance had cleaned up the battlefield during this period, the Heartless Sage's memory would not be wrong. This was Tianqi City.
"I just don't know if that kid is still in Tianqi City," the Heartless Sage said indifferently.
The Star Sword Venerable asked, "What secret does that kid have that you are so concerned about?"
The Heartless Sage chuckled. "What secret does that kid have? Am I not doing this for the Saintess? "
The Star Sword Venerable said coldly, "You can fool the Saintess with this, but don't fool me. You and I have been friends for many years. Do you still have to hide it from me?"
Seeing that the Star Sword Venerable was a little unhappy, the Heartless Sage finally said, "Well, since I am willing to invite you to the Central Domain, I naturally want to share that secret with you. I can only tell you that this secret is related to the Dragon Bloodline!"
"The Dragon Bloodline!"
Hearing the Heartless Sage's words, the Star Sword Venerable's face suddenly revealed a look of surprise. Then, it turned into an expression of disbelief. "Heartless, don't joke with me."
The secret of the Dragon Bloodline was the greatest secret of the four Divine Kingdoms.
The history of the four Divine Kingdoms was much longer than that of the Central Domain. On that land, various civilizations had existed for countless years.
Therefore, the history of the Divine Kingdoms could be summarized into the Ancient Times, the Middle Ages, and the Modern Times …
The Dragon Bloodline had been enemies with the Divine Kingdoms since the Ancient Times. In the Middle Ages, the Dragon Bloodline and the "seven Divine Kingdoms" of that time started an earth-shattering war. The True Dragon even appeared and helped the Dragon Bloodline to destroy the three Divine Kingdoms.
It was only in the Modern Times that the Divine Kingdoms finally gained the upper hand and completely destroyed the Dragon Bloodline. The Dragon Bloodline became a part of history and legend in the Divine Kingdoms.
Even though it was just a legend, there were still many inheritances and relics inside the Dragon Bloodline. These things were like gold mines, attracting the warriors of the Divine Kingdoms to excavate them.
Therefore, all the secrets related to the Dragon Bloodline were extremely precious in the Divine Kingdoms. After all, over the years, the warriors of the Divine Kingdoms had excavated too many benefits from the Dragon Bloodline …
"Why would I joke with you? When I catch this guy and search his soul, you'll know then!" The Heartless Sage revealed a confident smile.
Seeing that Heartless Monk was so convinced, Star Sword Esteemed Being also believed a little bit. Speaking of which, many warriors who had offended experts in the Divine Kingdom would find a chance to hide in the North Heaven Region. Those warriors who hid in the North Heaven Region would eventually die. Therefore, it was not impossible for the secret to be obtained by the warriors in the Central Region if those warriors brought it here.
"Since that guy has the secret of the Dragon Bloodline, his strength should not be weak. I wonder if he has any helpers?" The Star Sword Venerable's face revealed a hint of caution.
"Hahahaha …" Hearing the Star Sword Venerable's words, the Heartless Sage immediately laughed, "That guy's strength is indeed not bad. He killed a fourth level Life and Death Realm warrior when he was only in the Middle False Tribulation Realm!"
"Oh?" The Star Sword Venerable's eyes flashed. No matter if it was the False Tribulation Realm or the Life and Death Realm, they were not worthy of his attention. However, according to the Heartless Sage's tone, that guy could actually kill a fourth level Life and Death Realm warrior when he was only in the Middle False Tribulation Realm?
This was very powerful.
