Chapter 751
Even in the four Divine Kingdoms, warriors who could kill Life and Death Realm warriors at the False Tribulation Realm only existed in the legends of the Divine Kingdoms.
At the very least, Star Sword Venerable could not do it back then.
Therefore, Star Sword Venerable was even more interested in Luo Zheng after hearing Heartless Monk's words. Since Luo Zheng was so powerful, maybe it was because of the secret inheritance of the Dragon Bloodline Clan?
"Don't worry, Star Sword. When have I ever lied to you?"
After saying that, Heartless Monk suddenly increased his altitude. Facing Tianqi City, he took a deep breath. A steady stream of Heaven and Earth Yuan Qi gathered toward him. For a moment, the wind and clouds surged in the sky!
Then, Heartless Monk suddenly roared.
"Luo Zheng! Luo Zheng! Luo Zheng! "
"Come out! Come out! Come out! "
When he roared, everyone in Heavenly Awakening City could hear him clearly. His voice was like a clap of thunder that resounded in the ears of many cultivators.
It was a pity that the unlucky ones were the commoners in Tianqi City. Most of the commoners felt as if a few thunderbolts had exploded in front of their foreheads. Under the loud roar, they directly rolled their eyes and fainted!
Those weaker warriors also felt very uncomfortable. Under the vibration of the roar, they felt their Qi and blood surging. Their hearts were beating non-stop, and blood was flowing out of their ears.
This was Heartless Monk's intention to show off.
When an expert of the Divine Sea Realm showed off, how powerful was his aura?
Heartless Monk was also very pleased with himself. The warriors in Tianqi City were probably trembling in fear, right?
In the Divine Kingdom of Yu the Great, they did not dare to be so reckless. It was like a rich man in a big city. He might not be much in his original big city, but when he came to a small rural place, he had to show off and show off.
In the world of warriors, it became "showing off and showing off!"
Seeing Heartless Monk like this, Star Sword Venerable smiled and also flew up. He also absorbed a part of the Heaven and Earth Yuan Qi like Heartless Monk. "Heartless, you are in a good mood. Let me try too. Haha!"
"Luo Zheng! Luo Zheng! Luo Zheng! "
"Come out! Come out! Come out! "
Behind the two experts of the Divine Sea Realm, the four experts of the Life and Death Realm looked at each other with faint smiles on their faces.
As followers, they did not dare to steal the limelight from Heartless Monk and Star Sword Venerable. However, the people of the Divine Kingdom had had a sense of superiority wherever they went. There should be a good show later........ Anyway, killing warriors of the Central Domain was as easy as cutting vegetables..........................
Heavenly Awakening City was completely silent.
Such a large city, yet not a single martial artist appeared. It was as if it was a dead city ….
"Are you a coward?" Heartless Sweetheart sneered. He looked like a little boy, but the expression on his face was ferocious. His expression did not match his appearance.
"You don't dare to come out, right?" Xingjian Venerable said with a faint smile, "The warriors of the Tianbei Region are not only weak but also cowardly."
However, as soon as Star Sword Venerable finished his words, a fleeing light appeared in Tianqi City. The fleeing light came from afar and went straight for Heartless Monk.
It was Luo Zheng!
Luo Zheng wore a faint smile on his face, but he was very happy in his heart. He had been waiting for a long time, and finally, these guys had come!
Heartless Monk had fought with Luo Zheng once. How could he not recognize Luo Zheng's appearance?
Seeing Luo Zheng going alone, Heartless Monk also wore a smile on his face. He said to Luo Zheng, "You know what's good for you. You know you can't run away, so you took the initiative to show up. After I get the thing in your mind, I can leave your corpse intact."
Luo Zheng's eyes flickered slightly. "My corpse intact? Heartless Monk, I don't want to die! "
"What a joke. Do you think it's up to you to decide whether you want to die or not?" Star Sword Venerable said contemptuously. Luo Zheng was just a False Tribulation Realm warrior. Even if he was powerful enough to kill a Life and Death Realm warrior, how could Star Sword Venerable take him seriously?
Luo Zheng smiled indifferently. "It's up to me to decide whether I want to die or not. What do you think?"
Heartless Monk stretched out his hand and gently gestured toward Luo Zheng's body. "Your life is already in our hands, insignificant ant …"
"I'm not here to discuss this with you." Luo Zheng suddenly stopped smiling. "I'm here to ask you a question!"
The six experts of Yu the Great Divine Kingdom were a little confused when they heard Luo Zheng's words. Ask a question? What question? If he lost his life, he still planned to ask a question?
However, since Luo Zheng said so, Heartless Monk was really curious. He immediately asked, "What question do you want to ask?"
Luo Zheng took out a few broken pieces of ancient jade from his Sumeru Ring. The ancient jade had been broken and was not complete.
This ancient jade was the one Li Hetai gave to Luo Zheng. The life force in it had been absorbed by Ning Yudie. However, in order to find clues about the ancient jade, Luo Zheng naturally kept it.
When Luo Zheng took out the ancient jade, Heartless Monk, Star Sword Venerable, and the four Life and Death Realm warriors behind them were slightly stunned. Their faces revealed strange expressions.
"Hey, how did you get this thing?" Heartless Monk stared at the broken ancient jade in Luo Zheng's hand and asked.
Hearing Heartless Monk's words, Luo Zheng's heart slightly jumped. Sure enough, Heartless Monk recognized the ancient jade in his hand. This ancient jade must have come from the Divine Kingdom. Moreover, there were more in the Divine Kingdom!
"What is this ancient jade? Where did you get it? Are there more in the four Divine Kingdoms? "This was what Luo Zheng cared about the most. Therefore, he asked in one breath.
Unexpectedly, Heartless Monk sneered. "Although I don't know where you got it, I don't need to tell you."
Luo Zheng's eyes flashed. With a smile on his face, he said politely, "Please tell me, okay?"
"In the Divine Kingdom, this is not a great secret. However, it's meaningless to tell a dying man," Heartless Monk said coldly.
"No." Luo Zheng suddenly shook his finger and smiled. "It's very meaningful!"
Star Sword Venerable frowned. He was much more cautious than Heartless Monk. Seeing Luo Zheng's calm expression and tone, he felt that something was wrong. Therefore, he asked, "What's the meaning?"
Luo Zheng smiled proudly. The expression on his face was very pure, but also very cruel. "Among the six of you, I'll spare the life of whoever tells me first!"
Luo Zheng's sudden words stunned the six warriors of the Divine Kingdom of Yu the Great. For a moment, they all doubted their ears.
Heartless Monk's face revealed an expression of not knowing whether to laugh or cry. "What? Do you want to say it again? "
A little guy in the False Tribulation Realm said such words to a group of Life and Death Realm and Divine Sea Realm warriors. For a moment, they felt that there was something wrong with Luo Zheng's head.
"I'll say it again. I'll spare the life of whoever tells me the secret of this ancient jade first." Luo Zheng repeated and then added, "You can answer first!"
The Heartless Monk chuckled. "It's really the wrong choice to waste time with you. Xingjian, your Heavenly Astral Soul Search Technique can search through that guy's mind to the greatest extent. I'll capture him, and you can search his soul!"
Heartless Monk knew the secret in Luo Zheng's mind. At the same time, he also knew that the soul of the real dragon was not so easy to deal with. Last time, a soul that entered Luo Zheng's mind was destroyed. However, that soul was strengthened by the Spirit Goblet and was not weaker than his own soul.
Therefore, Heartless Monk did not dare to enter Luo Zheng's mind casually. If his soul was swallowed by the real dragon, the loss would outweigh the gain!
However, the Star-Sword Esteemed Being had been practicing a secret art of the soul, which happened to be able to overcome this situation. It was also the reason why the Heartless Monk brought the Star-Sword Esteemed Being here. Otherwise, no matter how close the Heartless Monk was to the Star-Sword Esteemed Being, how could he share such a secret with him?
Seeing that Heartless Monk was about to take action, Luo Zheng showed a regretful expression.

Chapter 752
The speed of a Divine Ocean Realm warrior was unimaginable.
Even a Divine Ocean Realm warrior would have a hard time killing another Divine Ocean Realm warrior who was running away at full speed.
In order to prevent these warriors from escaping, hundreds of warriors from the Sea God Continent were hiding in the surroundings. They even used the secret treasure of the Night Demon Clan, the Jade Tree, to cover their presence.
The Jade Tree was a special secret treasure of the Night Demon Clan. As long as the tree was planted, it would grow into a small tree that was as tall as a human in three days. These small trees could blend in with other trees and didn't look out of place. As long as one stood under the Jade Tree, he or she would be able to cover his or her presence.
However, the Jade Tree could only be used for ambush, not for escape. After all, three days of growth was too slow. Of course, it wouldn't be useful when escaping.
As soon as Luo Zheng finished speaking, hundreds of powerful auras were suddenly released. These auras instantly enveloped Heartless Monk, Star Sword Venerable Master, and the other experts of the Yu Divine Kingdom.
The six Divine Sea Realm Almighties of the Night Monster Clan and Situ Haotian of the Purple Heart Sacred Land also released their own domains.
Only those who had reached the Divine Ocean Realm could have their own Domains!
Everyone's Domains were different, but they were mostly based on the elemental cultivation method they practiced.
These domains overlapped with each other and shrouded Heartless Monk and Star Sword Venerable!
Heartless Monk and Xingjian Venerable didn't expect this to happen.
Nine Divine Ocean Realm warriors …
Even in the four Divine Kingdoms, mighty figures in the Divine Sea Realm were not as common as cabbages. Heartless Monk and Xingjian Esteemed Master both held important positions in the Divine Kingdom of Yu the Great. Even the Saintess of the Divine Kingdom of Yu the Great could not order them around.
Reaching the Divine Ocean Realm was equivalent to transcending the mortal world and becoming an immortal.
If it weren't for the abnormal changes in this world, they would have been able to ascend and become immortals long ago.
The Heartless Monk knew the strength of the warriors in the Central Domain very well. A Life and Death Realm warrior could stir up a storm on this continent. In fact, the Heartless Monk could defeat all the warriors in the Tianbei Domain by himself. If it were not for the fact that he was afraid of the real dragon in Luo Zheng's mind, the Heartless Monk would not have brought the Xingjian Esteemed Master with him!
In the eyes of the four Divine Kingdoms' martial artists, this North Heaven Region was as safe as their own backyard.
From the moment they set foot on this land, they had the power to decide the life and death of all the cultivators on this land.
But how could they have known that they would run into so many Spirit Sea Realm warriors here?
For a moment, Heartless Monk and Star Sword Esteemed Cultivator were both in a daze. A Life and Death Realm expert from Yu the Great's Divine Kingdom even rubbed his eyes, wondering if he had seen through it. How could such an unimaginable thing happen?
With the resources in the Central Domain, even one or two Spirit Sea Realm warriors could barely be cultivated. Suddenly, so many of them appeared out of nowhere. The Heartless Monk really wondered if he was still in a dream.
Led by Situ Haotian, the Spirit Sea Realm warriors from the Sea God Continent tightly surrounded the six people from Yu the Great's Divine Kingdom. But none of them said a word. Only Luo Zheng said with a faint smile, "Last chance. Where did you get this ancient jade?"
As Luo Zheng spoke, he raised the ancient jade in his hand again and asked.
The Xingjian Esteemed Cultivator and the Heartless Monk were thinking about how to get out of the encirclement of these powerful warriors. How could they have the mood to answer Luo Zheng's question?
However, although the Xingjian Esteemed Cultivator and the Heartless Monk were unwilling to answer Luo Zheng's question, the Life and Death Realm warriors behind them did not think so.
Spirit Sea Realm warriors could fight against each other. In a battle of this level, the Life and Death Realm warriors could only be cannon fodder.
One of the Life and Death Realm warriors thought quickly and answered immediately, "I know. This is the Origin Stone of Life! It was dug out from the Feather Emperor's Secret Realm! "
"Feather Emperor's Secret Realm?" Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows. Staring at the Life and Death Realm warrior, he continued to ask, "Is the Feather Emperor's Secret Realm in the four Divine Kingdoms?"
The Life and Death Realm warrior suddenly answered. The Xingjian Esteemed Cultivator and the Heartless Monk turned around and glared at him. The Heartless Monk even sneered, "Do you think he will let you go if you tell him?"
A hint of fear appeared on the Life and Death Realm warrior's face. However, his survival instinct still prevailed. No matter what, it was a chance for him to survive. He continued to say to Luo Zheng, "The Feather Emperor's Secret Realm is in the center of the four Divine Kingdoms, in the Heavenly Feather Holy Sea!"
The Feather Emperor's Secret Realm, the Heavenly Feather Holy Sea …
It seemed that the Origin Stone of Life was quite famous in the four Divine Kingdoms. There should be a lot of them.
Luo Zheng could go to the four Divine Kingdoms and check for this information. Right now, Luo Zheng was only making a preliminary confirmation. If these ancient jades were unknown in the four Divine Kingdoms and were extremely rare and hard to find in the Divine Kingdoms, Luo Zheng could choose other options.
For example, Luo Zheng could choose to enter the Rising Dragon Platform to ascend to the True Dragon World.
With his current strength, he was still far from being qualified to ascend to the Upper World. However, for Ning Yudie, Luo Zheng would risk his life to get to the Upper World.
After pondering for a while, Luo Zheng smiled faintly and said to the Heartless Monk, "You are right. He has answered my question. Of course, I will let him go! Everyone, let's do it. Just leave that one alive! "
When the other Life and Death Realm warriors heard Luo Zheng's words, their hearts skipped a beat. A hint of regret appeared on their faces. The Origin Stone of Life was not a secret in the four Divine Kingdoms. It was just that ordinary warriors had no chance to come in contact with it. If they had known, they would have … answered first.
But it was too late to regret now. After cultivating to this level, which one of them was not a decisive person? No one would choose to wait for death. Under Luo Zheng's order, everyone took action at the same time!
Both the Heartless Monk and the Star Sword Master released their Domains in an instant. Among them, the Heartless Monk shrouded Luo Zheng in his Domains. Then, in a flash, he charged at Luo Zheng!
The Heartless Monk was also suspicious. How could Luo Zheng, a warrior in the middle stage of the False Tribulation Realm, command these Life and Death Realm and Divine Sea Realm warriors? But he also understood that no matter what the truth was, if he wanted a chance to survive, he had to threaten Luo Zheng!
Therefore, after taking action, the Heartless Monk directly charged toward Luo Zheng.
Facing a Divine Sea Realm warrior, Luo Zheng had no choice but to retreat. With his current strength, he might be on par with a Life and Death Realm warrior of the fourth or even the fifth stage. In other words, he was a Life and Death Realm warrior who had gone through four or five Life and Death Tribulations.
He was still far from being a match for a Divine Sea Realm warrior.
As soon as Luo Zheng retreated, a figure appeared in front of Luo Zheng. That person had an immortal-like appearance. It was Situ Haotian.
Since they were helping Luo Zheng fight against a powerful enemy, how could they not have taken precautions?
Situ Haotian smiled faintly at the Heartless Master. "You should be my opponent."
The Heartless Monk gritted his teeth. A hint of ruthlessness flashed in his eyes. He clapped his hands, and a brown light swept out with him as the center!
A battle between Divine Sea Realm warriors was much more terrifying than a battle between Life and Death Realm warriors.
As a matter of fact, all the Life and Death Realm warriors here were just there for fun. Once they were dragged into the realm of a Divine Sea Realm warrior, they would end up in a miserable state. Therefore, those Life and Death Realm warriors kept a distance from each other and didn't make any moves.
As the brown light of the Heartless Monk spread out, he coldly roared, "Bewitching Realm!"
The brown light spread out in all directions at a visible speed!
All the warriors who were enveloped by the "Bewitching Realm" immediately felt their hearts beating wildly. They seemed to have fallen into some kind of frenzy and wanted to attack the warrior closest to them.
The Bewitching Realm of the Heartless Monk was also a very powerful Realm.
All the warriors who were drawn into the Bewitching Realm would lose their minds. Then, they would go berserk and attack the warrior closest to them!
If the Heartless Monk was given enough time to use this Bewitching Realm and cover the entire Tianqi City, in less than an hour, all the warriors in the city would kill each other!
However, today, the Heartless Monk was facing many Divine Sea Realm warriors! The minds of Divine Sea Realm warriors were firm. How could they lose their minds so easily?

Chapter 753
Many Divine Sea Realm warriors only felt their hearts beating wildly, but they returned to normal in the blink of an eye.
Divine Sea Realm warriors had a different way of fighting compared to ordinary warriors. First, they would suppress and cancel each other out with their Domains. Then, they would clash with their True Essence and physical body.
Domains had a great influence on a warrior's strength, and each warrior's Domain was different.
Some warriors' Domains were overbearing and could even be described as shameless. Some warriors' Domains only had some auxiliary effects.
For example, Heartless Monk's "Confusion Domain" could disturb his opponent's state of mind. If he were to fight against a Divine Sea Realm warrior alone, he could change his opponent's state of mind. In a short period of time, he could make his opponent extremely irritable and unable to exert his strength steadily.
But now, the nine Spirit Sea Realm cultivators were in Heartless Monk's domain. These nine Spirit Sea Realm cultivators were sharing the pressure of Heartless Monk's Confusion Domain. Naturally, they weren't much of a threat to them.
Inside Heartless Monk's Domain, Situ Haotian waved his hands gently. Streaks of colors spread out like spirals. After those spirals circled around Situ Haotian several times, they suddenly flashed with dazzling lights. At this moment, Situ Haotian looked like a small sun. Everyone was blinded by the dazzling light.
"Confusion Domain … interesting, but it's still too weak, haha!"
Situ Haotian's voice came out from the light. His words contained a domineering confidence!
Although the human race's Purple Heart Holy Land was ranked at the bottom of the Sea God Continent, it didn't mean that the top warriors of the human race were weak. If they were weak, the Purple Heart Holy Land would have been destroyed long ago and the human race would have been removed from the Sea God Continent.
The reason why the human race could survive until now was because of these three top Divine Sea Realm warriors!
Situ Haotian's strength might not be as good as Liu Yu or the leader of the Twin Devil Holy Land, but his individual strength could definitely be ranked in the top 10 of the Sea God Continent!
"Mind Splitting Domain!"
The Domain of the Divine Sea Realm was to use one's own strength to carve out their own territory. With their own strength, they could change the rules of the Domain within a limited range!
This was also the so-called "competition" for territory with the Heavens.
Of course, the Divine Sea Realm's Domain had a limited range and the rules it could change were relatively limited as well.
If the influence of this domain could encompass the entire universe and the rules could be changed at will, then this person would be equivalent to the Heavenly Dao.
Situ Haotian's Divine Clone Domain was able to create countless divine clones within the range of light!
When the light shone out, countless Situ Haotian's appeared. At a glance, it looked like an army of Situ Haotian's!
After tens of thousands of Divine Clones appeared, they surrounded Heartless Lord and attacked him.
The Heartless Monk was shocked when he saw the aggressive Divine Clones rushing toward him. He patted his head, and an eye condensed on top of his head. Just by looking at the eye, it gave people a suffocating feeling.
"Heart Fire Evil Eye! Destroy, destroy, destroy! "
"Hualala …"
Wisps of colorful flames shot out. This Evil Eye was an object with a great origin. Normally, the Heartless Monk wouldn't use it to deal with the enemy. However, now was the most critical moment for the Heartless Monk, so how could he hesitate?
The flames were really powerful. The sea of flames seemed to be able to restrain the Divine Clones created by Situ Haotian. As the flames swept over, the thousands of Divine Clones were burned clean!
"Not bad, but unfortunately … it's still far from enough." Situ Haotian was certain that he could defeat the Heartless Monk. Since he had personally come to the Central Domain under Luo Zheng's invitation, how could he not show off his skills?
In the domain of the Divine Clones, Situ Haotian was an invincible existence. Unless there was a powerhouse that could kill all of the Divine Clones at the same time, only then could Situ Haotian's true body be forced out. The indifferent Master Heartless was clearly unable to do this!
A steady stream of Divine Clones rushed towards the Heartless Monk. All of the Situ Haotian clones had faint smiles on their faces. Then, they gently pinched their fingers together and an invisible aura spread out.
At this moment, Situ Haotian was like an ancient Holy Emperor, exuding an aura of supremacy between the heavens and the earth!
"Imperial Heavenly Calamity Destroying Finger!"
Only those at the Divine Sea realm could call themselves Emperors!
However, in the general sense, Battle Emperors and Battle Emperors only existed within the four Divine Kingdoms.
The Sea God Continent was a Holy Land and didn't have the tradition of calling themselves Emperors. But, this didn't mean that they didn't have the momentum to call themselves Emperors!
Tens of thousands of Situ Haotian clones suddenly erupted with an aura that seemed capable of extinguishing all life in the world as they shot towards the Heartless Monk. Every Divine Clone had a thousandth of the strength of Situ Haotian's true body. With these tens of thousands of Divine Clones shooting out, it was the same as multiplying Situ Haotian's strength by ten times!
In other words, within Situ Haotian's Domain, one had to deal with the simultaneous attack of 10 Situ Haotians. Such a powerful force was almost unimaginable!
Luo Zheng stood atop the city walls of Apocalypse City, watching this scene with shining eyes. To be able to observe a battle between Divine Sea realm powerhouses was a heaven-sent opportunity for Luo Zheng.
Was this the power of a Domain?
Luo Zheng was lost in thought …
If he could step into the Divine Sea realm in the future and give birth to a Domain, what kind of effect would it have? Would it be able to compare with Situ Haotian's Domain?
For Luo Zheng, this was a learning opportunity. For the many martial artists in Apocalypse City, wasn't it also a learning opportunity?
When Divine Sea realm powerhouses fought, it was possible that the aftershocks would spread far and wide. Although there were other Divine Sea realm powerhouses guarding the area, if they didn't control it well, a huge wave of aura would come out and evaporate the entire Apocalypse City …
However, the martial artists of the Merchant Union still braved the great danger to observe the battle in the sky.
This was a battle that they wouldn't be able to see in their entire lives!
Many martial artists watched this scene, their minds constantly trembling. Some martial artists whose hearts of martial arts weren't stable enough even began to resist. They believed that they wouldn't be able to reach such heights in their lifetime and felt a sense of defeat.
As for some martial artists with a positive mindset, their eyes shined. They hoped that one day they could break through life and death, enter the Divine Sea realm, and obtain such a powerful strength!
This was the effect of a heart of martial arts. It could make a positive person even more positive!
"Bang!"
Another small sun appeared in the sky. At the same time, there was a loud explosion. The sound was dull and distant, spreading out for thousands of miles.
As the light gradually dissipated, a small boy appeared again. The moment the Heavenly Calamity Destroying Finger arrived, the Heartless Monk hid in a small turtle shell.
The turtle shell was emerald green, as if it were made from a large piece of jade. It was extraordinarily beautiful.
However, this turtle shell was able to withstand a massive portion of Situ Haotian's attack. Even so, the Heartless Monk within the turtle shell was still in a distressed state. An unhealthy red color appeared on his face; it was clear that his injuries weren't light.
Situ Haotian was surprised to see the Heartless Monk withstand his strike. The strength of the Central Domain was far inferior to that of the Sea God Continent. But, the inheritances of this Divine Kingdom should be even stronger than those of the Sea God Continent!
If it weren't for this, the Heartless Monk would have been annihilated by Situ Haotian's strike.
"Humph! Today, there will be a victor between us!" The Heartless Monk clenched his teeth and said to Situ Haotian.
"With me?" Situ Haotian faintly smiled, "It should be with us!"
This Heartless Monk actually wanted to fight Situ Haotian one on one. Although Situ Haotian had full confidence that he could defeat this person, it was still difficult for a Divine Sea realm Supreme Elder to kill him in a one on one fight. Situ Haotian didn't want to let this Heartless Monk escape …
Thus, as soon as Situ Haotian's voice fell, the other Divine Sea realm Supreme Elders also released their domains, covering the Heartless Monk.
Seeing this, the Heartless Monk's heart sank. It was over …
He had set out from the Great Yu Divine Kingdom and crossed the Chaotic Astral Sea. Just how high-spirited had he been at that time? How could he have imagined that the Central Domain would become the place where he would fall?
All the Divine Sea realm Supreme Elders attacked at the same time. All sorts of domains covered each other, firmly trapping the Heartless Monk and the Star Sword Esteemed Master. For a time, there were continuous explosions in the sky that made one's heart tremble.
The two dignified Divine Sea realm Supreme Elders of the Great Yu Divine Kingdom were finally beaten to death by nine people from the Sea God Continent. Shi Kefan and the others, who were watching the battle from Heavenly Awakening City, couldn't help but shake their heads. It was a bit pitiful for these people to fall like this …
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 754
Shi Kefan naturally didn't pity Heartless Monk. It was just that Divine Sea Realm experts were born from tens of millions or even hundreds of millions of warriors. They needed countless resources and time to cultivate to the Divine Sea Realm. However, it only took a short time for them to die, which naturally made people sigh.
This was the world of warriors. Even if you stepped into the Divine Sea Realm and called yourself an emperor, you still couldn't escape the fate of being killed.
In fact, it was very difficult to kill a Divine Sea Realm expert. Even in the four Divine Kingdoms, the death of a Divine Sea Realm expert was an earth-shattering event.
This time, Heartless Monk and Star Sword Master were too careless. They were tightly surrounded by nine Divine Sea Realm experts and had no way to escape, which was why they ended up in such a miserable state.
This battle only lasted for a few incense sticks' worth of time before it returned to silence.
With his hands behind his back, Luo Zheng slowly floated towards the sky and arrived in front of the last Life and Death Realm expert.
This Life and Death Realm expert answered Luo Zheng's question, but Situ Haotian and the others spared his life.
Luo Zheng gazed at this person's face indifferently. This person was an expert who had passed five Life and Death Tribulations and was even stronger than Cui Xie. However, facing Luo Zheng, who was in the middle stage of the False Tribulation Realm, his face revealed a trace of fear.
Luo Zheng certainly knew that this person wasn't afraid of him, but of the people behind him.
"You, you promised not to kill me …" The Life and Death Realm expert stammered.
Luo Zheng nodded and then said with a smile, "I did promise not to kill you, but I can't leave you in the Central Domain, and I can't let you go back to the Great Yu Divine Kingdom. You … think of a way?"
"This!" The Life and Death Realm expert's face froze. Wasn't this bullying? He originally intended to say this, but in the current situation, so what if Luo Zheng bullied him? Killing him was just a matter of a thought. After all, Luo Zheng didn't swear on his heart of martial arts.
After thinking for a while, the Life and Death Realm expert shook his head, "I can't think of a way!"
But at this time, behind Luo Zheng, Situ Haotian faintly smiled. "Isn't that simple? Can't you just cripple your own cultivation? "This suggestion was simple yet cruel.
A Life and Death powerhouse whose cultivation was crippled was equivalent to a dead man. There was a price to pay for fighting against the heavens. Breaking through the Life and Death Realm meant contending against the Heavenly Dao. After crippling one's cultivation, the minor heavenly tribulation wouldn't disappear!
Once the Heavenly Tribulation began, it would descend in an orderly manner according to a certain pattern. It would not fall on time, and there would be no mistakes.
It had been a hundred years since this Life and Death Realm expert had gone through his last Life and Death Calamity, and the sixth one would descend soon. He didn't have the confidence to resist the sixth Life and Death Calamity, so he followed Heartless Monk to the Central Domain. Firstly, he wanted to follow Heartless Monk to get some opportunities. Secondly, if Heartless Monk was willing to help him, his chances of surviving the next Life and Death Calamity would greatly increase.
If his cultivation was crippled, he would be nothing more than an ordinary person. How could he resist the next Life and Death Calamity?
Therefore, hearing Situ Haotian's words, the Life and Death powerhouse indignantly said, "Crippling my cultivation is no different from killing me!"
Situ Haotian coldly smiled, "This suggestion is good. Killing you is the simplest choice."
Luo Zheng's eyes flickered as he indifferently looked at the Life and Death powerhouse in front of him. As long as he said a word, Situ Haotian would take his life.
Seeing Luo Zheng's expression, the Life and Death powerhouse suddenly became anxious. His words were pleasant to hear, but who wasn't afraid of death? Moreover, he had already crossed five Life and Death Calamities. If he could survive the sixth one, he would be able to start attacking the Divine Sea realm. There was still a bright future ahead of him!
The weather wasn't hot, and a Life and Death powerhouse could remain calm even in a sea of fire. But at this time, sweat dripped down from his forehead. After thinking for a moment, the Life and Death powerhouse clenched his teeth and said, "I have a way!"
"What way?" Luo Zheng curiously asked. In Luo Zheng's view, crippling this person's cultivation was imperative. If it really didn't work, then it would be safer to kill him.
After all, Situ Haotian and the others couldn't guard the Central Domain for a long time. They had to return to the Sea God Continent. Once Situ Haotian left, who would be able to suppress this Life and Death powerhouse?
"There is a secret method in our Divine Nation! Slave Imprint! Plant a slave imprint in me! "The Life and Death powerhouse anxiously said.
"Slave Imprint?" Luo Zheng's eyes slightly moved.
"Yes, anyone who plants a slave imprint will not be able to resist the orders of their master, let alone go against their master's will. They will be completely loyal to their master. Plant a slave imprint in me, and you will be my master!" The Life and Death powerhouse hurriedly said.
"Is this slave imprint so powerful?" Luo Zheng stared at the man and said.
"Yes!" The man said with certainty.
The slave imprint mentioned by this Life and Death powerhouse was indeed extremely tyrannical. However, after being planted, one would be able to retain their own thoughts, and would only obey their master unconditionally.
No martial artist would be willing to have a slave imprint planted in them. Some arrogant martial artists would rather die than have a slave imprint planted in them. Which Life and Death Realm martial artist would like to be treated like a slave and ordered around by others?
However, this Life and Death powerhouse was afraid of death. If his cultivation was crippled, he would die. Even if he was killed on the spot, he would still die. After being planted with a slave imprint, he would still be able to live.
Moreover, in the future, perhaps Luo Zheng would take pity on him and release his slave imprint?
Therefore, in a moment of desperation, this Life and Death powerhouse came up with such a method.
"You said that this slave imprint is so mysterious. How do you plant it?" Luo Zheng asked.
The Life and Death powerhouse hurriedly took out a small brown stone tablet from his spatial ring. On this stone tablet, there was an incomparably exquisite rune.
When Luo Zheng saw this rune, his eyebrows suddenly twitched. This was … a divine rune?
At this time, Qinglong's voice sounded, "Yes, this is a divine rune."
"Why haven't I seen this divine rune before?" Luo Zheng asked suspiciously.
While Luo Zheng was cultivating, he didn't stop studying the divine runes. Although he couldn't draw all of the divine runes that Qinglong passed to him, Luo Zheng had already firmly memorized the principles and shapes of each divine rune.
Luo Zheng had never seen this divine rune before.
The Azure Dragon smiled faintly, "It's normal that you haven't seen it before. Divine Inscriptions don't belong to my Dragon Clan. The Divine Inscriptions I imparted to you are all inherited from the True Dragon Clan. The Divine Inscription in his hand is an inheritance from the Human Clan. Speaking of which, only the Human Clan would be able to develop such Divine Inscriptions and enslave others.
Qinglong was an expert in the Dao of Divine Runes, and seemed to be somewhat disdainful of this kind of divine rune.
"Are you sure?" Luo Zheng asked Qinglong again. Soon, he would definitely set foot in the four Divine Nations. If he had a Life and Death powerhouse as his guide, it would save him a lot of time and effort.
But if he was deceived by this guy, once Situ Haotian and the others left, wouldn't the Central Domain be turned upside down by him? Luo Zheng didn't want this kind of thing to happen.
Qinglong nodded with certainty, "This is a very common slave imprint. After enslaving someone, they can't resist. However, there's a strict requirement. Your soul must be stronger than the other person's. If your soul is weaker than the other person's, you'll suffer a backlash from the slave imprint."
"I understand." Luo Zheng was very confident in his own soul.
Even Heartless Monk's soul was only at the Battle Soul Realm. The soul of this Life and Death powerhouse couldn't be stronger than his own.
Therefore, Luo Zheng said to the Life and Death powerhouse, "Since it's like this, I can enslave you."
At this time, the Life and Death expert's face revealed a trace of reluctance. However, even though he was reluctant, he had no choice but to throw the brown stone tablet to Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng stroked the divine rune and immediately poured Zhen Yuan into it, instantly activating the slave imprint. Then, he threw the slave imprint at the other person.
The imprint instantly stuck to the Life and Death powerhouse's forehead and bloomed with a little light. When the light on his forehead completely disappeared, his eyes suddenly became lifeless. At the same time, he nodded to Luo Zheng and said, "My master, what are your orders?"

Chapter 755
Hearing Luo Zheng's words, a look of admiration appeared on the faces of Situ Haotian and the others. Of course, they knew that Luo Zheng wouldn't let the other party commit suicide, but this was the most effective test.
Hearing Luo Zheng's words, the Life and Death Realm expert's eyes gradually regained clarity, and he looked like a normal person. But under Luo Zheng's command, he suddenly stretched out his hand and condensed green Zhen Yuan. This Zhen Yuan condensed into sharp thorns and was about to hit his head.
If his hand hit his head, he would definitely die.
At this critical moment, Luo Zheng lightly ordered, "Stop!"
Hearing Luo Zheng's order, the Life and Death Realm expert's raised hand froze in the air. He looked at Luo Zheng with an inquiring gaze, as if waiting for Luo Zheng to give an order.
"Okay, you don't have to commit suicide. Tell me your name," Luo Zheng asked.
The Life and Death Realm expert nodded and replied, "My name is Cao Junfeng."
"Very good, follow me," Luo Zheng said, then flew down without looking back. Cao Junfeng nodded and followed closely behind Luo Zheng.
This slave imprint was very special. After being enslaved, he didn't lose himself and become a puppet. He still had his own consciousness. As long as he wasn't affected by Luo Zheng's orders, Cao Junfeng would still be like a normal person. Others wouldn't be able to see any clues.
It's just that he couldn't disobey Luo Zheng's orders.
In less than an hour, the biggest crisis in the history of the Central Domain was easily resolved.
Shi Kefan and the others felt a weight lifted off their shoulders.
However, they didn't have anything to worry about. From the moment Situ Haotian led this group of experts to the Central Domain, the fate of these people had already been decided.
In fact, Shi Kefan and the others knew that Luo Zheng had become the de facto leader of the Central Domain. Even if he didn't have the ambition to establish a Divine Nation, this fact had to be acknowledged.
Not to mention these hundreds of super experts, just Luo Zheng's strength alone was enough to make him undefeatable in the Central Domain.
This point was clear to all the cultivators in the Central Domain.
At the same time, they also felt lucky that Luo Zheng didn't have the ambition to unify the Central Domain. If Luo Zheng wanted to establish a Divine Nation, who would dare to disobey him?
But what they didn't know was that Luo Zheng wasn't without ambition. in fact, Luo Zheng's ambition was greater than anyone in the Central Domain. his goal wasn't limited to the Great world. He knew that his future lay was's' future!
However, this goal was only quietly in his heart.
After dealing with the martial artists of Yu Da Divine Kingdom, Situ Haotian and the other experts didn't seem to be in a hurry to return to the Sea God Continent.
Whether it was Situ Haotian or the cultivators of the Night Demon Clan, they were truly infatuated with the various delicacies and culture of Apocalypse City. With Liu Yu overseeing the Sea God Continent, it was rare for these powerhouses to enjoy such peace. This time, they naturally had to seize this opportunity.
However, Luo Zheng couldn't wait any longer. He didn't know how long Ning Yudie could last, but the sooner they set off, the more hope there would be. Therefore, Luo Zheng had already set a date for his trip to the four Divine Nations. The time was set to be seven days later.
When Situ Haotian heard Luo Zheng's plan, he originally wanted to send several powerhouses to follow Luo Zheng to the four Divine Nations.
However, Luo Zheng refused.
The four Divine Nations should be a continent even more powerful than the Sea God Continent. There were countless powerhouses there.
Leading a group of powerhouses to the four Divine Nations would only lead to greater trouble. This wasn't the result that Luo Zheng wanted to see. Right now, he only wanted to obtain enough Origin Life Stones to save Ning Yudie. As for fighting against the four Divine Nations? There wasn't any great enmity between him and the four Divine Nations. He would only do such a thing if he had nothing better to do.
Since Luo Zheng insisted on refusing, Situ Haotian and the others didn't propose any further. They decided to follow Luo Zheng back to the Sea God Continent.
Luo Zheng didn't send Ning Yudie back to the Cloud Hall. Although the Cloud Hall was safe, there were still some families that were ready to cause trouble. Now that Luo Zheng was in the Central Domain, he could still suppress them. No one dared to act rashly. However, if Luo Zheng left the Central Domain, it would be hard to say what would happen after a long time. People's greed always grew with the passage of time.
In contrast, Shi Kefan and the others didn't have any conflicts with Ning Yudie. With the current situation in the Central Domain, protecting Ning Yudie was more than enough.
Seven days later, in the Heavenly Hall.
Xi Youqin gently leaned against Luo Zheng's back. Her face was full of reluctance.
During this time, Xi Youqin also stayed with Luo Zheng in the Heavenly Hall. She watched as Luo Zheng looked at Ning Yudie and sighed. In her heart, she was jealous of Ning Yudie, but at the same time, she also felt sorry for Luo Zheng.
Unfortunately, she was powerless to change this. She could only accept that this man was about to go on an adventure for another woman …
The powerhouses in the Divine Nations were more than a hundred times stronger than those in the Central Domain.
Even Xi Youqin, who didn't care much about martial arts, was well aware of how dangerous Luo Zheng's trip was.
"Luo Zheng, can I go with you?" Xi Youqin said softly. She tried to make her voice as gentle as possible. Perhaps this way, Luo Zheng would be more likely to agree.
During this time, apart from serving Luo Zheng, Xi Youqin often went to the Extreme Purple Realm to cultivate. She didn't like to cultivate. This kind of boring cultivation was a kind of torture for her, but she was very clear about her potential.
As long as Xi Youqin cultivated to a certain level and paid a certain price, she would be able to communicate with the True Spirit.
The True Spirits in the Extreme Purple Realm all had the ability to destroy the world. If Luo Zheng encountered danger in the four Divine Nations, she could at least help him!
"No." Luo Zheng resolutely refused without hesitation.
Xi Youqin had probably expected Luo Zheng's refusal. She didn't look angry at all. Instead, she continued to say in an incomparably soft voice, "Just promise me this time, okay?"
Luo Zheng exhaled. He turned his head and looked at Xi Youqin. He reached out and gently touched her soft face. He smiled and said, "Except this time!"
Xi Youqin curled her lips and muttered unwillingly, "I don't know when the next time will be …"
"It won't be long." Luo Zheng could only comfort her.
Facing Xi Youqin, Luo Zheng now felt a faint affection for her. This woman might not be as quick-witted as Ning Yudie or as quick-witted as Xun. In the beginning, she was even very depressed and disgusting. However, after her personality changed, she was very lovable.
Xi Youqin's eyes were a little dim. She lowered her head and seemed to be thinking about something. After a while, she seemed to have thought of something. She suddenly raised her head and said with a smile, "Okay, you must promise me next time!"
"Promise you what?" Luo Zheng asked.
"No matter what I ask, you must promise me!" Xi Youqin said with a smile. Her elegant face was full of seriousness.
Seeing Xi Youqin like this, Luo Zheng's heart skipped a beat. He then nodded silently.
All the warriors in Tianqi City, whether they belonged to the World Business Alliance or not, had already walked out and looked up at the sky. Today was the day that the hundreds of warriors left.
The three Alliance Masters of the World Business Alliance naturally had to personally send them off.
However, what left everyone speechless was that Situ Haotian and the others didn't take away any valuable gifts. The several treasures that the World Business Alliance gave them were all politely declined.
After all, low-grade saint artifacts that were considered treasures in the Central Domain were only ordinary treasures in the eyes of Situ Haotian. How could they possibly take a fancy to the things that the World Business Alliance gave them?
But what made everyone smile was that Situ Haotian actually took away a lot of ingredients from the Central Domain. He even asked the World Business Alliance for several famous chefs …
These chefs were also martial artists, but their cultivations were only at the Xiantian realm. The most powerful of them were only at the Divine Illuminating Realm.
When these chefs heard that they could go to another continent and serve these Divine Sea realm masters, they naturally agreed. They had worked hard in the World Business Alliance for their entire lives, and they were only chefs. If they could serve these Divine Sea realm masters well, then as long as they were happy, they might even have a chance to make a breakthrough. For them, this was a rare opportunity.
Before Luo Zheng left, he took a deep look at Ning Yudie lying on the bed. After he left the Heavenly Palace, all the sadness and loss on his face disappeared, replaced by a resolute and resolute expression!
Then, Luo Zheng took the enslaved Cao Junfeng and joined up with Situ Haotian and the others. Then, they headed straight to the easternmost part of the Central Domain.

Chapter 756
The news of Luo Zheng quietly leaving the Central Domain was strictly controlled by the World Business Alliance.
Now, as long as the martial artists in the entire Central Region had a clear mind, they would understand that the General Commerce Alliance had firmly taken over the position of the Central Region's overlord, and the General Commerce Alliance now followed Luo Zheng's lead.
In fact, after Luo Zheng defeated Cui Xie, this situation had already taken shape. The other Fourth-Grade Sects had learned to collectively keep quiet, because they didn't have the courage and strength to compete with the World Business Alliance.
After Luo Zheng led a group of super experts out of nowhere and easily killed the experts of the Divine Kingdoms, the Blood Wood Cliff, Mysterious Yin Mansion, Black Mountain Sect, and other Fourth-Grade Sects could only choose to bow their heads. They were even more fortunate that Luo Zheng didn't intend to establish a Divine Kingdom.
If he really intended to establish a Divine Kingdom, incorporate all the Sects, and combine the influence of the Cloud Hall and the World Business Alliance, who in the Central Domain could resist?
In any case, Luo Zheng's influence had become a signboard in the Central Domain, which was why the World Business Alliance kept Luo Zheng's whereabouts secret. As long as he was here, he would be a considerable deterrent to the Divine Kingdom of Yu the Great.
Shi Kefan and the others certainly believed that if the Divine Kingdom of Yu the Great came out in full force, even Luo Zheng wouldn't be able to resist.
However, the four Divine Kingdoms weren't necessarily monolithic. Of course, they wouldn't go through so much trouble for a small Central Domain.
In the sky above the Chaotic Astral Sea, two escape lights shot straight to the east. After bidding farewell to Situ Haotian, Luo Zheng and the warrior named Cao Junfeng traveled together across the Chaotic Astral Sea!
The storms in the Chaotic Astral Sea would stop for about three to six months. Luo Zheng wanted to take advantage of this period to rush all the way across.
During this time, Luo Zheng roughly understood the situation of the Divine Kingdom of Yu the Great and the other three Divine Kingdoms through Cao Junfeng.
It was completely different from the situation in the Central Domain. Everything on this continent was controlled by the four Divine Kingdoms, and there were no Sects of any kind.
Establishing a Sect was tantamount to establishing one's own force, and a Divine Kingdom absolutely wouldn't allow such a situation to exist, because once a Sect slowly rose, it would fight against the Divine Kingdom, and a new Divine Kingdom might even be born.
Therefore, after three of the seven Divine Kingdoms were destroyed, the structure of the four Divine Kingdoms had been extended from ancient times to the present, and no new Divine Kingdoms had been born. It could really be considered a great unification that lasted for thousands of years.
However, martial artists needed to cultivate, and the Divine Kingdoms also needed to nurture successors and successors. Thus, within the Divine Kingdoms, there was another type of sect that existed, and that was the Martial House.
Whether it was the Divine Kingdom of Yu the Great or the other Divine Kingdoms, there were all kinds of Martial Houses. These Martial Houses were similar to the Sects of the Central Domain, but all the Martial Houses were controlled by the Divine Kingdoms. Large and small Martial Houses had become the cultivation holy lands of the Divine Kingdoms. If one wanted to cultivate, obtain more resources, and become more powerful, they had to join a Martial House.
"If you want to enter the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea, I'm afraid you have to join a Martial House," Cao Junfeng suggested as the two of them flew over the Chaotic Astral Sea.
"Why?" Luo Zheng's face showed a strange expression, and he immediately asked.
Cao Junfeng directly replied, "The Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea is a land of miracles. For so many years, it has been monopolized by the four Divine Kingdoms. No independent cultivator is qualified to enter, let alone enter to mine Life Source Stones."
Luo Zheng's eyebrows rose slightly, and he asked with even more confusion, "Since the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea is a sea, it should be incomparably vast. How can the four Divine Kingdoms guard this sea?"
"You don't know. Although the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea is a sea, it is in fact just an inland lake. It is a relatively large lake. In order to monopolize the many Secret Territories in the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea, the four Divine Kingdoms have placed a large number of troops around the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea. Not to mention … the many fortresses and large cities of the four Divine Kingdoms are built around the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea. About 70% of the entire Divine Kingdom's population and 90% of the powerhouses live near the sea. It is impossible to enter without permission," Cao Junfeng replied in full detail.
After being planted with the slave seal, Cao Junfeng would not deceive Luo Zheng. Luo Zheng could even perceive what Cao Junfeng was thinking in his mind. Naturally, he could tell that Cao Junfeng's words were not false.
This was a bit troublesome.
According to Luo Zheng's plan, he wanted to secretly enter the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea. If he could excavate the Life Origin Stone in the so-called Feather Emperor Secret Territory, he could leave immediately after obtaining it. Then, he would find a chance to return to the Central Domain.
But now, it seemed that entering the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea was a big problem.
"Does joining the Martial House guarantee that you are qualified to enter the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea?" Luo Zheng asked again.
"Haha," Cao Junfeng smiled faintly, "Every year, the four Divine Kingdoms will send people into the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea. There are four Secret Territories in the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea, namely the Feather Emperor Secret Territory, the Divine Emperor Secret Territory, the Dream God Secret Territory, and the last one is the Heavenly Stellar Secret Territory."
Luo Zheng nodded slightly. For the four Divine Kingdoms to be able to develop to such an extent, each Divine Kingdom must have several almighty Divine Sea Realm cultivators. Presumably, the resources in these Secret Territories would not be lacking. They might not be worse than the Sin Tower and the Mysterious Cave in the Sea God Continent.
However, Luo Zheng was most concerned about the Feather Emperor Secret Territory. After all, the Feather Emperor Secret Territory was the goal of Luo Zheng's trip. Once he obtained enough Life Origin Stones in the Feather Emperor Secret Territory, he could return home.
Then, Cao Junfeng said, "If you want to enter the first three Secret Territories, you must join a Martial House. Moreover, it must be a Martial House of at least the fifth rank. In the four Divine Kingdoms, all the Martial Houses of the fifth rank and above will have a certain number of quotas. If you want to enter the Feather Emperor Secret Territory, you must obtain these quotas."
"How do I obtain a quota?" Luo Zheng asked again.
Cao Junfeng said directly, "Join a Martial House and become the most outstanding warrior in the Martial House!"
Luo Zheng was still somewhat conflicted about joining other Martial Houses. Just like when he went to the Evil Tower, the human race of the Purple Heart Holy Land invited him to join, but he was rejected by Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng was a nostalgic person. Although the Cloud Hall could no longer accommodate him with his current strength, Luo Zheng was still determined to be a member of the Cloud Hall.
"Besides joining a Martial House, is there no other way?" Luo Zheng asked again.
"No, unless you can fight against those Battle Emperors and Battle Emperors of the Divine Kingdoms and kill your way into the Sky Feather Sacred Ocean …" Cao Junfeng said. Then, he looked at Luo Zheng strangely and said, "With your strength, it shouldn't be difficult for you to obtain a quota if you join a Martial House."
Before they left for the Central Domain, Cao Junfeng had heard about Luo Zheng from Master Heartless. Master Heartless had told them that a super genius had appeared in the Central Domain, and he was much stronger than those Divine Kingdoms' geniuses. He had even killed a fourth level Life and Death Realm warrior with just a Mid False Tribulation realm cultivation base.
A Divine Reflection realm warrior killing a Divine Core warrior wasn't something rare. Many Divine Realm geniuses were able to do it.
However, when a warrior cultivated, the higher the cultivation level, the greater the gap in their strength would be. Therefore, a Mid False Tribulation realm warrior killing a fourth level Life and Death Realm warrior was unbelievable.
Cao Junfeng was a Life and Death warrior, and every time he survived a Life and Death Tribulation, he would feel as if he had been reborn. Luo Zheng was able to challenge a Life and Death Realm warrior, it wasn't hard to imagine what kind of monstrous talent he had.
After giving it some thought, Luo Zheng finally let it go. Whatever, joining the other Martial Palaces was just a temporary measure. Right now, Ning Yudie's life was the most important thing.
Therefore, Luo Zheng made up his mind. After he entered the Divine Kingdom, he would first join a Martial Palace. He couldn't possibly fight those Combat Emperors and Combat Emperors all the way to the Heavenly Feather Saint Ocean …
Just as Luo Zheng was pondering, a huge whirlpool suddenly appeared on the ocean surface in front of him.
Even though the storms in the Chaotic Astral Ocean had stopped, waves as big as mountains were still rolling around in this ocean. When the whirlpool appeared, dozens of mountain-like waves immediately appeared.
"What a huge whirlpool!"
The whirlpool in this ocean of stars was about one-tenth the size of the Central Domain. Countless white bubbles were floating around the edges of the whirlpool, and the center of the whirlpool still retained its blue color. However, the bottom of the whirlpool was pitch black, as if the bottom of the whirlpool was connected to hell!
After seeing this huge whirlpool, Cao Junfeng's expression suddenly changed. He hurriedly said, "Quick, quick, fly up! Fly up to the sky!"
Although he was enslaved by Luo Zheng, he still had his own thoughts. He couldn't resist Luo Zheng's orders, but he was still afraid of death.
