Chapter 757
Through the slave imprint, Luo Zheng could feel Cao Junfeng's inner feelings. Moreover, there seemed to be a ferocious Demonic Beast coming into being. Luo Zheng's figure flashed, and he followed Cao Junfeng up into the sky.
The area of the ocean in this world was far larger than the land, and the number of Ferocious Beasts in the ocean was thousands of times more than the creatures on the land.
Humans were not the only dominators in this world. Even in the Sea God Continent, there were various kinds of intelligent races.
Under the ocean, there were all kinds of underwater races and Ferocious Beasts everywhere. The vast ocean gave these creatures more abundant resources and room for growth.
Just like when Luo Zheng had just entered the Heaven Mist Immortal Tomb, he had also encountered high-level Ferocious Beasts comparable to the Life and Death Realm. The Heaven Mist Immortal Tomb was not very far from the Central Domain …
As for the center of the Chaotic Astral Ocean, there were Ferocious Beasts comparable to the Divine Sea Realm, and even creatures more powerful than the Divine Sea Realm.
"Hua …"
Just as Luo Zheng and Cao Junfeng were flying up rapidly, a huge black shadow suddenly appeared from the bottom of the whirlpool!
With the sound of water flowing violently, Luo Zheng saw a huge creature emerge from the water and jump up into the sky!
"What is this?!" Seeing this huge creature, Luo Zheng's face revealed a trace of fear.
This Ferocious Beast was not much smaller than the "Ferocious Scorpion" in the Dark Abyss Cave!
However, Luo Zheng was now in the Chaotic Astral Ocean. Compared to the vast ocean, it was not as shocking as when he saw the Ferocious Scorpion in the Dark Abyss Cave.
However, the level of danger was not low.
"This is a Captive Aqua Beast! Its body is huge, and there are four of them in the Chaotic Astral Ocean! We have to keep going up. This thing can reach a height of hundreds of thousands of feet when it jumps! " Cao Junfeng was very reluctant to answer Luo Zheng's question. Right now, escaping was the most important thing. However, he had to answer Luo Zheng's question.
"Four of them? You even know this?" Luo Zheng's state of mind was a little calmer. He was not as frightened as Cao Junfeng. Of course, he still maintained his high speed and flew upwards.
"Of course. Everyone in the Divine Kingdom knows that the four Divine Kingdoms once joined forces with many Divine Sea Realm experts to capture the baby of the Cang Qiu Water Eye Beast in the Chaotic Astral Ocean. Later, one of the Cang Qiu Water Eye Beasts was so angry that it rushed all the way to the continent of the Divine Kingdom of Dayu. It didn't return to the ocean until it turned one-third of the territory of the Divine Kingdom of Dayu into ruins!" Cao Junfeng explained.
Hearing this, Luo Zheng was speechless.
The warriors of the four Divine Kingdoms were really crazy. A Ferocious Beast of this level was definitely not something a Divine Sea Realm warrior could fight against. They even dared to enter the Chaotic Astral Sea to capture their cubs!
However, although Cao Junfeng wasn't exaggerating when he said that the Great Yu Divine Nation had lost one-third of its territory, the Great Yu Divine Nation's losses weren't that great.
Just as Cao Junfeng had said before, the important cities of the four Divine Kingdoms were built around the Heavenly Feather Sacred Ocean in the center of the continent of the Divine Kingdoms. They were all very prosperous places around the Heavenly Feather Sacred Ocean. Each Divine Kingdom occupied one-fourth of the coastline of the Heavenly Feather Sacred Ocean.
However, the periphery of the continent of the Divine Kingdoms was basically a sparsely populated area. Even if one-third of the territory was destroyed, the loss to the Divine Kingdom of Dayu was not great.
However, this also meant that the warriors of the four Divine Kingdoms were even crazier and greedier!
Since then, whenever the Cang Qiu Water Eye Beasts found traces of humans, they would not hesitate to jump out of the water and attack.
After the huge Cang Qiu Water Eye Beast left the water completely, it rushed towards Luo Zheng and Cao Junfeng.
"Go, go! Go again! "Cao Junfeng said hurriedly.
Luo Zheng and Cao Junfeng's bodies kept rising. Soon, they broke through the clouds and flew towards the deep sky.
"This … Can that guy jump so high?" The thick clouds blocked the view below. Luo Zheng looked down and asked in a daze.
As soon as Luo Zheng's voice fell, he saw the huge monster break through the clouds and continue to chase after Luo Zheng and Cao Junfeng. Its huge body disturbed the airflow and even tore a big hole in the thick clouds!
"Good heavens! Continue to rise! "Luo Zheng did not hesitate at all.
As Luo Zheng continued to rise, he suddenly saw huge gusts of wind blowing towards them.
"It's astral winds!"
These astral winds were not colorless and invisible. Around the astral winds were inlaid golden lines. These were the astral winds in the deep sky. It was said that they could crush anything that got sucked into them!
Luo Zheng had seen these astral winds more than once.
In order to cultivate the Stellar Body, one must use the gravitational force of the stars to allow one's perception and physical strength to drift hundreds of millions of miles away. Every time Luo Zheng cultivated, he had to pass through these astral winds.
However, in the past, Luo Zheng had only used his perception to pass through. This time, however, he had used his physical body.
These astral winds could almost crush everything. Even if Luo Zheng had the body of a high-grade immortal tool, he might not be able to resist them!
Facing these huge astral winds, Luo Zheng twisted his body, drawing an astonishing arc in the air, and rapidly leaped towards the bottom of the astral winds.
Seeing this, Cao Junfeng also followed closely behind.
In fact, from Cao Junfeng's point of view, he could not wait for Luo Zheng to be killed by these astral winds. No one would bear a slave imprint and become someone else's slave. He could not even control his own life, let alone an expert of the fifth layer of the Life and Death Realm.
However, he did not dare to have such thoughts. After all, Luo Zheng controlled everything about him and had the power to take his life!
Luo Zheng's rapid change of direction allowed him to avoid the astral winds above him. At the same time, the huge Watcher Beast continued to charge upwards. The violent astral winds sliced into the Watcher Beast's back, instantly creating huge gashes!
"Roar!"
The back of the Watcher Eye Beast was suddenly badly mutilated. Blood splashed down like a huge waterfall.
The Aquarius Beast glared hatefully at Luo Zheng and Cao Junfeng with its huge eyes. Then, it lowered its head and plunged into the clouds below … …
Fortunately, this Watcher Eye Beast could not fly. It only relied on the power of one leap to rush up. If it could fly, perhaps Luo Zheng and Cao Junfeng would not be able to keep their lives.
Standing above the clouds, Luo Zheng still had a lingering fear. He glanced at Cao Junfeng beside him and said, "You four Divine Kingdoms have such a huge grudge with this Watcher Eye Beast. This huge beast actually wanted to kill people on sight …"
Cao Junfeng also touched the sweat on his forehead. He shook his head and guessed, "Maybe the cubs of this Watcher Eye Beast were stolen by those Divine Sea Realm experts."
The blood of the Watcher Eye Beast was like a waterfall, sprinkling in the sky. Many drops of blood had already merged with the clouds. In front of Luo Zheng, a huge blood-red cloud appeared. If this cloud drifted to the Central Domain or the Divine Kingdoms, it would probably rain blood.
"I think we should go above the clouds," Luo Zheng suggested.
Even though the storms in the Chaotic Astral Ocean had temporarily stopped, it was still full of dangers.
"Above the clouds? That's even more dangerous! "A trace of fear appeared on Cao Junfeng's face.
"There's also danger above the clouds?" Luo Zheng's eyes twitched.
Just as Luo Zheng finished speaking, a bitter smile appeared on Cao Junfeng's face. "Say it's dangerous and it's here. Let's go. It's safer for us to stay below the clouds."
At this moment, in the distance of the boundless sea of clouds, several erratic black shadows flew towards them. Although they looked very vague, they could still recognize that they were huge vicious birds.
Although they didn't know the strength of these vicious birds, they knew they couldn't fight against them.
The two of them stopped talking and plunged into the sea of clouds. They returned to the bottom of the Chaotic Astral Ocean and continued to fly towards the Divine Kingdom continent …
Above the huge waves of the Chaotic Astral Ocean, there was a large patch of dark red blood. Needless to say, it must have been left behind by the Watcher Eye Beast.
On the way, Luo Zheng also encountered many dangers.
For example, on the surface of the ocean, there were several reefs covered in dense fog. From those reefs came an incomparably beautiful song. That song seemed to be able to touch people's souls, making people unable to help but approach it.
Luo Zheng and Cao Junfeng almost fell for it. When Luo Zheng and Cao Junfeng got close to those reefs and saw several rugged black shadows faintly standing on the reefs, Luo Zheng was awakened by the Qinglong in his mind. Although he didn't know what those terrifying black shadows were, Luo Zheng also knew that once he got close to them, he would definitely die.
However, Cao Junfeng still wanted to rush in with a smile on his face.
At this time, Luo Zheng could only use the slave seal to force Cao Junfeng to follow him. Only then could he drag him back from the gates of hell …

Chapter 758
The continent before Luo Zheng's eyes was vast and boundless. He couldn't determine the size of this Divine Kingdom's continent.
However, Luo Zheng estimated that this continent of the four Divine Kingdoms should be much larger than the Sea God Continent and the Central Domain.
"Which Divine Kingdom's territory is this?" Luo Zheng asked.
Cao Junfeng nodded slightly and replied, "Crossing the Chaotic Astral Ocean from the Central Domain, one part of the Chaotic Astral Ocean is the Li Huo Divine Kingdom, and the other part is my Yu Da Divine Kingdom. This region should be the territory of the Li Huo Divine Kingdom. Which Divine Kingdom does Master want to enter?"
"I choose the Li Huo Divine Kingdom!"
Luo Zheng didn't intend to cultivate and develop in a Divine Kingdom. His goal was very clear, to obtain the Origin Stone of Life.
Since Luo Zheng couldn't enter the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea, he had to choose a Martial House.
The four Divine Kingdoms were extremely strange existences to Luo Zheng. Similarly, no one in the four Divine Kingdoms knew Luo Zheng. It wouldn't be like when he first entered the Sea God Continent, when he was chased by the Demon Race.
However, Luo Zheng had to pay attention to the Saintess of the Divine Kingdom.
When Luo Zheng killed the Prince of Yu Da Divine Kingdom in the Heavenly Mist Immortal Tomb, the Saintess had vowed to kill him for revenge. Naturally, Luo Zheng wouldn't be stupid enough to choose the Yu Da Divine Kingdom.
Although there were many people in a Divine Kingdom, experts were rare. Even False Tribulation Realm experts couldn't be found everywhere in a Divine Kingdom. If Luo Zheng chose the Yu Da Divine Kingdom, it would be troublesome if he encountered the Saintess.
Cao Junfeng didn't have any opinion on Luo Zheng's choice. He just carefully followed behind Luo Zheng. No matter what, he had narrowly escaped death in the Central Domain. Although he was now Luo Zheng's slave, as long as Luo Zheng died or didn't need him, he would eventually regain his freedom.
Just as Cao Junfeng said, the area near the sea was extremely sparsely populated. The two had been flying at high speed for a long time, but they hadn't even seen a small city, let alone a large one. This place was even more desolate than the Central Domain.
However, as the two flew forward, the surrounding cities and towns gradually increased.
Luo Zheng casually looked around and found that most of the people in these towns were ordinary mortals. Even if there were martial artists, they were only at the Xiantian level.
Naturally, he didn't stay here and continued moving forward.
As they went deeper into the Black Iron Divine Kingdom, some medium-sized cities appeared one after another. Slowly, some large cities appeared.
The layout of this Divine Kingdom was completely different from the Central Domain. In the Central Domain, there were cities scattered all over the Central Domain like chess pieces. But in the Divine Kingdom, the deeper one went, the more people there were, and the bigger the cities.
Luo Zhengcai discovered a few Martial Houses in these cities, and some of them were third-grade Martial Houses, and some were even fourth-grade Martial Houses. However, Luo Zhengcai still didn't stay. Just like Cao Junfeng had said, only fifth-grade Martial Houses and above had the qualifications to explore the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea.
After a few days, a huge city finally appeared in front of Luo Zheng.
At the front gate of the city, there was a three hundred zhang tall stone statue. This statue was almost as tall as a small mountain.
Seeing Luo Zheng looking at this statue, Cao Junfeng immediately told Luo Zheng, "This is the first Divine Emperor of the Black Iron Divine Kingdom, the Black Iron War Emperor! The Black Iron Divine Kingdom was built by him. "
"Is he dead?" Luo Zheng asked.
A Divine Core warrior could live for a few hundred years, a False Tribulation warrior could live for one to two thousand years, and a Life and Death warrior could live for several thousand years. As for a Divine Sea warrior, their lifespan was calculated in tens of thousands of years. There was no doubt that this Divine Kingdom had many legendary giants!
"He's dead. The Black Iron Divine Kingdom is one of the oldest Divine Kingdoms. This Black Iron War Emperor lived for 13,000 years, but he still died." A regretful expression emerged on Cao Junfeng's face when he said this.
There was an invincible curse above the heads of all warriors in the four Divine Kingdoms, and that was death. No one could escape from it.
Normally, once a warrior reached the Divine Sea realm, he would be able to ascend to the Heavenly Realm and become a stronger being. But, in this world, no one could ascend to the Heavenly Realm!
It was still fine in the Central Domain. Since the ancient times, almost no one had reached the Divine Sea realm. Therefore, it didn't matter if one could ascend to the Heavenly Realm or not. The strongest warriors were only at the Life and Death realm, and most of them had died in the Life and Death Tribulation.
But, in the four Divine Kingdoms, there were a large number of Divine Sea warriors. When these Divine Sea warriors gathered together and couldn't ascend to the Heavenly Realm, their lifespan would be exhausted, and they would wither away and die from the Heavenly Decay.
As the saying went, when a warrior was born, he had to fight against the Heavens. But, in this Heavenly Realm, the Heavens were not strong enough. How were they going to fight for their lives?
This was the reason why all the mighty warriors in the Divine Kingdoms couldn't do anything about it.
In fact, many mighty warriors from the Divine Kingdoms in the Sea God Continent, such as Liu Yu, were facing the same problem.
But, the Demon Night Clan was different from the other clans. They could communicate with the Heavenly Realm. If there was something important that could attract the attention of the Demon Night Clan, they had other ways to ascend to the Heavenly Realm. Just like how the True Dragon Clan had the Rising Dragon Platform, they were able to break through this restriction.
The city in front of him was called Solitary Crane City. It was the second largest city in the Black Iron Divine Kingdom, second only to the capital.
Of course, such a large city had a restriction on flying. Luo Zheng wouldn't be so arrogant as to fly directly into the city.
It wasn't until Luo Zheng entered Solitary Crane City that he felt the aura of a Divine Kingdom!
In the Central Domain, even in Apocalypse City, there were only a few dozen False Tribulation Realm warriors. In the huge Apocalypse City, there were probably more than a hundred million mortals and warriors combined. This ratio was shockingly small.
But not long after Luo Zheng entered Solitary Crane City, he encountered two or three False Tribulation Realm warriors. Judging from the ratio, the number of False Tribulation Realm warriors wasn't much higher than in Apocalypse City, but the absolute number was much higher.
"The Profound Jade Martial House in Solitary Crane City has the strength of a sixth-rank sect. It's a famous Martial House in the four Divine Kingdoms. With your current strength and cultivation, you should be able to join it," Cao Junfeng said to Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng nodded his head, but he still had some doubts. "Don't Martial Houses usually recruit disciples for those with lower cultivation?"
The Blue Cloud Sect in the Central Domain only recruited disciples for beginners. Even the Cloud Hall wouldn't recruit a Divine Core disciple. Those big sects even rejected independent warriors. After all, once a person reached a certain level of cultivation and joined another sect, it would be very difficult for them to maintain their loyalty.
Cao Junfeng shook his head and smiled, "Master, you're thinking too much. The Divine Kingdom is very different from the Central Domain. In the Divine Kingdom, as long as you're in it, you're considered a member of the Divine Kingdom. The Martial House doesn't want to cultivate its own disciples, it just wants to help the Divine Kingdom."
Luo Zheng was comparing the Martial House to the Divine Kingdom, but in fact, he should be comparing the Divine Kingdom to the Martial House.
For example, if the Cloud Hall was the same as the Divine Kingdom, then the Martial House could only be considered as an elite hall within the Cloud Hall. It wasn't an independent power, so all the resources in the Martial House were given directly by the Divine Kingdom.
Even if Luo Zheng called himself an independent warrior, the Martial House wouldn't really treat Luo Zheng as an 'independent warrior'. They would still consider Luo Zheng as a member of the Divine Kingdom.
No matter how big the world was, it didn't belong to the Divine Kingdom. The moment Luo Zheng stepped into the Divine Kingdom, he would be considered as a member of the Black Iron Divine Kingdom.
Only now did Luo Zheng understand what was going on.
After asking around for a bit, Luo Zheng and Cao Junfeng found the way to the Profound Jade Martial House. After that, they headed straight towards the Profound Jade Martial House.
They couldn't fly, so they could only walk. When they were passing by a street, Luo Zheng saw a tall red city wall on their right. They could hear the faint sound of waves coming from outside the city wall, and they could even smell the salty smell of the ocean.
"There's the smell of the ocean here? Could it be … "
Luo Zheng was deep inside the Divine Kingdom's mainland, far away from the Chaotic Astral Ocean. If there was ocean water outside the city wall, then it was very likely to be the Heavenly Feather Sacred Ocean!
Cao Junfeng immediately nodded and said, "Master guessed correctly. This wall is the Heavenly Feather Sacred Ocean!"
How mysterious was the Heavenly Feather Sacred Ocean? Even if they couldn't enter the Heavenly Feather Sacred Ocean, couldn't they even take a look?
Luo Zheng was even more puzzled.
Seeing Luo Zheng like this, Cao Junfeng didn't find it strange. He just smiled and said, "The Heavenly Feather Sacred Ocean is related to the ruling position of the four Divine Kingdoms. This inland sea is their exclusive domain. Ordinary warriors don't even have the qualifications to take a look!"
"And if they do?" Luo Zheng asked again.

Chapter 759
Luo Zheng was very curious about this Heavenly Feather Saint Ocean. It was just a small inland ocean, how could there be so many secret realms here?
After hearing Cao Junfeng's words, he became even more curious. What the hell was this Heavenly Feather Saint Ocean? Ordinary warriors weren't even allowed to look at it? Even looking at it was a crime punishable by death!
This was simply inexplicable!
"The entire Heavenly Feather Saint Ocean is surrounded by these walls?" Luo Zheng asked.
"That's right. These walls are built in a circle, and they surround the entire Heavenly Feather Saint Ocean. Furthermore, they are all equipped with a No-Fly Barrier. As long as anyone breaks through the barrier, Life and Death warriors will immediately arrive …"
Building a circle of walls around an inland ocean, this was an unimaginably huge project.
Hearing this, Luo Zheng was dumbfounded. Wasn't this a bit too exaggerated?
What kind of treasure was hidden in this Heavenly Feather Saint Ocean? Why were they so strict about it?
But, those Combat Emperors and Combat Emperors in the Divine Kingdom weren't idiots. They must have their own reasons for doing so.
This was how humans were. The more curious they were, the more they wanted to take a look. Luo Zheng really wanted to climb over these walls and take a peek at what this Heavenly Feather Saint Ocean was!
"Don't tell me you're thinking of breaking through the No-Fly Barrier? Even if you can fight those Life and Death warriors, those Divine Sea warriors will still come for you. "Cao Junfeng warned.
Luo Zheng smiled and shook his head. Of course, he wouldn't do something so stupid. He finally understood Cao Junfeng's warning a few days ago. If he tried to break through and enter the Heavenly Feather Saint Ocean by force, he would only be hunted down by those Combat Emperors. He wouldn't be able to enter the Feather Emperor's secret realm.
After that, Luo Zheng left the tall walls. After the time it took for an incense stick to burn, Luo Zheng finally found the legendary Profound Jade Martial Palace.
In any case, the Profound Jade Martial House was equivalent to a Martial House of a sixth-grade sect. The Martial House's entrance was also built in a very imposing manner. In front of the Profound Jade Martial House's entrance, there were countless warriors kneeling on the ground and kowtowing towards the Martial House.
Luo Zheng casually glanced around. There were over ten thousand warriors lying on the ground, and most of them were at the Divine Reflection realm or Divine Core realm. Looking at this scene, Luo Zheng furrowed his brows. "What's going on?"
Cao Junfeng shook his head and said, "This Profound Jade Martial House is the Martial Holy Land of the Black Iron Divine Nation. These martial artists want to enter, but they cannot obtain the qualifications. They only hope that their sincerity will influence the people of the Martial House and one day, they will be able to enter the Martial House."
Luo Zheng was also speechless. In order to enter a Martial House, they actually went to such lengths...
The warriors of the Central Domain could cultivate through many channels. For example, if they couldn't join a sect, they could join another sect or even become an independent warrior and cultivate on their own.
But within the Divine Kingdom, these Martial Houses that were supported by the Divine Kingdom held an absolute monopoly. If one wanted to cultivate to a higher level, there was no other way but to enter a Martial House!
In the beginning, many martial artists were unable to enter the Profound Jade Martial House and could only return home dejectedly.
Later on, there was a martial artist who didn't want to give up. He knelt in front of the Profound Jade Martial House's gate and refused to stand up.
He knelt there for ten years, rain or shine.
In the end, an Elder of the Profound Jade Martial House decided to accept him into the Martial House because of his sincerity, although he didn't have enough talent.
After that, things got out of hand.
Many martial artists who didn't have enough talent were forced to kneel here after failing to enter the Profound Jade Martial House.
Some of them knelt there for one or two years, and some of them knelt there for eight to ten years. However, the Profound Jade Martial House refused to accept these people.
A martial artist's perseverance was important, but so was their talent. As a Martial House, it was impossible for them to waste their time on these martial artists.
But, these martial artists were still trying to enter the Profound Jade Martial House with sincerity.
Luo Zheng didn't know whether to laugh or cry. After that, he walked up the stairs leading to the Martial House's entrance and walked towards the main entrance.
Many guards were standing on both sides of the Martial House. Most of them were Divine Core warriors. When they saw Luo Zheng's False Tribulation realm cultivation base, and the Life and Death warrior following behind Luo Zheng, their expressions were filled with respect.
Most likely, he was a young master from some big family, and he had brought his old servant here to register.
Since Luo Zheng was able to make a Life and Death warrior his old servant, the family behind him must be very powerful. He might even be a relative of the Emperor!
Even so, one of the guards still stopped Luo Zheng. With a polite smile on his face, the guard asked Luo Zheng, "May I ask what brings you to the Profound Jade Martial House?"
"I want to participate in the Profound Jade Martial House's exam." Luo Zheng said with an indifferent tone.
"Exam?" The few guards looked at each other, then one of them said, "The Profound Jade Martial House has an exam every month, but it's not today. The next exam will be held in half a month's time, and to participate in the exam, you need to …"
After that, the guard told Luo Zheng about the exam in detail.
What Luo Zheng was worried about didn't happen. Just like what Cao Junfeng had said, as long as one was in the Divine Nation, they would be considered one of its citizens. The Profound Jade Martial House didn't reject Luo Zheng, as long as he passed the exam, he would be considered a member of the Martial House!
Furthermore, the Martial House wasn't a sect, and there were no restrictions or requirements for Luo Zheng.
Rather than saying that one would become a disciple of the Martial House upon entering, it was more accurate to say that after becoming a member of the Martial House, one would become a partner of the Martial House. The relationship between both parties wasn't one of obedience, it was an equal partnership.
In fact, the four Divine Kingdoms were also competing with each other. In addition to the Heavenly Star Secret Domain, the Divine Kingdoms had invested a lot of manpower and resources into the other three secret domains. Therefore, the competition between the four Divine Kingdoms was extremely intense. Under such competition, the various benefits of the Martial House were naturally increased.
Luo Zheng nodded, then left with Cao Junfeng. Since he was here, he would act according to the plan step by step. There were some things that couldn't be rushed.
Today, Luo Zheng temporarily left.
In half a month's time, Luo Zheng roughly inspected the internal structure of the Black Iron Divine Nation, and his thoughts became clearer.
There were a total of two grade six Martial Houses in the Black Iron Divine Nation. One was the capital of the Black Iron Divine Nation, and the other was the Profound Jade Martial House.
Both Martial Houses held the quotas to enter the Heavenly Feather Sacred Ocean, but in order to obtain these quotas, one had to become a top existence in the Martial House!
This was because the cultivators who entered the Heavenly Feather Sacred Ocean were at least Life and Death experts. Even if Luo Zheng passed the assessment and obtained a good result in the Martial House, if he wanted to obtain a quota to enter the Heavenly Feather Sacred Ocean, he had to compete with those Life and Death experts.
Luo Zheng had already expected this situation, but he was still calm. In addition to cultivating, he even bought some books to understand the customs and customs of the four Divine Nations.
The more he understood, the more advantageous it would be for Luo Zheng. Opportunities were always given to those who were prepared. This trip to the Heavenly Feather Sacred Ocean was related to Ning Yudie's life, so Luo Zheng couldn't afford to lose.
Comparatively speaking, this trip to the Divine Nations was much more comfortable than the trip to the Sea God Continent.
However, Luo Zheng was also clear that he still had to face competition with many powerhouses.
Half a month later, Luo Zheng followed the instructions and signed up. After receiving a nameplate, he was finally able to enter the Profound Jade Martial House.
However, just as Luo Zheng entered, four Life and Death experts suddenly rushed over from behind him. Seeing Luo Zheng in front of them, one of them shouted, "Get lost!"
Hearing the voice behind him, Luo Zheng frowned slightly. Just as he turned his head, he was greeted with a whip!
He frowned, but Cao Junfeng took the initiative to twist the whip. Luo Zheng's gaze fell on a person behind the four Life and Death experts. It was a young master wearing a brocade robe. He looked to be seventeen or eighteen years old, but his cultivation was already at the late stage of False Tribulation Realm.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 760
This brocade-robed young master had red lips and white teeth. His bearing was out of the ordinary.
Being able to have four Life and Death Realm guards, his status was probably not much lower than the Divine Kingdom Prince Luo Zheng had encountered in the Sky Mist Immortal Tomb.
This young master should be an important figure in the Black Iron Divine Kingdom. At the very least, he should be at the level of a prince. Luo Zheng guessed in his heart.
He had just entered the Black Iron Divine Kingdom and was unfamiliar with the place. Therefore, he had been very careful along the way, lest he got into trouble.
However, even though Luo Zheng tried his best to avoid trouble, trouble still came looking for him.
The youth coldly glanced at Luo Zheng, his eyes full of provocation. At the same time, he coldly asked, "Are you also participating in the assessment?"
Luo Zheng's mind turned, but he suppressed his emotions. A smile even appeared on his face, "Yes."
"Not bad, my Black Iron Divine Kingdom has an independent cultivator with such a solid foundation. Follow me, I also want to participate in this round of the Profound Jade Martial House's assessment." Luo Zheng did not expect such a sentence to come out of the youth's mouth.
Martial Houses were not like sects. In fact, they gathered cultivators like this to select the younger generation with good strength to compete with other Divine Kingdoms in the Secret Territory.
If it were the Cloud Hall, perhaps they would only recruit cultivators from within the Cloud Hall. However, Divine Kingdoms had a different way of thinking. In their view, as long as one had the strength, they could enter the Secret Territory and excavate the treasures inside.
Follow this youth?
Luo Zheng's eyes narrowed, but he shook his head and refused this request.
"Sorry, I'm used to being alone. I'm not used to being someone's follower." Luo Zheng replied in a neither servile nor overbearing manner.
Hearing Luo Zheng's words, a Life and Death Realm expert shouted at Luo Zheng, "How dare you! My family's young master wants you to follow him because he thinks highly of you!"
Luo Zheng's expression did not change in the slightest. Instead, he indifferently replied, "Since you think highly of me, why force me?"
That young man stared at Luo Zheng with interest for a while, and then laughed, "Good, you've got guts. Let's go!"
Immediately after, the four Life and Death experts surrounded the youngster and left. On the contrary, a surprised look appeared on Luo Zheng's face. The worst case scenario was to fight on the spot. The four Life and Death experts had all crossed two or even three life and death calamities. It would not be easy for them to kill Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng was afraid of alerting the Spirit Sea Realm warriors in the city. In front of a Spirit Sea Realm warrior, Luo Zheng wouldn't be able to escape easily.
Looking at the youth's departing figure, Cao Junfeng let out a sigh of relief. He said to Luo Zheng, "Fortunately, you restrained yourself."
"What? Who is that guy? "Luo Zheng asked.
Cao Junfeng said, "If I'm not mistaken, that young man is King Yan of the Black Iron Divine Nation."
"Prince Yan? He's already a King at his age? "Luo Zheng asked curiously.
Luo Zheng had learned about the customs of the four Divine Nations from Cao Junfeng. Judging from the structure of the Divine Nations, they were very similar to the imperial courts in the secular world.
However, it was extremely difficult for a Divine Nation's Prince to be a King.
The national weapons of the Divine Nations were all in the hands of the Spirit Sea Realm warriors. The Monarch of each Divine Nation had to step into the Spirit Sea Realm to be able to control the overall situation.
The lifespan of a Spirit Sea Realm warrior was very long. Their lifespan was measured in tens of thousands of years. The number of their children was extremely large. It was said that the Monarch of the Great Yu Divine Nation had tens of thousands of sons!
These tens of thousands of sons were tens of thousands of Princes.
From the moment they were born, these Princes had to compete with others for their father's favor. Some Princes didn't even have a chance to talk to their father in their entire life!
Therefore, the competition between Princes was much more difficult and cruel than the competition between ordinary warriors!
Ordinary people without strength could still live a good life. However, Princes without strength had a fate worse than ordinary people.
This Prince was able to be a King at such a young age. Of course, his strength couldn't be underestimated.
"This King Yan is the pride of the Black Iron Divine Nation. Hehe, among the younger generation of the Black Iron Divine Nation, his status is second only to the Crown Prince. You better not have any conflicts with him. Although it's easy to deal with him now, if you offend those Spirit Sea Realm warriors, you'll have to bear the consequences!" Cao Junfeng warned.
Luo Zheng nodded his head, then he started walking towards the direction King Yan left.
In the Martial Palace, one could see many Warriors everywhere. From the most basic Xiantian realm warriors to the Divine Core realm, it wasn't rare to see them. There were so many of them, and all of them were in a hurry. They rarely paid attention to anything else.
Even when they noticed that Luo Zheng was a Void Tribulation warrior, and that he was followed by a Life and Death warrior, they only took a quick glance at him, then carefully lowered their heads and left. They didn't pay much attention to anyone else.
Following his instructions, Luo Zheng found the courtyard where the examination was held.
From the entrance of the Martial Palace to the foot of the mountain, there were countless courtyards lined up in a straight line.
Of course, those who came here to register were all independent warriors. However, because of the different realms of warriors, the places where they registered were all different.
The courtyard with the largest number of people was where the Xiantian realm warriors were. There was a long line in the middle of the courtyard, and the end of the line couldn't be seen. It looked like the long and narrow line was circling around the foot of the mountain. With a rough estimation, there were at least several tens of thousands of people.
"Why are there so many people registering? How long do we have to wait?" Luo Zheng looked at the long line and asked in a puzzled manner.
"Hehe, it's normal to line up for one or two months here. But, since you've already broken through to the Xiantian realm, it's nothing to line up for one or two months in order to enter the Martial Palace." Cao Junfeng said with a smile.
Luo Zheng nodded his head. In the open space outside the Martial Palace, some people had been kneeling for years, or even dozens of years, just to enter the Martial Palace.
The courtyard next to the Xiantian realm warriors was where the Divine Radiant realm warriors were recruited. There were many Divine Radiant realm warriors here as well, and the end of the long line was also nowhere to be seen. It was just that there were fewer Divine Radiant realm warriors compared to the Xiantian realm warriors.
Next to the courtyard was where the Divine Core warriors were recruited. There weren't many Divine Core warriors here, only a few hundred of them.
It looked like even in the Divine Kingdom, only a small number of people were able to break through to the Divine Core realm.
As for the courtyard opposite of Luo Zheng, there were only a few people who were recruited. There was no need to line up at all.
In the Divine Kingdom, those who broke through to the Void Tribulation realm were considered to have made a name for themselves. However, they weren't like those in the Central Domain, where they could almost rule over an entire region.
The reason why those warriors below the Void Tribulation realm were recruited into the Martial Palace was because of the resources, cultivation techniques, pills, secret cultivation rooms, and so on …
These resources were all established by the Divine Kingdoms, so they were extremely attractive to martial artists. The higher the rank of the Martial House, the more outstanding the resources would be. This was also the reason why so many low level martial artists would fight to enter a Martial House.
However, things were different for Void Tribulation warriors.
Void Tribulation warriors had walked the path of cultivation for a very long time, and ordinary cultivation places weren't very useful for them.
For example, the Tower of Sin was a divine item that could give birth to mighty warriors, but it could only allow a warrior to cultivate until he reached the peak of Combat Sovereign realm. After reaching the Void Tribulation realm, one had to rely on themselves to move forward. They could only train themselves in life and death situations, and slowly make breakthroughs in adversity. If one could find a good opportunity during this process, it would be the perfect path to enlightenment.
All the Void Tribulation warriors who came to participate in the exam had the same goal as Luo Zheng; to represent the Black Iron Divine Kingdom and enter the Ocean of Miracles!
Amongst these Void Tribulation warriors, a small portion of them were independent warriors, and the rest were warriors from other Martial Palaces. For example, some Void Tribulation warriors from Third Grade or Fourth Grade Martial Palaces. Their Martial Palaces couldn't give them a spot to enter the Ocean of Miracles, so they had to choose a higher ranked Martial Palace.
Of course, jumping from one Martial Palace to another didn't mean betrayal. After all, they all belonged to the same Divine Kingdom, and it was the same as jumping from the Elite Palace of the Cloud Hall to another Palace.
Right after Luo Zheng entered the courtyard, a Divine Core girl came up to him and greeted him. She had a nice attitude, and asked if Luo Zheng wanted to participate in the exam and fight for the Black Iron Order.
The Black Iron Order was the order to enter the Ocean of Miracles. According to the rules set by the four Divine Kingdoms, each Divine Kingdom could only issue a certain number of orders every year, and these orders were the same as the order to enter the Heavenly Feather Saint Ocean. Therefore, the order issued by the Great Yu Divine Kingdom was naturally the Great Yu Order.

Chapter 761
After that, the Divine Core girl registered Luo Zheng's name one by one.
Initially, Luo Zheng was worried that the girl would ask about Luo Zheng's background. He had discussed it with Cao Junfeng before this, and he had simply made up an identity.
Cao Junfeng told Luo Zheng not to worry, the Divine Nation wouldn't ask about his background, he could just treat him as an independent warrior who lived in seclusion.
After all, for the Black Iron Divine Nation, it didn't matter who he was. What mattered was that he had enough strength to fight for the resources in the Heavenly Feather Saint Ocean. Once he obtained those resources, the Divine Nation would get a share as well. Therefore, they didn't care about where he came from.
Besides, the Divine Nation was surrounded by the ocean, and for the past tens of thousands of years, all the warriors had come from the Divine Nation itself. There were very few visitors from other realms. In the eyes of the Divine Nation's Master, all the warriors from the Divine Nation belonged to the Divine Nation.
Therefore, Luo Zheng answered all the questions asked by the Divine Core girl. However, when Luo Zheng's name was mentioned, he hesitated for a moment, then he replied, "My name is Luo Tianxing."
The name Luo Zheng wasn't suitable in the Divine Nation, it was mainly used to guard against the Great Yu Divine Nation's Holy Maiden.
After finishing the registration, the girl smiled, then she sealed all the information in a bamboo plate. After that, she handed it over to Luo Zheng. "The first test will be held three days from now. At that time, all the warriors from the Martial Palace and independent warriors will participate. I hope you'll have a good performance!"
Although the Divine Core girl's words sounded pleasant, she had actually made a conclusion about Luo Zheng's fate. This warrior named Luo Tianxing was destined to be a supporting character, and he would never be able to escape his fate of being eliminated.
The competition in the Divine Nation was extremely fierce.
There were less than 50 spots in the Profound Jade Martial Palace, which meant 50 Black Iron Tokens.
In the Profound Jade Martial House alone, there were close to six hundred Nihility Tribulation Realm martial artists competing for the spot. Most of these Nihility Tribulation Realm martial artists were at the late stage of the Nihility Tribulation Realm. Their cultivation was much higher than this young man in front of him. Not to mention, the martial artists cultivated by the Profound Jade Martial House were much stronger than those independent martial artists!
Not only that, among the 50 places, some princes also participated in the competition. For example, Prince Yan, who had just finished his registration, was almost certain to get a Black Iron Token.
In addition, there were 200 to 300 independent martial artists like Luo Tianxing. Independent martial artists dared to sign up for the assessment and fight for the Black Iron Token. Their cultivations were basically at the peak of the late-stage False Tribulation Realm.
Although the youth in front of her seemed to have a good foundation and was already a martial artist at the middle stage of the False Tribulation Realm at the age of twenty, it was still too early for him to obtain the Black Iron Token.
The girl only thought this in her heart, but Luo Zheng also saw the meaning behind her modest smile.
Luo Zheng just glanced at him. How could he, who could see through people's minds, not understand?
Of course, he didn't care about this young girl's thoughts. He returned a smile to the young girl, put the bamboo token into his bag, and left the Profound Jade Martial House.
Three days later, Luo Zheng once again entered the Profound Jade Martial House. After showing the bamboo token in his hand, there was a person to guide him into the depths of the Martial House.
What appeared in front of Luo Zheng was a large square. There were already many martial artists gathered in the square, and each of them was emitting the aura of the False Tribulation Realm.
When Luo Zheng's gaze moved to the center of the square, his eyes suddenly focused. Almighty Divine Sea Realm experts! Moreover, there were three of them!
The Divine Nations really lived up to their reputation. Just a Martial House alone had three almighty Divine Sea Realm experts. Moreover, it was very likely that the Profound Jade Martial House had more than three almighty Divine Sea Realm experts!
However, the Profound Jade Martial House could be regarded as a sixth-grade sect. Even if it was separated from the Divine Nations, it could still be regarded as a Holy Land.
In the Sea God Continent, the human race's Purple Heart Holy Land could only be regarded as a sixth-grade Holy Land. It also had three almighty Divine Sea Realm experts. Based on this calculation, the Profound Jade Martial House could indeed be compared to a "sixth-grade Holy Land". Such a Martial House could only be regarded as a subordinate of the Black Iron Divine Nation. The number of experts in the four Divine Nations was indeed innumerable!
No wonder for so many years, they had regarded the Central Domain as a barren land. There were also very few Divine Nations' martial artists who were willing to go to the Central Domain.
Thinking back, when he encountered the Divine Nation Prince and the Divine Nation's Holy Land in the Heaven Mist Immortal Tomb, they were disdainful of the Central Domain. The pride and arrogance on their faces were also backed by strength.
Even so, Luo Zheng didn't shrink back in the slightest.
As the saying goes, the heart is as still as water. Luo Zheng's state of mind was incomparably calm. He knew that the road ahead was difficult and miserable, but he still pressed forward with indomitable will. There was no other choice. This was his character, and also fate.
After entering the square, the person who guided Luo Zheng told him to wait here and then floated away.
Around the square stood about a thousand False Tribulation Realm martial artists. After entering the False Tribulation Realm, one could be considered an accomplished expert. They all maintained a certain level of vigilance between each other, but they didn't reveal any hostility. On the contrary, most of them had a calm and composed appearance.
If one casually revealed hostility to others, not only would it attract some unnecessary trouble, but it would also put one at a disadvantage — only the weak would glare at others all day long. Weren't experts all indifferent?
Because of this, the atmosphere was somewhat strange.
Seeing this scene, a knowing smile appeared on Luo Zheng's face. He also stood in a corner with his hands behind his back.
Who knew that as soon as he stood still, he felt a gaze sweeping towards him. He turned his head and saw that it was the young "King Yan".
It seemed that even though he had been conferred the title of King, this King Yan still wanted to participate in the assessment himself and work hard to obtain the Black Iron Token!
What Cao Junfeng said was true. Even the prince of a Divine Nation wouldn't receive too much preferential treatment in this world where experts were as common as clouds. He still had to rely on himself to fight for everything.
The Divine Nation's approach was actually correct. Why could the Monarch control a Divine Nation? After all, he relied on his own strength.
If the princes and princesses behind him were too lazy to cultivate because of the Monarch, who would be able to inherit the position of Monarch after the death of the first Monarch? It would probably be taken away by other powerful Divine Sea Realm experts in an instant.
Only by constantly nurturing Divine Sea Realm experts, and also the outstanding ones among the Divine Sea Realm experts, could the Divine Nation continue to exist!
Noticing that the King Yan was staring at him, Luo Zheng gave him a faint smile.
The King Yan just glanced at Luo Zheng coldly and then shifted his gaze elsewhere.
After a while, when everyone had arrived, a deacon handed over a name list. One of the three Divine Sea Realm experts who were in charge of the assessment said, "The preliminary assessment for the Black Iron Token has begun. The content of this assessment is the simplest. Everyone will enter the square in batches. If you squeeze your opponent out of the red line with your own strength, you will be the winner! You can fight in the square, but you can't kill! Violators will be severely punished! "
As the Divine Sea Realm expert spoke, he reached out his hand and patted the ground lightly. Colorful patterns appeared on the ground in the center of the square.
The ground of the square was made of some unknown metal. It looked extremely heavy. The outermost circle of the patterns was arranged with a circle of red lines.
After hearing the rules announced by the Divine Sea Realm expert, many warriors of the False Tribulation Realm didn't have much expression on their faces.
Seeing this scene, Luo Zheng recalled the day when he had just entered the Blue Cloud Sect. Every time he heard the content of the assessment, many warriors would discuss it animatedly.
In comparison, the quality of the thousands of False Tribulation Realm warriors in front of him was naturally different. Divine geniuses like Luo Zheng and the King Yan, who had entered the False Tribulation Realm at a young age, were different. Most of the warriors of the False Tribulation Realm were not young, ranging from dozens to hundreds of years old. There were even warriors of the False Tribulation Realm who were hundreds of years old.
Naturally, they wouldn't act like rookies who had just entered the martial arts world and act like they were curious about everything.
Soon, the Divine Sea Realm expert began to announce the name list of the first round of assessment.
In this round of assessment, 30 people would participate. Among the 30 people, only three would survive. That was to say, 90% of the people would be eliminated.
Luo Zheng closed his eyes slightly and listened attentively to the names announced by the Divine Sea Realm expert. Soon, the 30 names were announced, but he didn't hear the words "Luo Tianxing". It seemed that he wouldn't be in the first round.
There were advantages and disadvantages to being called in the first round. The disadvantage was that they weren't familiar with the rules and the environment. After observing the rules, the latecomers could take precautions in advance. The advantage was obvious. Since no one was familiar with the rules, they could win in the chaos!
Soon, 30 warriors of the False Tribulation Realm entered the square at the same time and entered the red circle in the center of the square.

Chapter 762
As soon as they entered the red circle, many Void Tribulation Realm warriors rushed towards the center of the red circle.
The diameter of the red circle was only a few dozen feet. For Void Tribulation Realm warriors, they could cross it in one step. Every warrior knew that the moment they entered, they had to occupy an advantageous position to prevent others from squeezing them out.
"Get lost! This is my spot! "A warrior who was as sturdy as a gorilla roared.
This warrior stood firmly in the center of the red circle. After the other warriors failed to snatch the red circle, they stared fiercely at the sturdy warrior. However, this sturdy warrior was at the peak of the late stage of the Void Tribulation Realm. His temples were bulging, and a heavy aura was emitted from his body. It seemed that he cultivated an extremely overbearing cultivation method. No one dared to compete with him directly.
After the 30 people chose their spots, a Divine Sea Realm warrior announced, "The first round, begin!"
"Huff, huff, huff …"
Just as the Divine Sea Realm warrior gave the order, the True Essence in all the warriors' bodies erupted at the same time!
"Huh?"
On the contrary, Luo Zheng's face revealed a strange expression.
When the True Essence erupted, it seemed that all the aura was contained in the red circle!
Seeing the hundreds of warriors' expressions of indifference, Luo Zheng felt relieved. The Divine Nation had developed for so many years. It must have improved a lot compared to the Central Domain. It seemed that he was just not knowledgeable enough.
Not to mention other things, if Void Tribulation Realm warriors used their full strength, what kind of place could withstand the destruction?
If 30 Void Tribulation Realm warriors fought with their full strength, not to mention this square, they could probably destroy the entire Martial House in an instant …
It seemed that all the strangeness lay in the floor inlaid with various patterns and the red circle.
Just as Luo Zheng was guessing, the Void Tribulation Realm warriors in the red circle began to make their moves.
The rules were very simple. As long as they could blast the opponent out of the red circle, they could prevent themselves from being blasted out.
"Hehe, the sturdy warrior in the middle is probably out of luck," Luo Zheng said with a faint smile.
Although his voice was soft, the surrounding Void Tribulation Realm martial artists heard it. The Void Tribulation Realm martial artists immediately revealed looks of contempt.
Luo Zheng didn't know that the False Tribulation Realm martial artist in the center of the red circle was the same as Luo Zheng, an independent martial artist who had been famous for a long time. The sturdy martial artist cultivated both energy and body, and the cultivation method he cultivated was even more domineering and powerful.
In the eyes of many people, since this robust cultivator had chosen to participate in the test, then obtaining a Darksteel Token was something that was set in stone.
Therefore, when they heard Luo Zheng's words, they felt that Luo Zheng was too ignorant.
However, just as Luo Zheng finished speaking, there was a sudden change in the red circle!
There were only a total of 30 False Tribulation Realm cultivators in the red circle. However, just as the assessment began, nearly half of them suddenly attacked that sturdy cultivator!
No matter how powerful this sturdy cultivator was, how could he fight against more than a dozen False Tribulation Realm cultivators by himself?
However, the red circle was so small that the distance between False Tribulation Realm cultivators could only be considered "half a step". It was difficult for the sturdy cultivator to find a place to dodge, so he could only fight head-on!
The sturdy cultivator's expression changed drastically. He suddenly shouted and circulated his astral essence, forcefully resisting the attack of more than half of the False Tribulation Realm cultivators.
"Ha!"
"Bang!"
The disparity in strength between the two was not small!
More than a dozen False Tribulation Realm martial artists attacked together. The sturdy cultivator was like a kite with a broken string, directly blasted out of the red circle!
"Pfff pfff pfff …"
At the same time, the sturdy cultivator spat out a mouthful of blood that spilled all over the ground.
In less than a breath of time, the most popular figure had been eliminated, which made many martial artists' eyes pop out.
The three Divine Sea Realm experts looked at each other with a faint smile on their faces. The three of them seemed to have already expected this.
As for the few martial artists who previously mocked Luo Zheng, they glanced at Luo Zheng in surprise. They felt strange in their hearts. This guy's vision was quite sharp.
There were so many martial artists present. Perhaps only the three Divine Sea Realm experts and Luo Zheng could see this point.
Although the rules set by the Profound Jade Martial House were simple, in order to participate and gain the upper hand, in addition to strength, a certain amount of scheming was required.
Everyone was in the red circle. In the end, there would only be three people left. No one was willing to be squeezed out by others, so everyone's target was not the weakest person!
This was because every martial artist had an evaluation of their opponent. You look weaker than me? Good, you are not a big threat to me. Naturally, I will not deal with you personally. You will either be dealt with by others, or I will deal with you when most of the eliminated people are gone! In any case, you are weaker than me. Sooner or later, I will deal with you. Why waste my energy in the beginning?
Therefore, everyone's initial goal was not to squeeze out the weakest person, but to be the strongest!
Under this logic, who was the unluckiest?
Naturally, it was the guy who looked the strongest! Especially the most arrogant one, the one who dared to occupy the most advantageous terrain!
As a result, the sturdy cultivator who occupied the center at the beginning was unlucky. He was directly expelled from the red circle by more than a dozen False Tribulation Realm martial artists.
These martial artists joined forces in an instant only out of an instinctive fear.
In fact, this kind of rule was not conducive to the competition for the strongest. Unless one had absolute strength, the strongest person would become everyone's target from the beginning.
However, Luo Zheng understood the Profound Jade Martial House's intention after a second thought.
A martial artist's strength, cultivation, and strength were only a part of it. Strength also included luck and brains. Many times, even if one's strength was strong, they would still be killed by the opponent's schemes. That sturdy cultivator belonged to the typical type of person who had strength but no brains.
Of course, it could not be said that he was completely brainless. After all, there were not many martial artists who could see through the hidden mystery of this rule. Therefore, didn't it mean that everyone was brainless?
It could only be said that one could not be too arrogant. Staying low-key was the way to go.
"Boom, boom, boom …"
Immediately after, all the martial artists in the red circle began to engage in a chaotic battle.
That Divine Sea Realm powerhouse had also warned that no one was allowed to kill in the red circle. Therefore, everyone restrained themselves. Firstly, no one used weapons. Secondly, everyone's goal was to force the opponent out of the red circle, not to injure or kill.
Of course, when martial artists fought, it was unavoidable to injure others. Some of the martial artists were directly knocked unconscious by the combined attacks of several people. Then, they were kicked out of the red circle like a broken sack.
This chaotic battle lasted for the time it took for a stick of incense to burn. At the beginning, it was the peak time for people to be eliminated. Some people were directly blasted out, some were knocked out and thrown out, and some were timid and had no choice but to leave the red circle.
As the number of people in the red circle began to decrease, the remaining ten or so martial artists began to wander around carefully. They were looking for opportunities to blast their opponents out of the red circle and avoid being blasted out themselves.
How fast were the movements of Void Tribulation Realm martial artists? The ten or so figures were like shadows, swaying back and forth. However, there were still people who were blasted out of the red circle from time to time.
In the end, when there were only six martial artists left, very few of them were blasted out. At this time, everyone was even more cautious! Among the six martial artists, if three of them could stay, they had a 50% chance of passing the assessment!
For a time, there were even fewer people fighting each other.
However, if these Void Tribulation Realm martial artists did not fight, the Martial House naturally had ways to make them fight! Unexpectedly, the red circle began to shrink!
The red circle shook faintly and began to shrink towards the center. The shrinking speed was not slow. It was about the same speed as an old man strolling on the street.
The red circle continued to shrink towards the center. The range was getting smaller and smaller. Naturally, the space for movement was also getting smaller. Finally, some of the six martial artists became anxious.
"Boom, boom, boom …"
The martial artist who lost his cool first was the first to be blasted out of the red circle.
Immediately after, the remaining two martial artists were also blasted out in an instant …
The outcome of the first round of assessment was decided. The remaining three martial artists became the winners of the first round.

Chapter 763
After the 30 False Tribulation Realm warriors went through the first round of assessment, everyone understood the rules. First, they had to run. Secondly, they had to keep a low profile.
Therefore, the warriors who had yet to enter the red circle began to size each other up!
Many of them memorized the opponents in their hearts. If they encountered such an opponent in the red circle, they had to eliminate him immediately.
As a result, some of the famous warriors at the peak of the late stage of the False Tribulation Realm smiled bitterly.
Originally, their strength was strong enough to participate in the assessment, which should have been a big advantage. They did not expect that it would become a big disadvantage.
Everyone knew how to hide their strength. The problem was that many of these people's strengths were known by others. What was the point of hiding?
Many of them secretly sized up Yan Wang. Although this young Yan Wang was not from the Black Jade Martial House, he had obtained his own fief early on. His strength was almost second only to the Divine Kingdom's crown prince. Everyone was wondering if Yan Wang would be eliminated. How could Yan Wang not notice the gazes of these False Tribulation Realm warriors and the meaning behind their gazes?
He only snorted indifferently. The corners of his mouth curled up slightly as he mumbled, "Only the weak will try their best!" Since he had chosen to go to the Sky Feather Sacred Ocean in the name of a King, if he could not pass this assessment, he would be ashamed of his surname!
Luo Zheng still looked at the scene indifferently. The current situation was the most advantageous for Luo Zheng. Firstly, his cultivation was only at the middle stage of the False Tribulation Realm. Secondly, he was not famous in the Black Iron Divine Kingdom. It was impossible for anyone to notice him. Now, he only needed to bide his time and not make a move.
"Hehe, these guys already know how to play this game," a Divine Sea Realm warrior said with a smile.

The Divine Kingdom had spent a lot of resources to build the Martial House. It was not without a price! Their goal was naturally for the Martial House to nurture more elites and then enter the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea to 'mine' for ores. This would be a great return to the Scandinavian Empire and then develop the Martial House. This was a virtuous cycle!
It was because of this virtuous cycle that the four Divine Kingdoms were able to establish Martial Houses that were comparable to Holy Lands! That was why the four Divine Kingdoms were able to become so powerful.

"Bo Sifan!"
"Ao Gang!"
"Wan Jun …"
"Zhao …"

Luo Zheng was not in a hurry. It would always be his turn.
The second round of assessment soon began, and everyone once again paid attention to the battles within the red circle.
This round of assessment was a little faster than the first round. In less than the time it took for an incense stick to burn, the thirty people were quickly decided. The other twenty-seven people left resentfully, while the remaining three people were in high spirits.

After seven rounds, Luo Zheng finally heard his name.
"Luo Tianxing!" The Spirit Sea Realm warrior read out Luo Zheng's pseudonym.
Luo Zheng nodded slightly and then stepped forward.
Everyone's eyes lingered on Luo Zheng for a while, and then they looked away. After all, Luo Zheng was only at the middle stage of the False Tribulation Realm, and his face was unfamiliar to them. This kind of guy was no threat to them, so they didn't pay attention to him at all. They thought that even if this guy called Luo Tianxing could survive in the red circle, he would still be eliminated in the end!
On the contrary, King Yan revealed a smile on his face. This smile was directed at Luo Zheng, but Luo Zheng pretended not to see it.
The three Spirit Sea Realm warriors sized up Luo Zheng with a strange look on their faces.
"This independent warrior seems to have a very solid foundation. I'm afraid his strength is comparable to the late stage of the False Tribulation Realm!" A Spirit Sea Realm warrior said with certainty.
Another Spirit Sea Realm warrior nodded, "I don't know where this guy's master is, but he gives me a faint pressure. This guy will never be defeated by a late stage False Tribulation Realm warrior!"
"Hehe, you two are so conservative," the last False Tribulation Realm warrior said, "I think this guy can defeat a late stage False Tribulation Realm warrior!"
The Spirit Sea Realm warriors' evaluation of Luo Zheng was already very good. Even in the four Divine Kingdoms, there were not many examples of a False Tribulation Realm warrior defeating a Life and Death Realm warrior. After all, the gap between the two was too big!
Therefore, the three Spirit Sea Realm warriors could only come up with such an evaluation.
But they didn't expect that Luo Zheng had defeated Cui Xie, who was at the fourth level of the Life and Death Realm, with his own strength!
It was also for this reason that Luo Zheng lured Heartless Master to the Central Domain. If they didn't see it with their own eyes, it would be hard for ordinary people to believe it. Even though these Spirit Sea Realm warriors had sharp eyes, they still underestimated Luo Zheng's strength.
The Spirit Sea Realm warrior read out the names one by one. The 30 warriors were basically confirmed.
However, when he read out the last name, "Prince of Yan", the other 28 warriors couldn't help but tremble, except for Luo Zheng, who remained calm.
Everyone had long heard of the King of Yan's ability. No one was willing to encounter the King of Yan in the red circle. The other twenty-nine martial artists only had one choice, which was to gather everyone's strength and eliminate the King of Yan first.
Seeing everyone's fearful eyes, Prince of Yan smiled faintly. Then, as if nothing had happened, he stepped into the center of the red circle!
Since the first round of assessment, when that muscular warrior was driven out of the red circle by more than a dozen people, very few people were willing to occupy the center of the red circle.
Although the center of the red circle was the most advantageous territory, it was also the target of public criticism. Wasn't it courting death to occupy this position?
However, the King of Yan stood in the middle as if nothing had happened. He glanced at the other martial artists in the red circle indifferently. There was a contemptuous smile on his face. He wanted to prove that he had the real ability and that he could crush the other 29 people by himself!
A hyena might encounter resistance when it entered a flock of sheep. But what about a lion?
If the "powerful warriors" targeted in the previous rounds were hyenas, the flock of sheep still had the strength to resist. But now, Prince of Yan was the lion that entered the flock of sheep. The flock of sheep had no other choice but to run away in front of him.
Seeing that the Spirit Sea Realm warrior from the Martial House hadn't announced the start of the assessment, Prince of Yan said directly, "There will only be one winner in this round of assessment! I will drive the other 29 out! "
Hearing Prince of Yan's words, many warriors in the red circle were shocked. They looked at Prince of Yan with a trace of fear on their faces.
Previously, if some warriors were too aggressive, the other warriors would unite against a common enemy. But now, everyone looked hesitant. With Prince of Yan's strength, would it be useful for them to unite?
Luo Zheng stood in a corner of the red circle. He shook his head slightly. Prince of Yan was very smart. He was famous and couldn't hide his strength. So, he did the opposite. He might as well be arrogant to the end!
With such arrogance, he would disturb the mentality of the other warriors. If they were really afraid of Prince of Yan, it would be difficult for them to unite to fight against him!
However, one must have the strength to be arrogant. If Prince of Yan's strength wasn't enough, the other warriors in the False Tribulation Realm weren't fools. No one would listen to him.
With the command of the Spirit Sea Realm warrior, this round of assessment finally began.
The warriors who were eliminated and those who hadn't participated in the assessment started to discuss the situation.
"Who do you think will be the first to be eliminated? Will it be Prince of Yan? "

"I also think it will be him. Look at his body. How can he withstand the attacks of others? He will be sent flying with one punch! "
"Look, someone is making trouble for him. I guess he will be eliminated!"
Just as everyone was discussing, a warrior in the late stage of the False Tribulation Realm suddenly rushed towards Luo Zheng. That warrior looked even more ferocious. He said to Luo Zheng coldly, "You, you will be eliminated first!"

Chapter 764
Luo Zheng wanted to keep a low profile as much as possible and not reveal his strength. He wanted to be one of the last three people to remain in the red circle.
With Luo Zheng's current strength, this was not difficult. Although the red circle was small, Luo Zheng only needed to keep dodging, even though he had to face a group of warriors in the late stage of the False Tribulation Realm.
Moreover, with his cultivation, he was not the center of attention and would not become the target of others.
However, this guy in front of him attacked him for no reason and wanted to kick him out. This made Luo Zheng somewhat puzzled.

Luo Zheng's figure floated and stepped on the edge of the red circle to bypass it.

Logically speaking, that guy should have given up after dodging one attack. Luo Zheng did not want to find trouble and did not even launch a counterattack.
Who knew that the other party would not give up and followed Luo Zheng closely. The fist radiance in his hand became brighter, and he looked like he would not give up until he kicked Luo Zheng out!
"You're out!" That person sneered as he attacked Luo Zheng.
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh …"
Luo Zheng did not say a word. Afterimages stepped on the edge of the red circle and swept past, avoiding the other party's attacks one after another. However, his heart sank slightly. There was something wrong with this guy!
The other party had completely given up on defense. Under such an all-out attack, it would be easy for others to kick him out.
Luo Zheng had no enmity with him. There was no need for this person to attack him. This meant that he had completely given up the opportunity to pass the assessment and was desperately trying to drag him out!
Who ordered him to do this?
Luo Zheng glanced at the King of Yan from the corner of his eyes.
The King of Yan stood in the center of the red circle with his hands behind his back. Faint light particles revolved around him slowly.
Although the surrounding False Tribulation Realm warriors were fighting fiercely, no one dared to attack the King of Yan. The King of Yan's gaze was fixed on Luo Zheng!
"It's really this guy!"
Luo Zheng's face darkened slightly. Since he entered the Martial House, he had been targeted by this guy. He had been looking for trouble with him time and time again. What was the meaning of this?
Did he send someone to test my strength?
With Luo Zheng's current strength, it would only take him a blink of an eye to deal with this guy who had been chasing him. However, Luo Zheng could not help hesitating.
From Luo Zheng's point of view, using the most low-key method to obtain the Black Iron Token and then using the Black Iron Token to enter the Skyfeather Saint Sea and obtain the Life Origin Stone was the best possible result!

These Divine Sea Realm warriors paid attention to the new warriors, such as Luo Zheng.

The 40 people in the first and second echelon would definitely have the Black Iron Token. After all, there were 100 Black Iron Tokens in the Profound Jade Martial House and the Black Name Martial House. There were also geniuses in the other two or three Rank Five Martial Houses.
Luo Zheng could enter the third echelon and get the Black Iron Token, which was a good thing for the Profound Jade Martial House.
It was a pity that there were few independent warriors in the Black Iron Divine Nation. Even if there were, the three Divine Sea Realm warriors would have checked their names. On the contrary, this warrior named Luo Tianxing was a stranger to them.
In other words, this warrior came out of nowhere and seemed to be quite strong...
Luo Zheng moved around the red circle in circles. He was in a dilemma. He didn't want to fight, but he couldn't get rid of this guy. He was hoping that someone would kick this guy out. However, during this chase, many warriors were kicked out, but no one dared to do anything to the guy behind him.
This could not go on.
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng's eyes suddenly flashed. Like a snake, he twisted his body and dashed toward the center of the red circle.
As he sprinted, Luo Zheng deliberately avoided the King of Yan. However, Luo Zheng did not expect that the King of Yan, who was originally standing still at the same spot, would smile faintly when Luo Zheng approached the center of the red circle. Then, he turned one of his hands into a claw and waved it gently. Three sharp claw prints shot towards Luo Zheng in the air.
The claw prints were extremely fast and blocked Luo Zheng's left, middle, and right paths. If Luo Zheng wanted to pass through, he could only face them head-on.
As for the guy who was following Luo Zheng, his aura was even stronger. He was determined to force Luo Zheng into a desperate situation.
At this moment, Luo Zheng's figure flashed. He shuttled through space for a distance and walked out from the other side of the red circle.
The three claw prints that King of Yan swung out casually naturally missed their target. However, they went straight for the ancient martial art practitioner behind him.
At this moment, no one expected that Luo Zheng could travel through space directly. Even King of Yan revealed a surprised expression. The ancient martial art practitioner who was chasing after Luo Zheng didn't expect this at all. He collided head-on with King of Yan's claw prints.
"Puff! Puff!
Puff! "The three sharp claw prints instantly cut through the ancient martial art practitioner's robe. Then, a crisp sound was heard. No one knew what he was thinking. Although his attack didn't hit, he didn't chase after Luo Zheng. Instead, he stood still at the same spot.
With no one chasing after him, Luo Zheng instantly felt much more relaxed.
Now, there were only over ten people left in the red circle. These people were still fighting fiercely with each other.

Chapter 765
Under this kind of crazy competition, there were martial artists being kicked out one after another.
Due to Luo Zheng's vigilance, whenever someone tried to lock on to his target, he would suddenly move away from the other people's attack range.

According to his preliminary estimation, Luo Zheng's strength might not be able to enter the first echelon, but it shouldn't be much of a problem for him to enter the second echelon.
More importantly, this person was previously unknown. Of course, he would not be roped in by the Crown Prince. How could the King of Yan let go of such a fish that escaped the net?
The only thing that King of Yan had to do now was to find out the real strength of Luo Zheng!
The first round of testing had ended. Although Luo Zheng's display of the Law of Space had amazed the King of Yan, he wasn't satisfied at all. This kid's strength was definitely more than this.
The problem was that Luo Zheng was too cunning. He avoided almost all the warriors and didn't fight with anyone. In this way, it was difficult for the King of Yan to estimate Luo Zheng's strength.
As the number of warriors in the red circle decreased, a faint smile appeared on the King of Yan's face. There would always be a chance. It seemed that he could only test the bottom line of Luo Zheng's strength by himself!
Soon, there were only five warriors left in the red circle. Apart from the King of Yan and Luo Zheng, there were three other warriors of the False Tribulation Realm. After the space became bigger, it would be more difficult to expel the others from the red circle.
Among the five people, three would stay. Anyone who was eliminated at this time would definitely be unwilling. Therefore, at this time, everyone appeared to be very cautious. For a moment, the situation was in a stalemate.
At this time, the red circle began to shrink …
Through the previous observation, everyone was very clear that the red circle was shrinking very fast. Once the red circle began to shrink, the final result would be revealed in less than 100 breaths.

"Guiyuan Fist!"
"Heart-Splitting Palm!"
"…"

Luo Zheng also knew that it would be impossible for him to not counterattack now.

No matter what, he had to cross this step. This could be a crucial step that would affect his entire life!
Therefore, he didn't hold back at all against Luo Zheng. He was pursuing a sure-win with one strike!
In his eyes, Luo Zheng was a guy who only knew how to run away. He didn't know how to do anything else …
However, as that guy retreated, he appeared in front of him in the blink of an eye …
This …
This warrior's head didn't immediately turn around. Apart from a blank expression on his face, there was almost no reaction.
Luo Zheng didn't care about this guy's expression. He extended his hand and grabbed onto his opponent's hand. Then, with a light clasp of his fingers, he locked onto his opponent's wrist. With this, his opponent couldn't transfer his Astral energy to his hand, making it harder for him to break free from Luo Zheng's grip.
However, Luo Zheng didn't expect that his action was completely unnecessary. His opponent had completely forgotten to circulate his Astral energy and break free from his grip. While he was distracted, Luo Zheng grabbed him and spun him around like a windmill, throwing him out of the red circle.
His actions were done in a clean and tidy manner, without any hesitation.
In fact, this kind of situation was very common among warriors at a lower cultivation level. But, once a warrior reached the Divine Core realm, the strength he could unleash with his Astral energy would reach an extraordinary level. Therefore, who would let him get close to his opponent so easily?
Therefore, those who fought in the red circle were all 'blasted' out of the circle, but none of them were 'thrown' out.

Chapter 766

This False Tribulation Realm warrior was thrown out of the red circle and landed heavily on the ground.

This False Tribulation Realm warrior would rather be injured and thrown out than be thrown out like this. For him, this was a great humiliation.
