The knowledge of the warriors of the Divine Nation was much broader than that of the warriors of the Central Domain and the Deep Sea Continent. He knew that Luo Zheng's speed was indeed extraordinary. Whether it was the distance, the angle, or the timing, they were all very accurate!
Just now, he had finished accumulating power for an attack and was about to attack Luo Zheng. It was because of this that he was unable to react in time and allowed this kid to get close to him so easily.
There was only one explanation for Luo Zheng's judgment, and that was that this guy's reaction speed was astonishing, and his judgment was first-rate. Moreover, he had experienced countless battles to be able to train this kind of fighting instinct. Such a guy was undoubtedly very terrifying.

"With such strength, I'm afraid he can be ranked at the top of the second echelon!"

Warriors who cultivated both energy and body were rarely seen anywhere. In the Sea God Continent, there were very few warriors who cultivated both energy and body. In the Central Domain, there were almost none. In the Divine Nations, there were very few warriors who could cultivate both energy and body.
Just now, Luo Zhengcai had wanted to end this battle as quickly as possible. If this battle dragged on, it would only expose more of his trump cards. This was not something he wanted to see. Since that was the case, Luo Zhengcai directly used astral essence.
However, in the eyes of the three Divine Sea Realm warriors, they were still extremely shocked.
Zhan Yun, King Yan, Crown Prince, and Xiao Daoyuan formed the first echelon. They were the inheritors of the Black Iron Divine Kingdom. They were very likely to inherit the empire's legacy!
Therefore, even if an ordinary Void Tribulation Realm warrior was extremely strong, it was very difficult for them to be ranked in the first echelon. This was because the people in the first echelon were all monsters among monsters. They had the strength to absolutely suppress Deity rank geniuses.
This was the strength of the four Divine Kingdoms. They did not lack Deity rank geniuses!
. With the start of the Battle of the Era, more and more super geniuses appeared in this era.
Originally, a Divine Nation had 10 to 15 geniuses. If these geniuses were properly trained, they would definitely enter the Divine Sea Realm in the future. However, in the past few decades, due to the arrival of the Battle of the Era, the number of geniuses began to increase. In just a few years, more than 100 geniuses had appeared in the Divine Nation.
The four people in the first echelon were able to crush all the other geniuses!
Therefore, they did not put Luo Zheng together with King Yan and the other three. In their hearts, Luo Zheng was probably not qualified.
But now, the strength Luo Zheng casually displayed was enough for him to stand at the top of the second echelon. This was enough to make them look at Luo Zheng with new eyes.
It was not easy to stand in the second echelon. What was more, Luo Zheng could be ranked at the top of the second echelon?
There were countless big and small Martial Houses in the Divine Nation. The two big Martial Houses, the Jade Black Martial House and the Black Name Martial House, included 90% of the geniuses. These geniuses were like sand being washed away by waves. What came out of the water was glittering gold. Then, all the resources were poured into them and carefully trained. That was how these geniuses obtained such strength.
Luo Tianxing was just an independent warrior. No one knew where he grew up. How could his resources be comparable to the entire Divine Nation?
But he had appeared out of nowhere.
In the past, there would always be a few outstanding talents among the independent martial artists. However, Luo Zheng was so dazzling that it dazzled everyone's eyes.
With a smile, King Yan stared at Luo Zheng and said in a low voice, "Not enough, not enough. This is not your full strength. Let me see your full strength!"
Seeing everyone staring at him, Luo Zheng could only sigh inwardly. He had been too naïve. Whether it was in vision or strength, the Divine Kingdom's martial artists had far exceeded his expectations. If he wanted to easily pass the assessment without displaying his strength, it would be too difficult.
There would be more trouble later.
But he did not forget that King Yan had said before that he would be the only one to pass this round of assessment …
Just as Luo Zheng had expected, the outcome of the battle between the two cultivators on the other side had also been decided. One of them was directly blasted out of the shrinking red circle by the other.
According to the rules, it was time to announce the result. Luo Zheng, King Yan, and the other warrior had passed the assessment!
However, King Yan turned around and smiled faintly at the three Divine Sea Realm Mighty Figures. He gave them a look, and the Divine Sea Realm Mighty Figures did not announce the result.
The three Divine Sea Realm Mighty Figures also understood that King Yan had taken a fancy to Luo Zheng and wanted to test Luo Zheng's strength thoroughly.
The Iron Token was indeed hard to get for other warriors. However, with King Yan's status and strength in the Divine Nation, it was not difficult for him to get one. Even if Luo Zheng was really eliminated, since King Yan had taken a fancy to him, it should not be too difficult to get a Iron Token for him.
In that case … they also wanted to see Luo Zheng's real strength.
King Yan smiled and said, "I have said that only I can pass this round of assessment. Gentlemen, do you want me to send you out of the red circle, or do you want to go out by yourselves?"
Luo Zheng's eyes were calm. He had expected this to happen, so he was calm. However, he could not avoid fighting with King Yan now.
However, the other warrior was even more depressed. He revealed a trace of anger on his face. Even if you are King Yan, you can't bully others like this, right? I have worked so hard to pass the assessment, and you still intend to break the rules and drive me out?
However, there was not much time left. It had only been 100 breaths since the red circle started to shrink. The lightning-like confrontation just now, plus King Yan's words, had taken 45 breaths.
"I have passed the assessment. Why, King Yan, you …" The warrior was unwilling to accept the result, but he was unwilling to fight with King Yan. He could only argue.
"Shut up!" King Yan shouted at the warrior coldly, "I don't want to repeat my words."
The King of Yan was standing in the center of the red circle. Luo Zheng and the martial arts practitioner were on the left and right respectively. As the red circle continued to shrink, the two of them could only move their feet and move closer to the King of Yan.
When the warrior saw that King Yan was getting closer and closer to him, he gritted his teeth and sweat dripped down from his forehead. Of course, he was unwilling to accept the result! However, looking at King Yan, who was not far away, he knew that he was definitely not his match. If he fought with King Yan, not only would he be humiliated, but he might also be seriously injured and even bring about more unpredictable troubles!
In the end, the warrior gritted his teeth and stood on the spot. He allowed the red circle to shrink and move past his feet.
In other words, he was eliminated …
Many cultivators looked at that cultivator with pity in their eyes.
It was indeed a pity.

Chapter 767
However, the King of Yan was the King of the Divine Nation! He was also a super demon in the first echelon. So what if he was not satisfied?
The entire Black Iron Divine Nation belonged to him. Who in the Divine Nation would dare to resist him? Who could resist him?
The Divine Nation was like this. It was even more domineering, unreasonable, and unreasonable than the sects.
Therefore, the few Divine Sea Realm Almighties would give face to the King of Yan, a Virtual Tribulation Realm warrior!
However, Luo Zheng did not stop. He continued to move slowly as the red circle shrank. At this moment, his eyes were like water as he stared at the King of Yan in the center.
The King of Yan smiled at Luo Zheng and asked Luo Zheng, "What about you? Are you going to give up too? "
The King of Yan had already grasped Luo Zheng's character. He had always been good at judging people. Such a person would never give up easily!
Of course, Luo Zheng would not give up. He had come so far and this was not a big problem. Was he going to give up?
However, Luo Zheng was in a dilemma. Should he fight with the King of Yan?
This Yan Wang's strength was not weak. Although he had only casually attacked him once, Luo Zheng reckoned that his strength was perhaps slightly stronger than Ling Yan's.
Although Luo Zheng had not seen Liu Yu in the Tower of Sin, Luo Zheng estimated that the King of Yan's strength should be about the same as Liu Yu's when he was promoted to the Peak War Venerable 200 years ago in the Tower of Sin.
Of course, this was just an estimation.
With the strength that Luo Zheng defeated Cui Xie, the King of Yan in front of him was nothing to be afraid of.
However, it was just an assessment now. It was impossible for him to explode with all his strength like a life and death battle.
Therefore, Luo Zheng was considering how to deal with the King of Yan without exposing his strength.
If the King of Yan knew what Luo Zheng was thinking, he would probably faint from anger. However, it was impossible for the King of Yan to guess Luo Zheng's specific thoughts. He was trying his best to test Luo Zheng's strength now!
One breath after another passed.
The red circle was also shrinking. As Luo Zheng moved slowly, the distance between him and the King of Yan was getting closer and closer.
Ten meters, twenty meters, ten meters …
For a Void Tribulation Realm warrior, some moves and methods could no longer be used at such a close distance.
The two of them seemed to have understood something from each other's gaze!
Power!
It was a pure contest of strength!
At this moment, all the disciples in the square held their breaths.
Every martial artist's interest was piqued.
On one hand, they were very impressed by Luo Zheng. He was only in the middle stage of the False Tribulation Realm, yet he had managed to stay in the red circle. A person with such strength was actually unknown. They could not imagine how Luo Zheng had grown.
On the other hand, they also mourned for Luo Zheng in their hearts. Unfortunately, he was assigned to the same round of assessment as Prince of Yan.
If Luo Zheng had avoided King Yan, given his ability, he would have passed the assessment easily …
Now, he had to choose to face King of Yan directly. He wondered what would happen next.
Closer, closer and closer …
Luo Zheng became more and more vigilant.
Something was not right. The King of Yan's ability was probably beyond his estimation.
He had already revealed his strength and used his vital essence. King of Yan knew that he was not only a cultivator of vital essence, but also a cultivator of the body. Under such circumstances, he still dared to compete with him in terms of strength and explosiveness?
It was an extremely wrong choice to close the distance with a cultivator of the body.
However, King of Yan did not have any reservations. He waited for Luo Zheng to come closer. It had to be said that he must have some countermeasures. Perhaps he was also a cultivator of the body and a cultivator of both energy and body?
"Since you insist on not letting me go, I'm sorry." Luo Zheng sighed softly in his heart.
It was not that Luo Zheng looked down on King of Yan. The height that Luo Zheng was at now and the opponent that he had chosen was no longer on the same level as King of Yan.
When the two of them were five meters apart, King of Yan's lips curled up slightly. A beam of light blue vital essence emerged instantly. The moment the vital essence appeared, another beam of vital essence also emerged.
Vital essence and vital essence were two completely different types of energy. Vital essence was born from one's own body, while vital essence was obtained from heaven and earth.
Although Luo Zheng often used his vital essence and vital essence at the same time, King of Yan's vital essence and vital essence had fused into one!
Two completely different types of energy had actually been fused together by him? What the hell was the result of the fusion?
Even though Luo Zheng was very knowledgeable, he was still stunned when he saw this scene.
Sure enough, the Kingdom of Gods could not be underestimated!
After King of Yan had fused his vital essence and vital essence, he spread out his hands and pushed Luo Zheng away forcefully.
King of Yan did not throw out a punch. It seemed that he really wanted to compete purely with Luo Zheng. He did not have any intention of hurting anyone.
Seeing this, Luo Zheng's face revealed a trace of caution. He exerted his strength with both hands and the vital essence in his body exploded. At the same time, Luo Zheng also used the power of the dragon scale!
Originally, Luo Zheng planned to use his vital essence to deal with a warrior in the late stage of the False Tribulation Realm. No matter how powerful this guy was, it should be enough.
However, when Luo Zheng discovered that King of Yan was able to fuse his vital essence and vital essence, he immediately changed his mind. He could not underestimate King of Yan!
Even so, Luo Zheng had only absorbed about half of the power of the dragon scale!
After that, Luo Zheng also pushed out his palms. His speed was not fast, but this slow palm contained a shocking amount of power!
"Puff!"
Luo Zheng's palms and King of Yan's palms collided together.
A dull sound was heard.
The patterned floor beneath their feet suddenly shook violently, and cracks began to appear on it.
"Crack crack crack …"
Since this square was used for the assessment of warriors in the False Tribulation Realm, the materials on the floor had naturally been processed. Previously, when so many warriors in the False Tribulation Realm fought, they did not leave any traces on the floor. However, Luo Zheng and King of Yan were able to completely crack the floor with just one palm strike!
The two types of power had completely fused together. What kind of cultivation technique did King of Yan cultivate to be able to completely fuse his vital essence and vital essence? It was indeed magical!
In the end, Luo Zheng still managed to block this special power. Although the floor beneath his feet had cracked, he did not move at all. He stood at his original spot!
As for King of Yan, under the explosion of Luo Zheng's dragon scale and vital essence, his originally relaxed face suddenly changed color!
Luo Zheng's power was too great!
Although King of Yan vaguely sensed that Luo Zheng was not simple, he did not expect Luo Zheng's power to be so exaggerated!
King of Yan was born in the God's Nation. As he had outstanding talent since he was young, he was deeply doted on by his father. Although there were many princes in the God's Nation, if one had outstanding talent, he would be able to obtain abundant and generous resources! Under such circumstances, how vast was King of Yan's knowledge?
However, he knew that Luo Zheng's power should not have appeared in the False Tribulation Realm!
Even warriors who had experienced the Life and Death Calamity would find it difficult to resist Luo Zheng's power. How did this guy do it?
This power was so difficult to resist. It was like the tide of the sea. It was not something that could be stopped by human strength …
King of Yan was unwilling to retreat. How proud was he? How could he retreat so easily after boasting?
He would rather Luo Zheng retreat and leave the red circle. After this, Luo Zheng would not need to go through any assessment. He was willing to help Luo Zheng get a Black Iron Token!
It was just a Black Iron Token!
However, it was too late to say anything now. Under Luo Zheng's overwhelming power, he had no choice.
Therefore, King of Yan retreated under the watchful eyes of the crowd.
One step, two steps, three steps …
Almost without any suspense, King of Yan crossed the red circle. Moreover, as King of Yan retreated continuously, his footsteps became staggered. In the end, the crowd saw King of Yan's legs turn to jelly and he actually fell to the ground!
Everyone stared blankly at King of Yan, who was in a sorry state.
King of Yan, who was in the first echelon, had an overwhelming power over the younger generation. In the Black Jade Martial House, only Zhan Yun could follow in King of Yan's footsteps. Other than that, in the entire God's Nation, only Xiao Daoyuan and the Crown Prince could match up to him!
Although the two of them did not fight to the death and could not even be considered an ordinary exchange of blows, it was purely a contest of strength.
However, there was no doubt that in a contest of strength, King of Yan was at an absolute disadvantage!

Chapter 768
Around the square, hundreds of False Tribulation Realm warriors were silent as they stared blankly at the scene in front of them.
The eyes of the three Divine Sea Realm warriors shone brightly!
These three Divine Sea Realm warriors were the Mansion Masters of the Black Jade Martial House. One of them was the main Mansion Master, while the other two were the Deputy Mansion Masters. They were considered the mainstays of the Black Iron Divine Nation! Naturally, they knew King Yan's strength very well.
The King of Yan was not only a dual cultivator of body and energy. More importantly, his Dual Physique could perfectly fuse his Vital Essence and Vital Essence together with the cultivation techniques of the royal family. With such a fusion, the strength he could unleash would far surpass his peers.
It was true that King Yan did not use his trump card just now. He only used the fusion power. However, the ancient martial art practitioner called "Luo Tianxing" did not use any moves either. He only used his vital essence and pure strength!
Even so, Yan Wang was still at a disadvantage in the fight with Luo Zheng. How could the three Mansion Masters of the Profound Jade Martial House not be surprised?
Could this kid be ranked in the first tier? Could his strength be on par with King Yan and the Crown Prince?
Regardless of whether they believed it or not, what they saw now had proven their conjecture. Moreover, it was not just a matter of being on par. Luo Tianxing's strength might be even stronger than King Yan's!
"Old He, we misjudged him. This kid is a freak. I really don't know where he came from!"
"Hehehe, no matter what, our Black Iron Martial House has profited this time. Let's not talk about the dispute between King Yan and the Crown Prince. Since this kid is participating in our Black Jade Martial House's assessment, he must have been discovered by our Black Jade Martial House!"
"Luck, luck. That Xiao Daoyuan from the Black Jade Martial House is firmly suppressing our Black Jade Martial House. I think Luo Tianxing is on par with Xiao Daoyuan!"
The number one False Tribulation Realm warrior of the Black Jade Martial House, Zhan Yun, stared at Luo Zheng with a faint excitement on his face.
"Luo Tianxing, Luo Tianxing …" It was a very unfamiliar name. He had never heard of such a person in the Black Iron Divine Nation. Zhan Yun's heart thumped rapidly. If this was another place, Zhan Yun would have rushed up to challenge Luo Zheng. However, this was the Martial House assessment and in front of the three Mansion masters. Zhan Yun could not break the rules.
Zhan Yun did not dare to break the Profound Jade Martial House's rules, but Yan Wang was different.
Although there were many princes in Black Iron Immortal Kingdom, the status of each prince was greatly different from that of the other princes. As he had been crowned as a lord, half of Hegu City was his fief. Who could stop him from challenging anyone?
"Haha, Luo Tianxing, right? Good, very good! " A maniacal smile appeared on King Yan's face. He stared at Luo Zheng and said with a smile, "You have passed the assessment. This matter will come to an end for the time being. Then, I will challenge you now!"
As soon as King Yan finished speaking, a surging aura gushed out from his body. Under the envelopment of this aura, many Virtual Tribulation Realm cultivators immediately became agitated.
This was not an aura that a False Tribulation Realm cultivator could possess!
"King Yan's true strength is probably comparable to a False Tribulation Realm warrior!"
"He's really strong. I reckon that even a first stage False Tribulation Realm martial artist is no match for him!"
"That kid surnamed Luo is probably in trouble …"
Enveloped by King Yan's aura, Luo Zheng frowned slightly. He was very displeased. Why is this guy acting like this? Luo Zheng was here to participate in the assessment, not to challenge others. Why is he challenging me for no reason?
Hence, Luo Zheng shook his head. "I refuse!"
Hearing Luo Zheng's reply, King Yan was slightly stunned. "Refuse? What a joke! Who can easily reject the challenge of the Prince of Yan? Don't you want the Black Iron Token? If you agree to my challenge, regardless of whether you win or lose, I can give you the Black Iron Token and various rare treasures and elixirs for you to choose from. What do you think? "
King Yan's reward …
Everyone's eyes suddenly shot out a fiery glow.
Being crowned as a lord of Black Iron Immortal Kingdom meant that he had a lot of wealth. Just King Yan's wealth alone was comparable to the Black Jade Martial House's accumulation over the years. If they could enter King Yan's Mansion and casually choose a few treasures, they would make a fortune.
The problem was that regardless of whether they won or lost …
Even if they lost to King Yan, it would not be a shameful thing, right?
That kid Luo Tianxing is so lucky! That was what everyone thought in their hearts.
However, Luo Zheng still shook his head. "I refuse. I only need the Black Iron Token. With my strength, I can get it naturally. I don't need you to give it to me."
How arrogant was the King of Yan? He felt that his words were already very forthright! I, King Yan, am already giving you a lot of face by challenging you. Regardless of whether you win or lose, I will reward you handsomely. He did not expect Luo Zheng to be so unappreciative of his kindness!
King Yan's face instantly turned dark red. His face was filled with anger. He was like a volcano that was about to erupt at any time.
"Sigh, King Yan is talented, but he is still too young." A Divine Sea Realm almighty sighed deeply in his heart.
The three Mansion Masters of Black Jade Martial House also felt the same.
Now, in the entire Black Iron Immortal Kingdom, the Crown Prince and King Yan, the two youths with the royal bloodline, were fighting for supremacy!
King Yan was the youngest, but he was still firmly in the first echelon. It was undeniable that his future was more dazzling than that of the Crown Prince.
However, the Crown Prince's strength was similar to that of King Yan. After all, he was more than ten years older than King Yan. His character was also more mature than King Yan. Not only did the Crown Prince rope in Black Jade Martial House's Zhan Yun, but he also roped in Xiao Daoyuan, the number one expert of the younger generation in Black Jade Martial House.
There were only four warriors in the first echelon. Among them, the Crown Prince, Zhan Yun, and Xiao Daoyuan were in a group.
The most troublesome thing about King Yan was that he did not have any followers. Luo Tianxing was an excellent target. It seemed that King Yan wanted to offend Luo Tianxing!
How could this be?
Indeed, they could see that King Yan did not have any ill intentions towards Luo Zheng. He challenged Luo Zheng purely because he was unconvinced. They guessed that King Yan cherished talents and could not find a way out of this embarrassing situation. Therefore, he insisted on challenging Luo Tianxing.
The problem was that such monstrous characters were all unusually arrogant. It was inappropriate for King Yan to use his own name to suppress others. It was fine if Luo Tianxing did not accept his challenge, but how could he expect Luo Tianxing to bow down to him?
The three Divine Sea Realm experts sighed and felt that it was necessary to remind King Yan. Therefore, one of the Divine Sea Realm experts took a step forward with a benevolent smile on his face. He stared at King Yan and said, "It is not easy for King Yan to have the interest to issue a challenge today. However, this is the Black Jade Martial House's assessment after all. I am afraid it is inappropriate to issue a challenge here.
"Mansion Master He!" King Yan raised his eyebrows. He was still young and his character was extremely stubborn. Although he knew that he should be on good terms with Luo Tianxing now, he could not control his temper at this moment.
"King Yan, listen to me," Mansion Master He said with a faint smile. Then, his lips moved slightly as he spoke to King Yan using his True Energy Sound Transmission. Others could not understand what he was saying.
After a long while, King Yan nodded slightly. The aura in his body suddenly disappeared and was replaced by a carefree expression. Instead, he clasped his hands towards Luo Zheng and said, "Today, we only exchanged a blow. I, King Yan, have lost! However, I will treat this as a gift to a friend. I will invite you to a banquet in my King Yan's Mansion another day! "
Luo Zheng, on the other hand, was baffled. The contrast between Prince of Yan's attitude before and after was too great. Who knew what he was up to?
Of course, Luo Zheng did not know that there were various conflicts everywhere in the God's Kingdom. He had unintentionally exchanged a blow with King Yan and had implicated himself.
However, whatever medicine was sold had nothing to do with Luo Zheng. The banquet in his Mansion was just a formality. He was in the God's Kingdom and did not want to embarrass King Yan too much. Therefore, Luo Zheng nodded and clasped his hands towards King Yan.
After that, King Yan turned his head and walked away. The atmosphere at the scene finally relaxed.
However, Zhan Yun, who was quietly observing from the side, stared at King Yan's back as he left. Then, he secretly glanced at Luo Zheng. A trace of vigilance flashed in his eyes.
Although Zhan Yun was a martial artist of the Jade Profound Martial House, he was the Crown Prince's man. The conflict between the Crown Prince and King Yan had been going on for a long time. Now that Luo Zheng had appeared, whether Zhan Yun could challenge Luo Zheng was a secondary matter. However, such a person must not follow King Yan!
He had to inform the Crown Prince about this matter as soon as possible!
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 769
Luo Zheng didn't care about the internal strife of the Divine Kingdom, he only cared about how he was going to get the Black Iron Order.
After passing the test today, some people were sent back to the courtyard where they had registered. After passing the test, Luo Zheng was now considered a member of the Profound Jade Martial Palace.
The Divine Core female disciple who was in charge of registration threw a curious glance at Luo Zheng. She was curious, how did Luo Zheng pass the test with just a Mid False Tribulation realm cultivation base?
Using the bamboo token in his hand, Luo Zheng exchanged it for another purple token. This token was also not a black-iron token. Immediately, Luo Zheng was informed that he could use this token to enter the Profound Jade Martial House and study. He could also obtain a portion of the Profound Jade Martial House's resources and so on.
If it was any other independent warrior, they might be extremely excited. It was extremely difficult for an independent warrior to cultivate, and being able to join the Martial Palace was a great opportunity for them. But for Luo Zheng, it didn't mean much. He didn't lack any cultivation skills or pills, and he relied on himself to cultivate. The Martial Palace couldn't provide him with much help, and the only thing that could help him was this Black Iron Order.
After leaving the Profound Jade Martial Palace, Cao Junfeng had been waiting for him for a long time.
Luo Zheng had ordered him to wait for him here, and Cao Junfeng couldn't disobey this order. Accompanied by Cao Junfeng, Luo Zheng returned to his own courtyard.
Along the way, Luo Zheng was playing with the purple token in his hand. After thinking for a moment, he asked, "After I get the Black Iron Order, how long will it take for me to enter the Heavenly Feather Saint Ocean?"
Cao Junfeng replied with a smile, "Normally, the three great secret realms will open every year, but I'm not sure which one will open this year."
Luo Zheng knew that the Heavenly Feather Saint Ocean contained the Feather Emperor, Divine Emperor, and Dream God Secret Realms. Apart from that, there was also the Heavenly Star Secret Realm. Luo Zheng didn't care about the other secret realms, but if the Feather Emperor Secret Realm didn't open this year, Luo Zheng would feel depressed.
"Can you find out which secret realm will open today?" Luo Zheng furrowed his brows.
"Yes! However, the opening of each secret realm is controlled by the Monarchs of the four Divine Kingdoms. The four Monarchs have the final say on which secret realm to open. If you want to find out … it will be difficult for you, "Cao Junfeng replied.
Luo Zheng heaved a sigh of relief. If the other secret realms were to be opened this year, it was very likely that he would have to wait another year. However, there was not much time left for Luo Zheng. What should he do? With his current strength, it was impossible for him to influence the plans of the four Divine Kingdoms.
"I see. You can leave now," Luo Zheng said calmly.
Cao Junfeng nodded and left Luo Zheng's room.
Luo Zheng sat cross-legged. He circulated his Qi and slowly spread out his True Essence. He was preparing to cultivate quietly for a while.
However, at that moment, the Qinglong in his mind spoke again. "Luo Zheng, there is another mystic realm in the Sky Feather Sacred Ocean besides the Feather Emperor Mystic Realm. You must enter it."
"Hmm?" Luo Zheng's eyes flashed slightly. When he heard Qinglong's words, his face was filled with a strange expression.
Previously, he had obtained the information about the Sky Feather Sacred Ocean and the other secret realms from Cao Junfeng. Now that Qinglong suddenly mentioned them, could it be that these secret realms were related to Qinglong and the others?
Luo Zheng's goal was only the Feather Emperor Mystic Realm. Since the Life Origin Stone was obtained there, Luo Zheng naturally did not have much interest in the other secret realms.
"In addition to the three great secret realms, there is also the Tian Chen Secret Realm, right? You must enter the Tian Chen Secret Realm, "Qinglong said again.
"What do you need in the Tian Chen Secret Realm?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
Luo Zheng already knew that the nine Real Dragons in his mind actually came from the Real Dragon World. However, why the nine Real Dragons were attached to the mysterious furnace and why they turned into a page and finally came out of Luo Zheng's mind was a mystery.
He had asked Qinglong before, but every time he mentioned it, Qinglong did not give him any details. It seemed that there was something that he could not say.
When Luo Zheng entered the Real Dragon World through the Rising Dragon Platform, Qinglong was even more so. He was even unwilling to reveal his identity to the warriors of the Dragon Bloodline Clan.
Luo Zheng guessed that Qinglong might have some enmity with the Real Dragon Clan, or even some great hatred.
In the end, it was just a guess. If the Real Dragon did not mention it, Luo Zheng would not take the initiative to ask.
Even when Luo Zheng entered the Sea God Continent, Qinglong rarely gave any specific advice or took the initiative to ask for anything. This time, however, he had asked him to enter the Tian Chen Secret Realm. Presumably, there was something important in the Tian Chen Secret Realm that was related to the nine brothers.
Qinglong smiled sadly in Luo Zheng's mind and said, "Do you know the origin of the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea?"
Luo Zheng shook his head.
Luo Zheng was unfamiliar with the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea. He had only learned about it from Cao Junfeng. Because of these words, Luo Zheng had spared Cao Junfeng's life.
When Luo Zheng crossed the Chaotic Astral Ocean and rushed to the Black Iron Divine Kingdom, he discovered that the vast inland sea was actually built with towering walls …
He did not know why the four Divine Kingdoms had spent so much effort. What was it for?
"The Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea was originally the territory of the Real Dragon Clan," Qinglong said indifferently.
"The territory of the Real Dragon Clan?" Luo Zheng's eyebrows twitched as he asked softly.
"To be precise, it was the territory of the Dragon Bloodline Clan, a subordinate clan of the Real Dragon Clan," Qinglong said indifferently.
In the past, the Dragon Bloodline Clan was the true ruler of this continent. They occupied the most central area of the Divine Kingdom Continent, the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea. At the time, the Human Clan had the seven Divine Kingdoms, and they had allied together to resist the Dragon Bloodline Clan.
The war had been intermittent for tens of thousands of years.
In the end, the Dragon Bloodline Clan had been forced by the allied forces of the seven Divine Kingdoms to open a spatial passage with a blood sacrifice. They had released a dragon from the True Dragon Realm. With a single breath, they had destroyed three Divine Kingdoms.
However, the blood sacrifice had cost a considerable amount. At the time, the seven Divine Kingdoms had the support of the Human Clan from the Upper World. In the end, the remaining four Divine Kingdoms had destroyed the Dragon Bloodline Clan, resulting in the current situation.
"The so-called Four Great Mystic Realms, hehe, are actually the remnants of the Dragon Bloodline's foundation in this world. It's just that before the Dragon Bloodline's human race was destroyed, they used the power of blood sacrifice to seal off the entire Heavenly Feather Sacred Ocean with the lives of the entire race! It is not easy to take away the inheritance of the Dragon Bloodline Clan, "Qinglong said.
"It's just that after so many years, the power of the blood sacrifice of the Dragon Bloodline Clan has gradually weakened. The four Divine Kingdoms that were left behind have used the inheritance of the Dragon Bloodline Clan as a treasure trove and slowly developed. I didn't expect that a small sixth-grade Divine Kingdom would develop to this stage!"
In the past, the seven Divine Kingdoms had only been equivalent to sixth-rank Sects. However, the current four Divine Kingdoms were almost equivalent to eighth-rank Sects!
The difference between these two ranks was like the difference between heaven and earth!
On the Sea God Continent, the Demon Night Clan led by Liu Yu was only a seventh-rank Holy Land.
"What is in the Heavenly Chen Secret Realm?" Luo Zheng asked curiously. He could not think of anything else that Qinglong was determined to obtain.
Qinglong said, "In the past, the Dragon Bloodline Clan in the Lower World summoned the real dragon through the blood sacrifice. He is our big brother."
"Big brother?"
Luo Zheng's face was filled with surprise.
Nine real dragons were drawn on the mysterious furnace. The nine real dragons were of different sizes and lengths. However, right below the furnace, there was the thickest and most majestic real dragon! However, that real dragon had never awakened. So far, only Qinglong and the red dragon had awakened.
"Yes, our Dragon Bloodline Clan is spread across millions of Great Thousand Worlds. Even with the blood sacrifice, it is difficult to summon a real dragon. At that time, my big brother took action personally because the Dragon Bloodline Clan found an Ancestral Dragon egg in this world! This Ancestral Dragon egg is now in the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea, the last Heavenly Chen Secret Realm! "Qinglong said.
"Ancestral Dragon egg? What's the big deal?" Luo Zheng was confused.

Chapter 770
"Whoever controls the Ancestral Dragon is equivalent to controlling the True Dragon Clan! As the most powerful Super Divine Beasts, of course, we won't give the Ancestral Dragon egg to the Human Clan! "the red dragon continued.
Luo Zheng didn't say anything and continued to listen to the red dragon.
The red dragon said, "Therefore, the war on the Divine Kingdom Continent is actually a war between the True Dragon Clan and the Human Clan in the Lower World. Unfortunately, the Human Clan never expected that after my elder brother was summoned by the blood sacrifice, he destroyed the three small Divine Kingdoms. At the moment when the space collapsed, he used a secret method to destroy the ascension passage of this Great Thousand World! Therefore, all the creatures in this world can't ascend! "
"What …" Luo Zheng's eyes suddenly flashed.
The problem of not being able to ascend had always troubled this Great Thousand World.
Originally, when one's cultivation reached the Divine Sea Realm, he had already stepped into the Way of Immortals and broken away from the category of mortals!
For example, Situ Haotian, Liu Yu, and the Divine Sea Realm masters of the four Divine Kingdoms had been staying in this Great Thousand World and couldn't ascend!
Apart from the Rising Dragon Platform, there was no other way to ascend!
All the creatures knew that there seemed to be some problems in this Great Thousand World, but there was no correct answer as to what the problem was.
In the end, all the Divine Ocean Realm cultivators who could have ascended were still trapped in this Great Thousand World. They could only watch as they entered the Five Decay of Immortals, slowly exhausted their lifespans, and died of depression …
Luo Zheng didn't expect that the one who destroyed the ascension passage of this Great Thousand World was Qinglong's elder brother.
"Why did you destroy the ascension passage in the first place?" Luo Zheng asked again.
Qinglong sighed and then said, "Naturally, it's because of the Ancestral Dragon. This Ancestral Dragon egg was born in this Great Thousand World, so the True Dragon Clan naturally has to take care of it carefully and let it hatch slowly. However, the Human Clan also noticed this, so the essence of this war is around the Ancestral Dragon egg."
"I see." Luo Zheng was silent for a while. "In other words, you let me enter the Heavenly Star Secret Realm to obtain the Ancestral Dragon egg and send it to your True Dragon Clan?"
"True Dragon Clan? Hmph! "The crimson dragon snorted coldly. His voice was filled with anger.
"Seventh Brother …" The crimson dragon seemed to have something to say, but he was stopped by Qinglong. Then, Qinglong said, "Yes, I want you to take the Ancestral Dragon egg, but not necessarily send it back to the True Dragon Clan."
Luo Zheng was getting more and more confused. It seemed that Qinglong and the crimson dragon had some conflicts with the True Dragon Clan. When Luo Zheng entered the True Dragon Realm last time, he had already noticed some problems, but as an outsider, he naturally didn't say anything.
Luo Zheng would try his best to help him. After all, everything that Luo Zheng had achieved was thanks to Qinglong and the others.
However, Luo Zheng had his own doubts. "Even if the Ancestral Dragon egg is indeed in the Heavenly Star Secret Realm, those Divine Sea Realm experts can't obtain it. With my current strength, I'm afraid it's not enough!"
Luo Zheng didn't know the connection between these secret realms, but since these secret realms were in the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea, and the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea was almost surrounded by the four Divine Kingdoms, those Divine Sea Realm experts would have entered long ago if the Ancestral Dragon egg could be obtained.
Qinglong smiled. "The Heavenly Star Secret Realm is different from the other three secret realms. Of course, they can't obtain it, but if it's you, there's a slight chance."
Since Qinglong said so, Luo Zheng nodded silently. "In that case, I'll do my best!"
Although Luo Zheng wasn't confident that he could help Qinglong and the others obtain the Ancestral Dragon egg, since Qinglong had asked, Luo Zheng had to try. Of course, he had to enter the Heavenly Feather Secret Realm first and obtain the Origin Stone of Life.
Whether he could make it or not depended on the next step.
In this house, Luo Zheng didn't leave the house. He focused on cultivation every day. Without Luo Zheng's order, Cao Junfeng also stayed at the door.
However, three days later, Luo Zheng suddenly heard subtle footsteps at the door. Then, he heard a shrill voice. "The Crown Prince has come in person. Unrelated people, please leave for now!"
"The Crown Prince?" Luo Zheng frowned slightly.
Then, he heard Cao Junfeng's voice outside. "Master, the Crown Prince of the Black Iron Divine Kingdom is here!"
"Hmm," Luo Zheng replied. Then, he got up and went outside. He wore a faint smile on his face, but he felt quite strange. Why did the Crown Prince of the Black Iron Divine Kingdom come to find him?
As soon as Luo Zheng went out, he saw a big lineup outside the house. Dozens of Life and Death Realm experts, dressed in golden brocade robes, with silver sabers hanging from their waists, lined up at the door. Luo Zheng glanced at them casually. These sabers were at least low-grade saint tools. More importantly, each saber looked exactly the same.
It seemed that the Black Iron Divine Kingdom had the skill to mass produce saint tools.
In front of these Life and Death Realm guards, there was a young man sitting in a wheelchair. The young man looked sickly, but his eyes flickered with a strange luster.
Seeing Luo Zheng looking at him, the young man smiled faintly.
At this moment, a nearby eunuch said in a shrill voice, "Why don't you kneel down and salute the Crown Prince?"
How could Luo Zheng be willing to kneel down? He just coldly glanced at the eunuch, thinking to himself, "Why did you come to me and ask me to kneel down? What kind of logic is this?"
Unexpectedly, the Crown Prince rebuked, "Yu An, what nonsense are you talking about? This friend here is one of the top geniuses in the Black Iron Divine Nation. A man should not kneel to anyone. How can he kneel to anyone?"
"But Crown Prince, you are the emperor. In the future …" The eunuch called Yu An defended himself.
"Shut up!" The Crown Prince scolded him again. Then, he smiled at Luo Zheng and said, "My servants are used to this. Brother Tianxing, please don't take offense!"
Luo Zheng just looked at the scene coldly. He could tell at a glance that the Crown Prince and the eunuch were putting on a show. Their purpose was to give him the impression that the Crown Prince thought highly of him. The Crown Prince addressed him as "brother". However, this was the first time Luo Zheng and the Crown Prince had met. Playing such a trick gave Luo Zheng a feeling that the Crown Prince was very scheming.
Luo Zheng was not happy.
Moreover, he did not want to attract attention when he entered the Black Iron Divine Kingdom. In front of him was Prince Yan, and behind him was the Crown Prince. The more he did not want to be noticed, the more these guys insisted on coming to his door.
However, Luo Zheng could not act recklessly. After all, he was in someone else's territory.
Thus, Luo Zheng asked in a neutral tone, "What brings you here, Crown Prince of the Divine Kingdom?"
The Crown Prince smiled and said, "I heard that Brother Tianxing passed the Black Jade Martial House's assessment. I also heard that you were an independent warrior. Therefore, I wanted to see Brother Tianxing's elegant demeanor. Today, I see that you are indeed impressive. You are a dragon among men."
Luo Zheng thought for a moment and a smile appeared on his face. "Crown Prince, you are too polite. I, Luo Tianxing, am just a boor."
The Crown Prince continued to smile and said, "Brother Tianxing is too polite. Today, my residence is hosting a banquet. I hope that Brother Luo Zheng will do me the honor of attending." As he said this, the Crown Prince gave a meaningful look to the eunuch beside him, Yu An.
The eunuch's face lit up with a courteous smile. He took out a golden name card and handed it to Luo Zheng.
Looking at the invitation card, Luo Zheng was a little hesitant. Should he accept or not?
By now, Luo Zheng understood the Crown Prince's intention. He was most likely trying to rope him in like Prince Yan. However, compared to Prince Yan, the Crown Prince's attitude was more polite.
However, this was often the case in this world. The more polite someone was to you, the more beneficial they might be to you.
Just as Luo Zheng was hesitating, a voice came from not far away. "Big Brother, you came to Lonely Crane City, but you didn't even come to see me. You are too mean!"
The person who spoke was Prince Yan.
This time, the King of Yan did not bring anyone with him. He was alone.
He was dressed in a green robe and walked over confidently. He looked at the Crown Prince and then looked at Luo Zheng. He smiled and said, "Luo Tianxing, I trust you have been well since we last met!"

Chapter 771
A few days ago in the Black Jade Martial House, Prince Yan was still acting as if he would not give up until he was defeated.
Why was he so courteous today, as if they had been friends for many years …
Luo Zheng suddenly felt uncomfortable. However, a smile still appeared on Luo Zheng's face. He smiled at Prince Yan and said, "How have you been?"
Prince Yan walked towards Luo Zheng and took the golden invitation from Luo Zheng's hand. After glancing at it casually, he returned it to the Crown Prince and said indifferently, "Unfortunately, Luo Zheng has agreed to come to the Prince Yan's residence today. Brother, you are indeed late!"
This invitation was a hot potato in Luo Zheng's hands. Luo Zheng did not know how to deal with it. He did not expect Prince Yan to take it away and return it to the Crown Prince. However, when did he promise Prince Yan?
However, Prince Yan had helped Luo Zheng out of his predicament. Although Prince Yan was hot-tempered, he was more straightforward. Luo Zheng's first impression of the Crown Prince was that he was too shrewd.
Instead of being forcefully invited to the residence by the Crown Prince, he might as well cooperate with Prince Yan and get the show over with.
After Luo Zheng made up his mind, he smiled and said, "Your Highness is indeed late. I was also invited by Prince Yan earlier."
Hearing Luo Zheng's words, Prince Yan smiled even more brightly.
When Prince Yan's realm was still low, he was not noticed by the Crown Prince. Over the past few years, as Prince Yan's strength increased day by day, he gradually had the intention to be on par with the Crown Prince. The two gradually became enemies.
However, the Crown Prince was more than ten years older than Prince Yan. Moreover, he was chosen as the Crown Prince, which meant that he would be the future Crown Prince. Almost all the young experts in the God Nation stood on the Crown Prince's side. Although Prince Yan was conferred the title of Prince and proved his strength and talent, he could not make a comeback because of his disadvantage.
Today, the Crown Prince found out that there was a martial artist who defeated Prince Yan in a competition of strength among the independent martial artists in the Black Jade Martial House. How could the Crown Prince let such a person go? Therefore, he rushed to Hecu City from the capital overnight. His purpose was to rope in Luo Zheng.
However, he did not expect the King of Yan to appear on the way.
With the shrewdness of the Crown Prince of God Nation, how could he not see that Luo Zheng and King of Yan were putting on an act? However, he couldn't force the Crown Prince to do something against his will. Thus, the Crown Prince smiled magnanimously and said, "Since that's the case, I apologize for my abrupt visit."
"Brother, you're not just being rude. As a younger brother, I really admire your speed, "King Yan retorted sarcastically.
The Crown Princess' expression did not change, but there was a faint gleam that flashed in her eyes. She grabbed the wheelchair and turned it around. Then she led the many experts and left.
Looking in the direction where the Crown Prince left, a cold smile appeared on the King of Yan's face. It was the expression of a victor.
Luo Zheng shook his head. "Since we are brothers, why do we have to be so hostile?"
Luo Zheng did not understand the internal strife of the Kingdom of God Nation's royal family. Although the world of ancient martial art practitioners was dangerous and most people were extremely selfish, blood was thicker than water. The gains did not make up for the losses.
However, the King of Yan said coldly, "I treat him as my elder brother, but he doesn't treat me as his brother."
Seeing the King of Yan's expression, Luo Zheng knew that the King of Yan was definitely immersed in some bad memories.
However, this was the King of Yan's family matter. Luo Zheng could not ask about it.
Then, the King of Yan thought for a while. Suddenly, he revealed a sly smile at Luo Zheng. "Didn't you agree to go to my King of Yan's residence for a chat?"
"This …" Luo Zheng said this just now because he wanted to reject the Crown Prince's invitation. Now that the King of Yan spoke again, Luo Zheng did not know how to reject him.
"Don't worry. I won't make things difficult for you. We just need to decide who is better between us." The King of Yan continued to smile. At the same time, he lowered his voice and said, "My elder brother does things flawlessly. He will definitely send someone to keep an eye on you. If you don't go, he will definitely come again …"
Luo Zheng could only reveal a bitter smile. It seemed that he had no choice but to go. He could only nod.
Although the King of Yan's character was not likable, at least he was more straightforward than the Crown Prince. If Luo Zheng had to choose a side, he would rather choose the King of Yan's side.
Following a scheming person always made people on tenterhooks.
Therefore, under the invitation of the King of Yan, Luo Zheng brought Cao Junfeng to the King of Yan's residence.
The King of Yan's residence was located in the south of Hegu City. It was a huge residence. The construction of the residence was extraordinary. The scenery in the residence was exquisite and luxurious.
Just as they entered the residence with the King of Yan, four Life and Death Realm experts quickly surrounded them. They looked rather nervous. One of them even asked, "Your Majesty, where did you go just now? It took us a long time to find you."
The King of Yan looked at the Life and Death Realm experts coldly. Then, he asked, "Why? Do I still need to report my whereabouts to you all the time? "
"This … is not the case." One of the Life and Death Realm experts lowered his head and answered.
However, the King of Yan snorted coldly and walked forward. The four Life and Death Realm experts also wanted to follow. However, the King of Yan said, "The four of you, go and guard the door!"
"We …"
Although there were many Life and Death Realm experts in the Kingdom of God, they were not reduced to guarding the door. The four people's faces darkened.
"Why?" The King of Yan raised his eyebrows. "My words are of no use at all?"
Seeing that the King of Yan seemed to be about to explode, the four Life and Death Realm experts could only retreat and walk towards the door of the King of Yan's residence.
The King of Yan looked at Luo Zheng meaningfully. Then, he chuckled. "It's quite funny, isn't it?"
Luo Zheng also saw some clues. Then, he asked, "These people …"
"Hehe, everyone in the King of Yan's residence, from the guards to the servants, from the handymen to the maidservants, are all people arranged by my brother!" The King of Yan gritted his teeth and said.
At this moment, a woman dressed in silk walked out of the mansion. This woman was also at the False Tribulation realm. She was as beautiful as a celestial fairy. Her expensive silk clothes were tailored to fit her body perfectly. As she walked over with small steps and casually glanced up, it was enough to make all the men's hearts palpitate. This woman was definitely an excellent companion for a martial artist.
"Your Majesty is back! I am … "The woman had just opened her mouth when the King of Yan interrupted her." Get lost! "
"Your Majesty …" The woman was scolded by the King of Yan. Tears rolled down her face.
"Didn't you hear that I asked you to get lost?" The King of Yan scolded coldly and angrily.
The woman stood there for a while. Her face was full of grievance. In the end, under the King of Yan's scolding, she slowly retreated.
Then, the King of Yan smiled sadly. "I forgot to say. Even my wife is my brother's woman!"
This woman was also the Crown Prince's woman …
Luo Zheng was speechless.
The King of Yan had such talent and strength at such a young age. In the eyes of others, he was also someone who was envious and jealous of. They did not expect him to live in such an environment.
No wonder he had such an attitude towards the Crown Prince. There was probably a deep grudge between the two of them.
In fact, the fact that the King of Yan and the Crown Prince were not on good terms in the Black Iron Divine Kingdom had already spread throughout the entire continent. Even Cao Junfeng knew about this clearly.
The King of Yan was too outstanding. Although he had been conferred the title of King by the King, so what? As long as he was outstanding enough, he could still snatch the position of the Crown Prince when he grew up.
Because of his father, the Crown Prince of Black Iron Divine Kingdom could not attack his younger brother. However, he could restrict the King of Yan in all aspects.
Therefore, until now, the King of Yan did not even have a single person on his side!
The King of Yan was not a fool. He also wanted to develop his own power. Not to mention other things, just entering the mystic realm of the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea was not a matter for him alone.
Back then, the Crown Prince had hypocritically helped the King of Yan to form a team. The King of Yan naturally rejected such good intentions. The problem was, who could the King of Yan look for after rejecting the Crown Prince's offer?
Almost all the experts of the younger generation had been won over by the Crown Prince. As for the remaining False Tribulation Realm ancient martial art practitioners, the King of Yan did not take a fancy to them. Under such circumstances, the King of Yan still roped in a few ancient martial art practitioners who could barely enter the second echelon. However, the King of Yan knew that he was still too weak and could not compete with his brother.
This time, after encountering Luo Zheng, he had made up his mind to compete with Luo Zheng. It was only under the guidance of the Mansion Master of the Black Jade Martial House that the King of Yan managed to restrain himself. He hoped that Luo Zheng would be a great help to him.
When the two of them entered the main hall of the King of Yan's Mansion, Luo Zheng instructed Cao Junfeng to wait at the door. Then, a maidservant came to serve tea. After she was done, the King of Yan chased the servants out again.
The King of Yan then stared at Luo Zheng with burning eyes and asked, "Why did you enter the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea? I can cooperate with you."
