Chapter 772
Luo Nian couldn't convey his shock to Jiang Ziya, so he stared blankly for a long time.
Jiang Ziya stood there, wondering why Luo Nian didn't move.
However, the changes in the light spots still made Jiang Ziya suspicious.
Jiang Ziya used to be in a low-lying area in the World of Xuan Liang, so the things he could see were limited. Moreover, Jiang Ziya couldn't tell the difference between heaven and earth in the World of Xuan Liang.
Now, what appeared in front of Jiang Ziya were countless light spots …
Those light spots were dim and far away, but there were so many of them that it was beyond Jiang Ziya's range of acceptance.
When Jiang Ziya tried to distinguish these light spots, he felt an explosion of pain in his head, as if someone had stabbed him in the head with a knife.
In desperation, Jiang Ziya quickly diverted his attention and stopped trying to distinguish the dense light spots. After all, it was futile for him to distinguish them.
"There seems to be some buildings in the center of the four pillars …"
Looking into the distance, Luo Nian was also carefully distinguishing. He could faintly see a huge palace, but it was too far away, so it was still blurry.
After looking for a while, Luo Nian pulled Jiang Ziya forward.
Since there was a new discovery, Luo Nian didn't plan to retreat.
On the way to the four pillars, Luo Nian didn't forget Jiang Ziya's request and kept searching.
Other than the World of Chaos, he had already found the World of Poison Fog and the Empty World. He should be able to find more "worlds" along the way.
In fact, after staying in the World of Xuan Liang for a long time, Luo Nian had an illusion that these "worlds" weren't worlds, but huge caves, or another low-level space.
In comparison, the World of Xuan Liang was more harmonious.
Everything had energy. The energy in the low-level worlds like the World of Poison Fog and the World of Chaos were hidden. For example, a stone, a handful of water, or a blade of grass had energy hidden in them, and they only needed to be activated by various means.
Everything in the World of Chaos appeared in the form of light spots. The light spots themselves were a type of energy, so their existence was more direct and effective …
Of course, this was just Luo Nian's own opinion.
After all, he could move around in the World of Xuan Liang with ease …
For the creatures in these "worlds", the World of Xuan Liang was like a nightmare.
After constant searching, Luo Nian finally found the fourth world.
This world was soaked in a strange type of mercury, or rather, the entire world was filled with this type of mercury.
His Divine Sense was completely blocked by the mercury. Jiang Ziya could not use his Divine Sense to scan that world. At the same time, the Law of Space was also affected.
The distance of the Greater Teleportation was short. After a few teleportations, Jiang Ziya found that the mercury in this world was constantly flowing, and the space coordinates that were originally marked were also changing.
If they explored too deeply, they might get lost in the Mercury World. Jiang Ziya had no choice but to bring Luo Nian back.
In the process of exploring the Mercury World, Luo Nian also told Jiang Ziya about the four indescribably huge pillars and the looming palace. Jiang Ziya immediately connected the dots, and a trace of uncontrollable excitement appeared on his face.
It was no longer a coincidence that they had found nothing in three consecutive "worlds".
Those worlds must have been destroyed …
As for why the Chaotic World was able to escape this calamity, it was still unknown, but perhaps some answers could be obtained from the four huge pillars.
For the rest of the journey, Luo Nian no longer deliberately searched for those "worlds". Instead, he headed straight for the huge pillar.
…
The self-ouroboros in the Ultimate Circulation space stopped rotating, and the picture in front of Luo Zheng also disappeared completely.
Even though the picture had completely disappeared, Luo Zheng still could not pull himself out of the deep shock.
The huge Chaotic World was just a tool to provide energy for others. This result was indeed difficult to accept.
After leaving the Ultimate Circulation space, Luo Zheng returned to the first space, which was the space of infinite proliferation.
As soon as he entered this space, a trace of coldness suddenly blew from behind him.
At the same time, he heard a weird and cold voice.
"Do you want to stay here …?"
A little boy with a frightening appearance opened his mouth and asked.
When Luo Zheng had just entered this space, he had been chased by the little boy all the way.
Although Luo Zheng could fight back, under the infinite proliferation of the little boy, Luo Zheng had no choice but to escape …
But now, the moment Luo Zheng gazed at the little boy, he felt the Divine Blood of Origin in his body surging, like ripples on a lake. The attribute of the Divine Blood of Origin was constantly changing, and the energy of several attributes of life and destruction merged together.
"I can stay here," Luo Zheng raised his finger.
A grey thing appeared on the tip of his finger. It looked like a small worm.
The little boy originally had a gloomy appearance, and his eyes were filled with ridicule, but after seeing the small gray insect in Luo Zheng's hand, his eyes revealed great fear.
"Waaah —"
With a strange, ear-piercing cry, the little boy began to retreat.
This little boy was the embodiment of the Divine Blood of Origin, "Infinite Proliferation". The energy of the small insect in Luo Zheng's hand was the complete opposite of Infinite Proliferation, and could completely restrain the little boy. Naturally, the little boy was instinctively afraid of the small insect.
But just as the little boy retreated a few feet, Luo Zheng had already caught up. With a light slap of his hand, the small gray insect was slapped into the little boy's body.
Soon …
The little boy's body swelled with fist-sized blisters. His entire body stretched and twisted, and his appearance became terrifying and strange. Finally, everything inside his body dissolved, and the human skin disappeared in a pool of pus and blood.
Luo Zheng crouched down, reached out his hand, and lightly dipped it in the pus and blood. He then said, "If the Divine Blood of Origin that can multiply without limit and fuse with the abilities of eleven diagrams can be called an Divine Blood, then the one I just created can also be considered an Divine Blood!"
The small gray insect in his hand reappeared. This small gray insect was nothing more than Luo Zheng's instincts.
Then, Luo Zheng returned to the stone platform of the Hexagonal Star Array. Previously, he had used this stone platform to enter this space.
When Luo Zheng stood on it, the stone platform was completely still.
He lowered his head and glanced at it. He had already sensed the laws of space-time within the stone platform.
"Buzz!"
The Divine Blood of Origin in Luo Zheng's body surged again, and adjusted to the space-time attribute of his bloodline.

Chapter 773
Both the Feather Emperor Secret Realm and the Divine Emperor Secret Realm were extremely dangerous places for the warriors of the Holy Nations.
However, compared to the Tian Chen Secret Realm, they paled in comparison.
In the Feather Emperor Secret Realm, as long as one followed the rules and experience, there wouldn't be any major problems.
However, the Tian Chen Secret Realm was a place of depravity. Apart from the clansmen of the Dragon Bloodline, no other creatures were allowed to enter!
In order to solve this problem, the four Holy Nations joined forces and discussed many times. In the end, they came up with an alternative.
That was to make use of the corpses in the Divine Emperor Secret Realm.
The Divine Emperor Secret Realm kept the corpses of the Dragon Bloodline clansmen. Many of the corpses had been treated with a secret method and could withstand the baptism of time.
By occupying their corpses and using them to enter the Tian Chen Secret Realm, one wouldn't be rejected by the power of blood sacrifice in the Tian Chen Secret Realm!
However, even so, the four Holy Nations were still in the initial stage of exploring the Tian Chen Secret Realm. After all, there were many restrictions after occupying the corpses. Moreover, the corpses in the Divine Emperor Secret Realm were not easy to obtain.
After hearing King Yan's explanation, Luo Zheng finally had a rough understanding of these mystic realms. The so-called four mystic realms originally complemented each other. To put it bluntly, the Sky Feather Sacred Sea was the lair of the Dragon Vein Tribe. However, there were many restrictions and rules if one wanted to explore it.
Luo Zheng understood that he couldn't enter the secret realm recklessly. The four Holy Nations had spent so many years and entered it like treading on thin ice. It seemed that it wouldn't be easy to obtain the Origin Stone of Life and the Ancestral Dragon egg.
Even without the obstruction of the Divine Sea Realm and the Life and Death Realm experts, Luo Zheng couldn't enter easily …
Now, it seemed that it was inevitable to cooperate with King Yan.
"After I get the Iron Order, I will form a team and enter the Sky Feather Sacred Sea. Since Luo Tianxing wants to excavate the Origin Stone of Life, you will have to help me!" King Yan laughed. He was afraid that he would not be able to keep someone like Luo Zheng.
Even though he had been conferred the title of King and received many rewards every year, he still couldn't compare to the Crown Prince. The Crown Prince was the heir apparent, and the difference between the two was huge.
Putting aside whether Luo Zheng was a profit-oriented person, Prince Yan and Luo Zheng didn't have much of a friendship. On the contrary, they had some bad blood before! The only reason why King Yan could keep Luo Zheng was because of his sincerity.
King Yan was also an extremely intelligent person. Therefore, when he talked with Luo Zheng, he did not hide anything. Every word he said was from the bottom of his heart.
Now that Luo Zheng had the same goal as him, of course, he would go with the flow and pull Luo Zheng into his team.
King of Yan knew this very well. Luo Zheng used to be very wary of the people in the God's Nation. It was also because of King of Yan's straightforward character that he was willing to follow King of Yan to his mansion.
"No problem." Luo Zheng smiled and nodded.
"Good!" King of Yan clapped his hands and shouted, "Bring me the wine!"
Then, good wine and delicacies were placed in front of them.
This wine was as rich as blood. It didn't smell like alcohol at all. Instead, it gave off an extremely sweet fragrance.
Seeing the hesitation on Luo Zheng's face, King of Yan smiled and said, "This is the Black Iron Divine Nation's Qi Luo Fragrance! Even with our cultivation, we will also get drunk! "
Ordinary good wine had no effect on the body of a martial artist. Therefore, martial artists drinking wine was similar to ordinary people drinking water. No matter how much they drank, they wouldn't get drunk.
After King of Yan finished speaking, in order to dispel Luo Zheng's doubts, he took the first step and poured the blood-like Qi Luo Fragrance into his mouth. Then, he turned the jade cup over and smiled at Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng smiled and raised his cup as well. Then, he drank it in one gulp.
He was just curious about this wine. With his body that was immune to poison, it was harder to poison him than to kill himself …
Then, they touched each other and chatted happily.
After drinking a few pots of Qi Luo Fragrance, both of them were indeed a little drunk. King of Yan suddenly said to the maidservant, "Go and call the Princess Consort here!"
The maidservant hesitated for a while and said, "Your Highness, I'm afraid the Princess Consort has already gone to bed …"
"Even if she is asleep, call her here!" King of Yan roared.
Seeing that King of Yan was angry, the maidservant didn't dare to delay. She nodded and left.
On the other hand, Luo Zheng was baffled. What did the King of Yan mean by calling his woman over?
Seeing Luo Zheng's eyes, King of Yan smiled. He held a luminous cup with both hands and shook it in front of Luo Zheng. Then, he drank it in one gulp.
"There is a saying in our Black Iron Divine Nation. If you can see Hanxuan dance, you will be willing to give up a hundred years of your life. This woman is the most beautiful woman in our Black Iron Divine Nation! Brother Tianxing, I will open your eyes in a while! "King of Yan said with some drunkenness.
"Isn't this inappropriate?" Luo Zheng couldn't help but roll his eyes. He was speechless when he heard this.
He really didn't know what was wrong with King of Yan. Why did he ask his wife to dance in the middle of drinking? After all, she was the Princess Consort. How could he humiliate her like this?
"There's nothing inappropriate!" King of Yan waved his hand. "I'm happy today!"
The more King of Yan said this, the more uncomfortable Luo Zheng felt. Although King of Yan also said that this Princess Consort was arranged by the Crown Prince to be by his side, as a King, it must be very painful for his wife to sleep in the same bed as him.
But he couldn't humiliate her like this, right?
After a while, the woman walked in angrily under the lead of several maidservants.
King of Yan waved his hand and said to the woman, "Hanxuan! Come, perform … a song and dance for us! "
The woman named Hanxuan came in angrily. As the Princess Consort of King of Yan, how could she let her husband humiliate her like this?
Hanxuan's face was full of anger. Of course, she wouldn't obey. She was also born in a prestigious family. It was just that she had an outstanding appearance since she was a child. She was also very famous in the Black Iron Divine Nation. A woman of her status usually had no choice but to marry King of Yan under the arrangement of the Crown Prince.
For more than a year, the two of them didn't have any feelings for each other. After all, she was the Crown Prince's woman. They only maintained this relationship on the surface. The fate of her entire family was in the hands of the Crown Prince. She could only listen to the Crown Prince.
Under the Crown Prince's order, she had to truthfully report King of Yan's movements every once in a while.
King of Yan was also well aware of this. Everyone in the huge King of Yan's Mansion was the Crown Prince's people. Everything was arranged by the Crown Prince. To put it bluntly, the Crown Prince thought that King of Yan was too big of a threat, so he secretly put King of Yan under house arrest.
She naturally knew that King of Yan was also wary of her. But he couldn't humiliate her like this!
Of course, Hanxuan was furious at this moment!
Seeing that Hanxuan didn't move, King of Yan's face instantly revealed a furious expression. An extremely sharp killing intent was released and immediately shrouded her. "If I ask you to dance, you will dance. Otherwise, I will kill you!"
Under such a gaze, Hanxuan's heart instantly trembled. If King of Yan really killed her, no one would say a few words for her!
She had no choice but to grit her teeth and agree.
As the musician played the music, the woman danced gracefully in front of Luo Zheng and King of Yan …
Looking at her unreconciled expression and hatred, Luo Zheng also sighed. However, he sighed in his heart. This could be considered a misfortune of the family. Only the royal family would have such disputes.
However, Luo Zheng also knew that King of Yan had two motives for saying this. On the one hand, he hated everyone in the King of Yan's Mansion to the extreme. Even his own princess consort was the Crown Prince's woman. With King of Yan's character, it was indeed unbearable. He said this to vent his anger.
On the other hand, he had the factor of gaining Luo Zheng's trust.
However, King of Yan was still too young. He wasn't even 20 years old, but he didn't consider the consequences when doing things. He only wanted to achieve his own goals. If the princess consort personally came out to perform for the guests, he would lose half of his face if this spread out. It was really ridiculous!

Chapter 774
Although Hanxuan's dance was graceful, how could Luo Zheng be in the mood to appreciate it in such a strange atmosphere?
King Yan's face was extremely ferocious, as if torturing his consort was a way to vent his anger.
After the song ended, Luo Zheng got up and found an excuse to leave.
No matter how King Yan and the Crown Prince fought, Luo Zheng only needed to move forward according to his goal.
He had obtained some Life Origin Stones from King Yan's Mansion, but the quantity was far from enough. However, Luo Zheng had a clearer understanding of Sky Feather Sacred Ocean.
Luo Zheng had to enter Sky Feather Sacred Ocean, but Luo Zheng wouldn't waste the time.
Other than cultivating, he also wanted to purchase more Origin Stones of Life.
Lonely Crane City was the second largest city in Black Iron Divine Nation. Moreover, it had the Profound Jade Martial House, a cultivation holy land comparable to a sixth grade sect.
There were no sects in the Divine Nation, but there were various aristocratic families. The most powerful aristocratic family in the Central Domain was only a third grade aristocratic family. There had never been a fourth grade aristocratic family.
However, there were many fourth grade aristocratic families and even fifth grade aristocratic families in Lonely Crane City. The Patriarchs of these families were generally at the fourth or fifth level of Life and Death Realm.
Since King Yan's Mansion had Life Origin Stones, he should be able to find a lot in Lonely Crane City. If he could spend True Yuan Stones to buy them, Luo Zheng wouldn't let go of this opportunity.
Therefore, while cultivating, Luo Zheng looked for opportunities to inquire about Life Origin Stones.
However, after inquiring, he couldn't help but feel disappointed.
The Origin Stone of Life could replenish one's life force. It was an irresistible temptation for martial artists. Even if such a thing existed, it would be used up quickly. There were very few left. King Yan's residence had a lot of treasures. Therefore, there were only a few Origin Stones of Life that were taken out.
This cut off Luo Zheng's thoughts of spending money to buy Life Origin Stones. He could only wait to explore Sky Feather Sacred Ocean.
However, opening the Heavenly Feather Holy Sea wasn't something that Luo Zheng could decide on. Instead, the four Divine Kingdoms had to work together to allow the Divine Kingdom's martial artists to enter. Even if Luo Zheng was anxious, he couldn't rush it.
Half a month later, King of Yan came to visit again. After meeting him, King of Yan said, "Come, follow me! Let me take you to see my men! "
"Your men?" Luo Zheng was a little suspicious.
Luo Zheng did not probe further. He followed King Yan and headed straight to the Profound Jade Martial House.
The Black Jade Martial House occupied a huge area and there were countless halls and halls. The martial artists were very respectful when they saw King Yan. They bowed and made way for him.
As they continued forward, they arrived at a hall within the Martial House. Inside the hall, two men and a woman were seated. The three of them were also late stage Nihility Tribulation Realm cultivators.
With a casual sweep of his perception, Luo Zheng could tell that these three were not weak. They were basically god-grade geniuses.
The King of Yan stepped in. The three of them opened their eyes at the same time. The lady in her early twenties smiled at the King of Yan. "Huahua, you are here!"
"Huahua …" Luo Zheng did not know why King Yan had such a nickname.
King Yan pretended to be angry and said, "I have said that you are not allowed to call me that!"
"Huahua is the cutest when he is angry!" The lady was not offended. She was still smiling. Obviously, she was a very lively lady.
The other two people also said, "Yue Ying, you cannot be so rude to King Yan!"
Under such dissuasion, the lady named Yue Ying turned her gaze to Luo Zheng. Her beautiful round eyes kept looking at Luo Zheng. She then continued, "Huahua, who is this guy?"
King Yan smiled slightly. He patted Luo Zheng's shoulder and said, "Let me introduce him to you. This is Luo Tianxing. He will be joining us temporarily."
Then, King Yan said to Luo Zheng, "Brother Tianxing, these three people are the team that I have formed. They are Yue Ying, Zuo Chuandong and Lan Chen. With you, there are five of us!"
There was no limit to the number of people in the Secret Realm of the Heavenly Feather Holy Sea. After so many years of exploration, the four Holy Kingdoms had gradually optimized the best plan.
It was best to form a small team to explore the Secret Realm of the Heavenly Feather Holy Sea. The best number of people was five.
Every Holy Kingdom would send a few such small teams to enter the Secret Realm.
King Yan and the Crown Prince were secretly forming their own teams. However, compared to the Crown Prince, King Yan was naturally at a disadvantage. Moreover, it was not a small disadvantage!
King Yan's talent and strength were probably not below the Crown Prince. However, the three people in front of him could not be compared to Zhan Yun and Xiao Daoyuan.
This was also the reason why King Yan wanted to rope Luo Zheng in.
"The five of us are here? Huahua, are you joking? "The girl called Yue Ying sized up Luo Zheng. However, her eyes revealed a disdainful expression.
No matter if it was Yue Ying, Zuo Chuandong and the rest, they were all from the Rank Five Aristocratic Families in Lonely Crane City. Moreover, they were the disciples of the most elite aristocratic families.
Their strength might not be comparable to Zhan Yun's, but in the Profound Jade Martial House, they could be ranked at the top of the second echelon. After these three people were chosen by King Yan, they did not even participate in the assessment. The problem of the Black Iron Token was settled by King Yan.
During this period of time, they had also gathered together to train with each other in preparation for the expedition to the Heavenly Feather Holy Sea.
Now that King Yan had suddenly led a Middle Stage False Tribulation Realm warrior to join them, Yue Ying and the rest did not understand.
In the Profound Jade Martial House, there were many Late Stage False Tribulation Realm warriors. Why would it be this kid's turn?
Hearing Yue Ying's words, how could King Yan not understand what she meant? It was obvious that he looked down on Luo Zheng, this "teammate."
"Brother Tianxing, please don't be offended." King Yan was afraid that Luo Zheng might have some thoughts.
Who knew that Yue Ying's round eyes would turn, "What's there to be offended about? To be able to catch King Yan's eye and join us is also his fortune. But Huahua, I still don't understand why you want to pull a burden into your team."
King Yan smiled slightly, "Luo Zheng is not a burden. If I'm not wrong, he might be the pillar of our team!"
Hearing King Yan's words, Zuo Chuandong and Lan Chen's expressions immediately changed.
Even though their temperaments were not as noisy as Yue Ying's, people who could cooperate with King Yan would have some pride in their hearts. No matter where they were placed, they would be one in ten thousand people.
Luo Zheng's cultivation was only at the Middle Stage False Tribulation Realm. How could he enter their eyes?
But since King Yan had said so, this meant that this person was not to be underestimated! Pillar? In their team, there would always be only one pillar, and that was King Yan himself. Could it be that this ancient martial art practitioner called Luo Tianxing was more powerful than King Yan?
Yue Ying sneered and looked at Luo Zheng provocatively, "I don't believe it! Huahua is joking with us for no reason. "
"Joking?" King Yan also knew that if Luo Zheng did not show his skills, it would be very difficult to convince them, "If you think I'm joking, you can spar with Brother Tianxing!"
As soon as King Yan finished speaking, the three of them stared at Luo Zheng with burning eyes. It seemed that they all wanted to challenge Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng's face revealed a helpless smile, "This, there's no need, right?"
"You said there's no need, but it's indeed useless!" As soon as the woman called Yue Ying finished speaking, her figure suddenly flashed. She stretched out her jade-like hand and slapped towards Luo Zheng.
Just as she moved, thousands of jade-like hands suddenly appeared in front of Luo Zheng. This woman was like a thousand-hand Guanyin. In the blink of an eye, she had attacked thousands of times. It was almost watertight, sealing off all of Luo Zheng's escape routes.
"This woman's speed is abnormally fast!" Luo Zheng's eyes flashed slightly. He realized that a very special Power of Law had erupted from this woman's body. Luo Zheng had never seen such a Power of Law before!
"The Time Law?"
The two most powerful laws in the universe were the Time Law and the Space Law.
Luo Zheng had mastered the Space Law, but he had never seen anyone master the Time Law. He did not expect to see it from this woman called Yue Ying.
It was also because of the acceleration of the Time Law that this woman was able to attack Luo Zheng so many times in the blink of an eye!
"Yue Ying! King Yan only wants you to spar with him. Don't hurt him! "The warrior called Zuo Chuandong exhorted.
After all, Luo Zheng was only at the Middle Stage of the False Tribulation Realm. Yue Ying usually did not know the severity of the situation. If she seriously injured Luo Zheng, it would not be good to be blamed by King Yan.
However, King Yan's expression was calm, as if he was not worried about Luo Zheng at all.

Chapter 775
Yue Ying replied with a smile, "Don't worry, I won't hurt this kid!"
However, just as she finished speaking, the palm print she waved out became sharper and sharper.
Don't look at her slender jade-like hands. Even if it was a bronze statue made of fine iron, it would be shattered by her. If Luo Zheng was unable to deal with it, he would definitely be injured.
Luo Zheng's eyes burst out with a sparkling light as he tried to comprehend the Law of Time in Yue Ying's body.
"It's useless if you don't resist. I'll really attack!" When Yue Ying saw that Luo Zheng was in a daze, she felt even more disdain in her heart. Who knew what this guy used to coax Huahua into joining this team?
She chopped down at Luo Zheng's head with eighty percent of her strength!
Luo Zheng comprehended for a long time, but he shook his head. He reached out his hand and gently pinched, easily holding Yue Ying's jade-like hands in his hand. He then said softly, "The Law of Time contained in it is too little. I can't comprehend it."
He held Yue Ying's hands so easily, as if Yue Ying didn't attack Luo Zheng just now, but took the initiative to reach out and let Luo Zheng hold her hands.
Yue Ying was stunned for a moment. She wanted to take her hands back from Luo Zheng's hands, but she found that her hands were clamped by a pair of large pliers. She couldn't pull her hands out at all.
Zuo Chuandong and Lan Chen originally wanted to watch a good show. They wanted to see what kind of method Luo Zheng would use to resist Yue Ying's move.
Yue Ying's "Thousand Leaves Jade Hands" attack was like falling petals, dazzling people's eyes. It contained a trace of the Law of Time, which could change the flow of time in her body for a short period of time. It was impossible to break, and could only be broken by using more powerful methods.
However, they never expected that Luo Zheng would break it with just a casual pinch. This made the two of them look at each other in dismay. When their eyes met, they were filled with shock.
A faint smile appeared on King Yan's face, as if everything was within his expectations. However, there was still some surprise in his smile. He thought that Luo Zheng would be able to break Yue Ying's move, but he didn't expect it to be so easy and simple. It was as if he was eating and drinking water …
It seemed that Luo Zheng's strength had far surpassed the level of the False Tribulation Realm. Only like this could he reach such a realm!
"You, let go!" Yue Ying wanted to pull her hands out, but after trying a few times, she was unable to do so. Her round eyes were wide open as she stared at Luo Zheng. There was even a hint of crimson on her cheeks.
Only then did Luo Zheng gently release his hands, with a trace of a smile on his face. Before he could even speak, Yue Ying said: "What do you mean by the power of the Time Laws is too little? .............
Hearing this, everyone's face was filled with curiosity.
It was extremely difficult to comprehend the laws. Moreover, Yue Ying had used the law of time, which was activated by her own True Essence. The power of the law would become chaotic after being activated by her True Essence, and only the person herself could control it. This Luo Zheng wanted to comprehend the power of the law during a battle. This was simply wishful thinking.
What they didn't know was that after Luo Zheng had experienced the baptism of the power of law, he had the highest affinity with the power of law in the entire universe. Simply comprehending the power of law was quite difficult for others, but it wasn't difficult for Luo Zheng.
The two most powerful laws were the law of space and the law of time. Luo Zheng had already comprehended the law of space, but Luo Zheng had never come into contact with the law of time. This was the first time Luo Zheng had seen the law of time!
Luo Zheng smiled faintly and said, "If there's a chance to comprehend it, I will naturally not let it go …"
"Pah pah!" Yue Ying snorted coldly, "Even I have only comprehended 10% of the law of time! How can it be so easy to comprehend! "
"It's 10% of the first level of the law of time, right?" Luo Zheng asked without any understanding of the situation.
At this time, Yue Ying's face revealed an expression of wanting to kill someone as she stared fiercely at Luo Zheng!
Unlike the other basic laws in the universe, the law of time and the law of space were very precious and rare, and the difficulty of cultivating them was also much higher.
Luo Zheng's first level of the law of space was learned in the True Dragon World, and the second level of the law of space was learned from the talent stele in the Sin Tower. Whether it was the law of space or the law of time, it was very difficult to find a law stele in this vast world.
The law of time that Yue Ying cultivated was more difficult than the law of space. At the same time, its power was also more powerful than the other basic laws. Under the change of the flow of time, there was too much room for a cultivator to display!
Therefore, for Yue Ying to be able to comprehend 10% of the first level of the law of time was enough for her to be proud of her peers.
However, from Luo Zheng's point of view, it seemed a little regretful. Seeing Luo Zheng like this, Yue Ying was naturally angry!
At this moment, King Yan walked over with a smile and said nonchalantly, "Alright, let's stop probing. Brother Tian Xing's strength should not be below mine. If he can join our team, he will definitely be a great help."
"Hmph!" Yue Ying still had an unconvinced expression on her face.
However, just now, Luo Zheng had casually resolved her attack. In addition, under King Yan's appreciation, she already understood that Luo Zheng's strength was probably on par with people like Zhan Yun. He was a true monstrous genius of the first level!
How many cultivators were there in the Black Iron Divine Kingdom? At this stage, there were only four people. Luo Zheng's strength was probably not something she could imagine.
At this time, King Yan said to Luo Zheng, "I can see that you have always been a lone wolf. You don't seem to understand the meaning of a team?"
Hearing King Yan's words, Luo Zheng immediately thought of Cui Xie's Tian Xie Sect. Back then, the Tian Xie Sect had a special battle technique that could combine the vital essences of tens of thousands of Divine Reflection Realm cultivators, allowing these low-level cultivators to unleash a terrifying strength.
Luo Zheng nodded.
King Yan waved to Zuo Chuandong, Yue Ying and the others. He walked to the center of the hall and stretched out a hand to Luo Zheng. Then, he said, "You can try to attack me!"
Zuo Chuandong and Yue Ying stood on both sides of King Yan, while Lan Chen stood behind him.
"King Yan wants to combine the strength of the four of them?" Luo Zheng sized them up curiously. Since he had already agreed to join the King of Yan's team, he had to blend in as well. When he thought of this, Luo Zheng's figure suddenly swayed and he dashed towards the King of Yan.
Before Luo Zheng could get close, Zuo Chuandong stretched out his hand and rays of light purple vital essences spiraled out of his body. Those vital essences kept revolving around King Yan!
Yue Ying, who was at the side, smiled sweetly. With a wave of her jade-like hand, a thin curtain of light was formed. The moment Luo Zheng rushed towards the curtain of light, he immediately felt that everything around him seemed to slow down. Even his own speed was incomparably slow!
"It's the Law of Time! The three of them have completely become King Yan's assistants at this time … "A thought flashed through Luo Zheng's mind.
At this moment, King Yan's figure suddenly flashed and he came behind Luo Zheng in an instant. With a flip of his hand, a sharp dagger appeared and he placed it on Luo Zheng's neck. With the cooperation of these people, it was indeed a perfect combination.
However, Luo Zheng's reaction was also extremely fast. Yue Ying's understanding of the Law of Time was not deep. Luo Zheng's body twisted slightly like a fish and he slid away from King Yan's dagger. With a confident smile on his face, he stretched out his hand and wanted to snatch the dagger from King Yan's hand.
At this moment, Luo Zheng suddenly felt a huge shock in his mind. It was as if a mountain-splitting axe had chopped into Luo Zheng's head. It was the martial artist named Lan Chen who had launched a Soul Attack on him.
If it were other people, it would be difficult for them to withstand the Soul Attack under such multiple attacks. Even if they could barely withstand it, they would be distracted. How could they withstand King Yan's attack when they were distracted?
However, how could Lan Chen imagine that Luo Zheng's soul was about to enter the Blue Soul Realm? His level of Soul Attack was just a tickle to Luo Zheng!
However, Luo Zheng was unwilling to expose his full strength. He deliberately pretended to groan and leaned forward. King Yan's dagger was placed on Luo Zheng's neck again.
However, King Yan revealed a "successful" smile. He wanted to prove to Luo Zheng that their combined strength was far stronger than Luo Zheng's individual strength.
However, King Yan did not expect that even if the four of them joined forces, they were still no match for Luo Zheng. However, they were only here to learn from each other. Luo Zheng was unwilling to reveal too much of his strength.
"Hua Hua, kill him! Humph! " Seeing that Luo Zheng was restrained by King Yan, Yue Ying revealed a proud smile. It was obvious that she was still brooding over Luo Zheng's actions.
King Yan was only demonstrating to Luo Zheng once. Why would he listen to Yue Ying's words? He smiled and removed the dagger from Luo Zheng's neck.
"There is not much time left. After entering the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea, we will face a lot of competition. If you have time, you can practice with Yue Ying and the rest. The five of us must obtain the best results in this trip to the Secret Realm!" King Yan's eyes shone with a glimmer of hope.
He had been suppressed by the Crown Prince all this time. He was looking for a chance to turn the tables.
Although the Crown Prince was powerful, he did not control everything. If King Yan could gain the recognition of his father, the Crown Prince would definitely restrain himself.
Luo Zheng nodded. King Yan's goal did not conflict with his own. If he could achieve his goal and help King Yan, he would not shrink back!
During this period of time, Luo Zheng temporarily stayed in the Black Jade Martial House to "practice" with the three of them.
Luo Zheng slowly became familiar with the characters of the three of them. The warrior named Zuo Chuandong was more docile and easy-going. In comparison, Lan Chen was more rigid and not good with words. As for Yue Ying, she was lively, cheerful and carefree.
Luo Zheng finally understood why the King of Yan had chosen the three of them as his understanding of their cultivation method.
Whether it was Zuo Chuandong, Lan Chen, or Yue Ying, the three of them were not good at attacking. It seemed that the cultivation techniques they mainly cultivated were all auxiliary!
For example, Yue Ying's Law of Time was mainly used to slow down the opponent's speed. Under the cover of her Law of Time, she could make the opponent extremely slow in an instant. It was also in this instant that she could take the opponent's life!
As for Zuo Chuandong, he was good at creating many obstacles for the opponent. As for Lan Chen, he cultivated a cultivation technique that could disturb the mind!
This kind of martial technique inheritance was completely different from the Central Domain or the Sea God Continent!
The Central Domain and the Sea God Continent pursued the ultimate strength of an individual. They rarely cooperated with others.
This False Tribulation Realm warrior could achieve such an effect by cooperating with each other. What if five or six Divine Sea Realm warriors cooperated with each other? Luo Zheng could imagine that even if Liu Yu's individual strength was very strong, if five Divine Sea Realm warriors from the Sea God Continent were to fight against five Divine Sea Realm warriors from the Divine Kingdom, the chances of the Divine Sea Realm warriors from the Divine Kingdom winning would be much higher!
In this hall, Luo Zheng cultivated with them. Occasionally, they would exchange some questions about martial arts.
However, most of the time, it was Luo Zheng who asked them for advice.
The inheritances of the Divine Kingdom were indeed much richer than those of the Central Domain. Moreover, a considerable part of their inheritances came from the Human Clan of the Upper Domain. Unfortunately, Luo Zheng did not know which force the inheritances of the four Divine Kingdoms belonged to.
As time passed, King Yan seemed to become busy. He rarely appeared in the Martial House. He was busy with some affairs of the Imperial Family.
Three months later, the Profound Jade Martial House suddenly summoned all the False Tribulation Realm warriors who had passed the examination. Naturally, Luo Zheng was included.
Luo Zheng knew that the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea was about to open. The Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea was equivalent to the treasury of the four Divine Kingdoms. It was equivalent to the status of the Mysterious Underworld Cave in the Sea God Continent. He did not know how they would assign warriors to enter.
However, Yue Ying, Lan Chen, and Zuo Chuandong were not invited. They belonged to King Yan and did not participate in the examination. They would probably set off with King Yan.

Chapter 776
The Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea was somewhat similar to the Cursed Lands. It was also located in the center of a continent. For the vast majority of cultivators, it was also a forbidden land.
However, the Cursed Lands was a forbidden land for high level cultivators while the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea was a forbidden land for low level cultivators. There was a fundamental difference between the two.
Almost every year, the four Divine Kingdoms would jointly explore the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea. The sacred land left behind by the Dragon Bloodline Clan was an inexhaustible treasure trove for these Divine Kingdoms. The reason why the four Divine Kingdoms were able to develop to this day was not only because of the resources in the endless seas around the Divine Kingdoms' continents, but also because of the secret realms in the sacred sea!
In the eyes of the rulers of the four Divine Kingdoms, the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea was naturally a treasure among treasures.
All the selected cultivators of the Profound Jade Martial House boarded a huge ark. This ark was like a floating palace with hundreds of floors, enough to accommodate tens of thousands of people.
Luo Zheng followed the other cultivators who passed the test and entered the ark. After the ark rose into the sky, it sped towards the northwest.
It was obvious that every Void Tribulation Realm cultivator who passed the test was very excited.
To be selected by a Divine Kingdom and enter the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea to gain experience, they would all become proud sons of heaven in the future!
Someone once made a statistic. In the four Divine Kingdoms, almost 99% of the Divine Sea Realm cultivators who entered the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea for the first time achieved good results.
The secret realms in the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea were a measuring stick to assess the potential of a Divine Kingdom's cultivator. Therefore, every Void Tribulation Realm cultivator present would do their best without the slightest negligence.
"So beautiful! I've always heard that the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea is golden. Today is the first time I've seen it! "
"The golden light is said to be the power left behind by the Dragon Bloodline Clan after the blood sacrifice of hundreds of millions of lives!"
Most of the people crowded into the ark's Class-A, admiring the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea from high up in the sky.
Luo Zheng was also standing on the deck, admiring the golden sea. He could feel the power of the blood sacrifice contained in the golden light. The Dragon Bloodline Clan was defeated and hundreds of millions of lives were sacrificed. There must be a great deal of unwillingness contained within …
Luo Zheng gazed into the distance, trying to place his gaze even further away. However, under the cover of this golden ocean, Luo Zheng could not find any traces of those secret realms.
If Luo Zheng's guess was correct, the four mystic realms should correspond to the four islands of the Sky Feather Sacred Sea. However, although the Sky Feather Sacred Sea was only an inland sea, Luo Zheng was still unable to find those islands.
These days, Luo Zheng was very anxious, but sometimes it was useless to be anxious. Although he had no opponents in the Central Domain, Luo Zheng's strength was not enough to crush everything in the four Divine Kingdoms.
Even though the speed of the ark was extremely fast, it still took them a full two days and three nights to reach their destination. It wasn't until dawn of the third day that a large river finally appeared in front of them!
"It's the West Dragon River! It's the estuary of the Sky Feather Sacred Sea! "
One end of the West Dragon River was connected to the Sky Feather Sacred Sea. However, the spectacular thing was that the golden seawater of the Sky Feather Sacred Sea was like a cup overflowing with fine wine. Although a corner of the Sky Feather Sacred Sea was connected to the river, the golden seawater did not flow into the river.
Therefore, one end of the West Dragon River was azure in color, while the other end of the river suddenly turned golden. The connection was very abrupt.
On one side of the river, there were already more than twenty giant arks docked. It seemed that there were many cultivators from other Martial Houses here as well.
The Profound Jade Martial House's ark slowly docked. Under the lead of three Divine Sea Realm experts, many False Tribulation Realm cultivators also left the ark.
Just a few dozen li away from the West Dragon River, there was a huge palace. In front of the palace was a square that was over ten thousand mu in size. At this moment, there were many cultivators gathered in the square.
"Cultivators from the four Divine Kingdoms." Luo Zheng's eyes flashed.
After the Sky Feather Sacred Sea opened, the Black Iron Divine Kingdom was not the only one that entered. The Great Yu Divine Kingdom, the Heavenly Wind Divine Kingdom, and the Star Field Divine Kingdom also entered.
Today was the day that the cultivators of the four Divine Kingdoms gathered.
In this huge square, the young cultivators of the four Divine Kingdoms each occupied their own territory. Many Life and Death Realm experts and Divine Sea Realm experts occupied the steps in front of the palace.
Luo Zheng's eyes swept over and found that there were more than thirty Divine Sea Realm experts standing at the top of the steps.
Even though Luo Zheng was quite a distance away, Luo Zheng could still feel the terrifying aura emitted by these Divine Sea Realm experts!
"The foundation of the four Divine Kingdoms is indeed incomparable to that of the Central Region. In their eyes, the Central Region is just a small Uncivilized Land. I wonder if there are any movements in the Divine Kingdom after I killed the two Divine Sea Realm experts of the Great Yu Divine Kingdom?"
Luo Zheng was most worried about encountering the Saintess of the Great Yu Divine Kingdom.
Back in the Heavenly Mist Immortal Tomb, Luo Zheng had a big conflict with the Saintess. If the Saintess of the Divine Kingdom appeared here today, it would be a big trouble for Luo Zheng.
Not to mention so many Divine Sea Realm experts, even if Luo Zheng was targeted by only one Divine Sea Realm expert, it would be impossible for him to escape!
After Luo Zheng followed the disciples of the Profound Jade Martial House into the square, he heard a loud voice coming from the top of the palace. "Black Iron Divine Kingdom, Profound Jade Martial House has arrived!"
The voice was so sharp that it almost spread over a hundred li radius.
Luo Zheng mixed in with the other cultivators and entered the huge square. The three Divine Sea Realm experts of the Profound Jade Martial House stood on the steps of the palace.
However, the positions of the Profound Jade Martial House's three Martial House Masters were slightly lower, at the bottom half of the steps.
On the square, many False Tribulation Realm cultivators occupied different positions according to their respective vassal states. For example, the Profound Jade Martial House and the Black Name Martial House were both from the Black Iron Divine Kingdom, so the cultivators of these two Martial Houses were gathered together.
Not long after, a huge ark descended from the sky and stopped at the river's edge.
Then, the sharp voice sounded again, "Star Field Divine Kingdom, Great Xuan Martial House has arrived!"
From the ark, four Divine Sea Realm experts appeared and slowly flew to the top of the palace's steps. Compared to the positions of the three Martial House Masters of the Profound Jade Martial House, the positions occupied by the Great Xuan Martial House were several levels higher!
Seeing this scene, Luo Zheng understood in his heart.
Although this Divine Kingdom's mainland was occupied by the four Divine Kingdoms, as long as there were people, there would be differences!
The Black Iron Divine Kingdom might not be the strongest among the four Divine Kingdoms.
Although the Profound Jade Martial House was comparable to a sixth grade sect, it was also not the strongest Martial House. The position of the newly arrived Great Xuan Martial House should be above the Profound Jade Martial House.
Luo Zheng's guess was indeed correct.
For so many years, the four Divine Kingdoms had maintained a delicate balance, but there were differences in strength.
Over the years, these four positions had been swapped back and forth, and the seating arrangement had changed frequently.
However, in the last hundred years, the Great Yu Divine Kingdom was the most prosperous, followed by the Star Field Divine Kingdom, followed by the Heavenly Wind Divine Kingdom, and finally the Black Iron Divine Kingdom.
Of course, this ranking was only based on the overall performance of a country's forces and the younger generation. Among the kings of a Divine Nation, the War Emperor and War Emperor had a lifespan of tens of thousands of years. Under normal circumstances, the kings of Divine Nations would not fight with each other, so it was difficult to determine who was better.
Therefore, this ranking did not mean that the Black Iron Divine Kingdom was weaker than the Great Yu Divine Kingdom!
"Heavenly Wind Divine Kingdom, Spirit Communication Martial House has arrived!"
"Great Yu Divine Kingdom, Cloud Dragon Martial House has arrived!"
"…"
Every Martial House was at least a fifth grade sect. The number of Divine Sea Realm experts on the platform was constantly increasing, and the number of False Tribulation Realm warriors on the square was also increasing.
At this moment, Luo Zheng suddenly felt a ray of light blooming in the corner of his eyes. He looked up and saw a dot of light in the west. It was like a small sun, flying rapidly in the sky!
When the dot of light got closer and closer, Luo Zheng could tell from the dot of light that it was actually a golden palace!
The golden palace flew at an extremely fast speed and stopped at the West Dragon River in the blink of an eye. Then, a deep laughter came from the palace.
"Haha! It seems that this old man is early. Ao Xiao and the other old men are not here yet! "
Then, Luo Zheng saw hundreds of figures fly out from the golden palace. The one in the lead was a middle-aged man with a flushed face.
With just a glance, Luo Zheng could feel an irresistible aura coming from this person. This aura wasn't an aura released by soul pressure, but an aura that had been slowly cultivated by being in a high position for many years!
Even Liu Yu of the Heavenly Feather Holy Land did not have this kind of aura. After all, Liu Yu had only been in charge of the Heavenly Feather Holy Land for 200 years. Luo Zheng had only felt this kind of aura from Xun!
Of course, when Xun released his aura, it was even fiercer than this person's! This person's aura was only aimed at human martial artists, but Xun's aura could almost overwhelm all living beings.
"Star Field Divine Kingdom, Sky Fortune Kingdom's King has arrived!" A shrill voice sounded out.
"So it's the King of the Star Field Divine Kingdom! The four Divine Kingdoms are the rulers of this continent. Then, this person's strength should be at least in the top four of the Divine Kingdoms' continents? "Luo Zheng guessed.
The Sky Fortune King laughed, but he didn't obediently fly to the palace. Instead, he stopped above the square for a moment, sweeping his eyes over the many Virtual Tribulation realm martial artists. Then, the Sky Fortune King said, "Where are the disciples and grand disciples of my Star Field Divine Kingdom!?"
Hearing their King ask such a question, the martial artists of the Great Xuan Martial House and the other Martial Houses of the Star Field Divine Kingdom immediately responded like thunder, "Greetings, King! We are here! "
Several hundred Virtual Tribulation realm martial artists roared at the same time, causing the ground to tremble.

Chapter 777
"Not bad. Hehe, little brats, perform well in a moment!"
The Sky Fortune Kingdom King said with a smile. Then, he led the people behind him to stand on the highest platform.
Among the martial artists that followed behind the Sky Fortune Kingdom King, there were Life and Death powerhouses as well as many False Tribulation realm martial artists. These should be the princes and princes of the Star Field Divine Kingdom …
After the Star Field Divine Kingdom's King arrived, not too far away, a stream of blue light shot towards them. This stream of light was like a comet, dragging a long trail behind it as it shot towards them.
As this stream of light approached, Luo Zheng discovered that it was actually a giant sword that had been magnified countless times.
"The King of Yu Da Divine Kingdom has arrived!" Someone recognized the origin of this giant sword.
Sure enough, after the giant sword stopped at the riverside, many Divine Sea realm powerhouses appeared from the giant sword, followed by several hundred people.
At this time, a shrill voice announced, "The King of Yu Da Divine Kingdom, Battle Emperor Yu Da Yu has arrived!"
Compared to the Star Field Divine Kingdom's King Tian Yun, Battle Emperor Yu Da Yu's personality was much more gloomy. He flew all the way to the square, but didn't say a single word. He led his people directly to the top of the platform.
Luo Zheng carefully sized up the crowd and saw a woman with a veil on her face following behind Battle Emperor Yu Da Yu. The veil couldn't cover her face. Luo Zheng's eyebrows twitched and he shrank back into the crowd.
This woman was the Divine Kingdom's Saintess!
He didn't know what the status of this Saintess was in Yu Da Divine Kingdom. If she saw him, would those Divine Sea realm powerhouses make things difficult for him?
Just like the two Kings that had just arrived. Whether it was the Sky Fortune Kingdom's King or Battle Emperor Yu Da Yu, they were both Divine Sea realm powerhouses. However, there were also differences between Divine Sea realm powerhouses. With Luo Zheng's current strength, it was impossible for him to escape from their hands.
What if the Saintess said something? Would Battle Emperor Da Yu attack him?
Luo Zheng felt a little anxious, but he quickly took a deep breath and tried to calm himself down. He believed in his own luck. He had survived so many difficulties, and now Luo Zheng was trying to calm himself down.
Next, the rulers of the Heavenly Wind Divine Nation and the Black Iron Divine Nation arrived one after another. The ruler of the Heavenly Wind Divine Nation was called the Heavenly Wind War Emperor, while the ruler of the Black Iron Divine Nation was called Zhou Huang by his name.
The title of Monarch was indeed very confusing. The other warriors were used to it, but Luo Zheng felt very awkward.
This was because after reaching the Divine Sea Realm, one would be bestowed with the title of Emperor. However, some warriors still kept their lives, while others were called War Emperors or War Emperors …
Behind Zhou Huang, Luo Zheng found that King of Yan was among them. Together with King of Yan was the Crown Prince, who was sitting in a wheelchair.
On the steps, there were many Divine Sea Realm warriors and the Royal Families of the four Divine Kingdoms. In the square below the steps, there were the young elites of the four Divine Kingdoms.
After almost everyone had arrived, the sharp voice rang out again.
"The people of the four Divine Kingdoms are all here. According to the usual practice, we will first determine the winner and loser before we begin the sacrifice!"
Coming to the estuary of the Sky Feather Sacred Ocean, Luo Zheng also understood how the four Divine Kingdoms explored the Sky Feather Sacred Ocean every year.
In the eyes of the four Divine Kingdoms, the Sky Feather Sacred Ocean was like a huge cake. Every Divine Kingdom would cut a slice of the cake every year. However, the number of slices of the cake and who would cut it first was always determined in order every year.
Hundreds of years ago, the rulers of the four Divine Kingdoms basically quarreled with each other. There were a few times when they almost came to blows.
The fight between these War Emperors and War Emperors was too earth-shattering. Moreover, the four Divine Kingdoms were of the same breath and branch. Such a quarrel would hurt the harmony.
Therefore, before the sacrifice, the top warriors of the four Divine Kingdoms' young generation would have to determine the winner and loser. Firstly, it would be a way to assess the strength of each Divine Kingdom's young generation. Secondly, it would be a way to determine the order.
Over time, the four Divine Kingdoms began to pay more and more attention to this competition. There were even some martial artists of the Divine Kingdoms who gathered the results of these battles together to predict the future strength and fortune of each Divine Kingdom!
The four Divine Kingdoms' rulers stood at the highest point. After hearing these words, the Sky Fortune Kingdom King laughed and said, "Good! Little bastards of my Star Wilderness Divine Kingdom, step forward first! "
The Star Wilderness Divine Kingdom had three sixth-grade Martial Houses. There were as many as six martial artists that could stand on the first step. As the Sky Fortune Kingdom King's voice fell, a martial artist in the square suddenly turned into a stream of light and rushed out. He arrived at the bottom of the steps and bowed, loudly replying, "Kingdom King, I, Zhao Tianji, will represent the Great Xuan Martial House to fight!"
The Sky Fortune Kingdom King was the Kingdom King of the Star Wilderness Divine Kingdom, so he naturally had a good understanding of the young masters of his Divine Kingdom. He seemed to be very confident in this Zhao Tianji. He boldly laughed and said, "Good! Then you will fight! I wonder if the little bastards of the other Divine Kingdoms dare to challenge me? "
As the Sky Fortune Kingdom King's voice fell, a beam of light shot out from the square, instantly arriving at the bottom of the steps. This person was a martial artist from the Great Yu Divine Kingdom. His stature was tall and sturdy, as strong as a vicious beast.
"Great Yu Divine Kingdom, Wolf Shadow Martial House, Yun Longtao, accept the challenge!"
After the martial artist finished speaking, the gloomy face of Battle Emperor Yu of the Great Yu Divine Kingdom revealed a faint smile. He encouraged, "Longtao, I've heard that you are invincible under the False Tribulation Realm. This time, you won't disappoint me!"
"Reporting to the Monarch, being invincible below the Void Tribulation Realm is just a rumor." Although Yun Longtao said this, he seemed to exude a strong sense of confidence in his bones. It was as if he did not put the many Void Tribulation Realm warriors present in his eyes.
Since the opponents of the two Divine Kingdoms had jumped out, the battle naturally began.
When False Tribulation Realm martial artists exchanged pointers, the commotion was huge. Especially when the late stage False Tribulation Realm martial artists were strong enough to move mountains and drain seas. Even if the area was large, it wasn't enough for them to display their full strength.
However, two Divine Sea Realm martial artists walked down the steps. With a wave of their hands, thin lines appeared in the air. These thin lines formed a huge rectangular barrier in the air. This barrier was the space where they fought.
A barrier created by a Divine Sea Realm martial artist was far from being broken by a False Tribulation Realm martial artist.
Luo Zheng didn't take this battle to heart. In his opinion, this battle had nothing to do with him.
Originally, it was just a battle between the younger generation of martial artists from the four Divine Kingdoms. What he cared about was entering the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea …
At this time, he could only be patient and wait for a victor to emerge.
According to Luo Zheng's estimation, this battle wouldn't last too long. There were only four martial artists from the Black Iron Divine Kingdom who could stand on the first step. In a while, the Black Iron Divine Kingdom's martial artists would most likely be Prince Yan, the Crown Prince, Xiao Daoyuan, and Zhan Yun.
Even if the Star Field Divine Kingdom and the Great Yu Divine Kingdom were slightly stronger than the Black Iron Divine Kingdom, there wouldn't be too many martial artists who could stand on the first step. At most, there would be about six or seven martial artists.
On the square, many False Tribulation Realm martial artists were nervously watching the battle in the barrier.
The sense of belonging and identity of a Divine Kingdom's martial artist was stronger than that of a sect! Therefore, martial artists born in a Divine Kingdom paid more attention to the glory of their Divine Kingdom!
This battle was to determine the winner between the Star Field Divine Kingdom and the Great Yu Divine Kingdom. The martial artists from these two Divine Kingdoms were naturally the most nervous and concerned. As for the Black Iron Divine Kingdom and the Heavenly Wind Divine Kingdom, they were watching the battle with the mentality of watching a show.
"Rumble …"
The cuboid above everyone's head was filled with fierce flames. As soon as the two of them entered the arena, the situation had reached a climax.
Representing one's own Divine Kingdom in a battle, this glory was something that the younger generation of martial artists had to fight for no matter what!
"Hmph, this Yun Longtao claims to be invincible under the False Tribulation Realm? I think the Great Yu Divine Kingdom is getting better at bragging! "
"Yes, he is definitely not a match for Senior Brother Zhao!" The one who spoke was a martial artist from the Great Xuan Martial House. He should be Zhao Tianji's senior brother.
However, the martial artist from the Great Yu Divine Kingdom also retorted, "Hehe, in a while, Big Brother Longtao will beat your bullshit Tianji until he is looking for his teeth on the ground!"
"What the f * ck are you talking about! Do you believe that I will beat you until you are looking for your teeth on the ground right now? "
Just as they started fighting, the square was already in a heated argument, especially the martial artists from the Star Field Divine Kingdom and the Great Yu Divine Kingdom. They were all in high spirits and looked like they were about to fight.
Luo Zheng could only shake his head with a helpless smile on his face. These martial artists had already stepped into the False Tribulation Realm, but their mentality was still so immature …
In contrast, the False Tribulation Realm martial artists of the Central Domain and the War Reverends of the Sea God Continent were much more mature than them.
However, on second thought, Luo Zheng could understand. These False Tribulation Realm martial artists had all grown up in the Martial House. Furthermore, they were able to follow their Divine Kingdoms here. Basically, they were the elites of the elites and were generally young.
Unlike the False Tribulation Realm martial artists of the Central Domain, most of them had lived for more than 100 years.
As for the War Reverends of the Sea God Continent, they had been tempered by life and death in the Tower of Sin. Naturally, they would not be so naive and easily angered.
This scene happened about once a year. Many Divine Sea Realm martial artists on the platform turned a blind eye to it. Despite the argument, everyone's attention was still focused on the battle in mid-air.
Zhao Tianji's cultivation technique was of the fire attribute. Moreover, he seemed to have grasped the third level of the Law of Fire. His flames contained an extremely destructive power.
Luo Zheng sized him up. This person's strength was indeed not simple.
However, the martial artist named Hou Longtao was even stronger! He was indeed known to be invincible under the False Tribulation Realm. Space constantly twisted around his body, forcibly blocking all the flames outside his body!
"Eh? Using the power of space to create a thin layer of space as your own armor? " Luo Zheng's eyes flashed. This should be the third level of the Law of Space. Even Luo Zheng did not have the opportunity to comprehend it. The four Divine Kingdoms indeed had a deep foundation!
