Chapter 778
Not only was Yun Longtao proficient in the law of space, but his cultivation method was also very unique. He was able to compress space into a thin layer to protect himself.
It was equivalent to using space to create an armor for himself!
In the False Tribulation Realm, most of the martial artists did not have the strength to tear space. As long as they could not break the "space armor" in front of him, they would not be able to harm him!
Luo Zheng's eyes flickered slightly. The four Divine Kingdoms' inheritances were indeed profound.
Although Zhao Tianji's attacks were extremely fierce, under Hou Longtao's suppression, he did not have much room to display his strength!
Unless Zhao Tianji had the strength to tear space or was proficient in the law of space, no matter how fierce his attacks were or how powerful the law of fire was, they would not be able to harm Yun Longtao.
"I'm afraid this Zhao Tianji is going to lose." Luo Zheng shook his head and said.
The speaker did not mean it, but the listener did.
Zhao Tianji had steadily entered the first tier of the Star Field Divine Kingdom. He was one of the strongest in the Great Xuan Martial House. At this time, a group of martial artists from the Great Xuan Martial House were watching the battle with wide eyes.
Suddenly, they heard a martial artist from the Black Iron Divine Kingdom say these words. The martial artist from the Great Xuan Martial House was furious.
"Although our Senior Brother Zhao is going to lose, do you want to die?" Brat, are you courting death? "Several Void Tribulation Realm cultivators of Great Xuan Martial House turned their heads and glared at Luo Zheng.
"Senior Brother Zhao is our Great Xuan Martial House's Golden Crown Champion! He is also one of the six strongest martial artists from the Star Field Divine Kingdom. Especially you! "
"Black Iron Divine Nation, hmph hmph, ranked last every year. The Profound Jade Martial House can't even compare to the Black Iron Martial House, the last of the last. I heard that the strongest person in the Profound Jade Martial House is called Zhan Yun. Hehe, that brat isn't even qualified to carry shoes for our Great Profound Martial House! Why are you all paying attention to his words! "
Hearing the other party's sarcastic remarks, Luo Zheng rolled his eyes. He should not have spoken.
However, Luo Zheng was not angry at all. His expression was indifferent. Although the Divine Kingdoms' inheritances were powerful, these martial artists were still flowers that grew steadily in a greenhouse. Their thoughts were simple. Luo Zheng could understand them.
However, Luo Zheng did not speak. He wanted to keep the peace, but the Profound Jade Martial House's martial artists were not willing to do so!
"What did you say? Our Profound Jade Martial House is at the bottom! What nonsense is this dog spitting out?! "
"Look at how powerful the Great Xuan Martial House is. When our Senior Brother Zhan Yun goes up, he will beat the shit out of your Great Xuan Martial House!"
"Humph, a Martial House full of eunuchs dares to jump out and speak!"
The warriors of Profound Jade Martial House retaliated, which immediately enraged the Great Xuan Martial House. The first part of the sentence was still fine, but the last part poked the pain of many warriors of Great Xuan Martial House.
The Great Xuan Martial House was indeed the number one martial house in the Star Field Divine Nation.
However, Martial Houses were usually established with the support of the Divine Nations. Most of the Martial Houses were established by Battle Emperors and Battle Emperors. However, the Great Xuan Martial House was established by a group of eunuchs in the early years. There were also experts among the eunuchs!
As a matter of fact, eunuchs had a natural advantage when it came to cultivation. Once their Qi was removed, eunuchs would be able to guard their Yang Essence. Thus, they would be able to achieve twice the result with half the effort when cultivating some pure Yang Arts. There was no lack of Divine Sea Realm eunuchs in the four Divine Kingdoms.
Not only was the Great Xuan Martial House established by eunuchs, but there were also a few Divine Sea Realm experts in the Martial House. Moreover, they were all eunuchs …
Therefore, the warriors of the four Divine Nations privately referred to the Great Xuan Martial House as the Eunuch Martial House!
Even after so many years of development, the Great Xuan Martial House still couldn't wash off this name. It was fine for the other Divine Nations to talk about it privately. However, if the warriors of the Great Xuan Martial House heard it, they would inevitably fight.
What's more, the warriors of the Profound Jade Martial House were openly mocking them?
The warriors of the Great Xuan Martial House were all flushed with anger. They circulated their True Essence and were about to fight.
The Profound Jade Martial House was also unwilling to pass on their responsibility. They were all Void Tribulation Realm warriors and the geniuses of the Divine Nations. They were at the age where they were hot-blooded and vigorous. How could they be patient?
At this moment, a majestic aura suddenly enveloped them!
It was a Divine Sea Realm expert who enveloped them, trying to calm the commotion here.
How powerful was the aura of a Divine Sea Realm expert? That aura was as solid as a solid object, pressing down on the heads of many Void Tribulation Realm warriors. The warriors of the Profound Jade Martial House and the Great Xuan Martial House suddenly felt as if a mountain was pressing down on their heads.
Even Zhan Yun in the Profound Jade Martial House held his breath. He felt that his hands and feet were stiff, unable to move.
Only Luo Zheng looked indifferent. He had a faint smile on his face as he glanced at the Divine Sea Realm expert in the distance.
The Divine Sea Realm expert suppressed his aura and suddenly found that there was one person among the Void Tribulation Realm warriors who wasn't affected at all. He was slightly surprised. The Divine Sea Realm expert sized up Luo Zheng curiously before he slowly withdrew his aura.
He was puzzled. Since when did such a person appear in the Profound Jade Martial House? Can't even suppress my aura?
The soul of a Divine Sea Realm expert was basically at the Battle Soul Realm. Originally, under his aura, the warriors of the Great Xuan Martial House and the Profound Jade Martial House shouldn't be able to move. Although that kid didn't move, the Divine Sea Realm expert clearly felt that he wasn't affected by his aura!
A Void Tribulation Realm warrior with the soul of a Battle Soul Realm warrior? This was impossible, unless this kid had a magic weapon that could resist soul aura pressure …
But a magic weapon that could resist a Divine Sea Realm expert's soul aura pressure, even the Crown Prince of the Black Iron Divine Kingdom didn't necessarily have one.
The Divine Sea Realm expert was puzzled, but he didn't say anything. He was responsible for maintaining order in the square, but the Profound Jade Martial House belonged to the Black Iron Divine Kingdom. If he were to rashly ask, he was afraid that the Black Iron Divine Kingdom's Monarch, Zhou Huang, would be unhappy.
Therefore, he only paid a little attention to Luo Zheng and didn't say anything.
Just as the commotion died down, the outcome of the battle above had been decided.
Just like Luo Zheng said, Zhao Tianji's attack was indeed fierce. It would be difficult to find an opponent in the Void Tribulation Realm. The problem was that the young geniuses of the four Divine Kingdoms had all gathered here. Everyone had their own trump cards. Zhao Tianji couldn't break through Yun Longtao's "Space Armor" and was defeated in the end.
"Bang!"
Zhao Tianji fell from the sky and directly knelt on the ground. His face was dejected as he said to the Sky Fortune Kingdom's Monarch, "Monarch, I lost!"
The Sky Fortune Kingdom's Monarch faintly smiled and said, "Please rise! Victory and defeat are common in war! You don't need to worry about it! "
Although the Monarch was comforting him, Zhao Tianji was still very depressed. After all, he was representing the Star Field Divine Kingdom. At this time, he wasn't representing himself, but the Great Xuan Martial House and the entire Star Field Divine Kingdom!
All of the Star Field Divine Kingdom's martial artists had ugly complexions. Many of them were eager to jump out and challenge Yun Longtao.
After all, the Star Field Divine Kingdom had several other Martial Houses besides the Great Xuan Martial House, and some of them were no weaker than Zhao Tianji.
Yun Longtao stood with his hands behind his back, floating in the air. His face was full of arrogance as he swept his eyes over the Void Tribulation Realm martial artists in the square. He was waiting for others to challenge him.
Since Yun Longtao dared to call himself invincible under the Void Tribulation Realm, he had full confidence in his own strength!
"I'll go! I, Li Yulong, challenge you! "
"I'll go, I …"
"Let me deal with this guy first!"
The most excited ones were the Star Field Divine Kingdom's martial artists. They had just lost a battle, and now they were eager to prove themselves.
However, the sharp voice sounded again. "According to the rules, the challenges will take turns. Next, it will be the Sky Wind Divine Kingdom and Black Iron Divine Kingdom's turn to challenge!"
King Yan and the Crown Prince's eyes were sparkling.
This kind of challenge was to assess the strength of the younger generation of martial artists in the Divine Kingdom. Although King Yan and the Crown Prince were qualified to fight, they belonged to the royal family of the Divine Kingdom and had a noble bloodline. Under normal circumstances, they couldn't participate in this level of battle.
However, although they couldn't participate, they had the right to make suggestions! For example, the Crown Prince could order Zhan Yun and Xiao Daoyuan to fight on his behalf!
Although King Yan had his own men, the only one who was qualified and had the strength to fight on his behalf was Luo Zheng!
However, King Yan was hesitating. Would Luo Zheng be willing?
Just as King Yan was hesitating, another quarrel broke out in the square.
Due to Zhao Tianji's defeat, many of the Great Xuan Martial House's martial artists were unhappy. At this time, it wasn't the Star Field Divine Kingdom's turn to challenge. Many of the martial artists had nowhere to vent their anger, so they targeted the Profound Jade Martial House.
"Isn't your Profound Jade Martial House very powerful? If you have the guts, why don't you go up and try? "
"That Zhan Yun, the number one expert of the Profound Jade Martial House, why don't you go up and show us your skills? I'm afraid he will still be beaten to a pulp by Yun Longtao! "
"I'm afraid the Profound Jade Martial House will be even worse!"
The Great Xuan Martial House's martial artists were in an uproar again. When Zhan Yun's eyes met the Crown Prince's, the Crown Prince's face revealed a faint smile. He then held the wheelchair and said, "Zhan Yun, you will represent me in the battle!"
Zhan Yun was waiting for the Crown Prince to say this. He nodded and turned into a stream of light, shooting into the sky!
At this time, many of the Profound Jade Martial House's martial artists were in an uproar.
"Senior Brother Zhan Yun, do your best!"
"Zhan Yun, that brat only knows how to use the space law to block. You have to end the battle quickly!"
"Our Profound Jade Martial House can't be looked down upon by others!"
The atmosphere suddenly became lively. After Zhan Yun flew up, he suddenly pulled out a saber from his back. The saber's blade was wide and thick. The back of the blade was like a wolf's fang, flickering with a faint blue light.
"A False Tribulation Realm martial artist can be equipped with a mid-grade saint tool … I wonder if there are any divine tools in the Divine Kingdom's mainland," Luo Zheng guessed.
In all fairness, Luo Zheng wasn't very concerned about the result of the battle. He wasn't born in the Profound Jade Martial House, nor was he a citizen of the Black Iron Divine Kingdom. He came to the Divine Kingdom with the mission of saving Ning Yudie. He had to seize this opportunity to observe the battle in front of him for the rest of the journey.
In addition, he had some worries in his heart. Since the Crown Prince could send someone to represent Zhan Yun in the battle, wouldn't Yan Wang come looking for him?
Luo Zheng wasn't afraid of battle. However, if he participated in the battle, he would definitely attract the attention of the Saintess of the Great Yu Divine Kingdom. What should he do?

Chapter 779
A martial artist had to compete with others, with the earth, and with the heavens.
Luo Zheng wasn't afraid of being challenged by others, nor was he afraid of challenging others.
On the contrary, only by competing with the geniuses of the world could he go further on the path of martial arts.
If it wasn't for Ning Yudie, Luo Zheng would also step onto the Divine Kingdom Continent and challenge the geniuses there!
However, the most important thing for Luo Zheng now was to collect the Origin Stones of Life. He didn't want to get involved in other troubles. Moreover, if the Saintess of the Divine Kingdom found out about him, she would probably make things difficult for him. It might even lead to the attack of the Divine Sea Realm experts of the Divine Kingdom of Yu the Great.
To be safe, Luo Zheng had chosen to keep a low profile since he entered the Divine Kingdom Continent.
However, as the situation developed, someone would eventually push Luo Zheng out from behind the scenes. He secretly glanced at King Yan who was on the stairs and sighed in his heart. Looking at King Yan's eager expression, he was afraid that he wouldn't be able to escape.
Luo Zheng's guess was correct.
King Yan was the same as the Crown Prince. They both wanted to prove themselves in front of Zhou Huang. They wanted to prove not only their strength but also their talent.
If the Crown Prince could persuade people like Xiao Daoyuan and Zhan Yun, what about King Yan?
This was also the reason why King Yan treated Luo Zheng so well. In order to win over Luo Zheng, he even condescended and treated himself as an equal to Luo Zheng. He calmly opened his heart to Luo Zheng and told him about his current predicament. This was because King Yan also knew that people like Luo Zheng couldn't be moved by ordinary things. The only way was to treat him with sincerity.
Moreover, King Yan believed in his own judgment. Luo Zheng's strength was worthy of his friendship!
Therefore, King Yan wasn't thinking about whether Luo Zheng would fight, but when he would fight.
The battle in the sky was in full swing. Zhan Yun was indeed the strongest False Tribulation Realm expert in the Profound Jade Martial House. His attacks were even stronger than Zhao Tianji's!
In particular, the medium quality god artifact in his hand was able to leave a faint mark on Yun Longtao's Spatial Armour every time he slashed out. Although he was unable to shatter the Spatial Armour, everyone could see it clearly.
Those marks continued to increase and deepen. If they accumulated to a certain extent, the spatial armor would eventually collapse.
"My saber is called Dragon's Tooth. It was forged from the teeth of a true dragon. Blindly resisting is useless. This tortoise shell on your body will eventually be shattered by me! A martial artist who only knows how to retreat will eventually be defeated! "Zhan Yun sneered. His figure was like the wind, instantly transforming into a beam of light that rushed towards Yun Longtao.
"Lose!"
Clang!
Zhan Yun slashed out again, landing on Yun Longtao's spatial armor.
The saber intent that Zhan Yun possessed contained an aura that could cut through everything in the world. Many Void Tribulation Realm warriors looked over and their eyelids twitched.
Luo Zheng nodded slightly. Zhan Yun's attacks were indeed very sharp. If Yun Longtao continued to resist like this, Zhan Yun would eventually break through the spatial armor.
Unfortunately, it was still not enough … Luo Zheng sighed slightly.

"Hiss …"

Yun Longtao had a smile on his face as he lightly waved the Sky Slashing Sword in his hand. Indeed, he did not waste any more words and attacked Xiao Daoyuan a second time!
After Xiao Daoyuan activated the spirit of the spear, he waited for Yun Longtao to approach. Like a beast with a keen sense of smell, he automatically attacked Yun Longtao.
"Ding!"

This fellow had an invulnerable spatial armor and a sharp Sky Slashing Sword that could cut through everything. How could he fight him? This was simply unsolvable!
Just as everyone had thought, Yun Longtao, who had become serious, gave up on dodging and defending. He allowed Xiao Daoyuan's spear to stab straight at him while the Sky Slashing Sword in his hand continued to slide through space, slashing at Xiao Daoyuan.

Chapter 782
After Xiao Daoyuan's defeat, other than the Crown Prince and King Yan, the Black Iron Divine Nation had no other worthy fighters.

There was a huge gap in strength between the first tier and the second tier. Even if the top fighters of the second tier went up, they would be instantly killed by Yun Longtao.

The Crown Prince had to recruit such a person. That was why he hurried to Lonely Crane City. At that time, Zhou Ning thought that it would be easy to recruit Luo Zheng. After all, Zhan Yun also told him that King Yan had a bad relationship with this person.
However, he did not expect King Yan to appear halfway.
After listening to Zhan Yun's description, Zhou Ning only had a rough estimate of Luo Zheng's strength. This kid's strength was probably on par with Zhan Yun's, or maybe even stronger. Even if he went up, he would not be Yun Longtao's opponent. However, the Black Iron Divine Nation had no other worthy fighters.
These were not the main problem. The main problem was whether this kid would listen to his orders. What if he spoke and Luo Zheng did not agree? What should he do?
Seeing the Crown Prince's flickering eyes, King Yan sneered. He knew that it was time for him to go up. The Black Iron Divine Nation had just been defeated. Now, it was time for the Heavenly Wind Divine Nation and Star Field Divine Nation to send out their fighters. Whether they could challenge Yun Longtao or not, King Yan would send Luo Zheng!
Zhou Huang's two sons each had their own thoughts. However, everyone's attention was still on the Heavenly Wind Divine Nation and the Star Field Divine Nation.
The Heavenly Wind War Emperor was wearing a huge cloak. He stood on the platform at the top of the stairs. When the wind blew, the cloak fluttered loudly.
From the beginning to the end, the Heavenly Wind Divine Nation did not send out a single fighter. Now, it seemed that the Heavenly Wind War Emperor still did not plan to send out a fighter to challenge Yun Longtao.

At this moment, Battle Emperor Yu the Great's gloomy face finally relaxed. He suddenly turned his head and looked at Zhou Huang with a smile. "I wonder if there's anyone from the Black Iron Divine Nation who can still fight?"
There were only 20 to 30 people in the four Divine Nations who could stand on the first step. Everyone had a list of these people!

Chapter 783
The Saintess of the Esoteric Sect of the Great Yu Divine Kingdom was named Bai Yuerong. The Bai Family was an unknown warrior family in the Great Yu Divine Kingdom. However, Bai Yuerong was chosen as the Saintess of the Esoteric Sect after she was born.

It was said that there were a total of nine spirit lamps in the immortal tomb. Once they were opened, they could open a passage and enter an immortal tomb.
However, after waiting for many years, the spirit lamp in the immortal tomb had not been opened. Bai Yuerong was curious. On the third day of March every year, she and Liu Hongwen would open the spirit lamp in the immortal tomb together. Unexpectedly, a space passage really appeared!
Because the two of them did not expect to open the spirit lamp in the immortal tomb at this time, they hurriedly invited a few Life and Death Realm guards and entered the Misty Sky Immortal Tomb.

Any Divine Sea Realm almighty could flatten the entire Tianbei Region if they rushed across the Chaotic Astral Ocean!

Bai Yuerong arranged for the Heartless Sweetheart to do these things.

These two mighty warriors in the Divine Sea Realm died in such a strange way in Tian Bei Region, which indeed shocked the entire Yu Da's Divine Kingdom!
For the Divine Kingdom, Tian Bei Region was just a barren island. In such a place, who could threaten a mighty warrior in the Divine Sea Realm?

The strange death of two mighty warriors in the Divine Sea Realm alerted the Yu Da's Divine Kingdom immediately. However, the Yu Da's Divine Kingdom didn't take any action for the time being. When the next storm subsided, it would not be too late to form a team of mighty warriors in the Divine Sea Realm to explore the Tian Bei Region. If some mighty warriors really appeared in the Tian Bei Region, they would directly wipe out all the creatures in the Tian Bei Region.
As for Luo Zheng, although Bai Yuerong was concerned about him, now it involved the lives of two mighty warriors in the Divine Sea Realm, Heartless Beloved and Star Sword Esteemed. It was not something that Bai Yuerong could decide. She could only wait for the time being.
Today, when she heard the King of Yan call out Luo Tianxing, her gaze landed on that person. Although two years had passed and Luo Zheng's face was somewhat unfamiliar to her, it was difficult to change a person's spirit, spirit, and aura.
What was more, this guy's surname was Luo.
Luo Tianxing?

If you don't believe me, you can ask Elder Gong to come here! "

Although Bai Yuerong's sudden appearance seemed a little rash, according to Battle Emperor Yu the Great's understanding, although Bai Yuerong's character was jumpy, she wouldn't speak without thinking in this kind of situation!

Chapter 784

The powerful soul attack continued to attack Luo Zheng's soul. However, Luo Zheng's soul was extremely sturdy. It was just that the surface of his soul kept flashing with a jade-green light. His soul was fine!
"I'm afraid that the soul in his mind has already been shattered …"

Many Void Tribulation Realm warriors revealed expressions of lingering fear when they saw the powerful soul attack. Although the attack wasn't targeted at them, just feeling the lingering might of the soul attack was enough to make them feel fear.
King Yan closed his eyes and frowned deeply. Although he hadn't known Luo Zheng for a long time, he knew that Luo Zheng was not only extremely talented and powerful, but he also never pretended. This was why King Yan valued Luo Zheng. He treated Luo Zheng as a true friend.

If Luo Zheng's soul was completely destroyed, Luo Zheng's life force would disappear. However, if Luo Zheng's soul was destroyed, Luo Zheng would not die but would become an idiot. Zhou Huang couldn't stop him in time. He could only ask Liu Zan to leave Luo Zheng alive.
It was better to become an idiot than to die.
However, as soon as Zhou Huang finished speaking, Liu Zan's face revealed a strange expression. His eyes were fixed on Luo Zheng.
The stalemate lasted for a while. Many Divine Sea Realm warriors felt that something was wrong.
Not only Zhou Huang, but also the King of the Star Field Divine Nation and the Tianfeng War Emperor of the Tianfeng Divine Nation looked at Luo Zheng with strange expressions. At the same time, they shifted their gazes back to Battle Emperor Yu the Great.
Luo Tianxing's eyes were clear and bright. It seemed that he was fine.
What was going on?
Did Battle Emperor Yu the Great show mercy?
This was impossible. The four Kings had been in power for so many years. They knew each other's temperament very well.
Battle Emperor Yu the Great, Liu Zan, had a gloomy personality. He was cold-blooded and arrogant. If he chose to attack, there was no possibility of him showing mercy.
However, Luo Tianxing seemed to be fine. What was going on?
On the stairs, the Divine Sea Realm warriors' eyes flickered. They couldn't understand the situation at all.
As time passed, not only did the Divine Sea Realm warriors realize that something was wrong, but even the Life and Death Realm warriors and the False Tribulation Realm warriors could see that something was wrong.

Chapter 785
Everyone's first reaction was that Battle Emperor Yu the Great had held back.
After opening the Spirit Sea, martial artists would have their own Inner World and it would be easier for them to cultivate their souls.
The resources in the Lower World were scarce and could not be compared to the Upper World. Therefore, there were not many methods to cultivate one's soul.
Therefore, many genius martial artists in the Upper World could cultivate their souls to the Combat Soul Realm when they were in the False Tribulation Realm or the Life and Death Realm. However, in the Lower World, many martial artists had to open their Spirit Sea and become Divine Sea Almighties before they could cultivate their souls to the Combat Soul Realm.
As for Luo Zheng, he was an accident.
In a sense, it was not an accident because Luo Zheng did not cultivate his soul in the Lower World. He relied on his ascension to the True Dragon World. Under the test of the Evil Spirit of the Owl Beast, his soul had improved rapidly and he had absorbed a considerable amount of soul energy. Only then was his soul able to reach the Combat Soul Realm.
In the Lower World, Divine Sea Almighties could easily suppress Life and Death Realm experts. First of all, there was a huge gap between their souls.
Only after reaching the Combat Soul Realm could one's soul transform into a powerful weapon.
Battle Emperor Yu the Great, Liu Zan, looked at Luo Zheng with a complicated expression. He could feel that Luo Zheng's soul was not weaker than his. In fact, it was even stronger than his!
If Luo Zheng had chosen to fight back, Battle Emperor Yu the Great would have suffered a great loss. At the very least, the wisp of soul that attacked Luo Zheng would have been destroyed by Luo Zheng.
However, Luo Zheng did not fight back. He was probably afraid of Battle Emperor Yu the Great's strength.
Battle Emperor Yu the Great stared at Luo Zheng in a daze. After a while, he said, "Good, good, good. At such a young age, with such cultivation, your soul is close to the Blue Soul Realm! You are indeed a young hero! "
When Battle Emperor Yu the Great said this, everyone's expression changed drastically!
Everyone thought that Battle Emperor Yu the Great had held back. However, Battle Emperor Yu the Great told everyone that he had not held back. It was because Luo Zheng's soul was close to the Blue Soul Realm!
Below the Divine Sea Realm, it was extremely difficult for one's soul to reach the Combat Soul Realm. In other words, it was impossible in the lower realm!
There were too few soul cultivation techniques and pills, and their effects were usually very poor. Since ancient times, there had never been a Life and Death Realm warrior who had cultivated his soul to the Battle Soul Realm, let alone Luo Zheng, who was just a False Tribulation Realm warrior! Moreover, he was only at the middle stage of the False Tribulation Realm!
Therefore, how could everyone not be shocked when they heard Battle Emperor Yu the Great's words?
Upon hearing Battle Emperor Yu the Great's words, Bai Yuerong immediately replied, "That's right! The Heartless Master once said that the Spirit Goblet he set up in the Northern Heaven Region was destroyed by Luo Zheng. This person's soul force is so powerful that Elder Gong doesn't need to come and identify you. There's no doubt that you're Luo Zheng! "
"No matter who this kid is, how did he cultivate his soul to such a level? Even Battle God Yu the Great is helpless against him? "
"Isn't this too exaggerated? Moreover, he came from a small place like the North Heaven Region. It is said that even a Life and Death Realm expert could sweep through the North Heaven Region … "
"That's impossible. How could a place like the North Heaven Region give birth to such a monster?"
The False Tribulation Realm warriors who could stand in the West Dragon River were basically divine geniuses in the four Divine Kingdoms. They were geniuses at the first and second tier. Even geniuses at the third tier were rare.
These divine geniuses were likely to enter the Divine Sea Realm in the future! Of course, it was only a possibility. The number of warriors who could enter the Divine Sea Realm in the end was still small.
Since they were geniuses, they would be conceited!
But at this moment, they were all shocked. This shock wasn't because of Luo Zheng's strength. There were many ways to measure a martial artist's strength, such as cultivation methods, strength, speed, cultivation, soul, and so on.
Soul was only one of them.
Just like what Battle Emperor Yu the Great had said, Luo Zheng's soul was indeed very powerful. It was even close to the Blue Soul Realm. However, this did not mean that Luo Zheng was very strong in other aspects. After all, Luo Zheng had not shown his strength yet.
Even so, the fact that his soul was close to the Blue Soul Realm made them so jealous that they could not believe it. They did not want to believe it!
It was like a group of people of the same age who were originally at the same starting line. Everyone earned about the same amount of wealth every day. Occasionally, someone would earn twice or thrice as much as them. This was also within the normal range. Other than being envious, everyone would also work harder to catch up.
However, one day, a guy suddenly appeared. This guy was about the same age as them and had the same cultivation as them. In the end, he earned hundreds or even thousands of times more than them. How could other people of the same age feel at ease?
They did not even have the courage to catch up …
The three Divine Sea Realm warriors of the Profound Jade Martial House were even more shocked. They had already seen Luo Zheng's strength. This guy not only had an amazing understanding of the Dharma, but his physical strength had also reached a horrifying level. Now, the three of them realized that Luo Zheng's soul was also very powerful. This guy was a monster with no flaws!
When this guy reached the Divine Sea Realm in the future, what kind of terrifying strength would he have?
Crown Prince Zhou Ning stared at Luo Zheng. He murmured in his heart, "A soul close to the Blue Soul Realm … How could this be? How could this be? Alas, if this person could be roped in by me, he would be of great help to me in the future. Unfortunately, he chose to side with Yan Wang. Is this fate? "
Today, the rulers of the four Divine Kingdoms had arrived. Moreover, they had brought along their respective Crown Princes and Princes.
The Crown Princes and Princes who were able to follow behind the rulers were naturally not weak. They were all firmly ranked in the first tier of martial artists. Moreover, some of the Crown Princes and Princes were even able to stand at the top of the first tier!
These Crown Princes and Princes were the true protagonists of the four Divine Kingdoms' future!
As long as ordinary Divine Rank geniuses did not "fall", they would eventually reach the Divine Sea Realm. However, these Crown Princes and Princes of the Divine Kingdoms were almost certain to reach the Divine Sea Realm!
However, at this time, many Crown Princes and Princes of the Divine Kingdoms were extremely depressed.
They were originally the pride of their Divine Kingdoms. They were the strongest of the younger generation. Under the wings of their Emperor Father, they steadily grew. With the accumulation of vast amounts of resources and secret cultivation sites, their achievements and strength far exceeded their peers by several times!
However, now, they had been dealt a heavy blow by Luo Zheng.
As the saying goes, comparisons are odious. In the past, no one could compare with them because they were the first batch of martial artists. However, now that they compared themselves with Luo Zheng, they realized the gap between them and Luo Zheng.
"Hmph, it's only his soul that is strong. I think he must have encountered some great opportunity that caused his soul to mutate. I'm afraid his strength is not enough to withstand a single blow!"
"I would like to meet this guy. It's useless to have a strong soul. I have the secret treasure that my Emperor Father gave me to protect my soul. This guy can't do anything to me!"
"Let's see how it goes. If this guy can defeat Yun Longtao, maybe I'll be interested in going down to play with him!"
The Crown Princes and Princes of the Divine Kingdoms all had the same thought. Only by thinking this way could they feel better.
On the other hand, King Yan opened his eyes. His eyes were shining with excitement. He had long noticed that Luo Zheng was extraordinary. However, he did not expect Luo Zheng's soul to be so ridiculously strong. Even Liu Zan could not do anything to Luo Zheng. This guy had brought him a huge surprise.
At this time, King Yan suddenly became vigilant. He immediately used his True Essence to transmit his voice to Zhou Huang, "Emperor Father …"
How could Zhou Huang not know what King Yan meant?
Just now, Liu Zan had suddenly attacked Luo Zheng with his soul. Unexpectedly, Luo Zheng's soul was not much weaker than Liu Zan's.
Now, Liu Zan's heart was filled with killing intent. If he used his True Essence to crush Luo Zheng, how could Luo Zheng survive?
It would be fine if such a person did not appear in the Black Iron Divine Kingdom. However, once he appeared, King Yan would protect him no matter what!
Swoosh!
Zhou Huang's figure flashed and stood between Luo Zheng and Battle Emperor Yu the Great. Then, he looked at Battle Emperor Yu the Great indifferently and said, "Liu Zan, that's enough. As the Monarch, you have gone too far by attacking a junior. No matter whether he is Luo Zheng or Luo Tianxing, he is still a member of the Black Iron Divine Kingdom!"

Chapter 786
Where there were people, there would be competition. Although the four Divine Kingdoms were united, there had never been a large-scale "Divine Kingdom War" between them.
However, this did not mean that there was no competition between the four Divine Kingdoms.
On the contrary, because the four Divine Kingdoms were on good terms, they were unable to annex each other. Therefore, the competition between them was even more intense!
This was also the reason why the four Divine Kingdoms had so many Martial Houses. The purpose was to nurture new geniuses!
As the Monarch of the Black Iron Divine Kingdom, Zhou Huang was well aware of Luo Zheng's value. Although he had not seen Luo Zheng's true strength, he knew that Luo Zheng's soul was close to the Blue Soul Realm. This was enough to explain many things.
Although Zhou Huang spoke in an amiable manner, it did not mean that he was afraid of Battle Emperor Yu the Great!
In the past few years, Yu the Great Divine Kingdom's development was the strongest. In terms of overall strength, Yu the Great Divine Kingdom was ranked first while Black Iron Divine Kingdom was ranked fourth.
However, that was only in terms of overall strength. In terms of strength, Zhou Huang might not be inferior to Liu Zan!
Hearing Zhou Huang's words, Liu Zan's face turned gloomy. He asked, "Zhou Huang, what do you mean?"
Zhou Huang said indifferently, "My meaning is clear. I don't know if he is Luo Zheng or Luo Tianxing. He came from my Black Iron Divine Kingdom and the Black Jade Martial House. He is a subject of my Black Iron Divine Kingdom!"
Liu Zan snorted coldly as a sneer appeared at the corner of his mouth. "So, my son died for nothing?"
Zhou Huang's lips curled up and he replied indifferently, "If I remember correctly, Liu Zan, you have more than twenty thousand descendants, right?"
Some Monarchs liked women and naturally had many children. However, the Monarchs of the four Divine Kingdoms all had many children. After all, as Monarchs, the more successors they had, the better. Only then would they be able to find the best of the best and find the most outstanding heir!
However, Liu Zan, the Battle Emperor of Yu the Great, probably had the most children. This person was extremely fond of women. His harem occupied millions of hectares of land, which was equivalent to a medium-sized city. There were probably tens of thousands of beautiful women in his harem.
These concubines had probably given birth to more than twenty thousand princes. This Liu Zan was probably unable to remember the names of all his sons …
Princes were worthless in the Divine Kingdoms.
Even if he was a prince, he had to rely on his talent and strength to prove that he was not a good-for-nothing. Only then would he be able to eat a hot meal! Most of the princes who were not talented enough could only be cruelly eliminated.
From this point of view, it could be seen that the lives of the princes with inferior talent were much more miserable than that of ordinary people.
"Even though I have many children, Hongwen's talent is excellent and he is deeply favored by me. His relationship with the Saintess is also extremely good! Don't tell me that as a father, I can't ask for an explanation! "Liu Zan said coldly to Zhou Huang.
Hearing that Liu Zan wanted an explanation, Zhou Huang sneered in his heart. Finally, he revealed his true colors.
Among the four monarchs, Liu Zan's character was known to be sinister and treacherous. After saying so much, he was just trying to extort a sum of money from him.
"I will give you an explanation! But I'll say it again, Luo Tianxing is a warrior of my Black Iron Godly Country! "Zhou Huang slowly moved aside. At the same time, he turned around and said to Luo Zheng," Luo Tianxing! "
With a smile on his face, Luo Zheng cupped his hands towards Zhou Huang and said, "Here!"
"Since my son, Zhihua, asked you to fight on his behalf, then you should perform well! But you have to be careful. "Zhou Huang said faintly and returned to the high platform.
As a monarch, it was commendable for him to do this. After all, Luo Zheng was just a False Tribulation Realm warrior.
He reminded Luo Zheng to pay attention to the warriors of Yu the Great Divine Country. Since he had said this, Liu Zan certainly would not attack Luo Zheng.
However, the strength of the younger generation of warriors of Yu the Great Divine Country could not be underestimated. If a warrior of Yu the Great Divine Country accidentally "killed" Luo Zheng, Zhou Huang would not be able to protect Luo Zheng. He could only blame himself for not being strong enough.
Zhihua?
Black Iron Godly Country was the world of the Zhou family, so the full name of Prince Yan was Zhou Zhihua …
Luo Zheng was delighted. No wonder he was called "Huahua" before.
Luo Zheng was very touched by Zhou Huang's warning. After all, he was not a warrior of Black Iron Godly Country. For a False Tribulation Realm warrior like him, Zhou Huang was willing to protect him. This was already beyond Luo Zheng's expectations.
The disturbance came to an end. Luo Zheng turned around and flew towards the barrier in the air. His eyes finally fell on Yun Longtao.
Yun Longtao's mouth curved, but he still had a confident smile on his face. He said to Luo Zheng, "Your soul is very strong! But … I don't know how strong it is! "
With the soul of a Battle Soul Realm warrior, Luo Zheng's advantage was obvious when facing an ordinary False Tribulation Realm warrior.
Even the confident Yun Longtao had a deep fear. Although he had a magical treasure that could defend his soul, he was not sure if it could withstand Luo Zheng's soul attack.
Luo Zheng smiled and said, "Don't worry! I won't use soul attacks! "
These words made Yun Longtao's face sink. The kid in front of him seemed to be even more arrogant than him!
He obviously had an incomparable advantage, but he actually gave it up. Moreover, Luo Zheng had already seen Yun Longtao's strength. What did this mean?
"Good! You actually have such confidence! Longtao, this person deserves your full strength! "Liu Zan's voice sounded again.
The meaning of his words was very clear. It was equivalent to sealing off Luo Zheng's path of using his soul to suppress his opponent. He was going to rely on his own strength to challenge Yun Longtao!
Yun Longtao nodded. He was not scared. Moreover, Luo Zheng had given up his advantage in the soul. He had always believed that it would be difficult for him to find an opponent in the False Tribulation Realm. That was why he had the title of being invincible in the False Tribulation Realm. He was 80% sure that he could defeat Luo Zheng!
However, after hearing Liu Zan's words, Yun Longtao understood that he was not going to simply defeat Luo Zheng and take away Luo Zheng's arm. He was going to take Luo Zheng's life.
Luo Zheng slowly took out a long sword from his body. With a gentle shake, it glowed with a faint golden luster against the sunlight.
"Upper Grade Sacred Weapon?" Seeing Luo Zheng's long sword, Yun Longtao's eyes narrowed slightly.
Even in the Black Iron Divine Nation, which was famous for its blacksmiths, Upper Grade Sacred Weapons were rare. Even if there were, they were the weapons of the imperial family. It was very difficult for ordinary warriors to get their hands on them.
Therefore, when Zhou Huang saw the Upper Grade Sacred Weapon in Luo Zheng's hand, his face revealed a strange expression. "Zhihua, did you give him this Upper Grade Sacred Weapon?"
King Yan shook his head. "Luo Zheng didn't ask me for a Sacred Weapon. This should be his own."
Zhou Huang shook his head and smiled. "This person's origin, I'm afraid … it's not as simple as being born in the Tianbei Region."
His inference was very simple. It was impossible for a barren land like the Tianbei Region to give birth to a blacksmith who could refine Upper Grade Sacred Weapons. Even if there were, a blacksmith of that level would not be able to find the materials to refine Upper Grade Sacred Weapons!
Zhou Huang was a blacksmith himself. He knew very well what kind of price was needed to refine a Upper Grade Sacred Weapon.
King Yan nodded and said, "I understand. However, Luo Tianxing's character is excellent and we are like-minded. Therefore, I gave him my precious Origin Stone of Life."
"Well, well done. Such a person staying in the Black Iron Divine Nation will bring immeasurable benefits to the Divine Nation in the future," Zhou Huang said.
Hearing the conversation between King Yan and Zhou Huang, Crown Prince Zhou Ning's face gradually darkened!
Zhou Ning and Zhou Zhihua had been fighting for a long time. Even though Zhou Zhihua had been conferred the title of King, there was still a long way to go. Even though he was the Crown Prince now, he knew very well that this competition had not ended. Unless he could step into the Divine Sea Realm in the future and his father abdicated, his position as the Crown Prince would be threatened by King Yan!
Now, one could imagine the pressure that Luo Zheng brought to Zhou Ning.
While they were talking, the battle had already begun.
Yun Longtao had put away all his contempt. Layers of Space Armor condensed. At the same time, the Sky Slashing Sword in his hand constantly twisted the space. It was as if there were countless water ripples flowing slowly in the space. Under the sunlight, they shone with colorful lights.
Then, Yun Longtao took a step forward. A string of figures appeared in the sky. Then, he appeared strangely beside Luo Zheng.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 787
This was the tyrannical aspect of the Space Laws. From beginning to end, Yun Longtao had only used two simple moves.
Shuttling through space, attacking with his sword, shuttling through space, attacking with his sword …
These simple moves were impossible to guard against. He had easily defeated the geniuses of the Divine Kingdom's first step, even cutting off their arms!
However, it was not easy to cultivate the Space Laws. The vast majority of people did not have any comprehension of the Space Laws, let alone cultivate and apply them. Therefore, the moment Yun Longtao comprehended the third level of the Space Laws, his strength had already far surpassed his peers.
The moment Yun Longtao shuttled through space, he suddenly discovered that Luo Zheng's figure had also flickered, instantly disappearing in front of him. At the same time, he felt a familiar power of the Space Laws!
"This guy is also proficient in the Space Laws!"
Yun Longtao was shocked. The Space Slashing Sword in his hand was startled. He hurriedly slashed behind him, but this slash hit nothing but air.
Luo Zheng was standing not too far away, looking at him indifferently, a trace of ridicule on his face!
Seeing Luo Zheng's expression, Yun Longtao felt blood rush to his face. Since when did a cultivator of the same level dare to mock him!? Not to mention, this guy was only at the middle stage of the False Tribulation Realm!
"This Luo Zheng is also proficient in the Space Laws! And the number of layers isn't low, it seems to be two! "
"But Yun Longtao has already comprehended the third level of the Space Laws. I wonder who is stronger between the two of them."
"So what if it's the third level of the Space Laws? Yun Longtao hasn't completely comprehended the third level. At best, he has only comprehended 10% of the third level. The advantage isn't great. It still depends on how the two of them display their true strength! "
"If Luo Zheng doesn't use his soul to suppress Yun Longtao, I think his chances of winning are slim …"
Seeing Luo Zheng easily use the Space Laws, many of the Divine rank geniuses sighed. In their opinion, this guy was too lucky. Not only was his soul powerful, but even the Law he cultivated was the most powerful Space Laws!
"It looks like I should be more serious." Yun Longtao finally put away all his contempt and tightened his grip on the Space Slashing Sword.
"Are you still not serious enough?" Luo Zheng smiled faintly.
Yun Longtao clenched his teeth. "Die!"
After saying that, he took another step forward, but his figure suddenly disappeared.
"Hmm?" Luo Zheng's eyes flashed. He couldn't sense any of Yun Longtao's aura. This guy had completely disappeared from this world!
It should be the ability of the third level of the Law of Space. He didn't disappear, but was hiding in the void!
After comprehending the first level of the Space Laws, one would be able to cross short distances in space. Of course, this was only a superficial feeling. In truth, a martial artist was using the Space Laws to connect two space nodes together. From one space node, one could step out from another space node.
After comprehending the second level of the law of space, one could float outside of space. However, even Luo Zheng could only float outside of space for a short period of time. He was like a stone thrown into the sky. In the end, he couldn't escape the gravitational pull and fell back to his original place.
However, after cultivating the first and second level of the Law of Space, one could develop many other methods, such as condensing the "Space Armor" and the "Space Slashing Sword".
Luo Zheng didn't have the opportunity to comprehend the third level of the Law of Space. The obelisk in the True Dragon World only recorded the first level of the Law of Space, and Luo Zheng in the Sin Tower only cultivated the second level of the Law of Space. Therefore, Luo Zheng guessed that Yun Longtao could hide in the void by relying on the third level of the Law of Space!
A trace of vigilance appeared on Luo Zheng's face.
Even Luo Zheng had to admit that Yun Longtao's strength was extraordinary. An ordinary late stage False Tribulation Realm martial artist was simply no match for him.
If Yun Longtao hid in the void and launched a sneak attack, Luo Zheng wouldn't have a good way to deal with him. He could only let Yun Longtao attack …
"Hu …"
Just as Luo Zheng was on high alert, he suddenly felt a familiar fluctuation of the Law of Space beside him.
"It's coming!" Luo Zheng was startled. His figure suddenly twisted, and once again shuttled through space, leaving his original place.
After Luo Zheng and Yun Longtao had put some distance between them, the two looked at each other again. Yun Longtao's face had a cruel smile, and a few drops of red blood slowly flowed down from the transparent Space Slashing Sword.
"Your body is very unique! This sword should have cut open your neck! " Yun Longtao looked at Luo Zheng with a strange expression, then shook his head and grinned carelessly. He even stuck out his tongue and licked the blood on the Space Slashing Sword clean. "But it's still a bit slow. Next time, I'll be faster."
A thin line of blood appeared on Luo Zheng's neck. The Space Slashing Sword could shatter a Divine Tool, so his current high-grade Immortal Tool body naturally couldn't withstand it. However, if it were another person, their head would have been separated from their body.
Luo Zheng's eyes were indifferent, but he replied, "There won't be a next time. Even if you hide in the void, I can see through it."
"Is that so?" Yun Longtao didn't believe Luo Zheng's words, and once again disappeared in front of Luo Zheng.
The void and the entire world were separated. Generally speaking, the world inside the spatial crack was the void. It was like the reverse side of a mirror, the reverse side of the world. It was empty, and nothing existed!
This was not a concealment technique, and it was impossible for him to emit any aura for Luo Zheng to detect. Therefore, Luo Zheng couldn't see through it!
"Sure enough, it's not easy to deal with Yun Longtao!"
"Yun Longtao is the pride of our Yu the Great Divine Kingdom! He's invincible in the False Tribulation Realm! There's no way to deal with this method! "
"I'm afraid Luo Tianxing will die in Yun Longtao's hands. Since Battle Emperor Yu the Great has already hinted, why would he let Luo Tianxing live?"
Many warriors of the Profound Jade Martial House watched the battle in the sky nervously.
After the previous incident, many warriors of the Black Iron Divine Kingdom understood that Luo Zheng probably came from the Northern Heaven Region, and wasn't a member of the Black Iron Divine Kingdom.
However, Luo Zheng was, after all, representing the Black Iron Divine Nation and the Black Jade Martial House in this battle. This point was personally stated by the Black Iron Divine Nation's Ruler, Zhou Huang. They would not treat Luo Zheng as an outsider.
"Come on, Luo Tianxing!"
"Be careful of his assassination. Move quickly, he may not be able to catch up with you!"
The warriors of the Black Iron Divine Kingdom were also cheering for Luo Zheng.
Just after Yun Longtao entered the void, Luo Zheng raised the long sword in his hand. His eyes flickered slightly, and then there was an emerald light flowing within. It was the Clear Eyes Spirit Pupils!
The Clear Eyes Spirit Pupils allowed Luo Zheng to see through most of the illusions, and it also allowed him to detect the slightest changes.
Moving in the void and assassinating, this method was indeed troublesome for Luo Zheng, but it wasn't like he didn't have a way to break it!
When the emerald light appeared in Luo Zheng's eyes, the whole world seemed to be plated with a layer of green. Even if one walked on the reverse side of the world, it was impossible not to leave behind any clues.
Under the flickering of the Clear Eyes Spirit Pupils, Luo Zheng saw a small black line constantly spreading in the void. It was the trace left behind by walking in the void.
This black line couldn't be seen with the naked eye, and it couldn't be detected with the soul perception. However, under the Clear Eyes Spirit Pupils, it couldn't hide.
Of course, Luo Zheng wasn't a fool. Naturally, he wouldn't stare at the black line. His eyes were still sweeping around, but the corner of his eyes firmly locked on the black line.
Perhaps this Yun Longtao was really wary of Luo Zheng. He was actually very careful. After circling around Luo Zheng, he wanted to catch Luo Zheng off guard. He was actually slowly approaching Luo Zheng from the front!
As the black line got closer and closer, Luo Zheng still revealed a hesitant expression. The moment the black line got closer, Luo Zheng suddenly faced the front, revealing a sweet smile, and then suddenly stabbed out with his long sword!
The timing of his sword stabbing out was just right, and it just happened to collide with the Sky Slashing Sword!
Moreover, since Yun Longtao came out of the void, there would inevitably be a spatial crack that could allow one person to pass through. When Luo Zheng struck out with his sword, his body rushed forward. Not only did he repel Yun Longtao, he also followed closely behind and entered the spatial crack.

Chapter 788
With Luo Zheng's understanding of space, he couldn't escape into the void.
Even if he could see through Yun Longtao's path in the void, he could only dodge in advance. As long as Yun Longtao stayed in the void and attacked Luo Zheng, Luo Zheng couldn't do anything to Yun Longtao.
Therefore, the moment Yun Longtao attacked, Luo Zheng directly used his sword to push Yun Longtao back into the void. At the same time, he squeezed into the space crack!
This was also because Yun Longtao was too careless. He never thought that Luo Zheng could see through his position. Yun Longtao originally thought that this attack would be enough to cut Luo Zheng in two. If he was more cautious and didn't open too big of a space crack, Luo Zheng wouldn't be able to do anything to him.
After the two disappeared into thin air, the barrier in the sky was empty. The geniuses of the four Divine Kingdoms looked at each other and started to stare blankly at the sky.
Only the Divine Sea Realm experts and some Life and Death Realm experts stared at the sky without blinking.
If one wanted to see through the void, they naturally had to use their own magical powers. For example, Zhou Huang's eyes were shining with a golden luster. Through the golden luster, his gaze seemed to be able to see through everything in the world.
Battle Emperor Yu the Great's eyes were faintly flickering with a black light.
Some Divine Sea Realm experts even took out some magical treasures. For example, one Divine Sea Realm expert placed an almost transparent crystal in front of his eyes. Only then could he observe the battle in the void.
In the void, Luo Zheng and Yun Longtao were fighting fiercely.
After a short moment of surprise, Yun Longtao quickly calmed down. The Sky Slashing Sword in his hand was as sharp as a poisonous snake. It slashed at Luo Zheng again and again.
If it were an ordinary sacred weapon, it naturally wouldn't be able to withstand the slashes of the Sky Slashing Sword. However, the Sky Slashing Sword couldn't shatter a high-grade sacred weapon.
As the two of them exchanged blows, Yun Longtao's disadvantage became apparent.
The martial skills that Yun Longtao cultivated were relatively simple. Of course, it didn't mean that simple martial skills were inferior. His sword moves pursued speed, ruthlessness, and accuracy! He strived to achieve the best results with the most powerful sword moves in the shortest amount of time.
From this point of view, it was somewhat similar to the path of minimalism pursued by Luo Zheng.
However, Luo Zheng's "Formless" was the best way to restrain this type of sword technique.
There were only two ways to break Luo Zheng's move. The first was to win with pure strength, completely ignoring Luo Zheng's moves and directly suppressing Luo Zheng.
The second was to comprehend an even more exquisite move to deal with it. Martial artists with such martial skills might exist, but it was absolutely impossible for them to be born in the lower realms. Without a doubt, Yun Longtao was not one of them.
"My swordsmanship is at a complete disadvantage!"
Yun Longtao gritted his teeth, and the Sky Slashing Sword in his hand became even sharper. What kind of person was he?
Even if he didn't come from a noble background, relying on his own diligence and talent to achieve his current status step by step, becoming a prominent martial artist in the Great Yu Divine Kingdom and firmly standing at the top of the first step of the younger generation, it was impossible for him to give up so easily!
However, Yun Longtao couldn't figure out why the set of swordsmanship he cultivated was a secret inheritance of the Great Yu Divine Kingdom. It was a wonderful swordsmanship passed down from the upper realms. Why was it so easily broken by Luo Zheng and suppressed by him?
He felt that Luo Zheng's swordsmanship was like a hoop spell, tightly binding his every move. No matter how he struggled, he couldn't use his full strength!
In fact, from the beginning to the end, Luo Zheng's swordsmanship was very simple. Every time the Sky Slashing Sword stabbed out, he would lift his long sword, and the floating sword would casually block Yun Longtao's attack. Sword after sword, Luo Zheng's state of mind became more and more calm, and his sword attacks became more and more casual.
On the other hand, Yun Longtao became more and more impatient.
"Break for me! Sky Slashing Sword, Chaotic Void Slash! "
Yun Longtao clenched his teeth, and a dark red color flashed in his eyes. The Sky Slashing Sword in his hand suddenly transformed into thousands of sword shadows that chaotically flew out.
Luo Zheng still had a faint smile on his face, and the long sword seemed like a weak and powerless woman waving it.
"Ding!"
After comprehending the Formless Sword, Luo Zheng could almost see through most of the swordsmanship at a glance. Yun Longtao exerted the Sky Slashing Sword to the extreme, but Luo Zheng could still see through it in an instant!
The moment the weak and powerless sword blocked the Sky Slashing Sword, Luo Zheng smiled at Yun Longtao. Yun Longtao was stunned for a moment, and then he felt a huge and irresistible power pouring out from Luo Zheng's long sword!
The surging power gushed out in an instant, and Yun Longtao almost couldn't hold the long sword in his hand. If not for the fact that the Sky Slashing Sword was condensed by the Law of Space and was an existence that was one with him, it would have flown out of his hand.
Even so, Yun Longtao's sword-wielding hand was blocked by Luo Zheng's long sword, and the door in front of him was wide open!
Luo Zheng heard it from the front, and his fist directly smashed towards Yun Longtao!
"Bang!"
The first punch smashed into the Space Armor on Yun Longtao's chest. The power of this punch wasn't enough to break through space, and Yun Longtao was safe and sound. He only borrowed Luo Zheng's huge force to fly backwards.
The power wasn't enough …
Luo Zheng's eyes flashed, and his figure flashed as he chased after the constantly retreating Yun Longtao.
Under this pursuit, Yun Longtao's Sky Slashing Sword attacked back, but Luo Zheng didn't care at all. His long sword suddenly swung out, and the Sky Slashing Sword was knocked away again.
"Bang!"
Immediately after, a second punch smashed into the Space Armor. Luo Zheng opened the power of 300 dragon scales in this punch, and tiny cracks began to appear on the Space Armor, like fine spiderwebs.
"What a formidable strength. Not only is this fellow's sword skill incomparably exquisite, but he also possesses such strength. Is he a monster?"
It had been a long time since anyone had given him such a feeling. Among the younger generation, besides the royal family of the four Divine Kingdoms, only the little monster of the Nine Cauldron Martial House was worthy of his attention.
But now, in Yun Longtao's eyes, Luo Zheng seemed to be even more terrifying and formidable than the little monster of the Nine Cauldron Martial House.
That little monster was indeed formidable, but he had serious shortcomings. But this Luo Zheng … not only did he have a formidable soul, but he also cultivated exquisite sword skills. What was even more frightening was his strength. If it weren't for the fact that he condensed the Space Armor into an abnormally sturdy armor, a single punch from him would have been enough to cripple him.
I'm not his match …
As proud as he was, he had no choice but to lower his head and come to such an answer.
Beside the West Dragon River, the wind was howling. An ordinary person would find it difficult to even walk. If they were careless, they would be swept away by the wind and sent into the West Dragon River to die.
The warriors present were at least in the False Tribulation Realm. No matter how the wind blew, they stood still, quietly waiting for the outcome of the battle between Luo Zheng and Yun Longtao.
Those Divine Sea Realm warriors looked at the void, knowing the outcome of the battle. But the warriors in the square below didn't know.
"Who has the upper hand? I wonder if Luo Tianxing can do it!"
"I believe he can win …"
"I believe so too!"
The warriors of the Black Iron Divine Kingdom could only pin their hopes on Luo Zheng.
Hearing the words of the warriors of the Black Iron Divine Kingdom, the warriors of the Great Yu Divine Kingdom laughed.
"Haha, these guys of the Black Iron Divine Kingdom are too naive! Senior Brother Yun Tao, who has comprehended the third level of the Space Law, is almost invincible. Win? How can Luo Tianxing win? "
"It's unreasonable for a Mid False Tribulation Realm warrior to win against a Late False Tribulation Realm warrior. What's more, Senior Brother Yun Tao has the strength to fight against a Life and Death Realm warrior. They are dreaming!"
"Let them dream. But the reality is always cruel. When they wake up, they will cry."
The warriors of the Great Yu Divine Kingdom were very calm. After all, they knew how terrifying Yun Longtao was.
At this moment, everyone suddenly felt an energy fluctuation in the sky. This fluctuation was so obvious that almost everyone felt it at once.
"He's out!"
Everyone saw a strange crack appear in the sky, and then a figure stumbled out of it!
"It seems that the battle is over. I said that Luo Tianxing is not Senior Brother Yun Tao, right …" A warrior of the Great Yu Divine Kingdom was only halfway through his words when he saw the sorry figure. It was Yun Longtao!
"Senior Brother Yun Tao is actually at a disadvantage!" The warriors of the Great Yu Divine Kingdom were shocked.
A warrior with sharp eyes shook his head and said, "No, not only is he at a disadvantage, he is being suppressed by Luo Tianxing."

Chapter 789
Just like that cultivator said, when Yun Longtao escaped from the void, Luo Zheng's figure also floated over.
Yun Longtao's counterattack was still futile and powerless, and Luo Zheng still used "Formless" to easily parry the Sky Slashing Sword.
With a crisp sound, Luo Zheng punched out again.
"Ding!"
"Peng!"
These two moves suppressed Yun Longtao to the point that he had no chance to fight back.
With this punch, the space armor on Yun Longtao's chest had already reached its limit. It was covered in broken lines, as if it would collapse at any moment.
"Again!"
Yun Longtao's temperament was that of someone who would never admit defeat. He could take his life, but he would never give up resisting and admit defeat.
"Ding!"
"Peng!"
In a situation where both sides were equal in strength, the cultivator with the strongest willpower would often win.
Unfortunately, when the gap in strength was too large, willpower seemed to be stretched thin.
"Kuang dang …"
Under this punch, the sound of glass shattering could be heard. Yun Longtao's space armor could no longer withstand and shattered. Luo Zheng's punch directly hit Yun Longtao's chest.
A huge force passed through Luo Zheng's fist and hit Yun Longtao's chest. Like a falling meteor, Yun Longtao smashed downwards!
The two of them had just fought in the void and had already left the barrier. Without the barrier's obstruction, Yun Longtao directly smashed into the square. The cultivators below quickly dodged, and Yun Longtao's figure directly smashed a large hole in the ground.
Under the blowing of the strong wind, the rising smoke was like a wild wave of smoke. In an instant, it was blown away and dissipated into thin air.
Yun Longtao lay motionless in the large hole, and it was unknown whether he was dead or alive.
All the Divine Rank geniuses of the four Divine Kingdoms stared blankly at Yun Longtao. Just how mighty was Yun Longtao before?
He had consecutively killed several Divine Rank geniuses of the Black Iron Divine Kingdom and the Star Field Divine Kingdom. Moreover, the words he said made many cultivators feel ashamed. His talent was extremely high, but he thought that he was not worth mentioning. Such a character would definitely grow to a level that they could not look up to in the future.
He was invincible in the False Tribulation Realm. Many Divine Rank geniuses had already silently acknowledged this fact.
But now, Luo Zheng had smashed his space armor to pieces. Yun Longtao lay in the large hole like a dead pig. It had to be said that this was a crushing defeat.
"Good, very good, very good!" Zhou Huang suddenly said these words, a faint smile appearing on his face.
Under Yun Longtao's suppression, the Black Iron Divine Nation could be said to be dejected. Although the Black Iron Divine Nation did not have the upper hand in the past, the geniuses of the Divine Nation were still able to compete with other geniuses. They had never been so miserable.
Fortunately, Luo Zheng had stepped forward and saved the Divine Kingdom's face! Regardless of whether Luo Zheng was from Black Iron Divine Kingdom or not, at least he had passed the Black Jade Martial House's assessment and represented Black Iron Divine Kingdom in this battle! This victory had really come at the right time!
Prince Yan was also beaming with joy. He did not need to say much now. His father had always been fair. By relying on Luo Zheng, Prince Yan's status in his father's heart would rise significantly!
However, while there were happy people, there were also unhappy people.
Bai Yuerong's figure flashed gently, turning into a green light that swept across the square and flew into the large hole. Then, she took out a jade-white pill from her small pouch and stuffed it into Yun Longtao's mouth.
"Holy Maiden, is Longtao alright?" Battle Emperor Yu the Great asked with a gloomy face. The life of a False Tribulation Realm warrior was not worthy of Battle Emperor Yu the Great's personal concern. However, as the Monarch, he had to ask in front of everyone.
"His internal organs were shattered. It's nothing serious." Bai Yuerong shook her head and said. Then, she stared coldly at Luo Zheng in the sky and said, "Little monster, fight on my behalf! Kill him! "
Bai Yuerong's status was extraordinary. She was also qualified to choose a warrior to fight on her behalf.
Hearing Bai Yuerong's words, Luo Zheng was slightly stunned. Who was the Little Monster?
At this time, a sharp voice sounded, "According to the rules, Yu the Great's Divine Kingdom cannot send anyone to fight in this round!"
This round, it was Star Field Divine Kingdom and Sky Wind Divine Kingdom's turn to send a warrior to fight. However, Bai Yuerong was furious and did not think so much.
Under the sharp voice's reminder, Bai Yuerong gritted her teeth and said, "Then let's wait for the next round!"
Luo Zheng did not know who the Little Monster was, but the others knew.
In particular, King Yan's expression changed slightly. He did not expect Bai Yuerong to send the Little Monster to fight in her anger. Could Luo Zheng withstand it?
On the square, there was a thin and small warrior lying on the ground, snoring loudly.
From the beginning to the end, this warrior had not watched the battle in the sky. It was as if everything that happened around him had nothing to do with him.
However, after Bai Yuerong spoke, this thin and weak martial artist suddenly woke up. When he opened his eyes, he discovered that there was no white in his eyes. The eyeballs under his eyelids were pitch black, like black holes. It was a very strange sight!
This person was the Little Monster that Bai Yuerong had mentioned.
"Hehehe, Bai Yuerong, you want me to fight on your behalf?" The Little Monster's body was very strange. It was unknown how he moved his feet, but he came to Bai Yuerong's side in an instant.
It was a big taboo to call her name in front of so many people!
Bai Yuerong was the Saintess of the Esoteric Sect. Although she came from the Da Yu Kingdom, her influence spread across the entire continent!
After all, religions had no borders. Using the power of the Upper Realm, the Esoteric Sect had many followers in the four Holy Kingdoms. This was also the reason why Bai Yuerong's status was so high even though she was not strong.
Even the Battle Emperor Da Yu would not call her by her name. He had to call her Saintess. However, the Little Monster did not avoid this taboo.
Bai Yuerong glared at the Little Monster. If she had a choice, she would not want this guy to fight!
There were many Martial Houses in the four Holy Kingdoms. The number one Martial House in the Holy Kingdoms was the publicly recognized Nine Cauldrons Martial House!
The Little Monster was the strongest person in the Nine Cauldrons Martial House.
Even with Yun Longtao's pride, he did not dare to challenge the Little Monster.
Not only was the Little Monster powerful, but his character was also extremely bad. Not only did he like to kill the innocent, but he also indulged in debauchery. What was worse was that the women he had intercourse with were often tortured to death by him. Over the years, the number of beautiful women who had died in the Little Monster's hands was probably more than a thousand!
More than once, the Little Monster had designs on Bai Yuerong!
He was not lusting after Bai Yuerong's beauty. He was simply interested in her identity as the Saintess.
If it was any other warrior who dared to show any lust for Bai Yuerong, they would die without a burial place. How could the Saintess of the Secret Sect be blasphemed by others?
However, the Little Monster was an exception. He once said publicly that if he had the chance, he would definitely take Bai Yuerong into his harem. Then … Bai Yuerong was furious when she heard those words. However, Bai Yuerong could not do anything to him.
The Nine Cauldrons Martial House was already the number one Martial House in the Holy Kingdoms Continent. It was close to the rank of a seventh rank force. The Little Monster was too important to the Nine Cauldrons Martial House.
However, the Little Monster was important not because of his strength.
He was only at the Late Stage of the False Tribulation Realm. So what if he was powerful? He was no match for a Divine Sea Realm warrior.
The Nine Cauldrons Martial House did not lack Life and Death Realm warriors and Divine Sea Realm warriors. However, they could not do without the Little Monster. This was because the Little Monster was the reason why the Great Yu Kingdom was so prosperous in the past 30 years. The younger generation of warriors grew so quickly.
The Little Monster had a very special talent.
Any warrior who fought with the Little Monster could easily enter a state of epiphany!
For example, if a Divine Sea Realm warrior was stuck in a bottleneck, he could easily break through after fighting with the Little Monster.
The Little Monster's talent was not only effective for False Tribulation Realm warriors, but also for Life and Death Realm warriors and Divine Sea Realm warriors!
However, relatively speaking, the stronger one was, the weaker the effect. When one reached the realm of Battle Emperor Yu the Great, the effect would be negligible.
Regardless, this ability was a fatal attraction to almost all warriors. Even if the Little Monster's character was so bad, the Nine Cauldrons Martial House still treated him like a grandfather!
Many Divine Sea Realm warriors in the four Holy Kingdoms also planned to cultivate the Little Monster. The Little Monster was only at the late stage of the False Tribulation Realm. His talent had little effect on Divine Sea Realm warriors. What if he was cultivated to the Divine Sea Realm?
