Chapter 790
Battle Emperor Yu the Great, Zhou Huang, and the Heavenly Wind Emperor all wanted to improve themselves and reach a higher level of cultivation. Battle Emperor Yu the Great, Zhou Huang, and the Heavenly Wind Emperor all wanted to …
Due to the limited resources in the Lower World, most Divine Sea Realm warriors could not cross that hurdle.
This little monster had such a special talent, so he naturally attracted everyone's attention. He was a national treasure to the Great Yu Divine Kingdom and even the four Divine Kingdoms.
Because of this, the little monster was unscrupulous and no one dared to find trouble with him.
Back in the Great Yu Divine Kingdom, the little monster had taken a fancy to a courtesan and fought with a talented Prince of the Great Yu Divine Kingdom. In a fit of anger, the little monster killed the Prince of the Great Yu Divine Kingdom.
The Prince of the Great Yu Divine Kingdom was even more powerful than Liu Hongwen, second only to the Crown Prince of the Divine Kingdom. Even though the Great Yu Divine Kingdom was furious, he did not pursue the little monster's responsibility.
Bai Yuerong thought for a while and looked at Luo Zheng, who was floating in the air. She bit her lips and replied, "Yes, I want you to fight on my behalf!"
"Sure!" The corner of the little monster's mouth cracked open. Coupled with his strange black eyes, he did not look like a human, but a humanoid monster. He rubbed his hands together, then moved closer to Bai Yuerong and sniffed her. Then, he smiled and said, "I want a reward!"
A bad premonition arose in Bai Yuerong's heart. She was afraid that this little monster was going to spout some nonsense again.
"What reward do you want?" Bai Yuerong asked in a deep voice.
The little monster continued rubbing his hands and sent a message to Bai Yuerong using Zhen Yuan. No one else could hear it.
Bai Yuerong's face flushed red. She scolded angrily, "Do you want to die, little monster?"
In fact, Bai Yuerong knew that he would make such an unbearable request. Fortunately, this little monster knew some taboos and used true essence sound transmission to communicate with her. If others were to hear this, it would have a great impact on the Esoteric Religion.
His request was simple. He wanted her to sleep with him once.
How could Bai Yuerong agree to such a request?
Seeing that Bai Yuerong was really angry, the little monster restrained himself. "Since you don't agree to this request, then forget it. But the next time the Reverse Spirit Channel opens, if there are any good things released from the Upper World, will you let me choose first?"
This was the power of Esoteric Teachings.
This was the reason why the four Divine Kingdoms supported the Esoteric Sect so much that even the King had to be courteous to Bai Yuerong.
Only the Esoteric Sect could establish a connection with the Upper Realm's Human Clan. Every few years, the Upper Realm would use the Reverse Spirit Channel to distribute some treasures from the Upper Realm. However, these things were not distributed for nothing. The Upper Realm's Esoteric Sect still hoped that the Four Great Divine Nations would completely take over the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea and take out the most important things.
Generally speaking, after the heaven-defying passageway opened, the first to choose would naturally be the rulers of the four Divine Kingdoms. However, these items were also distributed by Bai Yuerong.
Although the little monster's request was excessive, Bai Yuerong could still accept it reluctantly. After all, the reverse spirit channel passed down not only treasures, but also many pellets for cultivation. Many mighty warriors of the Spirit Sea Realm were eagerly waiting for the little monster to enter the Spirit Sea Realm.
After a slight hesitation, she bit her lip and nodded. "Sure!"
"Alright, I'll help you kill him later!" After speaking, the little monster didn't look at the surrounding people. It didn't even look at Luo Zheng in the sky. It found an empty space again, turned over, and fell into a deep sleep.
After hearing the little monster's words, Bai Yuerong looked at Luo Zheng in the sky. Her thin lips curled up, revealing a cold smile. "Luo Zheng, oh Luo Zheng, I said I would kill you. Even after three years, you still can't escape."
Luo Zheng stood in the sky. At this moment, no warrior came up to challenge him, so he could clearly see the scene in the square.
This little monster was actually so arrogant. Luo Zheng's expression was calm, but his heart was vigilant.
No one who had cultivated to the False Tribulation Realm was a fool. Most of the arrogant people did it because they had real ability and strength. Looking at Bai Yuerong's cold smile, she seemed to be very confident in this little monster. Naturally, Luo Zheng became vigilant towards this little monster.
After defeating Yun Longtao, it was time for the Star Field Divine Nation and the Sky Wind Divine Nation to send out their warriors.
The Sky Wind Divine Nation didn't send out any warriors from the beginning. As for the Star Field Divine Nation, no one spoke either.
"No one wants to challenge me?" Luo Zheng quietly floated in the air. He was also puzzled. "How will the next battle proceed?"
In the four Divine Kingdoms, there were only a total of twenty to thirty first-tier martial artists.
Yun Longtao was already a peak existence among these twenty to thirty people. Previously, Zhao Tianji, Xiao Daoyuan, Zhan Yun, and the others were all pale. It was clear how powerful Yun Longtao was.
Such a powerful person had been easily defeated by Luo Zheng. Among the many Martial Houses present, no one was willing to face Luo Zheng. The difference in strength was too great.
Of course, this wasn't because no one from the younger generation of the four Divine Nations dared to step forward. Many Divine Nation Princes and Crown Princes were monstrous geniuses among monstrous geniuses. They either inherited some innate bloodline, cultivated some strange cultivation method, or had some precious treasure or secret technique.
However, how could a member of the imperial family casually step forward?
"Who else wants to challenge my Black Iron Divine Nation's Luo Zheng?" At this time, it was Zhou Huang's turn to speak. His face was filled with pride.
At this time, the Sky Wind War Emperor, who had been silent from beginning to end, opened his mouth. "Yun 'er, if you can't bear it, go and exchange some pointers with Luo Tianxing. Don't go too far!"
Following which, a feminine female voice rang out. "Yes, Father!"
Soon, a woman with long hair like a waterfall walked out from behind the Sky Wind War Emperor.
This woman's hair was really long. Half of her long hair hung down to her ankles. Moreover, her hair was braided. Otherwise, it would have dragged on the ground.
"It's the little princess of the Sky Wind Divine Nation! A member of the imperial family actually stepped forward! "
"It's been many years since a member of the imperial family stepped forward to battle …"
"Generally speaking, the imperial family doesn't step forward to battle, right?"
In a situation where no one was willing to step forward, the princess of the Sky Wind Divine Nation actually stepped forward. This was far beyond everyone's expectations.
The Sky Wind Divine Nation was the world of the Huo Family. As for this little princess named Yun 'er, she had a great background!
It was said that Huo Yun 'er was the most talented cultivator in the Sky Wind Divine Nation's imperial family. It was also because of Huo Yun' er that the Crown Prince of the Sky Wind Divine Nation was crippled.
A country of cultivators had always respected strength. Although Huo Yun 'er was a woman, if her strength matured and she reached the Divine Sea Realm in the future, she would be able to take over the Sky Wind War Emperor's position and become an empress.
However, in the history of the Divine Nation Continent, there had never been a woman who had ascended to the throne. Everyone was optimistic about Huo Yun 'er. She had a high chance of breaking the record and becoming the first empress of the Divine Nation Continent.
Huo Yun 'er looked very weak, so weak that she could not even stand up to a gust of wind. Her demeanor was also quite charming. Her figure gently turned and circles of fire lotuses appeared under her feet. She stepped on these fire lotuses and slowly floated over.
When she entered the barrier, she smiled sweetly and a faint flame circulated between her eyebrows. "My name is Huo Yun 'er!"
Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows. Since the princess of the Divine Nation Continent had such etiquette, Luo Zheng naturally would not slight her. "This one is Luo Tianxing."
"Imperial Father said that if the imperial family were to take action, it would be a loss of the imperial family's dignity. However, I think that Imperial Father was able to lead the Divine Nation not because of his dignity or anything else, but because of my Huo family's strength, my Imperial Father's strength … If our strength isn't enough, where would our dignity come from?"
Her voice was soft and gentle. It was like a sweet spring water that made people feel very comfortable.
"This one agrees wholeheartedly," Luo Zheng said with a nod.
All fame was backed by strength. Without this, it would be like duckweed without roots, water without a source. It would be a true false reputation!
"Therefore, I want to exchange pointers with you," Huo Yun 'er said with a gentle smile.
"This one is deeply honored to be able to exchange blows with the princess of the Divine Nation!" Luo Zheng immediately said.

Chapter 791
"Oh? Why is that so? "Luo Zheng's face revealed a strange expression.
"If you defeat me, the little monster will definitely challenge you. He is a madman, a real madman. He will kill you," Huo Yuner said with a face full of worry.
Hearing Huo Yuner's words, Luo Zheng suddenly smiled. Looking at Huo Yuner's expression, it didn't seem like she was faking it. She was really worried about him. She was indeed a kind-hearted little princess, kind-hearted and quite pure.
Only under the protection of the imperial family could she be nurtured to such a level of strength and such a pure martial artist … …
Luo Zheng didn't want to brush off her good intentions. He only said, "Thank you for your reminder, Princess of Scandinavia. I, Luo Tianxing, know what to do!"
Huo Yuner blinked, her pair of beautiful eyes full of charm. She also understood that Luo Zheng probably wouldn't listen to other people's advice. She nodded and then said, "Okay, I'm going to make my move. You be careful!"
Hearing Huo Yuner's words, Luo Zheng was speechless again. She even reminded him to be careful. This little girl was really too well-behaved.
However, although Huo Yuner was modest and well-behaved, when she made her move, Luo Zheng's complexion suddenly changed.
He saw her raise a jade hand, and then he saw that she was wearing a red jade bracelet on her wrist. From this bracelet, a kind of power that made Luo Zheng's heart palpitate was transmitted!
"This, what level of treasure is this?" Luo Zheng opened his eyes wide, staring at the fiery red bracelet.
Probably because she saw Luo Zheng's gaze, she didn't expect that the attack she was preparing to attack would suddenly stop. Then she pretended to be serious and said to Luo Zheng, "This is a jade bracelet. It was given to me by my father."
Hearing Huo Yuner's words, Luo Zheng almost fell down … …
Many martial artists in the square also almost fell to the ground one by one.
Wasn't this Huo Yuner too outrageous? She hadn't even started her attack, but she still stopped to tell him the origin of the bracelet.
"I didn't say anything. This little princess is too cute!"
"Sigh, she's the princess of Scandinavia. You can only think about how cute she is in your heart."
"Humph, don't underestimate me, Huo Yuner. Even if she tells you all of her weaknesses, you are still not her opponent. Although she is innocent, her strength is very formidable!"
Even the Tianfeng War Emperor had an awkward expression on his face.
However, Zhou Huang comforted him, "Old Huo, your daughter is not bad. She's quite cute."
Many of the martial artists in the square were still discussing Huo Yuner's outrageous actions, but Huo Yuner's next words caused them to fall silent.
"I like this bracelet very much. It's a semi-divine tool," Huo Yuner said as she gently stroked the bracelet.
Demigod Artifact …
Even Luo Zheng was shocked when he heard this.
Above Spirit Artifacts were Immortal Artifacts, above Immortal Artifacts were Saint Artifacts, and above Saint Artifacts were Divine Artifacts!
Luo Zheng now had a high-grade sacred tool, which was the long sword in his hand. But what was a divine tool like? He had never seen one before.
Previously, Luo Zheng had guessed that there might be divine tools in the Divine Kingdom Continent.
Now, it seemed that his guess had become reality. Demigod Artifact! Based on this, it was probably an existence that surpassed high-grade sacred tools. It should be infinitely close to becoming a divine tool.
In the square, all the martial artists stared at the bracelet. They knew very well how beneficial a semi-divine tool was to a martial artist!
Most of the martial artists in the Divine Kingdom Continent knew that there were indeed divine tools on this continent. However, divine tools were the foundation of a country. They were not willing to show others and were firmly in the hands of the country's rulers.
Under normal circumstances, they would not have the chance to see them.
Now, they were fortunate enough to see a semi-divine tool. It could be considered an eye-opener.
"Luo Zheng, be careful of the bracelet in this woman's hand." At this time, a voice transmitted from Luo Zheng's body. It was Xun.
Since entering the Divine Kingdom, Luo Zheng had concealed his strength and did not let Xun out for a breath of fresh air. At this time, Xun noticed the bracelet and could not help but warn him.
"En, I understand." Even without Xun's warning, Luo Zheng would have raised his vigilance.
"Okay, this time I'm really going to make a move!" After Huo Yuner finished speaking, she once again waved her wrist that was as white as a lotus root. Under this gentle swaying, the bracelet on her wrist also swayed.
"Buzz buzz buzz …"
Under this swaying, little red flames immediately covered her hand. It was as if her entire arm was condensed from flames.
"The Law of Fire, at least the third level …" Luo Zheng's eyes flashed.
Then, she took another gentle step and her figure suddenly disappeared. The distance between her and Luo Zheng was shortened by several feet.
"The Law of Space? No, this is the Law of Time! This Huo Yuner cultivates two Laws! Moreover, her attainments in the Law of Time are very high, far surpassing that woman called Yue Ying! "
The way Huo Yuner moved was somewhat similar to Luo Zheng's spatial travel, but that was only a visual effect.
When Huo Yuner closed the distance between them, she froze time at a point. Under this kind of movement, Luo Zheng did not have time to react because time was under her control.
Luo Zheng was very clear that he was in some trouble. A Semi-Divine tool, two Laws …
This woman's strength was not as cute as she looked on the surface. In fact, she was terrifying to the point of making people use their Laws. At the very least, Yun Longtao, who was said to be invincible in the False Tribulation Realm, was definitely not Huo Yuner's opponent.
The moment he came to this conclusion, Luo Zheng chose to retreat.
The Law of Time was even more terrifying than the Law of Space!
Luo Zheng used the Law of Space to travel through the void and appear in front of his opponent in an instant. However, he was still able to give his opponent time to react. It was just that it was very difficult to dodge.
However, the Law of Time was different. She directly compressed the power of time. Within a certain range, time was completely controlled by Huo Yuner. Luo Zheng was unable to predict her movements …
How could he fight this?
This was the terrifying part of the Law of Time. The only way to break it was to also comprehend the Law of Time.
Almost in the blink of an eye, Luo Zheng came to this conclusion.
In fact, Luo Zheng's conclusion was very correct.
Martial artists in the Upper Realm who cultivated the Law of Time were everywhere. Only the Law of Time could resist the Law of Time. Of course, the higher-level Law of Space also had a chance to resist the Law of Time. After these Law Forces were cultivated to a high level, they would undergo earth-shaking changes. There were also ways to restrain each other.
However, right now, Luo Zheng was unable to restrain himself.
The next moment, Huo Yuner's figure silently appeared beside Luo Zheng. Her jade-like hand, which was stained with raging flames, was already slapping towards Luo Zheng's head.
Because of the Law of Time, Luo Zheng had no time to react at all. However, at this moment, Huo Yuner did something that made people speechless. Her hand paused halfway and then said, "It will hurt a little to knock you out, but you won't be seriously injured!"
As soon as she said this, how could Luo Zheng not have time to react? He took a step forward, shuttled through space, and instantly pulled away from Huo Yuner. However, he did not relax at all. His face was full of seriousness.
Just now, he could safely avoid it, but Huo Yuner controlled the Law of Time. How could he avoid it?
"He ran away?" Seeing Luo Zheng disappear in front of her in an instant, Huo Yuner's face showed disappointment. She was thinking that she had kindly warned Luo Zheng, but he actually took advantage of her warning to escape. He was a little cunning …
Huo Yuner did not know that it was not others who were cunning, but herself who was too outrageous.
However, Huo Yuner did not care at all. She, who controlled the Law of Time, could easily seize this kind of opportunity. Luo Zheng had no power to fight back in front of her.
Immediately, she changed the flow of time again. At this moment, within a radius of hundreds of feet around her, time began to become sticky and slow. Luo Zheng also seemed to be performing slow motion, moving little by little.
Therefore, she gently floated over, and the flaming palm once again slapped towards Luo Zheng.
"I won't remind you this time," Huo Yuner thought at this moment.

Chapter 792
As the princess of a Divine Kingdom, Huo Yuner rarely had the chance to display her strength.
The Royal Families of the four Divine Kingdoms all had their own secret cultivation grounds, which were built using the power of the Upper Realm.
Since the beginning of the Upper Realm, Huo Yuner had almost never fought with others in public. Everyone's estimation of Huo Yuner's strength was only based on her status in the Heavenly Wind Divine Kingdom.
Therefore, no one was clear about Huo Yuner's main cultivation method or the Law she comprehended.
Everyone knew that she was strong, but they didn't expect her to be so strong that Luo Zheng couldn't even fight back in front of her!
Although she only used seventy percent of her strength in this slap, the bracelet on her wrist shook slightly. As it began to vibrate violently, an extremely huge force erupted.
"Poof!"
Everyone heard the sound of a watermelon being smashed.
Then, Luo Zheng's figure suddenly fell down and crashed into the barrier below.
It was fortunate that the enchantment was set up by a Divine Sea Realm warrior. Under the impact, the enchantment began to twist, deform, and sink deeply. However, as the eyes of the Divine Sea Realm warrior who set up the enchantment lit up, the enchantment recovered in an instant!
If it were any other warrior, it would be difficult for them to stand up after being hit by such a light blow.
However, Luo Zheng had an Immortal Tool Body. As soon as the tremendous force poured into Luo Zheng's body, it immediately turned into Primordial Astral Winds and began to permeate Luo Zheng's body.
However, ever since he was promoted to an Immortal Tool Body, Luo Zheng's body hadn't had the chance to be refined. In a battle of this level, the strength of an Immortal Tool Body was somewhat insufficient. Therefore, even though Luo Zheng's body was constantly transformed, only 70% of the force was converted. Thirty percent of the force still caused damage to Luo Zheng's body.
Luo Zheng could bear the remaining thirty percent of the force, which only caused him some minor injuries. With the current strength of his body, he could recover in a few breaths of time.
Huo Yuner's white-blue dress fluttered in the wind. She looked down at Luo Zheng with a worried expression on her face. She was worried in her heart. Although she had held back just now, she was still worried. Was that slap still a bit too heavy?
However, in the blink of an eye, her worries were dispelled, and a relieved smile even appeared on her face. That was because she saw Luo Zheng jump up from the barrier, and it seemed that his body was fine.
"This little girl is as kind as a saint." Luo Zheng sighed in his heart.
"Hmm? Have you taken a fancy to her? "Xun seized the opportunity to ask.
"Hehe." Luo Zheng didn't say anything. He immediately flew up and looked at Huo Yuner.
"Done!" Huo Yuner looked at Luo Zheng and suddenly said.
Luo Zheng was stunned. "What's done?"
This time, it was Huo Yuner who revealed a strange expression. She said foolishly, "Didn't we already have a winner?"
As soon as she said this, everyone revealed a strange expression. Then, the warriors of the Star Field Divine Nation, Great Yu Divine Nation, and Black Iron Divine Nation all laughed. Only the warriors of the Tianfeng Divine Nation didn't laugh. After all, she was the princess of their Divine Nation and the goddess in their hearts.
As for the Divine Sea Realm warriors, they also smiled. This child of the Huo Family was really …
The Tianfeng War Emperor revealed a helpless and bitter smile. He could only blame himself for this. Ever since he discovered Huo Yuner's talent, he had spoiled her too much. He had never let her go out alone to gain experience.
Of course, not going out alone didn't mean that Huo Yuner didn't work hard. In fact, Huo Yuner had spent all her energy on cultivation and almost abandoned all other activities. Therefore, her personality was still stuck in the state of a seven or eight-year-old girl. She was too kind and naive, and at the same time, she had terrifying strength and talent.
"We still haven't had a winner," Luo Zheng replied seriously.
Huo Yuner's expression was strange. After thinking for a while, she replied, "But, but you clearly lost just now …"
Luo Zheng smiled. Suddenly, a trace of slyness appeared on his face. He replied in the same tone, "But, but I haven't admitted defeat …"
Huo Yuner blinked her eyes and suddenly understood. "Oh! In other words, I have to make you admit defeat! I understand now! "
She just didn't know much about the world, but she wasn't stupid. In her opinion, Luo Zheng did lose just now. This was just a competition. They didn't have to fight to the death. Now that she understood, she only had one thought in her mind, and that was to make Luo Zheng admit defeat.
Therefore, after she finished speaking, her blue and white dress fluttered. She leaned forward slightly and floated towards Luo Zheng like a fairy flying in the sky.
At the same time, the bracelet in her hand began to tremble, and the Law of Time spread out from her wrist.
The bracelet in her hand was not an offensive treasure. Instead, it increased her strength and True Essence to a considerable extent. The moment the Law of Time spread out, Luo Zheng reflexively prepared to retreat.
Unfortunately, the Law of Time spread out too fast. What's more, the space inside the boundary was not large. There wasn't much space for Luo Zheng to escape.
During the time when he was shrouded by the Law of Time, Luo Zheng's figure suddenly became extremely slow. Although he had connected to a space node not far away through the Law of Space, before he could teleport, he was stuck by the Law of Time.
Oh … no …
Under the effect of the Law of Time, even the thoughts in Luo Zheng's mind became incomparably slow.
He saw Huo Yuner's speed suddenly magnified countless times, rapidly approaching him. However, after being shrouded by the Law of Time, Luo Zheng really didn't have much choice.
Huo Yuner approached Luo Zheng again. Her beautiful eyebrows were slightly knitted. In order to make her opponent admit defeat, she had to beat Luo Zheng into submission. This wasn't something she liked.
However, Huo Yuner also understood that she was representing the Tianfeng Divine Country. Moreover, she belonged to the imperial family of the Tianfeng Divine Country. She didn't want to lose this fight. Without a better way, she could only increase her strength again.
Ninety percent of her strength, no more …
Thinking this in her heart, she immediately patted Luo Zheng's forehead with her lily-white hand.
This pat looked incomparably gentle, as if she was gently stroking Luo Zheng's head. However, no cultivator would underestimate her strength. Fortunately, Luo Zheng's body was as tyrannical as a monster. If it were any other cultivator, their heads and necks would have been broken.
Even if Luo Zheng had an Immortal Tool Body, under this pat, he felt dizzy and saw stars.
"Pu!"
Luo Zheng's body once again heavily smashed into the barrier below.
Under Huo Yuner's Law of Time, Luo Zheng's falling speed was extremely slow. Of course, this was just the visual effect of the onlookers. After Huo Yuner removed the Law of Time, Luo Zheng's speed increased dramatically, and he heavily smashed into the barrier below.
"Do you admit defeat or not?" Huo Yuner asked with a frown.
"No!" Luo Zheng wiped a trace of blood from the corner of his mouth. "No!"
"Then let's do it again!"
As soon as her voice fell, Huo Yuner instantly appeared in front of Luo Zheng. Along with the vibration of the bracelet on her white wrist, her lily-white hand lightly brushed, and Luo Zheng's body crashed into the barrier above.
This time, Huo Yuner even used ten percent of her strength!
"Do you admit defeat?" Huo Yuner asked again. Her face revealed an extremely unbearable expression. It seemed that treating Luo Zheng like this made her extremely uncomfortable.
"Pu!" This time, Luo Zheng crashed to the left. This time, a trace of blood splashed out from the corner of Luo Zheng's mouth.
"Do you admit defeat?" Huo Yuner asked again.
"No." Luo Zheng looked at Huo Yuner indifferently and responded.
Now it was Huo Yuner's turn to be anxious. She never thought that there would be such a guy in this world. He was obviously not her opponent, but he still insisted on holding on. After thinking for a while, she suddenly opened her mouth and said, "I beg you, just admit defeat!"
Huo Yuner didn't want to kill Luo Zheng, but this guy just didn't admit defeat. For other warriors, this was a simple matter. At worst, they could just kill the opponent. In any case, it was you who didn't admit defeat no matter what.
For Huo Yuner, this was an extremely difficult problem.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 793
The Tianfeng War Emperor, who had been watching the battle closely, suddenly let out a long sigh.
"This time, it can be considered as a form of training for Yun 'er," he thought to himself.
Huo Yuner didn't have any experience in training, but that didn't mean she didn't have any battle experience. Ever since Huo Yuner stepped into the Xiantian realm, the Tianfeng War Emperor had arranged for one opponent after another to challenge her.
When Huo Yuner was able to defeat an opponent of the same level, the War Emperor would arrange for an opponent stronger than her. When Huo Yuner was at the third stage of the Xiantian realm, she could already defeat a seventh stage Xiantian realm warrior.
When Huo Yuner was at the tenth stage of the Xiantian realm, she could already defeat a seventh stage Divine Reflection realm warrior.
After that, Huo Yuner could basically defeat an opponent who was one stage higher than her.
Now, Huo Yuner had entered the late stage of the False Tribulation realm, and her opponents were at the second or even third stage of the Life and Death realm.
However, sparring of that level was limited. Huo Yuner was the princess of the Divine Nation. Even if the Tianfeng War Emperor ordered those warriors to go all out, who would dare to hurt her?
After all, this kind of experience was not a life and death experience. Facing Luo Tianxing now could be considered as a big test for Huo Yuner. He hoped that his precious daughter could make the right choice and not embarrass him too much.
Hearing Huo Yuner beg him to admit defeat, Luo Zheng didn't know whether to laugh or cry. However, after laughing, he still replied, "Even if you beg me, I won't admit defeat!"
This time, Huo Yuner was truly at her wit's end. She looked left and right, trying to find some help. However, when her gaze landed on the Tianfeng War Emperor, he closed his eyes. As a father, he didn't want to give his daughter any hints at this moment.
Of course, even if the Tianfeng War Emperor didn't give any hints, the other warriors would come up with suggestions.
When the warriors of the Tianfeng Divine Nation saw the expression on the princess's face, they immediately began to shout.
"Princess, just kill him. This brat doesn't know what's good for him. Princess, you showed mercy, but he still wants to torture you!"
Kill him? Huo Yuner bit her lips and quickly gave up on this idea. Ever since she could remember, she hadn't even killed an ant.
After becoming a Xiantian lifeform, she was able to sense all things in the world. When she walked on the grass, she would maintain a light and graceful posture, for fear of damaging the grass. If there were ants, locusts, and other creatures on the road, she would also carefully avoid them!
Do you expect Huo Yun 'er to kill people? Even if he killed her, it would be difficult for her to do so.
In the end, her mental strength was still too weak. In her anxiety, her eyes turned red, and crystal clear teardrops swayed in her eyes.
Seeing this scene, the hearts of many Deity geniuses in the Heavenly Wind Divine Nation broke. These Deity geniuses were all admirers of Huo Yuner, so how could they tolerate her being made to cry? For a moment, many Divine Grade geniuses hated Luo Zheng.
"Luo Zheng, you are no match for the princess of Tianfeng Divine Nation. Don't act like a scoundrel and obediently admit defeat!"
"As a man, you actually dare to act shamelessly. This is simply too shameful!"
"Hurry up and admit defeat. If you don't, I will definitely kill you!"
For a moment, the warriors of Tianfeng Divine Nation began to clamor.
At this moment, the little monster that had been sleeping all this time suddenly turned over and roared at the Deity geniuses of Tianfeng Divine Nation, "What are you making a ruckus for! You're disturbing my sleep! That … Tianfeng Divine Nation's Huo What? Hot beauty, if you don't want to kill him, then just knock him out. What a woman, you're still so conflicted over such a trivial matter! "
The little monster's roar had already incurred the wrath of the crowd. Although the little monster was powerful, he couldn't fight with so many Deity geniuses of Tianfeng Divine Nation. Therefore, these Deity geniuses couldn't help but mock the little monster.
However, the second half of the little monster's words immediately reminded these Deity geniuses of something.
"That's right, I didn't think of that just now. Princess, just knock Luo Tianxing out!"
"Knock him out and we'll win!"
Hearing this suggestion, Huo Yuner's eyes suddenly lit up. She might not be able to kill someone, but knocking someone out was still something she could barely do.
Therefore, she smiled faintly at Luo Zheng, "Since you won't admit defeat no matter what, then I'm sorry, but I'm going to knock you out!"
Hearing Huo Yuner's sweet and charming voice drag out a long 'oh', the minds of many Divine level geniuses were immediately shaken. A woman with such an appearance and such a voice possessed a fatal attraction to any martial artist of any age.
This time, Huo Yuner gently floated and used the Law of Time again, slowing down the flow of time.
Luo Zheng still looked like he couldn't resist at all. He stood there in a daze, watching as Huo Yuner approached him at an undetectable speed.
Thinking of knocking Luo Zheng out, Huo Yuner circled behind Luo Zheng. Her jade-like hand turned into a knife and chopped towards the back of Luo Zheng's head.
However, the moment Huo Yuner got close to Luo Zheng, a sudden change occurred!
Luo Zheng's movements, which were originally as slow as a snail, suddenly sped up. Although his speed couldn't catch up with Huo Yuner's, it was enough for Luo Zheng to protect the back of his head.
Therefore, Huo Yuner's knife hand landed on Luo Zheng's arm.
"Puff!"
Luo Zheng flew forward like a rubber ball. This time, because of the protection of Luo Zheng's arm, he wasn't injured. With a smile on his face, he shook his aching arm and returned to normal.
"Huh?"
Huo Yuner's face revealed a strange expression, "You, your movements just now, how could you be so fast …"
She had completely integrated the first layer of the Law of Time and could adjust the flow of time within a hundred feet to a ratio of 100: 1.
In other words, for every breath that passed outside, one hundredth of a breath would pass under her Law of Time.
This was also the reason why Luo Zheng's speed was as slow as a snail's.
After being slowed down by 100 times, no matter how fast Luo Zheng was, how fast could he be? How could he block Huo Yuner's attack?
However, at that moment, Luo Zheng protected the back of his head with his hand, and his speed suddenly increased by 10 times!
This was equivalent to Luo Zheng forcibly increasing his speed by 10 times …
This was simply impossible!
More importantly, she felt that there were still some remnants of the Law of Time around her in the air, and these weren't released by her!
"You comprehended the Law of Time? Just now? "Huo Yuner's face was also full of astonishment.
The Battle Emperors, Battle Emperors, and many Divine Sea Realm experts were also shocked. With their eyes, how could they not see the unforeseen event just now?
Before this, Luo Zheng had been taking a beating without any strength to fight back. This didn't mean that Luo Zheng wasn't strong enough, but that the Law of Time was too strong!
After comprehending the Law of Time, the advantage of fighting with warriors of the same level was unimaginable. Luo Zheng's loss to Huo Yuner was not unjustified.
Therefore, Zhou Huang thought that even if Luo Zheng was really defeated by Huo Yuner, he would still protect Luo Zheng.
At the same time, Zhou Huang was also wondering why Luo Zheng was so stubborn. Perhaps he was afraid that if he lost, the Black Iron Divine Kingdom would abandon him and eventually fall into the hands of Battle Emperor Yu the Great. He even wanted to send a message to Luo Zheng to dispel this doubt. It was fine if he couldn't defeat Huo Yuner. After all, the Law of Time was almost invincible. It was meaningless to drag on like this.
It was fun to beat people up, but it was not fun to be beaten up. Moreover, Huo Yuner was also being tortured by Luo Zheng …
But now, Zhou Huang understood what Luo Zheng was doing. This boy had actually comprehended Huo Yuner's Law of Time during the battle!
What was even more frightening was that he seemed to have comprehended a great deal!
The Heavenly Wind Battle Emperor took a deep breath and said, "I'm sure many of you are well aware of how difficult it is to comprehend the Law of Time. I want to clarify something. Did this boy really comprehend the Law of Time during the battle?"
The Sky Fortune Kingdom King, Battle Emperor Yu the Great, Zhou Huang, as well as many Palace Masters and other Divine Sea Realm Supreme Elders, all of them fell into deep contemplation.

Chapter 794
In fact, it wasn't that these Divine Sea warriors didn't believe it, they just found it hard to accept.
The law of time in the four Divine Kingdoms was recorded on a totem. Of course, it wasn't something from the Lower Realm, it was passed down from the Upper Realm through the reverse spirit channel of the secret sect.
However, totems that recorded the Laws of Time weren't in high demand. In comparison, third and fourth level fire attribute totems, ice attribute, metal attribute, and thunder attribute totems were much more in demand!
The reason was simple, the law of time was too difficult to comprehend.
They didn't know about the situation in the Upper Realm, but there were less than a hundred warriors in the four Divine Kingdoms who could comprehend the law of time!
Just like the female warrior who followed King Yan, Yue Ying, she didn't have any great talents, but because she had comprehended 10% of the first level of the law of time, it was enough for her to become one of the top warriors in the second tier.
Huo Yuner's talent was great, but it still took her seven years to fully comprehend the first level of the law of time!
It only took her 19 years to reach the Late Void Tribulation realm, but it took her seven years just to comprehend the law of time …
Because it was hard to comprehend, and it took a long time, many warriors gave up on comprehending the law of time totems. Many warriors tried their luck in comprehending the law of time totems, but when they realized they didn't have the talent, they gave up.
The law of time was indeed powerful, but it took too much time to comprehend it. For most warriors, it was useless.
Of course, there were also those with great perseverance in the Divine Kingdom continent. There was once a Divine Reflection Realm martial artist who was a prince of the Star Field Divine Kingdom. This prince possessed god-level talent and was obsessed with comprehending the Laws of Time. After spending a total of 563 years, he finally comprehended 60% of the first level of the Time Totem!
Because he had spent too much time on the law of time, he was only an Early Divine Core warrior when he was 500 years old. In the end, he passed away because his lifespan was exhausted.
Therefore, there were only two types of people who cultivated the law of time in the Divine Kingdom. The first type was the warriors who had a strong affinity with the law of time. Even in the Divine Kingdom, where geniuses were everywhere, there were only a few of them. Among the younger generation, Huo Yuner was the only one who had such talent!
The other type was the Divine Sea warriors.
After cultivating to their level, there was nothing else they could do. They couldn't ascend to the Upper Realm, so they could only cultivate all kinds of laws, or explore the ocean …
With the long lifespan of a Divine Sea warrior, even if they didn't have a strong affinity with the law of time, they could still comprehend it bit by bit.
This was the reason why those Divine Sea warriors couldn't accept it.
Luo Zheng and Huo Yuner's battle, but in such a short period of time, Luo Zheng's speed, he could change the time flow ten times faster?
Could't he be a monster?
The Divine's's Void Tribulation warriors's a Void Tribulation realm warrior ''s was to him. He didn't even care to the's Tribulation warriors to the Void to the Void Tribulation warriors' '' ''t's a's''t't care.

"Tsk tsk, this talent is quite extraordinary. If I devour you and take your talent, I'll …" Thinking of this, the Divine's Void Tribulation suddenly stuck out his tongue and licked the tip of his nose.
Besides his eyes, the Divine's Void Tribulation also had a strange tongue. It was like a snake's tongue, forked in the middle and extremely long. The tip of his tongue could lick his nose.
In fact, the Divine's Void Tribulation had a big secret about the Divine's Void Tribulation. This secret had always been a top secret among the upper echelons of the four Divine Kingdoms!
The Divine's Void Tribulation loved to devour human brains, and after devouring them, he could take the opponent's talent as his own!
Many Divine Sea warriors knew this secret about the Divine's Void Tribulation. The stronger the Divine's Void Tribulation talent was, the more it would leak out, benefiting the warrior who sparred with it. Therefore, warriors who sparred with the Divine's Void Tribulation could easily enter a state of epiphany and make a breakthrough.
Over the years, the four Divine Kingdoms had secretly 'sacrificed' many second-tier geniuses to the Divine's Void Tribulation for the Divine's Void Tribulation to devour.
Therefore, in the Martial Houses of the four Divine Kingdoms, there would occasionally be one or two Divine rank geniuses that would 'disappear'. It was just that the Divine Kingdoms were so vast that it was hard for anyone to notice the disappearance of a Divine rank genius.
Luo Zheng had a faint smile on his face as he looked at Huo Yuner not too far away. Then, he took out his spatial ring and gently tossed it into the air. The ring flew three feet above Luo Zheng's head. Luo Zheng's fingers stretched out as the power of the Laws of Time gushed out.
Then, a strange scene appeared.
Bit by bit, the ring landed on his hand at an extremely slow speed.
Seeing this, Huo Yuner exclaimed, "Your talent is amazing! In such a short time, you can compress time by 10 times! In the past, it took me three years to comprehend the Laws of Time to your level! "
Luo Zheng had experienced the baptism of the Laws, so his compatibility with the Laws was the highest. Naturally, his comprehension speed was fast. Several months ago, Luo Zheng had fought with Yue Ying because he wanted to comprehend the power of the Laws of Time through her. Unfortunately, Yue Ying had comprehended too little of the Laws of Time, so Luo Zheng hadn't been able to do so.
Some time ago, Luo Zheng had inquired about where Yue Ying had cultivated the Laws of Time. Later, he learned that the Laws of Time totem was placed in the Heavenly Wind Divine Kingdom. Moreover, it had been kept in the Imperial Family. Luo Zheng hadn't had the chance to comprehend it.
This time, Luo Zheng saw that Huo Yuner had the complete power of the Laws of Time. How could he give up now?
Luo Zheng faintly smiled and asked, "Shall we continue?"
Huo Yuner's delicate eyebrows rose slightly. Then, she nodded and said, "But don't be so smug! You've only compressed time by ten times. I can already compress time by a hundred times. So, in terms of absolute speed, I'm still ten times faster than you! "
After she finished speaking, she gently shook the jade bracelet and the power of the Laws of Time spread out once more.
Luo Zheng naturally knew the gap between him and Huo Yuner. However, Luo Zheng didn't agree with the ten times difference in speed that she spoke of.
If the two of them didn't use the power of the Laws of Time, Luo Zheng would use his full strength. Under the eruption of Vital Essence and True Essence, using the innate talent of the Cloud Chasing Lion, Luo Zheng's speed would be two times faster than Huo Yuner's!
After offsetting the difference in the Laws of Time, Huo Yuner's absolute speed was estimated to be only five times faster than Luo Zheng's.
A five times difference should be acceptable …
"Hu hu hu …"
Huo Yuner's expression became more and more serious. Although her temperament was naive, when it came to cultivating martial arts, she had an incomparable seriousness that ordinary people couldn't compare with. After all, in the martial arts world, it was impossible to rely on talent alone. Without perseverance, persistence, enthusiasm, and seriousness, it was difficult to go far on this road no matter how talented one was.
Huo Yuner's figure floated and circled behind Luo Zheng again. Luo Zheng's speed was still very slow, but it was still much faster than before.
"Pu!"
This time, Huo Yuner slapped Luo Zheng's back heavily. Luo Zheng twisted his body and blocked Huo Yuner's blow with his elbow.
"Bang!"
With a muffled sound, Luo Zheng was sent flying again. At that time, Luo Zheng was in a defensive posture and wasn't injured.
As a result, the battle inside the barrier became extremely anxious.
Huo Yuner sent out palm after palm. Although she had a big advantage in terms of speed, Luo Zheng had combat experience that Huo Yuner couldn't compare with. He could always calculate in advance and take the initiative to defend against Huo Yuner's attacks!
What was worse was that not only could Luo Zheng resist Huo Yuner's attacks, but his comprehension of the Laws of Time was also increasing rapidly!
When Luo Zheng resisted Huo Yuner's thirteen palms, he could compress the time to about eleven times. When Huo Yuner sent out twenty palms towards Luo Zheng, Luo Zheng could compress the time to about twelve times.
Everything was difficult at the beginning. As Luo Zheng's comprehension of the first layer of the Laws of Time increased, his speed of comprehension also became faster and faster.

Chapter 795
The Divine Sea Realm expert wasn't the only one who had noticed this. The Tianfeng War Emperor, Zhou Huang, and the other monarchs had also noticed this.
Without using the Law of Time, there was a huge gap between Huo Yuner and Luo Zheng's speed.
The key was that Huo Yuner had the advantage in speed, but she couldn't do anything to Luo Zheng. Luo Zheng's body was too strong. It was simply tyrannical.
As long as he continued to resist, his understanding of the Law of Time became deeper and deeper. In the end, Huo Yuner would be the one to lose.
"Well, this is bad." Huo Yuner blinked her eyes. She also felt the change in Luo Zheng. His speed … was getting faster and faster.
Overall, there was still a four-fold difference in speed between them. However, this gap was shrinking rapidly …
Huo Yuner didn't want to lose to Luo Zheng. She really didn't want to. Therefore, after this palm strike, Huo Yuner didn't choose to attack again. Her slim and graceful figure floated in a corner of the boundary. After a while, she said to Luo Zheng, "Luo Tianxing, you should admit defeat!"
"Why?" Luo Zheng didn't know what this little girl was thinking. However, he was probably used to Huo Yuner's way of thinking.
There were all kinds of people and all kinds of personalities. Back then, Xi Youqin's personality had made Luo Zheng understand this principle.
"I'm going to use fire. I'm afraid you won't be able to resist this move. I'm afraid you'll die!" Huo Yuner said seriously.
"Fire?" Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows slightly.
Since the last time he refined his body, Luo Zheng's Primordial Chaos Dipper had been accumulating in his body. It was time for Luo Zheng to refine his body again and upgrade it to the next level.
However, all this time, Luo Zheng hadn't been able to find a suitable fire source.
What kind of fire source could upgrade Luo Zheng's body to the next level? Luo Zheng himself didn't know. However, in general, the stronger his body was, the more powerful the fire source he needed.
Luo Zheng also had a powerful fire source, which was the black flame in the mysterious furnace. However, the black flame was too overbearing, so Luo Zheng didn't dare to try it.
Huo Yuner was most likely not only proficient in the law of time, but also in the law of fire. If Luo Zheng could borrow her flames to promote his body to a sacred artefact, it would be a good thing for him.
"That's right." Huo Yuner nodded. "So you should give up. Don't force me to use that move. I can't knock you out right now, and your comprehension of the Law of Time is so fast. I'm afraid it won't be long before you completely comprehend the Law of Time. At that time, I won't be your opponent …"
She actually told Luo Zheng what she had analyzed in her heart bit by bit …
When Huo Yuner explained it like this, the False Tribulation Realm geniuses below all sighed.
These god-level geniuses were known as monstrous existences in their own territories. They were also the absolute pride of their own families!
However, compared with Luo Zheng, what was there to be proud of? This was too much of a blow to their pride. This guy comprehended the Law of Time just like that. He could no longer be described as a monstrous genius or a monster.
"I can only say that this guy is not human!" someone said angrily.
"If he's not human, then what is he?" someone asked.
"He's an animal, a beast!" It seemed that only calling Luo Zheng this way could make them feel better.
Seeing that Luo Zheng didn't answer, Huo Yuner clenched her teeth slightly and said, "Give up, I beg you! I don't want to use that move. "
This was the second time Huo Yuner had begged Luo Zheng …
Luo Zheng shook his head and said with an indifferent smile, "I'm sorry, but it's really difficult for me to beg you like this. If it were anything else, I'm afraid I would agree, but if you want me to give up, forgive me for not being able to comply!"
Huo Yuner puffed up her cheeks. Her cute little action caused many of the martial artists below to look at her with pity and anger. The one they loved was naturally Huo Yuner, but the one they were angry about was Luo Zheng treating the goddess in their hearts.
Wasn't it just admitting defeat? What was the big deal?
"There's no other way," Huo Yuner sniffed and raised her right hand high.
Her arm was slender, so the fiery red jade bracelet was very flexible on her wrist. When she raised her hand, the jade bracelet slid down her wrist, all the way to her elbow, and only after it reached the upper part of her arm did it settle down.
Then, Huo Yuner's hands suddenly clasped together.
Originally, every time Huo Yuner circulated her True Essence, the jade bracelet would vibrate rapidly. However, after her arm fixed the jade bracelet, it was not the jade bracelet that was vibrating, but her body!
Huo Yuner's figure was trembling slightly. Because the speed was too fast, she gave off a blurry feeling at first glance!
As she was shaking, the jade bracelet emitted a gorgeous fiery red color!
When the Divine Sea Realm cultivators of the Tianfeng Divine Nation saw this scene, they all looked at their Monarch, the Tianfeng War Emperor.
Seeing the Tianfeng War Emperor's indifferent expression, the Divine Sea Realm cultivators were filled with worry. One of them couldn't help but ask, "Monarch, aren't we going to stop her?"
The Tianfeng War Emperor's face flickered with a complicated expression. To be honest, he didn't want Huo Yuner to use that move. However, this time, the War Emperor had made up his mind. He had spoiled Huo Yuner too much in the past, so she had developed such an immature personality.
Even a lion knew how to push a cub down a cliff to train its offspring. How could the Tianfeng War Emperor not do it?
This time, he would let Huo Yuner decide how to face Luo Tianxing. That was what he thought. As long as Huo Yuner's life wasn't in danger, he wouldn't stop her.
"Forget it. It's all up to her," the Tianfeng War Emperor replied.
Since the Monarch had said so, the other Divine Sea Realm cultivators of the Tianfeng Divine Nation had nothing else to say.
As the flame on the jade bracelet beside Huo Yuner's arm became more and more gorgeous, a deep mark appeared between her eyebrows. Then, a surge of energy escaped from the mark!
"True Phoenix Fire, come out …"
An extremely condensed phoenix phantom suddenly appeared behind Huo Yuner!
Immediately, everyone in the square felt the intense heat wave. Although the heat wave was weakened by the barrier set up by the Divine Sea Realm cultivators, it was still hard to resist. Many False Tribulation Realm cultivators had to condense their True Essence to protect themselves from the heat wave.
"True Phoenix Bloodline, Huo Yuner has the True Phoenix Bloodline? She's a descendant of the Phoenix Clan? "someone shouted.
"The density of her True Phoenix Bloodline is ridiculously high. It's very likely that she's a descendant of the Phoenix Clan!"
Luo Zheng's eyebrows twitched when he saw the flames that were slowly condensing.
Luo Zheng had absorbed the Blood of a True Dragon, and even the Blood Essence of a True Dragon. After absorbing the Blood Essence of a True Dragon, Luo Zheng's body was greatly improved.
However, the Blood Essence and Blood Essence of a True Dragon that Luo Zheng absorbed were counted in drops. Overall, the Blood Essence of a True Dragon didn't make up much of a difference in Luo Zheng's body. More importantly, he absorbed the Blood Essence of a True Dragon after he was born, so he hadn't mastered the Divine Powers of a True Dragon.
As for Huo Yuner, she seemed to be a descendant of a Super Divine Beast. The density of her True Phoenix Bloodline was ridiculously high, and the True Phoenix Phantom was condensed to such an extent!
When Luo Zheng was in the Blue Cloud Sect, he had seen Mo Yuxin with the Blue Luan Bloodline. However, the Blue Luan itself couldn't compare to a Super Divine Beast, and it was far inferior to Super Divine Beasts like the True Dragon and True Phoenix. Furthermore, Mo Yuxin's True Phoenix Bloodline wasn't very rich, and it could even be said to be a little heterogeneous.
Huo Yuner was different. She seemed to have a complete True Phoenix Bloodline …
"Huff huff huff huff …"
Flames emerged from between her eyebrows. As the flames continued to condense, they slowly fused into a Phoenix!
The Phoenix that Huo Yuner condensed was different from Ning Yudie's. Ning Yudie's Ice Phoenix was a visualization object, but this Phoenix seemed to be a real Phoenix!
