Chapter 796
Super Divine Beasts were the same as Ferocious Beasts.
Level 9 Ferocious Beasts were equivalent to humans in the False Tribulation Realm. Level 10 Ferocious Beasts were equivalent to humans in the Life and Death Realm.
Level 11 Ferocious Beasts were equivalent to Divine Sea Realm Cultivators. Regardless of whether they were Super Divine Beasts or Ferocious Beasts, once they reached level 11, their Beast Cores would liquefy and they would be able to create their own world.
However, Ferocious Beasts, Divine Beasts, and Super Divine Beasts were also quite different.
Super Divine Beasts like the Real Dragon and Real Phoenix were far superior to ordinary Divine Beasts and Ferocious Beasts in terms of growth speed and special abilities. They were equivalent to some super geniuses in the human race. It was easy for them to challenge someone who was one level higher than them.
"With spirit as the contract and soul as the contract, Real Phoenix 'Huo Yao', listen to my command and travel with me!" At this moment, Huo Yuner's voice became very serious.
This Real Phoenix named Huo Yao was Huo Yuner's contract beast. The more powerful one was, the more powerful the contract beast would be. Huo Yuner was only in the late stage of the False Tribulation Realm. Even in the Upper World, it was rare to have a Real Phoenix as a contract beast!
Just like the Real Dragon Race, the Real Phoenix Race was also very rare. For example, there were 49 Real Dragons in the Real Dragon Realm. Including the Real Dragons that were not fully grown, there were only a few thousand of them. The Real Phoenix Race was slightly more, but it was still difficult to exceed 10,000!
There were more than a trillion warriors in the Upper World. Taking a Real Phoenix as a contract beast! It was easier said than done.
Only the descendants of Heavenly Lords could receive such treatment!
The strength that Huo Yuner displayed was indeed astonishing to many Divine Sea Realm Cultivators. This little girl from the Huo Family had silently grown to such a level.
However, when they saw the Real Phoenix 'Huo Yao', their eyes almost popped out of their sockets. At this moment, they were beyond shocked.
"Old Huo, you really hid your strength well!" Battle Emperor Yu the Great mocked coldly.
After all, the four Divine Nations were of the same breath and branch. There were no secrets between them. For example, the secret of the little monster in the Nine Cauldron Martial House was also shared by the Battle Emperors and Battle Emperors.
However, the Tianfeng War Emperor had managed to nurture such a character without anyone noticing.
Maybe Huo Yuner's strength was not enough to threaten anyone. But what about when Huo Yuner and her Real Phoenix grew? When this real phoenix reached LV 11, which Divine Sea Realm warrior would be a match for it?
If everyone knew about this matter, it would be fine. However, no one knew that the Tianfeng War Emperor had secretly nurtured such a person. It was inevitable that the Kings of the other three Divine Kingdoms would be suspicious.
The Tianfeng War Monarch smiled calmly and said, "Liu Zan, why speak with such an expression? If I wanted to hide it, I naturally wouldn't let Yun 'er fight. "
He was right. From the beginning to the end, the Tianfeng War Emperor did not give Huo Yuner any hints. She was completely on her own. When Huo Yuner chose to reveal her contracted beast, the Tianfeng War Emperor did not stop her.
Hearing this, the monarchs' expressions relaxed. From another point of view, Huo Yuner having a True Phoenix as her contracted beast was actually a good thing for Scandinavia Continent.
There was a limit to their growth in the Divine Kingdom. No one could rebuild the ascension tunnel. All the Divine Sea Realm warriors would be trapped here until their deaths.
There was almost no limit to the growth of a true phoenix. If this true phoenix could grow up, perhaps there really was a chance to rebuild the ascension passage.
"Yo!"
The True Phoenix named Huo Yao suddenly let out a high-pitched cry. It circled around Huo Yuner and then rose up high from behind her. It spread out its wings that were dozens of feet wide. The phoenix's head was at the same level as Luo Zheng, exuding supreme dignity. The phoenix's feathers that were hundreds of feet long circled down and enveloped Huo Yuner.
On each of the feathers of the True Phoenix, there was a burning flame. Strands of Law of Fire spread out along the edge of the flame.
"The third level of Law of Fire …"
When Luo Zheng sensed the strength of the Fire Laws, he had a rough estimate in his heart. The flames that were emitted were endless. While they could bring about destruction, they also contained a kind of exuberant vitality within them.
Although this True Phoenix was only at the ninth level, it gave Luo Zheng a strong sense of pressure. It was even greater than when he faced Cui Xie. A Super Divine Beast was indeed not to be underestimated.
If Huo Yuner had summoned a True Dragon or an Ice Phoenix, the pressure on Luo Zheng would have increased exponentially. The strength of this Super Divine Beast was comparable to a powerful warrior of the fourth or even the fifth level of the Life and Death Realm. Adding Huo Yuner to the mix, even Luo Zheng wouldn't have the confidence to win.
However, Huo Yuner's main cultivation was Law of Fire and Law of Time. The contract beast she summoned through her own bloodline was also a Fire Phoenix!
Luo Zheng had the body of an Immortal Artifact, so he was completely immune to fire. He was just worried that he couldn't find a way to strengthen his body!
A stronger physical body meant that Luo Zheng could use more of the power of the dragon scales. It was no longer difficult for Luo Zheng to collect weapons and refine the Heaven's Evolution Essence. However, Luo Zheng's physical body could not withstand too much power. If he used too much of the power of the dragon scales, his physical body would disintegrate.
Huo Yuner leaned back slightly and sat on the Phoenix's feathers. Then, she pointed at Luo Zheng and said, "Fire Star, Yang Yan Impact!"
"Yo!"
The True Phoenix suddenly raised its head and opened its mouth. The Yuan Qi of Heaven and Earth began to surge and converge into its mouth.
"It's directly absorbing the Yuan Qi of Heaven and Earth?" Luo Zheng was slightly surprised.
When it had absorbed almost all the Yuan Qi of Heaven and Earth, it stared at Luo Zheng, who was not far away.
However, when the True Phoenix was about to spit out flames, Huo Yuner's figure suddenly flashed and came to the Phoenix's head. She put her hand on the side of the Phoenix's head and whispered a few words to it. Then she said to Luo Zheng, "Luo Tianxing, I've already proved it! Now you should understand that if Fire Star spits out Yang Yan Impact, you will be turned into ashes! So you'd better think carefully. It's not too late to regret! "
Luo Zheng was still wondering what Huo Yuner wanted to do. He didn't expect that she would order her contract beast to stop attacking at the last moment. Luo Zheng almost fainted on the ground.
However, her reminder was indeed out of kindness. Although this was a young Phoenix and only at Level Nine, it was still a Super Divine Beast. It was far from being comparable to ordinary Fierce Beasts.
"I think Luo Tianxing should just admit defeat. He's already in the limelight by forcing the Imperial Family to use a Super Divine Beast. I think the Black Iron Divine Kingdom will reward him greatly!"
"A person like Luo Tianxing won't admit defeat so easily. He would have done so before."
"If he doesn't admit defeat, he'll just wait for death. If he wants to court death, no one will stop him. Our Princess Yuner has already given him a chance!"
Most of them didn't like Luo Zheng.
The warriors of the Divine Kingdom's mainland were much more knowledgeable than those of the Central Domain. Moreover, they had a lot of information passed down from the Upper Domain.
Regarding Divine Beasts and Super Divine Beasts, everyone had a rough idea of their strength. For example, the strength of this ninth grade True Phoenix was estimated to be at least at the Life and Death Realm. Even a Life and Death Realm martial artist at the fourth or fifth stage would find it hard to contend with it.
Luo Zheng was a Middle Stage False Tribulation Realm warrior. Even if his talent was amazing, what could he use to fight against this True Phoenix? He would probably be burned to ashes before he could even get close.
Under the watchful eyes of the crowd, Luo Zheng spat out two words, "Thank you, and I'm sorry."
'Thank you' was naturally to thank Huo Yuner for giving him another chance. 'Sorry' was naturally to tell Huo Yuner that he still wouldn't admit defeat.
Huo Yuner's face revealed a regretful expression. When she summoned Huo Yao, she had already made a decision in her heart. Since she was a warrior, she would never be able to escape from fighting with others. Just like how humans were born to eat, this was a rule she couldn't avoid.
Since she couldn't avoid it, she could only try to accept it. Huo Yuner didn't choose to escape.
In other words, this battle with Luo Zheng made her understand her mission and responsibility. She also clearly felt her Imperial Father's expectations for her!
If it were in the past, she would rather choose to admit defeat than summon Huo Yao to kill Luo Zheng.
But this time was different. She didn't want to disappoint her Imperial Father.

Chapter 797
Ordinary flames spread their heat in all directions, making it very difficult to bind them together.
However, the Yang Flame Impact released by this True Phoenix firmly restrained the heat within, so when this Yang Flame Impact was released, it didn't emit too much residual heat. However, there was an unimaginable problem hidden in the blazing white flames.
This Sun Flame was extremely fast, and only one thought flashed through Luo Zheng's mind. His clothes might burn away …
Just as this thought flashed through his mind, his entire body was wrapped in Sun Flame.
"Huff … huff …"
However, from beginning to end, Luo Zheng wasn't worried about his own safety. He was immune to most flames. So far, apart from the mysterious furnace's black fire, Luo Zheng wasn't afraid of any other flames.
Although the Sun Flame spat out by this True Phoenix was frightening, it still couldn't do anything to him by relying on the third layer of Fire Laws.
If an adult True Phoenix came, Luo Zheng wouldn't be able to resist it.
Of course, an adult True Phoenix was a level 14 or even level 15 Super God Beast, comparable to a Realm Lord or an Empyrean. A Super God Beast of that level would be several thousand li long. Against the current Luo Zheng, a single swipe of its claws would be enough to crush him to pieces.
Luo Zheng's body was immersed in the flames and started to rapidly heat up. His entire body was red, just like a piece of red-hot iron.
"It should appear soon." Luo Zheng was also somewhat expectant. His body entering the Saint Tool grade was a considerable upgrade for him. After all, Astral Essence combined with the power of several thousand dragon scales would instantly erupt. When Luo Zheng reached late False Tribulation realm, he might be able to sweep through Life and Death powerhouses!
However, after this Sun Flame attack lasted for a dozen breaths, Luo Zheng's brows furrowed.
"Eh? Why hasn't it appeared … "
Just his body being roasted by the flames wasn't enough for Luo Zheng's body to upgrade. The most important thing was those golden runes. After those runes appeared, Luo Zheng's body could completely absorb the flames and integrate them into his body.
"The flame isn't strong enough." Luo Zheng quickly came to this conclusion.
He did not expect that the flames condensed from the third level of the Law of Fire would not be able to upgrade his body. This was a little troublesome.
That Phoenix should have comprehended more than three levels of the Laws of Fire …
The moment this Phoenix descended, Luo Zheng felt a mysterious Fire Attribute Principle. This Fire Attribute Principle was much more complex than the Fire Attribute Principle contained in this Yang Yan. It should be a higher level Fire Attribute Principle.
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng unexpectedly went against the flow of Yang Yan's attack and approached Huo Yuner.
This Yang Yan Impact spewed out in a straight line and hit the edge of the boundary, bursting into endless sparks.
Under the continuous impact of the Yang Yan, the barrier condensed by the Divine Sea Realm master became somewhat unstable, and one side of the barrier began to sway like ripples in water.
If the barrier he condensed was destroyed by a False Tribulation Realm cultivator, that would be a real disgrace. Therefore, the Divine Sea Realm master waved his hand and a blue light merged into the barrier, stabilizing it even more.
"That's why I said that sometimes people can't be too rigid. Isn't it just admitting defeat? Why be stubborn for the sake of face? Now that he has been burned to ashes, he didn't cherish himself. Losing his life is a small matter, but the most important thing is that he made our Princess feel uncomfortable. This is the biggest sin! "
"That's right, this Luo Tianxing is like a dead rat. Stepping on a dead rat is more disgusting than stepping on it! Princess Yuner didn't want to kill, but he forced her to do it. What a bitch! "
"This kind of person, it's best to kill him a thousand times to vent our hatred!"
The Heavenly Wind Divine Country cultivators saw Huo Yuner leaning on the Phoenix's shoulder with a sad expression on her face. They all hated Luo Zheng to the bone.
In their opinion, Luo Zheng had no chance of survival under the impact of the Blazing Sun, but he had made Huo Yuner feel so uncomfortable. This was the biggest sin.
Huo Yuner's heart was indeed very uncomfortable. Her hand tightly grasped the feathers of the True Phoenix Flame, and her mood fell to the bottom of the valley. She knew that the world of cultivators was so cruel, but couldn't everyone just decide the winner and loser? Why did they have to fight with their lives at every turn? Was it worth it?
In her eyes, Luo Zheng was also a martial artist with a bright future. Dying like this really made her feel very uncomfortable, and even very painful!
But she also understood that her approach was correct. She couldn't retreat. She couldn't let down her father's ardent hope.
But just as she was entangled in distress and self-blame, a head suddenly emerged from the top of the Blazing Sun.
When Huo Yuner saw the head, she was stunned. Then she heard Luo Zheng say, "Stupid bird! Put more effort! "
"Huh? Luo Tianxing, you're fine! " Seeing her opponent come out unscathed, Huo Yuner should have been very depressed, but instead, she had a surprised and happy expression.
This scene between the two of them once again stunned everyone.
It had to be said that these two were the weirdest of weirdos.
This Luo Zheng was actually able to withstand the Blazing Sun's impact, and it looked like nothing had happened to him. Everyone was shocked, but they couldn't figure out why he jumped out and angered the Phoenix. Was a Super Divine Beast so easy to provoke? Did this guy have a masochistic tendency?
And when Huo Yuner saw that Luo Zheng was fine, she was pleasantly surprised …
"Are they … fighting?"
"You ask me, but who should I ask?"
Many cultivators in the square suddenly felt that their worldview had been turned upside down. The scene in front of them made them somewhat unable to understand.
The intelligence of a Super Divine Beast was very high, so how could this True Phoenix named Huo Yao not understand Luo Zheng's provocation?
Huo Yao was Huo Yuner's Contract Beast, but it did not submit to Huo Yuner. The relationship between the two was similar to a friendship, not a slave, so most of the time it would listen to Huo Yuner's orders, but if it became angry, even Huo Yuner would find it difficult to control it.
As soon as Luo Zheng's voice fell, Huo Yao's eyes revealed a raging anger. As a Super Divine Beast, how could it allow a little human to trample on its dignity?
So it suddenly stopped spitting out Blazing Sun. The moment the Blazing Sun's impact ended, Luo Zheng's body was exposed. The temperature of the Blazing Sun's impact had already burned Luo Zheng's clothes to ashes.
"Ah!" Huo Yuner suddenly screamed and turned her head to the side.
Just now, Luo Zheng had gone against the flow of Blazing Sun's impact, and the distance between him and Huo Yuner had shortened a lot. Now that a naked man was facing her, how could she dare to look?
There were some female cultivators present, and some of them turned their heads to the side, but there were also some masculine women who stared at Luo Zheng without the slightest hesitation, even admiring him with relish.
Not to mention Huo Yuner being shy, even Luo Zheng himself was a little embarrassed, but for the sake of this Phoenix's stronger flames, he was willing to risk everything.
"Yo yo!"
Even Luo Zheng did not expect that his provocation would be so effective. This Phoenix could not wait to prepare for the next wave of attack.
All the feathers on its body stood up in an instant, declaring its anger to all living creatures. Even Huo Yuner herself was unable to withstand the heat transmitted from its body, but she had no choice but to temporarily leave this angry phoenix.
"You, why are you still provoking it! Huo Yao is really angry! "Huo Yuner turned her face to the side and complained to Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng did not answer Huo Yuner's question. The battle between him and Huo Yuner was actually a secondary event. Now, he was fully focused on feeling the power of the flames emitted by this Phoenix. He wondered if this wave of attacks would allow him to break through.
After a while, Luo Zheng saw a ring of fire appear around the Phoenix. At this moment, Luo Zheng felt a special Flame Principle power from this ring of fire.
"It's this power!" Luo Zheng's eyes flashed slightly. The moment the Phoenix descended, it contained this special Flame Principle power, but it did not exist in the Blazing Sun's impact.

Chapter 798
The Tianfeng War Emperor was also a cultivator who specialized in fire-type cultivation methods. His comprehension of the fire-type law was much deeper than Huo Yuner's.
Huo Yuner had already comprehended the third level of the fire-type law. Furthermore, she was wearing a jade bracelet that was a semi-divine tool. However, this didn't mean that she could withstand all the power of fire. For example, the phoenix's attack!
The Tianfeng War Emperor was only testing Huo Yuner. He absolutely didn't want to see Huo Yuner suffer any harm. That was why he pulled her out of the barrier.
At this moment, even Zhou Huang wanted to pull Luo Zheng out of the barrier. Since he chose to protect Luo Zheng, he also didn't want Luo Zheng to lose his life here. The problem was, what did Luo Zheng's words mean?
Previously, under the phoenix's attack, Luo Zheng wasn't injured at all. Moreover, his face revealed an unsatisfied expression. What was the purpose of angering the phoenix? Could it be that he really wanted the phoenix to use its full strength?
With this thought in mind, Zhou Huang didn't want to make a move.
Just as the Tianfeng War Emperor pulled Huo Yuner out of the barrier, the phoenix's body suddenly curled up and began to shrink. The ring of fire around it also began to shrink rapidly.
Huo Yuner stared blankly at her contracted beast outside the barrier and said, "Luo Zheng angered it. It actually wanted to release the Nirvana Flames to burn Luo Zheng …"
The phoenix was the symbol of immortality!
The overall strength of this species was slightly weaker than the true dragon clan. However, their vitality was much stronger than the true dragon clan. Even the nine-headed snake divine beast clan with super healing abilities couldn't be compared to the true phoenix clan.
This was because they could continuously be reborn. As long as there were flames, they could be reborn!
Why did Luo Zheng feel a power that surpassed the third level of the fire-type law when Huo Yuner summoned Huo Yao? It was because this phoenix was directly "reborn" from the flames. In other words, before Huo Yao was summoned, it had already "died" once.
From this, it could be seen that the word "death" was a powerless threat in front of the true phoenix clan. This was because they could choose to die at any time, or they could choose to be reborn in any flame!
During Nirvana, the Fire Phoenix was able to release the Nirvana Flame, which was the fourth level of the Law of Fire, also known as the Flame of Rebirth!
There were ten levels to the Laws of Fire. The first level of the Laws of Fire was called 'Blazing Fire', the second level of the Laws of Fire was called 'Burning Ash', the third level of the Laws of Fire was called 'Destruction', and the fourth level of the Laws of Fire was called 'Rebirth'.
After incinerating all things, it could bring forth new life. That was how it was named!
As the phoenix became smaller and smaller, it finally curled up and turned into a round egg!
The phoenix egg flickered with an orange-red luster. It looked very smooth, like a huge gem. However, at this time, no one dared to approach it because endless fire laws began to emerge from the phoenix egg. These laws were basically all fire laws of the fourth layer.
When these laws left the phoenix egg, they transformed into a golden flame storm that began to engulf everything around it!
The Divine Sea Realm powerhouse who created the barrier had a solemn expression. The difference in power between each level of the Laws was huge. Even this Divine Sea Realm powerhouse found the fourth level of the Laws of Fire tricky. However, if this False Tribulation Realm, no, it should be said that this ninth level phoenix could break his barrier, he would lose face. Therefore, he was trying his best to stabilize this barrier.
"Hualala …"
Under the sweeping of this flame storm, the cuboid formed by the barrier had become a fiery hell. At this moment, there was nothing but flames.
Many Divine Sea Realm geniuses in the square were already worried. Although this barrier was created by a Divine Sea Realm powerhouse, what if it was broken? If the flame storm swept out, it would affect many people …
Although they were afraid, seeing that no one retreated, they held back. After all, they were a group of Divine Sea Realm geniuses. They couldn't lose face for their own Martial House and the Divine Nation.
Under the sweeping of this flame storm, Luo Zheng's entire body was red. After a while, a golden rune suddenly appeared on his body!
"It's done!" Luo Zheng was overjoyed.
Then, more and more golden runes began to appear on his hands, feet, face, and shoulders …
Then, those golden runes kept spinning and absorbing the flames. After a while, Luo Zheng looked like a golden bronze man.
Wrapped in these golden runes, Luo Zheng was not only unharmed by the flame storm, but also felt very comfortable, except that his body was a little hot.
Therefore, Luo Zheng walked through the flame storm and slowly approached the phoenix egg.
The phoenix egg was still floating in the center of the barrier. On the surface of the egg, the Law of Fire was constantly flashing, urging an even stronger flame storm to sweep over.
Luo Zheng was not afraid at all. Instead, he flew towards the flame storm and reached out his hand towards the phoenix egg. His fingers gently stroked the surface of the egg.
"It's a little hot, and I can feel that there are living creatures jumping inside. This is the case for the phoenix egg. I wonder if it's the same for the dragon egg …" Luo Zheng did not forget that he had entered the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea. If there was a chance, he would help Qinglong take away the Ancestral Dragon egg.
"Crackle, crackle …"
Under Luo Zheng's touch, the surface of the phoenix egg began to crack rapidly. Suddenly, a small miniature phoenix shot out from the egg. This miniature phoenix circled around and began to grow rapidly.
As the two phoenix cries rang out, the flaming storm finally slowed down …
The phoenix looked at Luo Zheng with a complicated expression. Its flames were completely ineffective against this person!
This ninth grade phoenix was not very old. Otherwise, it would not have been able to form a contract with Huo Yuner and become her contract beast. It could only use the third layer of the Law of Fire. As for the fourth layer of the Law of Fire, it could only be released in its Nirvana state!
Therefore, Nirvana was its most powerful move. Originally, it wanted to use the Nirvana Flame to burn this rude human to ashes. However, it now understood that this guy did not seem to be afraid of flames. What was going on?
This phoenix, which was still in its infancy, could not figure out what was going on.
At this moment, Luo Zheng smiled at the phoenix and said, "Thank you!"
Why are you thanking me? Didn't you just scold me? The real phoenix was somewhat puzzled. However, beasts were more realistic than humans. Even if it was a super divine beast, once it determined that it was not a match for Luo Zheng, it would maintain an attitude of reverence towards Luo Zheng. This was its life instinct after countless years of evolution.
Therefore, the phoenix let out a long cry and flew out of the boundary.
"Remove the boundary and let Huo Yao out," Huo Yuner suddenly said.
Hearing Huo Yuner's words, the Divine Sea Realm powerhouse immediately opened the boundary. After Huo Yao came out of the boundary, he stuck close to Huo Yuner.
After Huo Yuner comforted the phoenix for a while, the real phoenix seemed to be in a better mood. Then, Huo Yuner slowly approached the boundary and said to Luo Zheng, "I lost this battle. You are stronger than me."
Just now, when the Tianfeng War Emperor took Huo Yuner out of the boundary, she had already lost.
Now that she admitted defeat, Huo Yuner was sincerely convinced. She really could not do anything to Luo Zheng.
For some reason, Huo Yuner's heart suddenly calmed down when she did not have to compete with Luo Zheng. She was quite satisfied with this result.
Luo Zheng nodded and smiled, "You are not bad yourself."
"Yes, I know." Huo Yuner was not modest and admitted it generously. She also felt that she was very strong …
However, after looking at the square, she lowered her voice and said to Luo Zheng, "But I still have to remind you. That little monster is really strong. You must be careful. Otherwise, he will kill you!"
In fact, Huo Yuner also realized that Luo Zheng was able to win because of Luo Zheng's special talent. He did not seem to be afraid of flames at all. However, the little monster was not so easy to deal with.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 799
After Luo Zheng's victory, none of the Divine Rank geniuses from the four Divine Kingdoms spoke a word. Most of them were listless.
Previously, when Luo Zheng had defeated Yun Longtao, they might have had the urge to catch up. But now, as long as they had some self-awareness, they knew that even if they tried their best to catch up with him for the rest of their lives, they might not be able to catch up.
Under such terrifying flames, he had actually survived. The only thing he had lost was his clothes. Many people suspected that Luo Zheng wasn't human at all. Or rather, they didn't know what his body was made of. It didn't make sense for him to come out unscathed!
Luo Zheng naturally wouldn't care about their logic. When the golden runes disappeared, Luo Zheng's body had already become a Divine Artifact. More importantly, he had accumulated a considerable amount of Primordial Astral Nova in his body. He could use it to temper his body again.
Although tempering his body was painful, using the Primordial Astral Nova to temper his body was greatly beneficial to Luo Zheng's body. Naturally, he had to endure it.
He stretched his body and felt that his body had entered a new level. A trace of joy appeared on his face. Finally, his gaze fell on Yan Wang, Zhou Huang, and the others on the high platform.
Yan Wang smiled and gave Luo Zheng a thumbs up. Luo Zheng nodded slightly. Then, he looked around and asked in a clear voice, "Who else wants to challenge me?"
The Heavenly Wind Divine Nation had lost. Next, it was the Star Field Divine Nation and the Great Yu Divine Nation who had the right to challenge.
It had to be said that the ones most aggrieved right now were the martial artists of the Wild Star Divine Kingdom. From start to finish, the martial artists of the Wild Star Divine Kingdom had never won. Although the Heavenly Wind Divine Kingdom had lost, the Heavenly Wind Divine Emperor had only sent a single martial artist, and that was Huo Yuner!
Although Huo Yuner had lost, the strength she had displayed had proven everything. Among the younger generation of cultivators in the Divine Kingdom Continent, there weren't many who dared to say that they were her match!
Luo Zheng had first defeated Yun Longtao, then Huo Yuner. Many of the first-tier cultivators in the Martial House didn't dare to challenge him. With their strength, they would be defeated by Luo Zheng in an instant. Other than losing face, there was nothing left.
However, the princes of the four Divine Kingdoms had a chance to fight with Luo Zheng. It was just unknown if they would take that step.
Just as the Royal Family of the Star Field Divine Nation was thinking about it, someone stood out. This person was the Little Monster from the Nine Cauldron Martial House.
In fact, there were many top warriors in the Great Yu Divine Kingdom, including the Nine Cauldrons Martial House. Some of them were even as strong as Yun Longtao. However, only the little monster dared to stand out.
His hunched back suddenly leaped into the air. He somersaulted in the air and instantly disappeared into the barrier. His eyes were filled with intense interest as he looked at Luo Zheng.
"Finally, it's the little monster's turn to appear. I wonder if that brutal guy will be taught a lesson this time!"
"Teach the little monster a lesson? I also want to see this scene. But have you ever seen the Little Monster lose? "
"No, never …"
Even the cultivators in the Nine Cauldron Martial House didn't like the Little Monster. If it wasn't for the Heavenly Feather Sacred Ocean, the Nine Cauldron Martial House wouldn't be willing to bring the Little Monster here.
In fact, the Great Yu Divine Nation didn't intend to send the Little Monster. In the past, when the Heavenly Feather Sacred Ocean opened, the Little Monster was sleeping on the ground. The battles between cultivators had nothing to do with him.
This time, in order to deal with Luo Zheng, Bai Yuerong had personally invited the little monster out.
Therefore, at this moment, Bai Yuerong had a complicated look on her face. She didn't know if her approach was right or wrong.
After seeing the Little Monster, Luo Zheng frowned. He hated this kind of look. After Huo Yuner's reminder, he became more vigilant.
Huo Yuner's strength was already very amazing, but she was so afraid of the Little Monster. This guy's strength couldn't be underestimated.
However, Luo Zheng also knew that the main reason Huo Yuner was afraid of the Little Monster was because of the Little Monster's personality. The other reason was because of his strength.
"Hehe." The Little Monster smacked his lips. He stared at Luo Zheng and said, "How about I challenge you?"
Luo Zheng replied indifferently, "Since you're already standing here, you're naturally challenging me. Why do you need to ask again?"
The Little Monster shrunk his long neck and continued to laugh, "If I lose, can I kill you?"
This guy really talked a lot of nonsense. Luo Zheng could only reply coldly, "According to the rules, it's not allowed."
"That's a pity. I don't know how to fight with others. I only know how to kill. I don't have a good sense of propriety. What should I do?" The Little Monster's black eyes stared at Luo Zheng with a sinister smile.
Luo Zheng smiled slightly, "There's no 100% guarantee in a battle between cultivators. I think we'd better start!" He was too lazy to talk nonsense with the Little Monster.
"Okay!" As soon as Luo Zheng's voice fell, the Little Monster suddenly opened his mouth and let out a sharp smile. The teeth in his mouth didn't look like human teeth. They were extremely sharp, like the teeth of some wild beast.
He suddenly stepped on the barrier below. Like a dexterous monkey, he rushed along the barrier toward Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng didn't like this guy. He even felt a sense of disgust. This kind of disgust was somewhat similar to the disgust of the same sex. Or it could be said that Luo Zheng rejected this guy from the bottom of his heart!
Even Luo Zheng himself couldn't explain why he felt this way.
"Whoosh …"
The Little Monster kept crawling. Suddenly, he jumped up high. With a strange smile on his face, he suddenly grabbed at Luo Zheng!
Luo Zheng's figure flashed slightly. In the blink of an eye, he crossed the space and avoided the Little Monster's grab. He felt very strange. Was this Little Monster going to fight with him?
After the Little Monster entered the arena, he didn't take out his weapon. Secondly, he didn't use his True Essence. He just attacked Luo Zheng with his physical body. Therefore, he even gave up flying. He just ran by stepping on the barrier condensed by the Divine Sea Realm.
Luo Zheng, who had just entered the body of a Divine Tool, was quite confident in his physical body. If the Little Monster wanted to fight with him, Luo Zheng would accompany him to the end. However, he couldn't figure out what the Little Monster was up to at the moment, so he avoided it at once.
After the Little Monster missed its attack, he fell on the barrier again. Then he clicked his tongue and said, "Your strength and talent are not bad. But you're too timid!"
Hearing the Little Monster's provocation, Luo Zheng became more vigilant. What did this guy mean? Did he really want to fight with him with his physical body?
"Don't think too much!" The Little Monster grinned and said, "To deal with you, I don't even need to use my True Essence! I just need to rely on my physical body! "
After the Little Monster finished speaking, many warriors were in an uproar. They all knew that the Little Monster was very powerful. Strictly speaking, although he was a warrior of the False Tribulation Realm, he was not at the same level as these God-level geniuses.
However, everyone had seen the strength Luo Zheng had just shown clearly. Luo Zheng was also very powerful. Even the Real Phoenix couldn't do anything to him!
Wasn't the Little Monster a bit too arrogant to say such words?
Hearing the Little Monster's words, Luo Zheng suddenly showed a weird smile on his face. "You don't seem to be a Body Refining Warrior. You don't have Vital Spirit?"
"No! I don't need Vital Spirit to deal with you! "the Little Monster said again.
"Good." After saying that, Luo Zheng suddenly rose up and directly rose to the top of the barrier.
When warriors of the False Tribulation Realm fought, there was always not enough space. Therefore, the barrier set up by that Divine Sea Realm warrior was not small. Luo Zheng flew up and directly reached the top of the barrier. Looking down at the Little Monster, he asked faintly, "If so, how are you going to fight with me?"
"You!" The Little Monster's face suddenly froze.
Even without using True Essence, the Little Monster's physical strength was very powerful. The problem was that Luo Zheng was far away from him. How was he going to fight with him?
If it were another warrior who wanted to compete with him in physical strength, Luo Zheng would have agreed. Whether it was Vital Spirit, True Essence, or physical strength, Luo Zheng was not afraid of any of them. However, he just couldn't stand this Little Monster being so arrogant in front of everyone.
There were plenty of people who didn't like the little monster. Even though there were plenty of god-level geniuses who had gained enlightenment through sparring with the little monster, breaking through their own bottlenecks and obtaining great benefits, the little monster was not likable in the first place. How many people in this world would like a fellow with a repulsive face, a violent and eccentric temperament?

Chapter 800
This little monster had just said that he wouldn't use Zhen Yuan. Without Zhen Yuan, he couldn't even fly. Luo Zheng had been maintaining that height, so how could he fight with Luo Zheng?
However, the little monster had already boasted, so he didn't use Zhen Yuan, and stepped towards the edge of the barrier.
"Sprint!"
The little monster's hands and feet were long. Although he looked extremely uncoordinated, once he started moving, every part of his body seemed to play a role, and there wasn't the slightest extra movement on his body.
Once he stepped forward, he was like a gecko climbing a wall, quickly advancing along the side of the barrier. When the little monster climbed halfway, he suddenly kicked the barrier wall in front of him, and his whole body directly flipped over, and the direction he fell was exactly where Luo Zheng was.
Seeing that the little monster really didn't use Zhen Yuan, and was actually rushing towards him, Luo Zheng's face revealed a sneer.
Martial artists could use Zhen Yuan to fly, and they could change their position up, down, left, and right. They were very flexible, but the little monster only used the momentum of his sprint to jump towards him.
Luo Zheng's figure flashed, and directly floated behind the little monster.
"Naive!"
After he sneered, he punched the back of the little monster.
Luo Zheng opened the power of 300 dragon scales for this punch. Under the explosion of this power, the little monster was like a rubber ball, and instantly smashed into the bottom of the barrier.
"Huh?" Although Luo Zheng's attack was successful, he found some strange things. The little monster seemed to have no bones, and it gave Luo Zheng a soft feeling. Even after opening the power of 300 dragon scales, the little monster didn't seem to be injured.
The little monster was lying on the bottom of the barrier, and then slowly climbed up. Its dark pupils were like a wild beast in the forest, staring at Luo Zheng, but there was a ferocious smile on its face.
"Don't you plan to use Zhen Yuan?" Luo Zheng asked indifferently, with his hands behind his back.
"No need!" The little monster sneered, "You are not qualified for me to go all out!"
Hearing the little monster's provocation, Luo Zheng's face was still indifferent. Then he said, "If you want to be beaten, then continue!"
That little monster was truly stubborn, but relying on his physical strength alone, he simply wasn't able to resist Luo Zheng. He knew that Luo Zheng was proficient in the Space Laws, so if he didn't rely on his True Essence to fly, he could only use his pure strength to erupt. He simply couldn't even touch Luo Zheng's sleeves.
"Sou!"
The little monster's figure flashed and jumped up again. This time, it didn't climb to the edge of the barrier. Instead, it jumped up high and pounced on Luo Zheng.
It had to be said that this little monster's physical body was also abnormally powerful. Not to mention the late stage of False Tribulation Realm, even Life and Death Realm experts couldn't possess such a powerful physical body.
However, no matter how strong his jumping ability was or how fast his speed was, he still couldn't catch Luo Zheng.
The moment the little monster arrived in front of him, Luo Zheng took a step forward, shuttled through space, and arrived behind the little monster again. Then, he punched again.
With this punch, Luo Zheng activated the power of five hundred dragon scales. In addition to Luo Zheng's own strength, the power of this punch was extremely terrifying.
The little monster jumped up high. Since it had no place to borrow strength in the air, it simply couldn't dodge Luo Zheng's punch.
"Bang!"
The solid punch once again hammered the little monster's back. Thus, the little monster was once again smashed down by Luo Zheng.
He still couldn't injure the little monster. Luo Zheng clenched his fists, and his eyes flickered. This guy's ability to resist attacks was probably not far from his own body's.
However, Luo Zheng and the little monster were fundamentally different. Luo Zheng had transformed his body into a magical treasure body. His body was incomparably sturdy. After being refined by Nirvana Flame, Luo Zheng now possessed a sacred tool body. How could it be so easy to injure him?
The little monster's body was at the other extreme. Its body seemed soft and boneless, and couldn't bear any force at all. Thus, it was very difficult for Luo Zheng to injure it.
"Continue?" Luo Zheng asked indifferently.
The little monster spat, and a trace of a sinister smile appeared on its face. It didn't speak, and with a spring of its legs, it pounced toward Luo Zheng.
This time, Luo Zheng appeared behind the little monster once again. Since the power of five hundred dragon scales wasn't enough, he planned to directly activate the power of eight hundred dragon scales.
However, just as Luo Zheng was about to punch, the little monster's hand suddenly twisted.
Luo Zheng was originally behind the little monster, but with a twist of its arm, it unexpectedly grabbed Luo Zheng from behind!
It was almost impossible for a human to complete this action. This action was similar to twisting one's neck in a full circle. An ordinary person's arm would probably be directly twisted off.
However, the little monster's body was very flexible. With a twist of its arm, it wrapped around Luo Zheng's arm!
The little monster's arm was like a soft hemp rope, and it actually coiled around Luo Zheng's arm.
"Hehe, I already said that to deal with you, I don't need to use true energy." After the little monster finished speaking, a huge force was transmitted from the little monster's body, and it abruptly threw Luo Zheng down!
"Bang!"
Caught off guard, Luo Zheng was smashed to the bottom of the barrier by the little monster. However, the little monster's attack wasn't over. Its arm was still tightly wrapped around Luo Zheng's body. Moreover, the little monster borrowed Luo Zheng's strength to stay in the air, and its arm actually stretched out to a length of seven to eight feet!
"This guy's body is really a monster!"
Lying at the bottom of the barrier, Luo Zheng secretly thought to himself as he looked at the little monster above him.
Then, the little monster's extended arm suddenly began to shrink. With the help of this shrinking force, the little monster rapidly closed the distance between him and Luo Zheng. Its other fist was like a meteor, fiercely smashing toward Luo Zheng's head!
Its body was so soft, but the power it erupted with was extremely astonishing. This guy hadn't even used true energy …
At this critical moment, Luo Zheng stopped thinking. His head tilted, and the little monster's fist hit empty air. The entire barrier also began to shake under the little monster's enormous strength.
"These two guys are both monsters." Bai Yuerong's expression was complicated as she stared at the battle inside the barrier.
At first, when Bai Yuerong found the little monster, she felt that Luo Zheng was doomed to be doomed.
In the four Divine Kingdoms, where there were many God-ranked geniuses, the little monster could become a monster in the eyes of others not because of its special talent, but because of its incomparably strange strength.
It seemed like this guy was an anomaly amongst all humans.
When the little monster was first discovered, the Nine Cauldron Martial Palace stopped him from participating in a trial in the forest.
The trial took place in the Seven Waters Forest located to the north of the Great Yu Divine Kingdom. The forest wasn't very dangerous, and the strongest demon beast was only a Third to Fourth Grade demon beast. Therefore, the Nine Cauldron Martial Palace would usually organize several tens of thousands of Xiantian warriors to participate in the trial.
But, during the trial, they encountered the little monster.
The little monster didn't have a name, and no one knew where it came from. When the little monster was first discovered, it didn't even know how to speak the human language, and its entire body was filled with the characteristics of a demon beast. It loved to eat raw meat, especially the head.
After the few hundred men from the Nine Cauldron Martial Palace entered the Seven Waters Forest, they were all killed by the little monster alone. Everyone died in different ways, but the only similarity was that their heads were smashed open, and their brains were sucked clean!
This incident shocked the upper echelons of the Nine Cauldron Martial Palace, and they sent over a thousand Divine Core warriors into the Seven Water Forest to investigate the cause. In the end, they found this little monster, and it took them more than ten days to finally capture it.
At that time, the little monster was only seven years old, and he was only at the Marrow Refining realm. No one taught him any martial skills, and all his cultivation was based on his own comprehension.
After capturing the little monster, the Nine Cauldron Martial Palace didn't kill him. Although the little monster was dangerous, he was only a threat to ordinary Xiantian warriors. The reason why the higher ups of the Nine Cauldron Martial Palace spared the little monster's life was because they wanted to unravel the little monster's secrets.
But, what they didn't expect was that the little monster's secrets kept increasing. The little monster's bones seemed to be extremely soft, and also extremely tough!
